### [1] [Drift-Aware Multi-Target Space Tug Logistics Using Natural Orbital Precession](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22974)
*Omer Burak Iskender,Leonard Felicetti,Kaan Gokturk,David Tellett,Adam Baker*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种漂移感知（drift-aware）轨迹优化框架，将地球扁率引起的节点进动（nodal precession）从传统任务设计中的扰动转化为任务设计资源，用于降低主动碎片清除和在轨服务任务中多交会（multi-rendezvous）的高昂推进剂成本。该框架的三大贡献包括：增强漂移轨道设计、全局每航段时间预算优化器以及序列恢复测试。在ESA Kessler Run竞赛基准问题上，仅使用解析转移模型和单核心分钟级计算即可接近基准成本，并支持在轨快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动碎片清除（active debris removal）和在轨服务（in-orbit servicing）等任务需要访问多个轨道面朝向不同的目标，传统任务设计将地球扁率导致的节点进动视为扰动并加以抵消，导致推进剂成本过高。现有工作在太阳同步轨道（Sun-synchronous regime）下仅调整高度来控制漂移，效率有限。

Method: 提出了三项方法论贡献：(1) 增强漂移轨道（enhanced drift orbit）——同时调控中间轨道的大小、形状和倾角（inclination），利用倾角作为主导杠杆来调节差分进动率，比仅调整高度的方案更廉价；(2) 全局每航段时间预算优化器——显式处理并解决漂移时间分配与下游节点几何之间的耦合，避免贪婪分配（greedy allocation）策略；(3) 序列恢复测试——仅从碎片目录独立推导出一个与已发布参考结果一致的移除顺序。

Result: 在竞赛转移时间规则下，将该框架应用于已发布的任务划分上，仅使用解析转移模型和分钟级单核心计算，即紧密匹配已发布的基准成本。通过与广泛使用的飞行动力学工具进行交叉验证，量化了解析模型的保真限度并限定了实际执行的成本上限。

Conclusion: 该框架的构造性算法（constructive nature）支持在错过机动后快速进行在轨重规划（in-orbit replanning），为未来大规模主动碎片清除任务提供高效、可部署的轨迹优化方案。

Abstract: Multi-rendezvous missions for active debris removal and in-orbit servicing incur prohibitive propellant costs when their targets differ in the orientation of their orbital planes, and classical mission design treats the nodal precession induced by Earth's oblateness as a perturbation to be cancelled. This paper presents a drift-aware trajectory optimisation framework that instead exploits that precession as a mission-design resource, benchmarked against the published winning solution to the European Space Agency's Kessler Run trajectory-optimisation competition as a trusted reference. Three methodological contributions are made. First, an enhanced drift orbit shapes the size, shape, and inclination of an intermediate orbit to tune the differential precession rate, substantially cheaper than the altitude-only designs of prior work in the Sun-synchronous regime, where inclination is the dominant lever. Second, a global per-leg time-budget optimiser makes explicit and resolves the coupling between drift-time allocation and downstream nodal geometry that defeats greedy allocation. Third, a sequence-recovery test independently re-derives a removal order consistent with the published reference from the debris catalogue alone. Applied to the full debris campaign on the published mission partition under the competition's transfer-time rules, the framework closely matches the published benchmark cost using only analytical transfer models and minutes of single-core computation. A cross-validation anchored to a widely used flight-dynamics tool quantifies the fidelity limits of the analytic model and bounds the cost of literal execution, and the constructive nature of the algorithm supports rapid in-orbit replanning after a missed manoeuvre.

</details>

### [2] [A TQFT-based Platform for Efficient Computation of Knot Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23551)
*Amena Al Rawi,Hisham Sati,Vivek Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个交互式网络平台，统一了费曼带状图（Feynman ribbon diagrams, FRDs）的构造、高阶陈-西蒙斯（Chern–Simons）扭结不变量的计算，以及高交叉数下FRD型扭结的识别。这是首个将图示化构造、张量网络（tensor network）评估、不变量计算与扭结识别整合在同一工作流中的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏一个统一的交互式工具，能够同时支持费曼带状图的构造、高秩陈-西蒙斯扭结不变量的计算以及FRD型扭结的识别，导致相关工作流割裂，限制了研究效率与可重复性。

Method: 开发了一个交互式网页平台，用户可在单一可视化环境中将FRD构造为张量网络，计算其陈-西蒙斯不变量，并利用得到的拓扑不变量数据区分和识别对应的扭结。平台采用树状结构（tree-structured）的图示方法，自然地表示树状扭结（arborescent knots）。

Result: 该平台成功实现了FRD的图示化构造、张量网络评估、高阶陈-西蒙斯不变量计算以及FRD型扭结的识别功能，并在高交叉数下完成了对FRD型扭结的区分与匹配。

Conclusion: 该平台是首个将图示化构造、张量网络评估、不变量计算和扭结识别整合在统一工作流中的工具，为扭结理论（knot theory）与张量网络交叉领域的研究提供了新的基础设施。

Abstract: We present an interactive web platform that unifies the construction Feynman ribbon diagrams (FRDs), the evaluation of higher-rank Chern--Simons knot invariants, and the identification of FRD-like knots at higher crossing numbers. These tree-structured diagrams naturally represent arborescent knots, which we refer to throughout as FRD-like knots. Within a single visual environment, users can construct an FRD as a tensor network, evaluate its associated Chern--Simons invariants, and use the resulting invariant data to distinguish and identify the corresponding knot. To our knowledge, this is the first platform to combine diagrammatic construction, tensor-network evaluation, invariant computation, and knot identification within a unified workflow.

</details>

### [3] [Generalized Bloch-like ground states in distinct electron-site configurations derived from the same quasiperiodic structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23180)
*Yuki Yamamoto,Nayuta Takemori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种基于广义傅里叶变换（generalized Fourier transform）的系统性方法，用以在准周期系统（quasiperiodic systems）中构造Sutherland-Kalugin-Katz (SKK)态，并成功将其应用于Ammann-Beenker (AB)拼图的顶点模型（vertex model）与对偶模型（dual model），揭示了两者基态中SKK态的结构对应关系，表明该态反映了AB拼图的内在准周期结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期系统因缺乏平移对称性（translational symmetry）无法直接应用布洛赫定理（Bloch's theorem），SKK态被提出作为广义布洛赫态，但此前研究多依赖猜设性论证（ansatz-based arguments），缺乏从给定哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）出发系统构造SKK态的方法。

Method: 基于紧束缚模型（tight-binding models）中产生算符（creation operators）的广义傅里叶变换，发展了一套构造性方法；再将该框架应用于源自同一准周期性但电子位点配置不同的AB拼图顶点模型与对偶模型。

Result: 该构造复现了顶点模型基态中先前报道的SKK态，并以高保真度（high fidelity）在对偶模型基态中发现了一个对应的SKK态。

Conclusion: 两种SKK态之间的结构对应关系表明，SKK态并非依赖于特定的电子位点（electron sites）选择，而是反映了AB拼图内在的准周期性（quasiperiodicity）。

Abstract: Sutherland-Kalugin-Katz (SKK) states have been proposed as generalized Bloch-like states in quasiperiodic systems where the absence of translational symmetry prevents the application of Bloch's theorem. However, previous studies have largely relied on ansatz-based arguments, and a systematic procedure for constructing the SKK state from a specified Hamiltonian has remained unclear. In this work, we develop a constructive approach based on a generalized Fourier transform of creation operators in tight-binding models. We then apply this framework to vertex and dual models on the Ammann-Beenker (AB) tiling that stem from the same quasiperiodicity but differ in their electron-site configurations. Our construction reproduces the previously reported SKK state in the ground state of the vertex model and reveals a corresponding SKK state in the ground state of the dual model with high fidelity. The structural correspondence between the two SKK states indicates that such states are not tied to a particular choice of electron sites and reflect the underlying quasiperiodicity of the AB tiling.

</details>


### [4] [Local micromechanics in a mean-field model of glasses reveal key properties of its non-equilibrium RSB phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23603)
*Makoto Suda,Edan Lerner,Eran Bouchbinder*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在一个平均场玻璃模型（mean-field glass model）中，通过定义力单极子响应函数（force monopole response），建立了非平衡复制对称破缺（Replica-Symmetry-Breaking, RSB）相与有效随机势描述之间的精确关系，并提取了与实验室玻璃中玻色峰（boson peak）相关的特征频率标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥合平均场玻璃模型与有限维玻璃（finite-dimensional glasses）之间的鸿沟，作者借鉴计算机玻璃领域的最新进展，在平均场框架内定义微观力学响应函数（micromechanical response function），旨在建立非平衡RSB相与有效随机势描述之间的联系，并给出可实验观测的桥梁性物理量。

Method: 在平均场玻璃模型中定义对局部力单极子（local force monopole）的线性响应函数，建立力单极子刚度（force monopole stiffness）与全局磁化率（global susceptibilities）之间的精确关系。随后利用该响应统计学计算模型的振动谱（vibrational spectrum），并采用边缘稳定性判据（marginal stability criterion）提取软玻璃态模的特征频率/刚度标度。

Result: 获得了非平衡RSB相与有效随机势描述之间的严格对应关系；振动谱可完全由单极子响应统计量计算得到；通过边缘稳定性判据提取的特征频率标度与有限维实验室玻璃中的玻色峰相关联。

Conclusion: 该微观力学可观测量构成了模型集体自由度（collective degrees of freedom）的具体实现，为连接平均场玻璃模型与有限维玻璃提供了桥梁；提取的特征频率标度与实验室玻璃的玻色峰存在关联。

Abstract: A recently formulated mean-field model of glasses features an equilibrium, zero-temperature Replica-Symmetry-Breaking (RSB) transition in some parameter range. In this range, the model's solution in the Replica-Symmetric phase is expressed in terms of an effective, self-consistent random potential for uncoupled degree of freedoms, where the transition to the RSB phase is characterized by the emergence of spectral-edge localized modes and a pseudogapped quartic vibrational spectrum, resulting in a finite spin-glass susceptibility. These properties are preserved in numerical solutions of the model under non-equilibrium conditions, i.e., upon an instantaneous quench. Inspired by recent advances in computer glasses, we define a micromechanical response function --- the linear response to local force monopoles --- in the framework of the mean-field model. We establish exact relations between the force monopole stiffness and global susceptibilities, which suggest a close correspondence between the non-equilibrium RSB phase of the model and the above-mentioned effective random potential description. As such, the obtained micromechanical observables constitute a concrete realization of the collective degrees of freedom of the model, offering a bridge between a glassy mean-field model and finite-dimensional glasses. We show that the model's vibrational spectrum can be computed solely from the monopole response statistics and, by employing a marginal stability criterion, we extract a characteristic frequency/stiffness scale of soft glassy modes, which is related to the boson peak in finite-dimensional, laboratory glasses.

</details>


### [5] [Nanoscale linear response of strongly disordered stable solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23768)
*D. V. Babin,I. O. Raikov,Y. M. Beltukov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 强无序固体在纳米尺度下，其无序平均线性响应可由一种修正的连续介质理论描述，其中响应系数是局域的但非局域地依赖于结构性质；稳定性条件自然导致相关Wishart无序，在强无序极限下长波长响应简化为标量无序诱导的对比场，弹性中该场描述了刚性纳米颗粒与边界周围硬化壳的形成，其特征范围由非仿射长度决定，分子动力学模拟和弹性核直接计算验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无序固体（strongly disordered solids）在纳米尺度表现出传统连续介质理论（continuum theory）无法描述的独特性质，因此需要发展一种新的理论框架来刻画其无序平均响应行为。

Method: 提出一种修正的连续介质理论，其中响应系数在统计上是局域的，但非局域地依赖于结构性质；稳定性条件要求响应算子半正定，自然导出相关Wishart无序（correlated Wishart disorder）；通过分子动力学模拟（molecular-dynamics simulations）对Lennard-Jones玻璃和模型聚合物进行验证，并直接计算非局域弹性核（nonlocal elastic kernels）。

Result: 在强无序极限下，长波长响应简化为标量无序诱导的对比场（scalar disorder-induced contrast field）；在弹性中该场描述了刚性纳米颗粒和边界周围形成硬化壳（stiff shell），其特征范围由非仿射长度（nonaffine length）确定；分子动力学模拟证实了理论预测；弹性核的直接计算表明在硬化界面尺度上响应主要是局域的。

Conclusion: 所提出的修正连续介质理论能够有效描述强无序固体在纳米尺度的无序平均线性响应，揭示了非仿射长度作为硬化壳特征尺度的物理意义，且响应在相关尺度上主要表现为局域性。

Abstract: Strongly disordered solids exhibit distinctive properties at the nanoscale, where conventional continuum theory breaks down. We show that their disorder-averaged linear response can be described by a modified continuum theory in which the response coefficients are local but depend nonlocally on the structural properties. The stability criterion requires the response operator to be positive semidefinite, which naturally leads to a correlated Wishart disorder. In the limit of strong disorder, these equations reduce the long-wavelength response to a scalar disorder-induced contrast field. In elasticity, this field describes the formation of a stiff shell around rigid nanoparticles and boundaries, whose characteristic extent is set by the nonaffine length. The predictions are confirmed by molecular-dynamics simulations of a Lennard-Jones glass and a model polymer. Direct calculation of the nonlocal elastic kernels further shows that the response is predominantly local on the scale of the stiffened interphase.

</details>


### [6] [Coupling of Cation and Anion Dynamics in Solid Electrolytes Revealed by Virtual Isotopic Substitution Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24133)
*Xintian Wang,Andrew L. Goodwin,Theodosios Famprikis*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过虚拟同位素替代分子动力学（VISMD）模拟，本文表征了模型体系α-Li₂SO₄中阳离子平移动力学与阴离子转动动力学之间的相互耦合，发现锂扩散与硫酸根转动的扩散率均与各自同位素质量呈幂律关系，且二者之间存在双向耦合；锂扩散的表观活化能随硫酸根动力学加速而降低，为阳离子与阴离子动力学之间的模式耦合（mode-coupling）提供了明确证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于快离子导体中阳离子平移与阴离子转动动力学之间的耦合机制尚缺乏系统理解。本研究旨在通过独立调控锂和氧的同位素质量，分别控制锂平移扩散率与硫酸根转动扩散率，揭示二者之间的相互作用关系。

Method: 以α-Li₂SO₄为模型体系，采用虚拟同位素替代分子动力学（virtual isotopic substitution molecular dynamics, VISMD）模拟方法。通过独立改变锂和氧的质量，分别调控锂的平移扩散率和硫酸根的转动扩散率，并在变温/变质量条件下系统采集数据。

Result: 锂平移扩散率和硫酸根转动扩散率均与各自组分的同位素质量呈幂律关系；改变任一扩散率会对另一扩散率产生可观测的影响，即阴离子与阳离子扩散动力学之间存在双向耦合（bidirectional coupling）；锂扩散的表观活化能（apparent activation energy）强烈依赖于同位素质量，随硫酸根动力学加速而降低。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了α-Li₂SO₄中阳离子平移运动与阴离子转动运动之间存在显著的模式耦合效应，该结论为理解快离子导体中不同自由度之间的协同动力学行为提供了微观层面的支持。

Abstract: We characterise the interplay between cation-translational and anion-rotational dynamics on the model system $\rm α\text{-}Li_{2}SO_{4}$ through virtual isotopic substitution molecular dynamics (VISMD) simulations. We independently control in turn the lithium translational- and sufate rotational- diffusivity by varying the masses of lithium and oxygen, respectively, showing that both diffusivities exhibit power-law relationships with the masses of each individual component. Changing either diffusivity produces an effect on the other; i.e. there exists a bidirectional coupling between anion and cation diffusive dynamics. From our variable-temperature/variable-mass dataset we demonstrate that the apparent activation energy for lithium diffusion depends strongly on the isotope mass, decreasing with accelerated sulfate dynamics; an observation which provides clear evidence of mode-coupling between cation and anion dynamics.

</details>


### [7] [A Grand-Canonical Solution to a Class of Random Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24455)
*Izat B Baybusinov,Enrico M Fenoaltea,Zhen Han,Yi-Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，将匹配问题、旅行商问题（Traveling Salesman Problem）以及最小权重k-因子问题（minimum weight k-factor problem）等组合优化问题重新表述为排列模型（arrangement model），并利用巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）和化学势（chemical potentials）松弛拓扑约束，得到一个多项式时间近似算法，揭示了组合优化与无序系统统计力学（statistical physics of disordered systems）之间的新联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题（如匹配问题、旅行商问题）在计算上通常属于NP-hard，现有算法在效率和通用性方面存在局限；作者希望构建一个统一的理论框架，既能近似求解多类问题，又能揭示其与统计力学的深层关联。

Method: 将组合优化问题重新表述为排列模型（arrangement model），并将其转化为巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble），引入化学势（chemical potentials）来松弛严格的拓扑约束，从而获得解析解。

Result: 该解析解可作为多项式时间（polynomial-time）算法，为任意k值和链接权重分布（link-weight distributions）计算近似最小代价。

Conclusion: 该框架与现有方法互补，并揭示了组合优化（combinatorial optimization）与无序系统统计力学（statistical physics of disordered systems）之间的新联系。

Abstract: We introduce a general framework to solve a class of combinatorial opti- mization problems, including the matching problem, the Traveling Salesman Problem, and also the minimum weight k-factor problem. By reformulating these problems as an arrangement model, we recast the optimization task into a grand-canonical ensemble, where chemical potentials are used to re- lax strict topological constraints. The analytical solution found can serve as a polynomial-time algorithm to compute an approximate minimum cost for arbitrary k and link-weight distributions. Our framework is complementary to existing approaches and reveals new connections between combinatorial optimization and the statistical physics of disordered systems.

</details>


### [8] [Learning limit cycles via Hebbian synaptic plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24478)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体物理中，变分拟设（variational ansätze）是使用有限资源逼近复杂基态的核心工具。近期量子技术催生了基于分层参数化演化（layered parameterized evolutions）的新型拟设，但判断它们是否能表示临界基态（critical ground states）面临根本性困难：临界态关联跨越所有尺度，而有限电路深度限制了关联的传播范围。

Method: 借鉴张量网络中的有限资源标度（finite-resource scaling），为每个拟设定义一个涌现关联长度（emergent correlation length）ξ_D，即拟设能忠实捕获临界关联的最长距离。ξ_D 随精炼参数 D 增长满足 ξ_D ∝ D^κ，定义标度指数 κ 衡量架构将资源转化为长程关联的效率。将该框架应用于临界横向场伊辛模型，以参数化演化的电路深度为 D，比较了近邻与长程生成器（generators）以及生成器分开作用或合并作用的层结构。

Conclusion: 准粒子分析（quasiparticle analysis）表明有限深度会在负责长程关联的低能模周围留下一个宽度为 ξ_D^{-1} 的未分辨窗口。涌现关联长度因此充当红外分辨尺度（infrared resolution scale），为临界态制备提供基准，并为设计资源高效的变分架构提供指导。

Abstract: Variational ansätze are a cornerstone of quantum many-body physics, providing compact approximations to complex ground states using finite resources. Recent quantum-technology advances have introduced a new class based on layered parameterized evolutions. Assessing whether they can represent critical ground states is challenging: correlations span all length scales, while finite circuit depth limits how far they extend. Building on finite-resource scaling from tensor networks, we assign each ansatz an emergent correlation length $ξ_D$, the longest range over which it faithfully captures critical correlations. Its growth with refinement parameter $D$, $ξ_D \propto D^κ$, defines an exponent $κ$ measuring how efficiently an architecture converts resources into long-distance correlations. Applying this framework to the critical transverse-field Ising model, with $D$ the circuit depth of parameterized evolutions, we compare ansätze with nearest-neighbor and long-range generators, and layers where generators act separately or combined. All ansätze are compatible with algebraic growth, but fitted exponents range from $κ\simeq1$ to $κ\simeq3$. Exponential interactions give the largest exponents, while power-law interactions stay close to nearest-neighbor behavior, showing long-range support alone gives no scaling advantage. Combined-generator layers generally outperform separable ones, so layer organization matters alongside interaction range. Finally, a quasiparticle analysis shows finite depth leaves an unresolved window of width $ξ_D^{-1}$ around the low-energy modes responsible for long-distance correlations. The emergent correlation length thus acts as an infrared resolution scale, providing a benchmark for critical-state preparation and a guide for designing resource-efficient variational architectures.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: De Finetti定理将排列对称性转化为乘积态的近似混合，为经典与量子统计中的广泛约化提供了理论依据。作者希望将这一框架推广到状态受量子信道（quantum channel）不动点约束的情形——该条件涵盖了任意紧致对称群（compact symmetry group）的不变性——从而发展出更一般的de Finetti层次结构。

Result: 证明了多个de Finetti定理：以O(√log n/n)收敛速度的双面扩展定理（double-sided extension theorem）；维度依赖性仅由不动点代数的块结构决定、在极大环面情形恢复与维度无关的经典行为的插值定理（interpolation theorem）；以及Bose对称变体（Bose-symmetric variant）。对于固定局部维度，舍入方案可在1/ε的多项式时间内实现，补全了已知的高效外层次结构。

Abstract: De Finetti theorems convert permutation symmetry into approximate mixtures of product states and thereby justify a wide range of reductions in classical and quantum statistics. In this work we study de Finetti hierarchies in which the feasible states are additionally constrained to be fixed points of quantum channels, a condition that subsumes invariance under arbitrary compact symmetry groups. Combining the mean-ergodic theorem with the structure theory of conditional expectations, we prove a tight bound on the entanglement-assisted classical capacity of the dual of a conditional expectation, block-wise distortion bounds for informationally complete measurements adapted to fixed point algebras, and an exact type-based refinement of the chain rule for permutation-invariant states. Exploiting Schur-Weyl duality and Gelfand-Tsetlin bases, we further show that the rounding scheme producing certifiably good separable inner approximations for (constrained) separability problems can be implemented in time polynomial in $1/ε$ for fixed local dimensions, complementing the known efficient outer hierarchies. Result: 将PEPRino算法应用于两比特和三比特量子系统的量子傅里叶变换（QFT）最优实现，并在两比特场景下与基于Nelder-Mead方法的Chopped Random Basis (CRAB) 算法进行对比。结果表明，PEPRino在迭代步数和计算时间上均展现出更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: PEPRino算法在量子最优控制中无需超参数调优和梯度计算，能够高效生成高保真度控制协议，在收敛效率上优于传统CRAB算法，展示了响应理论框架在量子控制领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Optimal control problems arise in a wide range of scientific disciplines, but the corresponding optimization algorithms often display a strong dependence on hyperparameters that significantly influence performance and convergence. For the optimal implementation of quantum algorithms, these challenges are further amplified by high-dimensional control landscapes and the need for high-fidelity operations. Here, we propose an algorithm for optimal control problems in quantum computing to efficiently generate high-fidelity control protocols for multi-qubit systems in a hyperparameter and gradient free manner. The method, referred to as Pulse Engineering via Projection of response functions at infinite nonlinear order (PEPRino), leverages the framework of response theory to navigate the control landscape to find high-fidelity implementations. This is achieved by determining the control landscape via response functions to infinite order, efficiently evaluated by resummation in terms of the first and second order response function. To demonstrate the approach, we apply it to quantum systems consisting of two and three qubits for the optimal implementation of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT). We benchmark the proposed algorithm against the Chopped Random Basis (CRAB) algorithm utilizing the Nelder-Mead method, focusing on the 2-qubit scenario. The results indicate faster convergence regarding iteration steps and computational time, highlighting the advantages of our approach.

</details>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [31] [A uniqueness theorem for the variational free energy decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22710)
*Michael P. Rubin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个变分自由能（variational free energy）及其精确分解的唯一性刻画定理：在若干温和正则性条件下，唯一满足$\log Z = -G + \Delta$形式精确分解的泛函对$(G, \Delta)$就是变分自由能$F$与相对熵（relative entropy）$D$，从而将变分推断中的证据分解（evidence decomposition）从Gibbs-Bogoliubov不等式推广为一种唯一的泛函恒等式（exact identity），并给出了每个假设条件都是必要的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于$\alpha$散度（$\alpha$-divergence）或Rényi散度（Rényi divergence）构建的变分目标函数虽然保留了$\log Z$的有用界（bounds），却不再具备类似Gibbs-Bogoliubov不等式或贝叶斯证据分解那样的精确恒等式（exact identity）。这自然引出了一个根本性问题：究竟哪些泛函能够允许这种精确分解形式？

Method: 作者将问题转化为寻找满足$\log Z = -G + \Delta$的泛函对$(G, \Delta)$，其中$\Delta$仅依赖于$Q$和$\pi$，$G$关于参考测度和权重可加分离，且$\Delta$非负且在$Q=\pi$处精确为零。通过将假设条件约化为一个从正函数乘法群到实数加法群的同态（homomorphism），并利用相对熵在最小值处的二阶消失（second-order vanishing）性质消去该同态，从而完成刻画。

Result: 证明了在该条件下唯一可能的解是$G=F$（变分自由能）且$\Delta = D(\cdot\|\pi)$（相对熵）。给出了反例说明每个假设条件都是不可或缺的。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画的是分解式（decomposition）而非散度本身，因此与Shore-Johnson、Csiszár和Amari等人对相对熵的公理化刻画（axiomatic characterizations）在逻辑上互补，既不依赖也不扩展这些经典结果。

Abstract: For a finite system with reference measure $p$ and positive weight $\ell$, the variational free energy $F(Q;p,\ell)=D(Q\,\|\,p)-E_Q[\log\ell]$ satisfies the exact identity $\log Z(p,\ell)=-F(Q;p,\ell)+D(Q\,\|\,π)$, where $Z$ is the partition function and $π$ the associated Gibbs measure. For a uniform reference measure this is the Gibbs-Bogoliubov inequality of mean-field theory; for a Bayesian model it is the evidence decomposition of variational inference. Variational objectives built from $α$- or Rényi divergences retain useful bounds on $\log Z$ but not identities of this form, which raises the question of which functionals admit an exact decomposition. We prove the following characterization. Suppose a pair $(G,Δ)$ satisfies $\log Z=-G+Δ$ with $Δ$ a function of $Q$ and $π$ alone; suppose $G$ is additively separable into a term depending on the reference measure and a term depending on the weight, with mild regularity; and suppose $Δ$ is nonnegative and vanishes precisely at $Q=π$. Then $G=F$ and $Δ=D(\cdot\,\|\,π)$. The proof reduces the hypotheses to a homomorphism from the multiplicative group of positive functions into $(\mathbb{R},+)$ and removes it using the second-order vanishing of relative entropy at its minimum. Counterexamples show that each hypothesis is needed. The result characterizes the decomposition rather than the divergence, and is therefore complementary to the axiomatic characterizations of relative entropy due to Shore-Johnson, Csiszár and Amari, which it neither uses nor extends.

</details>

### [32] [Autonomous Physical Computation: A Categorical Closure Criterion for Physical and Neuromorphic Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23902)
*Nima Dehghani*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出自主物理计算（autonomous physical computation）的闭合判据（closure criterion），基于波-粒子漫步者（wave–particle walker）模型将记忆与计算区分开来，并指出只有当物理读出的盆（basin）耦合回操作选择时，记忆才转化为自主计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层（physical reservoirs）、神经形态器件（neuromorphic devices）和波介导系统（wave-mediated systems）虽具备记忆、反馈和丰富的状态依赖动力学，但这些特性本身并不等同于自主计算。本文旨在建立一个判据，以区分纯粹的物理记忆系统与真正的自主物理计算系统。

Method: 将波-粒子漫步者建模为频闪储层（stroboscopic reservoir），其波场存储粒子撞击的指数衰减痕迹，并通过局部斜率耦合（local slope coupling）引导后续运动。该模型将物理写入、存储、读取、反馈和外部触发的擦除过程加以分离。在此基础上，将计算定义为鲁棒的粗粒化过渡保持（robust coarse-grained transition preservation），并引入闭合条件（closure condition）：内部物理读出状态必须选择下一步物理操作。

Result: 该判据将波-粒子漫步者归类为具有真正图灵类原语（Turing-like primitives）的波记忆机器（wave-memory machine），但不是闭合的自主物理计算机，因为擦除相移（erasing phase shift）是由外部施加的。这一区分表明，当物理读出盆耦合回操作选择时，记忆便成为自主计算。

Conclusion: 该框架将上述区别转化为一个设计原则：记忆转化为自主计算的关键在于物理读出盆与操作选择之间的回环耦合（coupling back）。这为设计真正的自主物理计算系统提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Physical reservoirs, neuromorphic devices, and wave-mediated systems often possess memory, feedback, and rich state-dependent dynamics, but these properties do not by themselves establish autonomous computation. Here we develop a closure criterion for autonomous physical computation, motivated by the wave--particle walker. We formulate the walker as a stroboscopic reservoir with state, where the wave field stores an exponentially decaying trace of previous droplet impacts and guides future motion through local slope coupling. This model separates physical writing, storage, reading, feedback, and externally triggered erasure. We then define computation as robust coarse-grained transition preservation: a physical map implements an abstract transition only when a coarse-graining satisfies compositionality, with abstract states realized by separated physical basins and transitions stable under noise. Autonomous physical computation requires a further closure condition: an internal physical readout state must select the next physical operation. This criterion classifies the wave--particle walker as a wave-memory machine with genuine Turing-like primitives, but not as a closed autonomous physical computer, because the erasing phase shift is externally imposed. The framework turns this distinction into a design principle: memory becomes autonomous computation when physical readout basins are coupled back to operation selection.

</details>

### [33] [pyALDIC: A Python Implementation of Augmented Lagrangian Digital Image Correlation with a GUI, Adaptive Meshing, and Mask-Aware Subset Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22755)
*Zixiang Tong,Jin Yang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: pyALDIC 是一个基于增强拉格朗日数字图像相关法（AL-DIC）的开源 Python 实现，用于全场位移与应变测量，集成了图形用户界面、可脚本化 API、自适应四叉树网格、掩码感知子集分割以及 Numba 加速，并附有自动化测试、文档和可复现示例，支持跨平台使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一款开源、可复现且跨平台的数字图像相关法（DIC）软件实现，填补增强拉格朗日数字图像相关法（AL-DIC）在 Python 生态中的工具缺口，并兼顾易用性与计算效率。

Method: 采用增强拉格朗日数字图像相关法（AL-DIC）算法框架，结合自适应四叉树网格（adaptive quadtree meshing）与掩码感知子集分割（mask-aware subset splitting）处理裂纹与孔洞区域；提供可选的局部数字图像相关法（Local DIC）与 AL-DIC 求解器模式，并利用 Numba 加速计算。

Result: 通过合成位移场、刚体运动、I型开裂、自适应细化及实验单轴拉伸等验证案例，展示了软件的正确性与有效性。

Conclusion: pyALDIC 以 BSD-3-Clause 许可通过 PyPI、GitHub 和 Zenodo 公开发布，为全场位移与应变测量提供了一款可靠、可复现且跨平台的开源工具。

Abstract: pyALDIC is an open-source Python implementation of augmented Lagrangian digital image correlation (AL-DIC) for full-field displacement and strain measurement. The software combines a graphical user interface with a scriptable Python API and supports adaptive quadtree meshing, mask-aware subset splitting near cracks and holes, and selectable Local DIC and AL-DIC solver modes. Numba acceleration enables efficient analysis, while automated tests, documentation, and reproducible examples support reliable use acrossWindows, macOS, and Linux. Verification cases include synthetic displacement fields, rigid-body motion, Mode-I cracking, adaptive refinement, and experimental uniaxial tension. pyALDIC is distributed through PyPI, GitHub, and Zenodo under a BSD-3-Clause license for reproducibility. pyALDIC is openly available at https://github.com/zachtong/pyALDIC.

</details>

### [34] [Scale Weight Decay and Train Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23777)
*Anuj Apte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种缩放权重衰减（scaled weight decay）方法，通过将权重衰减系数按峰值学习率分数 $\eta/\eta_{\max}$ 缩放，在保留权重衰减稳定性的同时避免了持续权重衰减引入的渐近偏差，并在混合专家模型的训练中实现了约 30% 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放律（scaling laws）推动神经网络的训练数据量持续增长，但标准训练中使用的恒定解耦权重衰减（constant decoupled weight decay）会导致网络权重在训练过程中持续缩小，引入了额外的渐近偏差（asymptotic bias）。

Method: 受 Robbins–Monro 条件启发，提出将权重衰减系数按峰值学习率分数 $\eta/\eta_{\max}$ 缩放。证明了该缩放权重衰减保留了随机梯度下降（stochastic gradient descent, SGD）和非欧几里得谱优化器 Muon 对应的无正则化方法的渐近平稳性（asymptotic stationarity）保证。通过稳态分析（steady-state analysis）解释了标准权重衰减下权重范数持续缩小，而缩放权重衰减下权重范数趋于常数的原因。

Result: 在参数规模为 72 到 9.3 亿的混合专家模型（mixture-of-experts, MoE）上，以每活跃参数约 600 个 token 训练时，采用缩放权重衰减的 Muon（Muon-SW）在达到相同验证损失（validation loss）时比使用相同超参数的 Muon 一致地快约 30%。

Conclusion: 若该趋势在更大规模上持续成立，该方法有望显著加速前沿模型的预训练，且实现仅需数行代码。

Abstract: The discovery of scaling laws has motivated training neural networks on ever increasing quantities of data. This is typically done with a constant decoupled weight decay which causes the network weights to shrink steadily over the course of training. Taking inspiration from the Robbins--Monro conditions, we propose to scale weight decay by the fraction of the peak learning rate $η/η_{\max}$. We prove that this scaled weight decay preserves the asymptotic stationarity guarantees of the corresponding unregularized methods for both stochastic gradient descent and the non-Euclidean spectral optimizer Muon, thereby avoiding the additional asymptotic bias introduced by constant decoupled weight decay. This retains the stability benefits of weight decay without changing the asymptotic optimization target. Using a steady-state analysis, we explain why under standard weight decay the weight norm shrinks steadily as training proceeds, whereas under scaled weight decay it settles to a roughly constant value. When applied to the training of mixture-of-experts models, Muon with scaled weight decay (Muon-SW) consistently outpaces Muon with identical hyperparameters, reaching the same validation loss $\mathbf{30\%}$ faster at our largest scale across models from $72 - 930$ million parameters trained at $\sim 600$ tokens per active parameter. If this trend continues to hold, the method promises to substantially accelerate the pre-training of frontier models while requiring only a few lines of code to implement.

</details>

### [35] [Intertwiners of representations of exceptional type quantum affine superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24379)
*Keshav Dahiya,Evgeny Mukhin*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文给出了 D$_{2\vert 1;α}$、F$_{3\vert 1}$ 和 G$_{2\vert 1}$ 三类量子仿射超代数（quantum affine superalgebras）的最小非平凡不可约表示 $V$ 的显式公式，及其对应的 $R$-矩阵（$R$-matrices）的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仿射超代数的表示结构及其对应的 $R$-矩阵是理解相关可积系统（integrable systems）的关键，但复杂类型（如 D$_{2\vert 1;α}$、F$_{3\vert 1}$、G$_{2\vert 1}$）的最小非平凡表示的显式构造尚未完善。

Method: 通过分析三种量子仿射超代数的不可约表示结构（D$_{2\vert 1;α}$：全费米子宇称（all-fermionic parity），$\dim V=18$；F$_{3\vert 1}$：区分宇称（distinguished parity），$\dim V=41$；G$_{2\vert 1}$：区分宇称，$\dim V=32$），在 Drinfeld-Jimbo 实现和新 Drinfeld 实现（new Drinfeld realizations）两种框架下给出显式公式，并据此推导相应 $R$-矩阵。

Result: 得到了三种量子仿射超代数在两种实现下最小非平凡不可约表示的显式表述，并给出了对应的 $R$-矩阵的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 本文的显式构造为量子仿射超代数表示理论及其在可积模型中的应用提供了具体计算基础。

Abstract: We give explicit formulas for the smallest non-trivial irreducible representation $V$ of quantum affine superalgebras in types D$_{2\vert 1;α}$, $\dim V=18$ (in the all-fermionic parity), F$_{3\vert 1}$, $\dim V=41$ (in the distinguished parity), and G$_{2\vert 1}$, $\dim V=32$ (in the distinguished parity), both in the Drinfeld-Jimbo and in the new Drinfeld realizations. We use this information to obtain an explicit expression for the corresponding $R$-matrices.

</details>

### [36] [VecTree-RAG: An Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework Combining Vector and Tree Retrieval for Efficiency and Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23006)
*Xinyan Zhong,Yuwei Shi,Yuqi Wei,Chen Shen,Tianhang Zhou,Zhenghao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VecTree-RAG是一个面向科学文献问答的智能体框架（agentic framework），通过将全局向量检索（vector search）与基于推理引导的树结构遍历（reasoning-guided tree traversal）相结合，分别解决论文筛选和证据定位两个子问题，在QASPER、LitQA2和MOSAIC三个基准测试上均取得了最高分数，并显著提升了证据定位精度（evidence-page precision）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问答（scientific question answering）需要检索系统解决两个不同的问题：识别哪些论文是相关的，以及在这些论文中定位支撑证据。传统检索增强生成（retrieval-augmented generation, RAG）通常通过基于固定长度段落（fixed-length passages）的相似性搜索同时处理这两个任务，这一方式扁平化了文档结构（document structure），并将科学主张与其方法性和论证性上下文分离。

Method: VecTree-RAG将上述两个任务分配给互补的检索机制：向量搜索（vector search）在语料库范围内对紧凑的文档和章节表示进行排序；随后，基于推理引导的遍历（reasoning-guided traversal）对已筛选论文中经过来源验证的章节树（source-verified section trees）进行搜索，以定位证据。全文保留在页面存储（page store）中，仅在结构定位完成后才逐步暴露。

Result: 在300个QASPER问题、54个LitQA2问题和49个多文档MOSAIC问题上进行评估。VecTree-RAG在所有三个基准测试上均获得了最高的答案分数：QASPER上LLM评判正确率为0.800，LitQA2上准确率为0.925，MOSAIC上复合得分为0.547。在QASPER上，其证据页面精确率（evidence-page precision）为0.274，而基线方法仅为0.046–0.071。LitQA2消融实验表明，完整的向量-树架构（vector–tree architecture）所需的推理token数少于缺少树遍历或语料级向量路由的变体。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，向量检索缩小了语料级别的搜索空间，而树遍历则将阅读集中在结构相关的证据上。尽管多轮推理的成本仍高于单次调用检索，VecTree-RAG为科学文献问答提供了一种结构感知（structure-aware）且可追溯（traceable）的架构。

Abstract: Scientific question answering requires a retrieval system to solve two distinct problems: identifying which papers are relevant and locating the supporting evidence within those papers. Conventional retrieval-augmented generation typically addresses both through similarity search over fixed-length passages, flattening document structure and separating scientific claims from their methodological and argumentative context. We present VecTree-RAG, an agentic framework that assigns these tasks to complementary retrieval mechanisms. Vector search ranks compact document and section representations across the corpus, whereas reasoning-guided traversal of source-verified section trees localizes evidence within shortlisted papers. Full text is retained in a page store and exposed progressively only after structural localization. We evaluate VecTree-RAG on 300 QASPER questions, an open-access subset of 54 LitQA2 questions, and 49 multi-document MOSAIC questions. Compared with Dense RAG, reranked Dense RAG, RAPTOR, and Search-o1, VecTree-RAG obtained the highest observed answer score on all three benchmarks, reaching 0.800 LLM-judge correctness on QASPER, 0.925 accuracy on LitQA2, and a 0.547 composite score on MOSAIC. On QASPER, its evidence-page precision was 0.274, compared with 0.046--0.071 for the baselines. LitQA2 ablations further showed that the complete vector--tree architecture required fewer inference tokens than variants without tree navigation or corpus-level vector routing. These results indicate that vector retrieval narrows the corpus-level search space and tree navigation concentrates reading on structurally relevant evidence. Although multi-turn inference remains more expensive than single-call retrieval, VecTree-RAG provides a structure-aware and traceable architecture for scientific literature question answering.

</details>

### [37] [New class of exactly flat topological bands - compact localised states protected by local graph topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22831)
*Tamaghna Hazra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在任意图（graph）的面（face）上构造无限族严格平坦能带（flat band）哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的通用方法，并利用图论版本的阿蒂亚-辛格指标定理（Atiyah-Singer index theorem）证明了其宏观简并性（extensive degeneracy）受面图连通性的局部拓扑保护，生成的紧致局域态（compact localised states）因跃迁路径中的阻挫（frustration）而保持时间局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子物质（strongly correlated quantum matter）的核心在于非对易量子算符（non-commuting quantum operators）之间的张力，而具有宏观简并（macroscopic degeneracy）的哈密顿量对任何非对易微扰都表现出无限敏感性，因此在凝聚态物理学中具有基础性意义。在莫尔异质结（moiré heterostructures）中，利用动能哈密顿量中的这种广泛简并性来产生平带（flat band），可以为涌现奇异强关联相（strongly correlated phases）提供沃土。

Method: 作者提出了一种构造规则，用于在任意图的面（faces of arbitrary graphs）上构建无穷多个精确的平带哈密顿量家族，并在四种布拉维晶格（Bravais lattices）的面结构上演示了该算法。利用离散图论推广版本的阿蒂亚-辛格指标定理，作者证明了面图哈密顿量（face-graph Hamiltonians）的宏观简并性受面图连通性的局部拓扑（local topology of the face-graph connectivity）保护。

Result: 该算法生成的宏观零空间（macroscopic null spaces）产生了紧致局域态，这些态由于跃迁路径中的阻挫而随时间保持局域化。证明了平带哈密顿量的宏观简并性由面图连通性的局部拓扑结构保护。

Conclusion: 这些非色散量子模式（non-dispersing quantum modes）在多种场景中具有广泛的应用前景，包括量子网络（quantum networks）中的受阻动力学（arrested dynamics）、量子机器学习算法（quantum machine learning algorithms）以及无马约拉纳拓扑量子计算（Majorana-free topological quantum computation）。

Abstract: Strongly correlated quantum matter is fundamentally defined by the tension between non-commuting quantum operators. Hamiltonians exhibiting macroscopic degeneracies are of general interest in this field because they imply an infinite susceptibility to any non-commuting perturbation. In moiré heterostructures, engineering such extensive degeneracies in the kinetic Hamiltonian creates a fertile garden for exotic strongly correlated phases of matter to emerge from the resulting flat bands. Here, we introduce a prescription to construct an infinite family of exact flat band Hamiltonians supported on the faces of arbitrary graphs. We demonstrate this algorithm on the faces of four Bravais lattices. Using a discrete graph generalization of the Atiyah-Singer index theorem, we prove that the extensive degeneracies of such face-graph Hamiltonians are protected by the local topology of the face-graph connectivity. The resulting macroscopic null spaces yield compact localised states that remain localised over time due to frustration in hopping pathways. We discuss the broad implications of such non-dispersing quantum modes in diverse settings, from arrested dynamics in quantum networks and quantum machine learning algorithms to Majorana-free topological quantum computation.

Abstract: We study the real-frequency structure of the self-energy in the bilayer Hubbard model, using the dynamical cluster approximation. At half filling, the Mott insulator-band insulator (MI-BI) crossover involves a rearrangement of self-energy poles between the bonding and antibonding bands; these poles cross as the interlayer hopping $t_{\perp}$ is varied. Upon doping, this pole structure produces a band-selective pseudogap and enhances $s^{\pm}$-wave superconductivity. The order parameter is maximized near the MI-BI boundary, where low-energy anomalous self-energy poles develop simultaneously in both bands and cooperatively enhance the pairing. We further show that these self-energy poles can be interpreted as emergent fermionic excitations, offering an enhanced-pairing mechanism in common with the single-layer Hubbard model. The controllability of these poles through $t_{\perp}$ makes the bilayer system an unconventional platform for optimizing strongly correlated superconductivity.

Conclusion: 所提出的局域基矢旋转策略能有效缓解量子蒙特卡洛模拟中的符号问题，扩展了可访问的参数空间和温度范围，可作为处理受挫量子磁性体系的有效数值工具。

Abstract: The sign problem breaks the polynomial scaling of Quantum Monte Carlo methods. We alleviate it by rotation of the local basis of the Hilbert space, such that the phase of off-diagonal matrix elements of the rotated Hamiltonian is minimized. These minima coincide with optima of the average sign. We benchmark our method for frustrated Heisenberg antiferromagnets in one dimension and on the two-dimensional maple-leaf lattice. Our approach reveals more efficient bases with improved sign in a large part of the parameter space for all models considered, enabling us to reach lower temperatures, on par with state-of-the-art numerical linked cluster expansions which we directly compare to.

Result: 数据集以局域YAML谱为核心：稳定性标签、最低采样频率以及任何阈值依赖的重新标注均由此谱导出。数据通过Science Data Bank开放获取，并配有GitHub仓库提供计算代码和轻量访问工具。

Abstract: Imaginary phonon modes remain a practical bottleneck in computational materials screening because otherwise plausible structures can be locally dynamically unstable under a chosen workflow. Here we present PhononBench-MP40, a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset of Materials Project-derived crystals for workflow-defined phonon stability. The dataset starts from 47,969 MP40 workflow tasks and provides 46,899 completed records with paired stability labels and local phonopy YAML spectra, including 16,683 Stable records and 30,216 completed-phonon unstable records. A further 1,067 relaxation failures are reported separately rather than merged into the completed phonon denominator. The release centers on the local YAML spectrum: the stability label, the lowest sampled frequency and any threshold-dependent relabeling are derived from that spectrum. The dataset is openly available through Science Data Bank at https://doi.org/10.57760/sciencedb.38735. A companion GitHub repository provides the calculation code and lightweight access utilities. PhononBench-MP40 provides an auditable reference for workflow-defined stability classification, minimum-frequency analysis, threshold studies and failure-aware triage, while keeping the reference workflow, data schema and interpretation boundaries explicit.

</details>

<details>

Method: 提出一个基于智能体（agent）的系统，嵌入在URSA框架中，实现原子模拟的自动化设计、执行与验证。系统使用LAMMPS工具，能自主选择原子间势函数（interatomic potential）、构建并运行模拟，并在闭环工作流（closed-loop workflow）中进行迭代式错误恢复（iterative error recovery）。

Conclusion: 该框架减少了人工干预和试错过程，从而提升了原子建模的严谨性（rigor）、可重复性（reproducibility）和可扩展性（scalability）。

Abstract: Atomistic simulations are central to materials design, but their execution involves complex, multi-step workflows that require significant human expertise. Here, we present an agent-based system embedded within the URSA (Universal Research and Scientific Agent) framework that automates the design, execution, and validation of atomistic simulations, demonstrated using the Large-scale Atomic/Molecular Massively Parallel Simulator (LAMMPS) tool. Our system autonomously selects interatomic potentials, constructs and runs simulations, and performs iterative error recovery within a closed-loop workflow. We evaluate the scientific reliability of the agent by benchmarking its outputs against LAVA, a high-throughput toolkit for LAMMPS and the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) calculations. Our framework reduces manual intervention and trial-and-error, thereby improving the rigor, reproducibility, and scalability of atomistic modeling.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修正电子色散关系（modified electron dispersion relations）如何改变冷白矮星的结构特性，以检验电子运动学（electron kinematics）对致密星结构的影响。

Method: 仅将变形引入简并电子状态方程（equation of state），通过单粒子能量和群速度（group velocity）修改电子贡献，而通过电荷中性条件（charge neutrality）保持离子主导的质量密度；修正后的状态方程与标准TOV方程（Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff equations）耦合，不修改引力场方程。

Result: 在未变形极限下，以碳和氧为基线（μ_e=2）恢复钱德拉塞卡质量标度（Chandrasekhar scale）的最大质量，验证了计算的规范性；第一种修正模型仅产生轻微的刚度增加，而对数模型则强烈依赖于变形参数符号，负值提高压力与最大质量，正值则使序列软化。

Conclusion: 白矮星可以隔离修正电子运动学对致密星结构的影响，但对数模型需进一步通过物理域（physical domain）、稳定性条件（stability conditions）以及质量与半径的观测约束加以限制。

Abstract: We investigate how modified electron dispersion relations affect the structure of cold white dwarfs (WDs). The deformation is introduced only in the degenerate electron equation of state, through the energy of a single particle and the group velocity, while the stellar mass density remains dominated by ions through the relation $ρ\simeq μ_e m_u n_e$ imposed by charge neutrality. The resulting equations of state are coupled to the standard TOV equations, with no modification of the gravitational field equations. For a baseline composed of carbon and oxygen with $μ_e=2$, the undeformed limit recovers a maximum mass in the Chandrasekhar scale, validating the normalization of the calculation before the modified cases are considered. The first model of modified dispersion produces only a modest stiffening over the parameter range studied, whereas the logarithmic model depends strongly on the sign of the deformation parameter: negative values increase the pressure and the maximum mass, while positive values soften the sequence. These results show that WDs can isolate the impact of modified electron kinematics on compact star structure, but the logarithmic branch in particular requires further restrictions from its physical domain, stability conditions, and observational constraints on mass and radius.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过改进Latała的论证，并利用Kearns–Saul不等式，推导出在条件β² q / arctanh q < 1下重叠浓度和自由能收敛的误差界O(N⁻¹)。其中q = q(β, h)是复制对称自洽方程（replica-symmetric self-consistency equation）的解。

Result: 证明了当β² q / arctanh q < 1时，重叠浓度成立且自由能以O(N⁻¹)误差收敛至复制对称公式。该条件在任意β<1和任意实外场h下均满足；对每个非零h，该区域还包含β>1的非空区间。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Latała论证从β < 1/2扩展到更广的参数区域，为谢林顿-柯克帕特里克（Sherrington–Kirkpatrick）模型在确定性外场下的复制对称解提供了更严格的收敛保证。

Abstract: In this note, we consider the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model with deterministic external field. Let $q=q(β,h)$ denote the solution of the replica-symmetric self-consistency equation \[ q=\mathbb E\tanh^2\!\left(h+β\sqrt q\,Z\right), \qquad Z\sim N(0,1), \] where $β$ and $h$ are inverse temperature and external field, respectively.
  By refining Lata\la' s argument, previously limited to \(β< \frac{1}{2}\), and using the Kearns--Saul inequality, we prove overlap concentration and convergence of the free energy to the replica symmetric formula with error \(O(N^{-1})\) whenever
  \[ β^2\frac{q}{{\rm arctanh}q}<1. \]
  Note that for any $β<1$ and $h\in \mathbb R$, the condition above is satisfied. Moreover, for every nonzero $h$, this region contains a nonempty interval with $β>1$.

Conclusion: 格点能量条件下局域能量尺度的系综等价性成立，但该等价性对能量值的离散结构（lattice vs. nonlattice）敏感，非格点情况会破坏精确壳等价性。

Abstract: Let $Λ$ be a Euclidean lattice, and consider $N$ labelled, noninteracting copies with one-site energy $H=\lVert\cdot\rVert^2$. We prove equivalence of canonical Gibbs and microcanonical ensembles at a local energy scale: on reachable exact shells for lattice-valued energies and in fixed-width windows otherwise. *Reda Chhaibi,Nguyen Viet Dang,Yannick Guedes Bonthonneau,Gabriel Rivière,Tat Dat Tô*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有杨-米尔斯测度的构造依赖于格点逼近（lattice approximations，Chevyrev 在平坦环面上的工作以及作者之一与 Nohra 对一般紧致黎曼曲面的推广），本文旨在提供一种无需离散化、直接在连续空间上构造该测度的新方法。

Method: 首先引入莫尔斯规范（Morse gauge）的概念，并求解与莫尔斯-斯梅尔向量场（Morse–Smale vector fields）相关的随机上同调方程（random cohomological equations）；其中利用了两类对莫尔斯-斯梅尔梯度流（Morse–Smale gradient flows）指数收敛到平衡态（exponential convergence to equilibrium）的改进结果。接着，将随机解与莫尔斯复形（Morse complex）数据结合，定义了自由杨-米尔斯测度（free Yang–Mills measure），并借助随机微分方程（stochastic differential equation）的工具，使沿大类别曲线的随机连接和乐具有严格意义。最后通过对自由测度施加关于随机和乐的条件化（conditioning），定义杨-米尔斯测度，并计算其配分函数及随机和乐的分布。

Result: 成功给出了紧致黎曼曲面上杨-米尔斯测度的直接连续构造；计算了该测度的配分函数（partition function）以及随机和乐相对于该测度的分布律，所得结果恢复了 Migdal、Witten 和 Lévy 工作中的公式。

Conclusion: 所提出的莫尔斯理论框架为杨-米尔斯测度提供了一种不依赖于格点近似的连续构造方案，并验证了其与经典结果的一致性。

Abstract: We introduce a Morse theoretical approach to the construction of the Yang--Mills measure on the space of connections of a compact Riemannian surface. This provides a direct continuous version of this measure which was previously obtained through lattice approximations by Chevyrev in the case of the flat torus and by one of the authors and Nohra for general compact Riemannian surfaces.
  The starting point is the new notion of a Morse gauge together with the resolution of random cohomological equations associated to Morse--Smale vector fields. This is achieved by improving exponential convergence to equilibrium results for Morse--Smale gradient flows that were obtained by two of the authors in the context of the study of Ruelle spectra and by Jia, Stewart and Sverak in the context of simplified models from fluid mechanics. Combining these random solutions with the data given by the Morse complex, we introduce a free Yang-Mills measure on space of connections and, using classical tools from stochastic differential equations, we show how to make sense of holonomies for random connections along a large class of curves.
  Finally, by setting a proper conditioning of this free measure through these random holonomies, we define the Yang--Mills measure and we compute its partition function together with the law of random holonomies with respect to this measure, recovering the formulas from the works of Migdal, Witten and Lévy.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种仅利用秩（rank）信息而非具体数值来重新加权概率密度函数的算子，并系统刻画其数学结构、动力学性质与变分原理。

Method: 通过证明该算子在单调变量变换下的等变性（equivariance），分析其通过区间映射的复合结构，从而得到精确可解的动力学方程；引入Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子的基态平方作为规范核，建立变分原理和贝叶斯更新；利用等谱Sturm-Liouville系统和Virasoro代数对偶进行理论提升。

Conclusion: Derangetropy算子提供了一种仅通过秩信息重构概率密度的一般性框架，其动力学完全可解并具有丰富的物理对应（sine-Gordon方程、Sturm-Liouville系统、Virasoro代数），在随机采样、乘法混沌和高维输运中具有潜在应用。

Abstract: A derangetropy operator reweighs a probability density by a fixed profile of its own cumulative distribution function, acting through ranks alone. We prove that these operators are precisely the transformations of absolutely continuous laws equivariant under monotone changes of variable, and that they compose through interval maps, making their dynamics exactly solvable: iteration condenses every law onto its median with a universal Koenigs limit law, the continuous flow is solvable in closed form with the Cauchy family as invariant hyperbolic manifold, and balanced against diffusion the distribution function obeys an overdamped sine-Gordon equation whose unique steady law, the hyperbolic secant, is a globally stable kink. A variational principle selects the canonical kernel, the squared ground state of the Dirichlet Laplacian on the unit interval, whose update is a Bayesian posterior costing exactly one bit for every law, and a unitary lift equips each law with an isospectral Sturm-Liouville system and a quantum carpet: a strong law of large numbers for the critical Sobolev mass, with sharp constant given by Wiener's jump statistic, proves the fractality of the Schrödinger density for arbitrary real bounded-variation data with a jump, previously known only for rational step data. A lift to the dual of the Virasoro algebra, at central charge one, a normalization, identifies laws with disconjugate Hill potentials, tails with conformal weights, and the Cauchy family with the exceptional orbit. Randomized phases yield an exact sampler and a multiplicative chaos, log-correlated in the weakly tempered regime; in several dimensions, dependence stratifies into a conserved interaction, a torsion driven by maximal correlation, and a flat Sinkhorn transport.

</details>


### [62] [Mixing times and spectra of non-equilibrium symmetric exclusion processes on general graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22991)
*Leonard J. Schulman,Alistair Sinclair*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平衡排斥过程（non-equilibrium SEP），在任意图与任意热浴设定下，给出了混合时间（mixing time）的紧界（误差在双对数因子内），并基于此设计了计算稳态联合占据分布（steady-state joint occupation distribution）的算法，同时证明了该非可逆马尔可夫链的谱（spectrum）与热浴温度无关且为实数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称排斥过程（symmetric exclusion process, SEP）是图上一类经典的相互作用粒子模型。其粒子数固定的平衡版本已通过可逆马尔可夫链（reversible Markov chains）得到充分理解。然而，当部分顶点与不同温度的外部热浴（heat baths）耦合时，系统进入非平衡态，对应的马尔可夫链不再可逆，导致稳态分布极其难以刻画。现有研究几乎仅限于有限格点（lattice）子集，对混合时间等重要量的了解十分有限。

Method: 作者针对任意图与任意热浴设置下的非平衡SEP，推导了混合时间的一个上界，该界由单个粒子到达任意热浴的最坏情况首达时间（worst-case hitting time）决定，且紧到仅差两个双对数因子。基于该混合时间界，进一步设计了一个时间复杂度约为 $n^{O(k)}$ 的算法，用于计算任意 $k$ 个顶点的稳态联合占据分布。此外，证明了该非可逆动力学的马尔可夫链的谱与热浴温度无关。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 混合时间上界，精确到两个对数因子；(2) 一个计算 $k$ 顶点稳态联合占据分布的算法，运行时间约为 $n^{O(k)}$；(3) 谱独立于热浴温度，且始终为实数。

Conclusion: 本文为非平衡SEP在任意图上的混合时间提供了紧界，给出了实用的稳态分布计算算法，并揭示了谱的不变性。这些结果为非平衡排斥过程的理论与计算研究提供了新的工具与见解。

Abstract: The symmetric exclusion process (SEP) is a classical model of interacting particles on a graph, in which multiple particles execute random walks subject to the constraint that no two particles may occupy the same vertex. The version of the process in which the number of particles is fixed, is by now very well understood, through the study of reversible Markov chains.
  In this paper, we study the non-equilibrium version of the SEP, in which some vertices of the graph interact with external heat baths, held at different temperatures, that mediate transport through the graph. The resulting steady-state distribution is still the stationary distribution of a natural Markov chain, but the chain is no longer reversible. This means that even the steady-state distribution is very hard to describe, and indeed is the subject of a rich literature, which has focused almost exclusively on graphs which are finite subsets of the lattice $Z^d$. Very little is known about other important quantities, notably the mixing time.
  We derive various quantitative results about the non-equilibrium SEP for arbitrary graphs with arbitrary heat baths. Our main result is a bound on the mixing time in terms of the worst-case hitting time of a single particle to any of the heat baths; this bound is tight up to two logarithmic factors in the size of the graph. We then use this result to derive a simple algorithm, running in time roughly $n^{O(k)}$, that computes the steady-state joint occupation distribution for any set of $k$ vertices; since the steady-state distribution is very elusive, this provides a potentially useful tool to study its $k$-wise correlations for small values of $k$. Finally, we prove that the spectrum of the Markov chain associated with the non-reversible dynamics for the SEP is independent of the heat bath temperatures; thus in particular the spectrum is always real.

</details>

### [63] [Corner contributions to Neumann jump determinants: three model calculations and a BFK conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23912)
*Victor Kalvin*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对分段实解析切割曲线上的Neumann跳跃算子（Neumann jump operator）的行列式，提出了一个局部角因子猜想（local corner-factor conjecture）。该猜想给出了单连通测地多边形在镜像加倍下的显式行列式公式，依赖于内角参数和边界长度，并通过平面多边形、球面纺锤和球面Coxeter三角形三个模型计算得到支持。该猜想还将常曲率多边形的Dirichlet行列式约化到其闭加倍的情形，并讨论了与Wiegmann-Zabrodin、Wang等人的Neumann跳跃行列式以及角域Coulomb气体的Grunsky算子方法之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在揭示分段实解析切割曲线上Neumann跳跃算子行列式的结构，特别是其对角度的依赖关系，为理解带角域（corners）上的谱问题提供精确的解析公式。

Method: 作者提出一个局部角因子猜想，给出Neumann跳跃算子行列式的显式表达式，并通过三个模型计算（平面多边形及其加倍、球面纺锤分割为全等的月形、球面Coxeter三角形）来验证该猜想。同时，借助Burghelea-Friedlander-Kappeler胶合公式（gluing formula）定义内在行列式（intrinsic determinant），并利用奇点异常公式（singular anomaly formula）处理加倍构型。

Result: 猜想公式为 Det'_∠ 𝒩 = (length(∂P)/2) ∏_{j=1}^N α_j^{-1/2}。三个模型均支持该猜想：平面多边形加倍得到 (1/2)∏_j α_j^{-1/2}；球面纺锤给出 1/(2α)；球面Coxeter三角形给出 (1/2)√(pqr)，包括八分球的 √2。该猜想还将常曲率多边形的Dirichlet行列式约化到闭加倍的行列式。

Conclusion: 该猜想为带角度的曲面上谱行列式的计算提供了统一框架，但非零曲率情形下的显式求值仍需要解决均匀化问题（uniformization problem）。文章还指出了该猜想与Wiegmann-Zabrodin、Wang等人的Neumann跳跃行列式以及基于Grunsky算子的Coulomb气体方法之间的关联。

Abstract: We formulate a local corner-factor conjecture for the determinant of the Neumann jump operator on a piecewise real-analytic cutting curve. For the mirror double of a simply connected geodesic polygon with interior angles $πα_1,\ldots,πα_N$, the conjectural determinant is \[
  \Det_{\angle}'\cN
  =
  \frac{\length(\partial P)}2
  \prod_{j=1}^Nα_j^{-1/2}. \] Here $\Det_{\angle}'$ denotes an intrinsic determinant, still to be constructed, that is required to satisfy a Burghelea--Friedlander--Kappeler gluing formula; only its quotient by $\length(\partial P)$ is purely angle-dependent. Three model calculations support the conjecture: a flat polygon and its double give $\frac12\prod_jα_j^{-1/2}$; a spherical spindle split into congruent lunes gives $1/(2α)$ for two angles $πα$; and every spherical Coxeter triangle with angles $(π/p,π/q,π/r)$ gives $\frac12\sqrt{pqr}$, including $\sqrt2$ for the octant. The conjecture also reduces the Dirichlet determinant of a constant-curvature polygon to that of its closed double. Although the singular anomaly formula applies to the double in general, an explicit evaluation in nonzero curvature requires solving its uniformization problem. We discuss connections with the Neumann jump determinants in the work of Wiegmann--Zabrodin and Wang and with the recent Grunsky-operator approach to Coulomb gases on domains with corners.

</details>

*Florian Effenberg,Jonathan D. Coburn,Luca Cappelli,Simon D. Corah,Amoolya Grandhi,Jerome Guterl,Charlie Hirst,Mike Jackson,Dylan A. Kohler,Rob Kolasinski,Erick Martinez-Loran,Ria Meston,Lauren Nuckols,Angelica Ottaviano,Zana Popovic,Sergey Tsurkan,Tessa Van Volkenburg,Daniel Velazquez,Aaliyah Zuniga,Arunodaya Bhattacharya,Jose Boedo,Kent Christian,Giacomo Dose,Eric Hollmann,Mykola Ialovega,Florian M. Laggner,Samara Levine,Xavier Navarro,Andrew Shone,Lance L. Snead,David Sprouster,Jason Trelewicz,Jayson Barr,Ryan Hood,Charlie Lasnier,Yan-Ru Lin,Vincent Mazon,Jun Ren,Gilson Ronchi,Dmitry Rudakov,Dinh Truong,Cedric Tsui,Shawn Zamperini,Weicheng Zhong,Tyler Abrams,Anthony W. Leonard,Roberto Maurizio*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本论文报告了DIII-D装置上针对44种先进等离子体面向材料（plasma-facing materials）的协同暴露实验与比较评估，涵盖钨基材料、耐火多主元合金、陶瓷以及可再生硼源等，为聚变示范堆壁和偏滤器材料的筛选提供了跨材料基准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变示范堆和商业聚变电站需要筛选耐受极端热负荷和粒子通量的面向等离子体材料（plasma-facing materials），但目前缺乏系统、跨机构的材料比较实验。本研究旨在通过统一暴露条件，对来自12个机构的44种先进材料进行横向对比评估，以支持材料下行选择（down-selection）。

Method: 在DIII-D装置上利用偏滤器材料评估系统（Divertor Materials Evaluation System, DiMES）对样品进行暴露。实验覆盖欧姆（Ohmic）、L模和H模（含边界局域模）放电条件；平板几何（flush geometry）样品热流密度为0.2–2.5 MW m⁻²，10°倾斜角（angled geometry）样品为10–15 MW m⁻²。通过质量损失、微观结构完整性和氘滞留等指标评估性能。

Result: 工程化钨（engineered tungsten）结构保持完整，长纤维Wf/W表现出最清晰的止裂（crack-arrest）行为。W-Re和掺杂K的W性能与ITER级W相近；增材制造W-Ta的平板样品质量损失0.64 mg，倾斜样品损失2.19–2.87 mg。经550°C、0.3 dpa中子辐照后，ITER级W的氘滞留量是未辐照W的2.8倍。TiB₂在欧姆组中D₂释放最低。VTaHfMo是最稳定的耐火多主元合金（refractory multi-principal-element alloy）。NbC和(Nb₀.₅Ta₀.₅)C质量损失仅0.02–0.03 mg，而ZrC损失7 mg。CVD SiC保持宏观完整但有效硅侵蚀产额（effective Si erosion yield）约0.5，比DIII-D先前趋势高5–10倍。可再生硼粒棒（renewable boron pebble rods）发生可控烧蚀，13%释放的硼在外靶点附近电离，局域回收率最高达50%。原位铬粗侵蚀（gross-erosion）测量值约10⁻²量级。

Conclusion: 这些结果为聚变示范堆材料下行选择提供了跨材料基准（cross-material benchmarks），并为未来基于人工智能/机器学习（AI/ML）的等离子体面向材料开发建立了比较数据库。

Abstract: A coordinated DIII-D campaign exposed and comparatively assessed 44 advanced plasma-facing materials from 12 institutions, including four public-private fusion partnerships, to support fusion pilot plant wall and divertor material down-selection. Samples were exposed using the Divertor Materials Evaluation System (DiMES) under Ohmic, L-mode, and H-mode conditions with edge-localized modes, at 0.2-2.5 MW m$^{-2}$ on flush geometries and 10-15 MW m$^{-2}$ on 10$^{\circ}$ angled geometries. Engineered tungsten architectures retained integrity; long-fiber Wf/W showed the clearest crack-arrest behavior. W-Re and K-doped W showed near-ITER-W-like responses, while additively manufactured W-Ta showed heat-flux-sensitive mass losses of 0.64 mg for the flat sample and 2.19-2.87 mg for angled samples. After irradiation to 0.3 dpa at 550$^{\circ}$C, neutron-irradiated ITER-grade W retained 2.8 times more deuterium than pristine W, while TiB$_2$ showed the lowest D$_2$ release in the Ohmic set. VTaHfMo was the most stable refractory multi-principal-element alloy. NbC and (Nb$_{0.5}$Ta$_{0.5}$)C retained integrity with 0.02-0.03 mg mass loss, whereas ZrC lost 7 mg. CVD SiC retained macroscopic integrity but exhibited an effective Si erosion yield of 0.5, about 5-10 times above prior DIII-D trends. Renewable boron pebble rods underwent controlled recession; 13% of released boron was ionized near the outer strike point and up to 50% was recovered locally. Initial in-situ chromium gross-erosion measurements yielded values of order $10^{-2}$. Together, these results provide cross-material benchmarks for fusion pilot plant down-selection and future AI/ML-assisted plasma-facing-material development.

</details>

### [65] [Spatial spread of infection: transitions between pulled and pushed fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23728)
*Evgeniy Khain,Rohan Sukumar*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过理论分析和数值模拟，探讨了流行病（epidemic）向不稳定健康状态空间传播时，感染传播脉冲（propagating pulse）在牵引前缘（pulled front）与推进前缘（pushed front）之间的相变行为，发现了连续、非连续及双稳态三种转变类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当传播率（transmission rate）依赖于感染者比例时，感染脉冲可呈现牵引前缘或推进前缘两种不同模式，理解两者之间的转变机制对于预测疾病空间传播行为至关重要。

Method: 通过数值求解空间SIR偏微分方程组（spatial SIR partial differential equations），并结合前缘传播现象的理论分析，确定了牵引前缘与推进前缘区域在参数空间（phase space）中的分布。

Result: 发现了牵引前缘与推进前缘解之间同时存在连续转变（continuous transition）和非连续转变（discontinuous transition）；在非连续转变中，前缘速度（front speed）在某一参数跨越临界阈值时发生跳跃；此外还发现了一个双稳态区域（bistable region），在该区域内牵引前缘和推进前缘两种传播脉冲均可实现。

Conclusion: 理论与数值结果在转变附近的行为分析中表现出良好的一致性，揭示了传染病传播前缘类型之间更为丰富的相变结构。

Abstract: We consider the spatial spread of epidemic into an unstable, healthy state. When the transmission rate depends on the fraction of infected, the propagating pulse of infection can be either a pulled front or a pushed front. We determined the phase space of parameters for the pulled and pushed regions both by numerically solving the spatial SIR partial differential equations and by a theoretical analysis of the front propagation phenomenon. We found both a continuous and a discontinuous transition between the pulled and pushed front solutions; in the latter transition, the front speed undergoes a jump as a certain parameter crosses the critical threshold. The behavior of the propagating pulses near the transitions has been analyzed and a good agreement between the theory and numerics has been observed. Finally, a bistable region where both pushed and pulled propagating pulses can be realized has been discovered.

</details>


### [66] [The role of nestedness and saturating feedback in bipartite ecological systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24328)
*Nirbhay Patil,Ada Altieri*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Monod型饱和机制解决了广义Lotka–Volterra模型中互惠关系（mutualism）导致失稳的悖论，发现该机制可扩大稳定相（stable phase）并提高物种存活率；网络架构虽能调控稳定性，但嵌套性（nestedness）本身并不提供内在稳定优势，而是高连通性度分布（degree distribution）的副产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义Lotka–Volterra模型中，互惠关系会放大无序性（disorder）并引发无界增长，从而使系统失稳；而现实中的大型生态系统却能同时维持竞争与合作。该矛盾亟待理论解释。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）与随机矩阵分析（random-matrix analysis）相结合的理论方法，研究Monod型饱和机制对生态系统稳定性的影响。

Abstract: We develop a motif-based framework for spatiotemporal chaos in spatial evolutionary games and use it to map the dynamical phase diagram in the payoff plane. These thresholds are obtained from payoff balance at contested motif interfaces and recover classical invasion thresholds of spatial evolutionary games, which emerge here as boundaries of the chaotic phase. Combining the Derrida slope with the asymptotic Hamming distance, we obtain a four-region cartography: ordered, transient-chaotic, sustained-chaotic, and subcritical-chaotic dynamics. The phase diagram is organized by density-dependent motif selection: different initial cooperator densities activate different instability mechanisms, yet a small set of motif-instability lines consistently bounds the sustained-chaos region across densities. This cartography reveals a subcritical chaotic phase (Derrida slope $s<1$ but asymptotic Hamming distance $d_\infty>0$), where infinitesimal perturbations decay while finite-amplitude perturbations sustain chaos. ### [68] [Gravity-Aligned Vertical Electron-Positron Linear Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24179)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水平式希格斯工厂面临成本与规模的双重困境（cost-and-scale impasse），亟需探索可大幅降低建设复杂度和占地面积的新概念方案。

Method: 采用垂直竖井布局，电子从底部向上加速、正电子从地表向下注入，在深地下的对撞点（interaction point）相遇；采用高梯度、高效率的常温加速结构（normal-conducting accelerating structures）配合低温运行与双束功率供给方案（two-beam power-delivery scheme）；计划复用KEK/SuperKEKB现有加速器设施。

Result: 垂直取向具有多项优势：地表占地面积小、重力载荷呈本征方位角对称、对撞点深地振动隔离、重力辅助低温工程、天然垂直对准参考、深地对撞点宇宙射线噪声低、可现场实现电力自给自足，并提供了社会/产业评估的独特机会。主要挑战包括所需的高加速梯度以及数公里深竖井的施工。基于公开信息，在地质条件适宜的场地开挖约3 km深度的竖井具有可行性。

Conclusion: 该概念目前处于概念层面（concept-level），旨在激励技术创新、可行性研究，以及探索通过垂直对撞开展超出标准模型（Beyond-Standard-Model）搜索的新渠道。

Abstract: A gravity-aligned vertical electron-positron linear collider is proposed as a concept to break the cost-and-scale impasse that confronts conventional horizontal Higgs factories. The accelerating axis is oriented along the local gravitational field inside a single deep vertical shaft. Positrons are injected downward from the surface while electrons are accelerated upward from the bottom, the two beams meeting at an interaction point located deep underground. Targeting a staged center-of-mass energy in the Higgs factory range (250-500 GeV), this concept assumes high-gradient, high-efficiency, normal-conducting accelerating structures operated at cryogenic temperature together with a two-beam power-delivery scheme. It can be designed to reuse the existing KEK/SuperKEKB accelerator complex at the KEK Tsukuba Campus. We discuss the advantages of the vertical orientation, namely a small surface footprint, intrinsic azimuthal symmetry of the gravity load, deep vibration isolation of the collision point, gravity-assisted cryogenics, a natural vertical alignment reference, a cosmically quiet deep interaction point, an opportunity for on-site electricity self-sufficiency, and a unique opportunity in social/industrial evaluation, as well as the principal disadvantages and challenges, including the required accelerating gradient and the construction of a kilometers-deep shaft. Using only publicly available information, we argue that excavating a shaft with a depth of ~3 km is feasible at a geologically favorable site. This paper is deliberately concept-level and is intended to motivate technological innovation, feasibility studies, and the exploration of possible new channels for Beyond-Standard-Model searches through vertical collisions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙与晚期宇宙测量之间持续存在的系统性偏差（即宇宙学张力，cosmological tensions），使得检验标准Λ冷暗物质（ΛCDM）模型成为当前宇宙学的核心课题。本文旨在探讨熵推广Chaplygin气体模型——一种统一暗扇区（unified dark sector）的唯象方案——能否通过改变与这些张力相关的参数简并性（parameter degeneracies）来缓解矛盾。

Method: 作者采用联合观测约束方法，使用的数据集包括：PantheonPlus+SH0ES和DES–Dovekie的Ia型超新星数据、暗能量光谱巡天（DESI）的重子声学振荡（BAO）测量、红移空间畸变（RSD）增长数据、压缩版普朗克2018距离先验以及普朗克透镜幅度先验。通过对比熵gCg模型与ΛCDM的后验分布，并利用Tensiometer工具量化公共导出参数空间$(H_0,Ω_m,S_8)$的后验位移（posterior shifts）来评估模型效果。

Result: 熵gCg模型在背景膨胀（background expansion）上紧密复现ΛCDM，同时引入受控的晚期距离–红移关系形变，使$(H_0,Ω_m,S_8)$的偏好区域发生平移，部分减轻了以$H_0$为主的背景驱动张力。后验位移主要沿背景敏感方向（background-sensitive directions），表明模型对晚期膨胀扇区的修改比对增长扇区（growth sector）更为有效，因此$S_8$的残余差异仍然较为稳健。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持$H_0$张力与$S_8$张力可能源于不同物理扇区的观点：背景尺度的修改有助于缓解$H_0$矛盾，但$S_8$张力对增长扇区的敏感性更高，需要另行建模。

Abstract: Persistent discrepancies between early- and late-Universe measurements have made cosmological tensions a major test of the standard $Λ$CDM model. We investigate whether an entropic generalized Chaplygin-gas cosmology, describing a unified dark sector, can modify the parameter degeneracies associated with these tensions. The model is constrained using Type Ia supernova data from PantheonPlus+SH0ES and DES--Dovekie, DESI BAO measurements, RSD growth data, compressed Planck 2018 distance priors, and a Planck lensing-amplitude prior. We find that the entropic gCg model closely reproduces the $Λ$CDM background expansion while introducing a controlled late-time deformation of the distance--redshift relation. This shifts the preferred $(H_0,Ω_m,S_8)$ region and partially reduces background-driven discrepancies, especially those involving $H_0$. Using \texttt{Tensiometer} to quantify posterior shifts in the common derived parameter space $(H_0,Ω_m,S_8)$, we show that the displacement relative to $Λ$CDM occurs mainly along background-sensitive directions. The model therefore modifies the late-time expansion sector more effectively than the growth sector, leaving residual $S_8$ discrepancies comparatively robust. These results support the possibility that the $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions arise from distinct physical sectors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从静态球对称带电解出发，将积分常数（尤其是质量与电荷）推广为超前或延迟零坐标的任意函数，从而获得相应的动力学（Vaidya型）解。为确保场方程成立，额外引入载流零流（charge-carrying null currents）和零流体通量（null fluid fluxes）。该形式体系适用于一大类非线性电磁理论。

Result: 成功构造了任意时空维度下准拓扑引力耦合非线性电动力学的Vaidya型解，并证明通过适当引入载流零流与零流体通量，场方程得以自洽满足。

Conclusion: 本文提出的形式化为准拓扑引力中生成精确辐射带电解提供了一种简单、系统的方法，且适用于广泛的非线性电磁理论。

Abstract: We construct Vaidya-type solutions of quasitopological gravity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics in arbitrary spacetime dimensions. Starting from the corresponding static spherically symmetric charged solutions, we obtain their dynamical counterparts by promoting the integration constants, in particular the mass and electric charge, to arbitrary functions of the advanced or retarded null coordinate. We show that the resulting field equations are satisfied provided suitable charge-carrying null currents and null fluid fluxes are included. The formalism applies to a broad class of nonlinear electromagnetic theories and provides a simple and systematic method for generating exact radiating charged solutions in quasitopological gravity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 首先采用卡西米尔能量密度的一般形式构造虫洞解，并选择特定的红移函数（redshift function）及合适的模型参数，使得在虫洞喉部（throat）附近因高阶曲率项（higher order curvature terms）而满足能量条件；随后在非恒定红移函数情形下检验能量条件；最后利用Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程分析稳定性，并研究零测地线和时序粒子在虫洞时空中的轨迹。

Result: 通过选择合适的模型参数，在虫洞喉部附近以及无穷远处均能满足能量条件；卡西米尔可穿越虫洞解在TOV方程框架下具有稳定性；虫洞时空中零粒子和时序粒子的运动轨迹得以分析。

Conclusion: 在EGB引力框架下，基于卡西米尔能量密度可以构建稳定的可穿越虫洞解，且高阶曲率项有助于在喉部附近及无穷远处满足能量条件。

Abstract: In this work, we study Casimir wormhole solutions and investigate satisfaction of energy conditions in the framework of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) gravity. We firstly find traversable wormhole solutions supported by a general form for the Casimir energy density. Applying a specific redshift function, we choose suitable parameters that respect the energy conditions in the vicinity of the throat due to the presence of higher order curvature terms. Then, we check the energy conditions considering non-constant redshift function and for suitable choice of model parameters, we find that these conditions are satisfied at infinity as well as in the vicinity of the throat. The stability of the Casimir traversable wormhole solutions are investigated using the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) equation. Finally, we study trajectory of null as well as timelike particles in the wormhole spacetime.

</details>

*R. Daniel Murphy,Anthony Mezzacappa,Colter J. Richardson,Pedro Marronetti,Eric J. Lentz,Ryan E. Landfield*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过将多个外部发展的通用关系（universal relations）应用于二维和三维核心坍缩超新星（CCSN）模拟产生的原中子星（PNS）演化数据，比较了通用关系预测的PNS振荡频率与模拟频谱图中引力波峰值频率的一致性，并反向验证了通用关系对PNS属性的预测能力。结果表明，使用这些通用关系需谨慎，但进一步的发展是有益的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于星震学（asteroseismology）技术发展出的通用关系，能够将原中子星（PNS）的准正则非径向振荡模式频率与核心坍缩超新星（CCSN）产生的引力波高频分量联系起来。然而，这些关系在实际CCSN模拟数据中的预测准确性尚缺乏系统验证，因此本文旨在评估其可靠性。

Method: 将多个外部发展的通用关系应用于由二维和三维CCSN模拟生成的PNS演化数据（包括质量M和半径R），比较通用关系预测的PNS振荡频率与模拟产生的频谱图中直接计算的引力波峰值频率。同时，反向利用引力波频谱峰值频率作为通用关系的输入，比较预测的PNS属性与模拟确定的真实PNS属性。

Result: 研究展示了在最理想情况下（即无探测器噪声），这些通用关系对PNS属性及其演化的预测表现。结果表明，星震学预测与模拟结果之间存在一定程度的一致性，但必须谨慎使用这些通用关系，尤其是在从引力波探测数据推断PNS演化时。

Conclusion: 当前通用关系在预测PNS演化方面存在局限性，需谨慎解读其预测结果；同时，鉴于星震学预测与模拟结果之间已观察到的部分一致性，进一步发展和改进这些通用关系将是有益的。

Abstract: Using asteroseismology techniques, several relations have been developed that relate the quasi-normal, non-radial oscillation mode frequencies of the proto-neutron star (PNS) to the high frequency component of core collapse supernova (CCSN) generated gravitational waves predicted from simulation. These relations are universal in the sense that they are parameterized entirely by PNS properties, e.g., mean density or surface gravity, and are independent of both progenitor properties, e.g., zero age man sequence (ZAMS) mass or metallicity, and the physics included in CCSN simulations, e.g., nuclear equation of state (EOS). In this work, we apply several externally developed universal relations to PNS evolution data--specifically the mass, M, and radius, R,--generated from both two- and three-dimensional CCSN simulations and compare the resulting PNS oscillation frequencies predicted by the universal relations to the peak gravitational wave frequencies computed directly from the simulation-produced spectrogram. Additionally, we use the gravitational wave spectrogram peak frequencies as input to the universal relations and compare the predicted PNS properties from each relation to the true PNS properties as determined by the simulations. In this way, we show what these universal relations would predict for the PNS properties and their evolution from a real CCSN gravitational wave detection in the best case scenario, i.e., no detector noise. Our results indicate that caution must be exercised when using these universal relations, particularly when interpreting their predictions for PNS evolution from a gravitational wave detection, and that, given the extent to which we do see agreement between asteroseismological predictions and simulation outcomes, further development of universal relations would be beneficial.

</details>


### [73] [Quasinormal modes of a quantum inspired black hole in four dimensions with cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22967)
*R. D. B. Fontana,Jeferson de Oliveira,B. Eslam Panah,Ángel Rincón*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文通过WKB近似和双零特征积分两种方法，研究了含宇宙学常数的量子修正黑洞在标量场和狄拉克场扰动下的拟正则模（QNM），验证了广义相对论极限下的结果收敛性，并分析了额外量子参数对de Sitter和Anti-de Sitter解频率谱的影响，确认所有扰动稳定并表现为拟正则振荡塔结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究含宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）的量子修正黑洞在标量场（scalar）和狄拉克场（Dirac）线性扰动下的动力学行为，特别关注额外量子参数对拟正则模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）谱的影响。

Abstract: The LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration has reported the results for searches for sub-solar mass components in compact binary coalescence during observing run O4a. For uncharged Taub-NUT, the sum sector closes with the mass and NUT charge, whereas the difference sector closes, within a restricted homogeneous diagnostic class tested here, only after including the rotation-like thermodynamic secondary hair $J_n=mn$. Thus, within this sector diagnostic, the NUT parameter enters through both $N=n$ and the homogeneous combination $J_n=mn$. The secondary hair is defined off shell in the homogeneous mass representation, not as a new metric parameter or asymptotic charge. Hidden conformal symmetry of the rotating Kerr-NUT parent gives a consistency check, without assuming a holographic dual.

</details>


### [88] [Upper bounds on the force function in spatially regular self-gravitating matter configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23661)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导球对称自引力物质构型中无量纲力函数${\cal F}$的严格解析上界，以检验和支撑广义相对论中的最大力猜想。

Method: 利用非线性的爱因斯坦-物质场方程，结合主能量条件与正能量-动量迹条件，对通用（非各向同性）和各向同性两类物质构型分别进行解析推导。

Result: 得到四个上界定理：通用物质构型下${\cal F}\leq 2$（主能量条件）和${\cal F}\leq 1$（非正能量-动量迹）；各向同性物质构型下${\cal F}\leq 1$（主能量条件）和${\cal F}\leq 1/2$（非正能量-动量迹）。

Abstract: We use the non-linearly coupled Einstein-matter field equations to prove four theorems that bound from above the dimensionless force function ${\cal F}=4πr^2\cdot p(r)$ in spatially regular curved spacetimes of spherically symmetric self-gravitating matter configurations [here $p(r)$ is the radially-dependent pressure inside the spatially regular matter configurations]. In particular, for generic (not necessarily isotropic) matter configurations it is proved that: (i) ${\cal F}\leq 2$ for matter fields that satisfy the dominant energy condition, and (ii) ${\cal F}\leq 1$ for matter fields with a non-positive energy-momentum trace. In addition, for self-gravitating isotropic matter configurations we derive the stronger upper bounds: (iii) ${\cal F}\leq 1$ for matter fields that satisfy the dominant energy condition, and (iv) ${\cal F}\leq 1/2$ for matter fields with a non-positive energy-momentum trace. Our analytically derived results are in accord with the spirit of the maximum force conjecture in general relativity.

</details>

By varying the action with respect to the metric, we derive the modified field equations and cast them in an effective Einstein-like form containing additional geometric contributions that depend on $Q$ and $T$. We conclude that the $f(Q,T)=αQ+βT+γT^{2}$ framework offers a viable arena for constructing physically realistic wormholes without invoking severe energy condition violations, opening new pathways for connecting modified gravity phenomenology with astrophysical signatures of non-trivial spacetime topologies.

The resulting spacetime is a deformed BTZ black hole whose conserved mass, horizon radius, Hawking temperature, and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy acquire finite Planck-scale corrections. We further investigate Hawking radiation using the Hamilton-Jacobi tunneling formalism and show that the return time of an emitted massless particle receives two distinct contributions: a geometric correction induced by curved momentum space and a dynamical correction arising from Hawking backreaction. Remarkably, the null geodesic equations remain unchanged, indicating that the observable effects originate entirely from the deformation of the effective spacetime geometry rather than from modifications of particle trajectories. These results provide a concrete semiclassical mechanism through which quantum kinematics encoded in curved momentum space can generate observable gravitational phenomena without requiring a quantization of spacetime itself, thereby offering a phenomenological bridge between quantum geometry and black-hole physics.

Using the Brans--Dicke scalar as a relational clock, we show that the apparent discrepancy between the reduced Hamiltonians does not indicate a physical inequivalence of the two frames. Instead, it arises from a mismatch in the canonical embedding of the clock sector prior to reduction. While the scalar configuration variable is preserved under the conformal transformation, its conjugate momentum is shifted by a contribution involving the gravitational momentum trace. Consequently, relational dynamics are determined not by the clock variable $T$ alone, but by the complete canonical clock pair $(T,P_T)$. We construct a frame-adapted canonical chart in which the clock sector is consistently transformed prior to deparameterization. The resulting reduced Hamiltonian coincides with that of the Einstein frame, restoring the equivalence of the reduced relational dynamics. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 将上述框架应用于Schwarzschild透镜构型，通过严格理论推导和详细数值分析，发现透镜化GW信号中出现表观矢量模（vector modes）和标量模（scalar modes），这些模式由偏振面涂抹和波前畸变引起。

Conclusion: 透镜化引力波信号中出现的表观矢量模和标量模并非真实的动力学自由度，而是引力透镜强加的传播效应（propagation effects）。该框架弥补了先前理论在描述透镜偏振特性方面的不足。

Abstract: In this work, we study the gravitational lensing of gravitational waves (GWs) by extending the geometric-optics approximation to higher order. With the help of the Newman-Penrose formalism, we reexpress the GW propagation equations as a series of scalar equations and present explicit expressions for the Weyl scalars that describe the GW polarizations. By combining the approaches of solving geodesic deviation and transport equations, we construct a solvable system of equations that describes the evolution of GW polarization along null geodesics. This framework fills the gap left by the leading-order geometric optics and the Kirchhoff diffraction integral, neither of which captures the polarization characteristics of GWs during the lensing process. This work applies the above framework to a Schwarzschild lensing configuration. Through a rigorous theoretical formulation and detailed numerical analysis, our results reveal the emergence of apparent vector and scalar modes in lensed GW signals, which originate from the smearing of the polarization plane and distortion of the wavefront and do not represent genuine dynamical degrees of freedom but rather arise as the propagation effects imposed by gravitational lensing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
