Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of scalar perturbations and strong cosmic censorship (SCC) in regular Ayón-Beato-García-Bronnikov-de Sitter (ABGB-dS) black hole spacetime. The main motivation of this work is to clarify whether the regularization of black hole core, which removes the central singularity, can also change the fate of SCC connected to the Cauchy horizon. We first study the dependence of the scalar QNM frequency (QNF) on the scalar mass and black hole parameters. For angular number $l=0$, a small scalar mass can give rise to a slowly decaying mode which is purely imaginary. We also find that the black hole charge and cosmological constant affect the QNM spectrum in qualitatively opposite ways. We then examine SCC under massless and massive scalar perturbations. For massless perturbations, the QNMs spectrum can be organized into photon sphere (PS), de Sitter (dS) and near-extremal (NE) families. Compared with the SCC in the Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter (RN-dS) case, SCC is generally more easily violated in the ABGB-dS spacetime, although this tendency is reversed for sufficiently large cosmological constant. For massive scalar perturbations, we find that the scalar mass does not restore SCC. Instead, it can enhance the violation by driving the system into the regime $β>1$. At last, we discuss what we may expect from the violation of SCC in a regular black hole spacetime.

</details>


### [94] [Landauer's Principle as a Criterion for Thermodynamic Consistency in Generalized Black Hole Entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24132)
*Fatemeh Sadeghi,Ahmad Sheykhi*

TL;DR: 本文基于黑洞视界面积量子化假设，利用兰道尔原理（Landauer's principle）检验多种黑洞熵关系的信息论一致性。研究发现：Bekenstein-Hawking熵每步蒸发能量恰好饱和兰道尔界；广义熵模型分为三类——约束霍金温度、约束自由参数、或完全不相容（如Kaniadakis熵）。对于相容模型，导出了面积量子化参数与能谱，其在经典极限下相对间距消失，揭示了信息论与黑洞热力学之间的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞视界面积量子化且每次霍金蒸发（Hawking evaporation）对应抹除一个比特信息的框架下，检验各种黑洞熵关系是否满足兰道尔原理（Landauer's principle）的信息论一致性约束。

Method: 将黑洞蒸发过程解释为面积能级间的跃迁，每次跃迁对应抹除一比特信息；应用兰道尔原理，计算每步蒸发能量并与兰道尔界（Landauer bound）比较；对Bekenstein-Hawking熵及多种广义熵模型（generalized entropy models）进行系统分类与分析。

Result: Bekenstein-Hawking熵每步蒸发能量恰好饱和兰道尔界；广义熵模型产生三类结果：第一类对霍金温度及黑洞质量施加约束，第二类限制熵模型的自由参数，第三类（如Kaniadakis熵）与兰道尔原理不相容。所有相容模型的面积量子化参数在经典极限下相对间距消失。

Conclusion: 信息论（information theory）与黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）之间存在深层联系；兰道尔原理可作为检验黑洞熵模型有效性的重要判据。

Abstract: Under the assumption that black hole horizon area is quantized, each Hawking evaporation step, during which the black hole loses mass and transitions to a lower area level, is interpreted as the erasure of one bit of information. In this paper, by employing Landauer's principle, we test the consistency of various black hole entropy relations with this information-theoretic framework. For the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, the energy emitted per step saturates the Landauer bound. We extend this analysis to a broad class of generalized entropy models, yielding three distinct outcomes. In the first category, Landauer's principle imposes constraints on the Hawking temperature and, consequently, on the black hole mass. In the second, it restricts the free parameters of the entropy model. The third category, exemplified by Kaniadakis entropy, proves incompatible with Landauer's principle. For all compatible models, we derive the area quantization parameter and the corresponding area spectrum. While this parameter is constant for Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, it becomes level-number-dependent for many generalized models. Nonetheless, the relative spacing between successive area levels vanishes in the classical limit. Our findings point to a deep link between information theory and black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [95] [Gravitational Wave Signatures of Periodic Orbits around a Schwarzschild-like Black Holes Submerged in an Exponential Density Dark Matter Profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24144)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未充分揭示暗物质晕参数（质量与空间延展）对黑洞强引力区轨道及引力波信号的独立影响，亟需厘清二者在极端质量比旋进（EMRI）波形中的可区分性。

Result: 暗物质晕对MBO和ISCO的影响并非简单的平移：特征半径和能量随 $r_0$ 呈非单调变化（先低于Schwarzschild值再恢复），角动量则单调递减；随 $M_0$ 的变化响应甚至可能反转符号，仅ISCO能量始终保持单调。$r_0$ 和 $M_0$ 在周期轨道谱上留下清晰可辨的印记。在EMRI波形中，改变 $r_0$ 会产生强烈的单调效应（轨道扩大、径向周期延长、明显失相），而 $M_0$ 的类似变化几乎不改变信号，除非 $M_0$ 达到黑洞质量的相当比例。

Conclusion: EMRI波形原则上能够区分暗物质晕的总质量与其空间延展，为利用强引力波探测超大质量黑洞周围的暗物质分布提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study a Schwarzschild-like black hole embedded in an exponential-sphere (ESM) dark matter halo, characterized by a halo mass $M_0$ and a scale radius $r_0$. We first show that the halo's effect on the marginally bound orbit (MBO) and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) is richer than a simple shift: the characteristic radii and energies vary non-monotonically with $r_0$, dipping below their Schwarzschild values before recovering, while the angular momenta decrease monotonically; as a function of $M_0$, the response can even reverse sign depending on how extended the halo is, with only the ISCO energy remaining monotonic throughout. We classify periodic orbits by the rational frequency ratio $q=ω_φ/ω_r-1=w+v/z$ and find that $r_0$ and $M_0$ leave clearly distinguishable imprints on the orbit spectrum. Using the numerical kludge framework, we compute the corresponding extreme-mass-ratio-inspiral (EMRI) waveforms and show that varying $r_0$ produces a strong, monotonic effect enlarging the orbits, lengthening the radial period, and introducing a clear dephasing while comparable variations in $M_0$ leave the signal nearly unchanged unless $M_0$ becomes a sizable fraction of the black hole mass. Together, these results indicate that EMRI waveforms can, in principle, disentangle the total mass of a dark matter halo from its spatial extent, offering a strong-field probe of the dark matter distribution around supermassive black holes.

</details>


### [96] [Gravitational wave signatures of magnetized Ernst black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24154)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用zoom-whirl表示法（以三个整数$(z,w,v)$标记）对束缚测地线（bound geodesics）进行系统分类；在一个结合精确测地线运动（exact geodesic motion）与四极近似（quadrupole approximation）的数值框架中计算引力波形；该近似特别适用于极端质量比旋进（extreme mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）。

Result: 内在磁场会在引力波信号上印刻出特征性特征（characteristic features），表明引力波天文学是探测磁化黑洞时空的有前景的手段。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational wave (GW) emission from periodic timelike orbits of a test particle around a magnetized Ernst black hole and the gravitational waveforms generated by their orbital dynamics. Gravitational waveforms are computed within a numerical framework that combines exact geodesic motion with the quadrupole approximation, which is well-suited to extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs). This analysis is particularly relevant for assessing the capability of future gravitational-wave observations to detect the effects of magnetic fields. Our results show that an intrinsic magnetic field imprints characteristic features on the GW signal, highlighting GW astronomy as a promising avenue for probing magnetized black hole spacetimes.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Hayward黑洞热力学相结构的完整解析描述，并提出了一个三参数热力学态空间诊断（thermodynamic state-space diagnostic），可追踪正则黑洞从经典热蒸发到冷残留锁定的连续演化，为量子引力现象学（quantum-gravity phenomenology）提供了定量分析框架。

Abstract: We establish exact analytical thresholds and present a unified thermodynamic state-space representation for the regular Hayward black hole, resolving the full phase structure without reliance on numerical approximations. Within the intermediate regime $\sqrt{3}l < r_h < 3l$, we uncover a distinct horizon bistability where two distinct horizon radii share identical free energy and temperature profiles. We demonstrate that the Davies singularity acts as a precise thermodynamic divide, separating a locally stable ($C_V > 0$) quantum Small Black Hole branch from an unstable ($C_V < 0$) Large Black Hole branch. Furthermore, we evaluate the exact integrated non-area law entropy at the critical turning point, yielding $S_{turn} = πl^2 \left[ \frac{63}{8} + 2\ln(8l^2) \right]$. Finally, we introduce a compact, 3-parameter thermodynamic state-space diagnostic that tracks the continuous evolution of regular black holes from classical thermal evaporation to cold remnant lock, offering a quantitative framework for quantum-gravity phenomenology.

Abstract: We study the inflationary primordial perturbations in $κ$-Minkowski non-commutative space-time, a Lie-algebraic type deformation of canonical space-time motivated by quantum gravity scenarios. Employing the $κ$-deformed star product formalism, we construct the bilinear action for curvature perturbations and derive the $κ$-deformed Mukhanov-Sasaki equation and obtain the perturbative solutions. Further, we compute the primordial power spectrum and spectral index, showing that the leading order corrections to the power spectrum induces scale-dependent term proportional to $(\ln k)^2$. The spectral index also exhibits an explicit $\ln k$ dependence, which persists even when the slow-roll parameters are constant. We also perform a Bayesian MCMC analysis using ACT DR6 data and constrain the $κ$-deformation length scale to $λ=6.32^{+6.00}_{-4.30}\times10^{-30}m$ at $1σ$ CL, approximately four orders of magnitude larger than the Planck scale, demonstrating that the $κ$-deformed space-time offers a potential window into quantum gravity phenomenology through precision cosmology.

### [99] [The Magnusian generator for dissipative systems and application to leading 2.5PN radiation-reaction dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24335)
*Francisco M. Blanco*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Magnusian框架推广至含耗散和非局域时间相互作用的系统，基于in-in形式论（Schwinger-Keldysh/Galley形式论），证明广义Magnusian仍可生成有限时间演化，并以牛顿束缚运动在2.5后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）辐射反作用力下的情况为例进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Magnusian作为一个通过嵌套泊松括号（nested Poisson brackets）生成有限时间演化的相空间函数，已与经典动力学中的多个生成元（如径向作用量、eikonal相位等）建立了联系。本文旨在将该框架推广至更一般的系统，特别是包含耗散（dissipation）和非局域时间相互作用（nonlocal-in-time interactions）的情形，例如双星动力学中引力场积分导致的辐射反作用与遗传效应。

Method: 采用in-in形式论（in-in formalism），即Schwinger-Keldysh或Galley形式论，对Magnusian框架进行扩展；对于含耗散和记忆效应的动力学系统，推导出广义Magnusian并证明其仍能生成有限时间演化。

Result: 成功推导了广义Magnusian，并证明其在含耗散与非局域时间相互作用的系统中仍然能够生成有限时间演化。以牛顿束缚运动受2.5PN辐射反作用力影响的情形为例，构造了相应的Magnusian，该生成元定义了从一个周期到下一个周期的离散演化映射，且演化结果与数值解一致。

Conclusion: Magnusian框架可以被系统地扩展至耗散性和非局域时间相互作用的系统，为经典动力学中的有限时间演化生成提供了统一的理论工具，并在双星动力学等实际物理问题中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: The Magnusian is a phase-space function that generates finite-time evolution through nested Poisson brackets. It is related to several familiar generators of classical dynamics, including the radial action, the eikonal phase and related quantities. In this work, we extend the Magnusian framework to systems with dissipation and nonlocal-in-time interactions using the in-in formalism, also known as the Schwinger-Keldysh or Galley formalism. This framework is particularly natural for binary dynamics, where integrating out the mediating gravitational field can produce both dissipative radiation-reaction effects and hereditary, nonlocal-in-time interactions. We derive the generalized Magnusian and show that it continues to generate finite-time evolution. As an application, we construct the Magnusian for Newtonian bound motion subject to the leading 2.5PN radiation-reaction force. The resulting generator defines a discrete evolution map from one cycle to the next and describes the evolution of the system in agreement with numerical solutions.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-gravity cosmology beyond pairwise interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24347)
*J. Flinckman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为 Hassan–Schmidt-May 多引力理论（multi-gravity）构建了宇宙学框架，该理论是无鬼影（ghost-free）的双度规理论（bimetric theory）的推广，包含多个自旋-2 场之间真正的非配对相互作用。作者推导了背景动力学方程、有效流体描述、高低密度分支行为、零锥结构以及线性的度规扰动方程，为超越双度规理论的宇宙学与微扰研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无鬼影多重引力（multi-gravity）理论在宇宙学层面尚缺乏系统的背景解与扰动分析，尤其是包含非配对（non-pairwise）自旋-2 相互作用的模型。本文旨在建立该理论的完整宇宙学框架，以描述背景演化、有效流体行为及线性扰动结构。

Method: 采用同时均匀且各向同性的（simultaneously homogeneous and isotropic）度规假设，将背景动力学约化为修正的弗里德曼方程（modified Friedmann equation）以及关于标度因子比值的一组耦合四次多项式方程组。通过分析有效能量密度的分支行为识别高密度与低密度分支，并求解非度规（non-metric）vielbein扰动的代数方程及一阶一致性条件。

Result: 自旋-2 相互作用在弗里德曼方程中表现为一个额外的有效流体。高密度分支趋近于标准辐射主导或物质主导宇宙；低密度分支趋近于比例真空解，其中相互作用充当宇宙学常数（cosmological constant），有效自旋-2 能量密度的首阶修正提供额外的物质贡献。高密度分支上额外零锥（null cone）张角大于物质耦合零锥，真空极限下所有零锥重合。导出了标量、矢量及张量扰动方程组。

Conclusion: 本文建立的多重引力宇宙学框架可用于研究超越双度规理论及其配对相互作用扩展的宇宙学解与扰动，为后续的观测约束和理论深入提供了基础工具。

Abstract: We develop the cosmological framework for Hassan--Schmidt-May multi-gravity, a ghost-free extension of bimetric theory with genuinely non-pairwise interactions of multiple spin-2 fields. For metrics that are simultaneously homogeneous and isotropic, the background dynamics reduce to a modified Friedmann equation supplemented by a system of coupled quartic polynomial equations for the scale-factor ratios. The spin-2 interaction contributes to the Friedmann equation as an additional effective fluid. We identify a regular high-density branch that approaches the standard radiation- or matter-dominated Universe, while regular low-density branches approach proportional vacuum solutions, where the interaction acts as a cosmological constant and the leading correction to the effective spin-2 energy density provides an additional matter contribution. We also determine the relative null-cone structure: on the regular high-density branch, the additional null cones approach wider opening angles than the matter-coupled null cone, while all null cones coincide in the vacuum limit. At linear order, we solve the algebraic equations determining the non-metric vielbein perturbations, derive the first-order consistency equations and their scalar and vector projections, and obtain the complete system of coupled tensor perturbation equations. These results establish a framework for studying cosmological solutions and perturbations in ghost-free multi-gravity beyond bimetric theory and its pairwise-interacting extensions.

</details>


### [101] [Entropy dynamics in gravitational collapse: From Minkowski breaking to de Sitter thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24349)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文整合了OCK黑洞构造与Volovik de Sitter真空热力学两种框架，指出哈勃参数（Hubble parameter）的符号是统一两者的关键，共同描绘了引力坍缩过程中熵流动的一致图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞熵的起源是现代物理最深层的谜题之一，需要从不同理论视角寻找统一的解释。

Method: 对比分析两种互补框架：OCK构造（内视界携带正Bekenstein-Hawking熵，随核心收缩逐步释放）与Volovik de Sitter真空局域热力学（均匀de Sitter区域的总熵依赖于哈勃参数的符号）。将哈勃参数的符号作为统一要素，检验两种框架的兼容性。

Result: OCK核心仅在$n>2$时具有de Sitter性质；Minkowski阻截（Minkowski breaking）是经典运动学障碍而非向收缩de Sitter相的动力学转变。两种框架均体现相同的热力学原理：内部收缩驱动系统朝向总熵增加。OCK参数的整数值暗示了熵的量子化谱和黑洞熵的统计诠释。

Conclusion: 两种框架共同给出了引力坍缩中熵流动的一致图景；广义第二定律（generalized second law）在两种框架中均成立，在设想的量子连接中也成立（需恰当计入环境熵）。

Abstract: The origin of black hole entropy remains one of the deepest mysteries in modern physics. Two recent developments offer complementary perspectives on this puzzle: the entropy release from regular Schwarzschild black holes with a de Sitter core (the OCK construction), and the local thermodynamics of the de Sitter vacuum (Volovik). In the OCK framework, the inner horizon carries positive Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S_{\rm inner}=πh_c^2$ that is gradually released as the core shrinks, until a classical Minkowski breaking obstructs the evolution at $n=0$. In Volovik's framework, the total entropy of a homogeneous de Sitter region is $S_{\rm dS}= \operatorname{sgn}(H)\,π/H^{2} = \operatorname{sgn}(H)\,A/4$: expanding de Sitter ($H>0$) carries positive entropy, while contracting de Sitter ($H<0$) carries negative entropy. We show that the sign of the Hubble parameter is the unifying element linking the two pictures. The OCK core is de~Sitter only for $n>2$; the Minkowski breaking is a classical kinematic obstruction, not a dynamical transition to a contracting de Sitter phase. Any connection between the expanding and contracting regimes would require a quantum mechanism that remains speculative. Nevertheless, both frameworks embody the same thermodynamic principle, the shrinking of the interior drives the system toward increased total entropy, and together they provide a consistent picture of entropy flow during gravitational collapse. The integer nature of the OCK parameter suggests a quantized entropy spectrum and a statistical interpretation of black hole entropy, while the generalized second law is satisfied in both frameworks, and in the speculated quantum connection provided the environmental entropy is properly accounted for.

</details>


### [102] [The Higgs vacuum in the presence of strong gravitational fields: curvature induced electroweak symmetry restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24469)
*Paul G. Abel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过在希格斯场与 Kretschmann 标量（Kretschmann scalar）之间引入一个受普朗克尺度抑制的耦合项，研究了 Schwarzschild 时空中希格斯场的行为，发现时空曲率会连续降低希格斯真空期望值（vacuum expectation value），并在临界曲率处导致电弱对称性（electroweak symmetry）恢复；进而给出了 Schwarzschild 黑洞事件视界内对称性恢复的自洽区域及相应的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强引力场（特别是 Schwarzschild 黑洞附近）如何通过曲率-希格斯耦合（curvature-Higgs coupling）修正希格斯有效势（Higgs effective potential），从而影响电弱对称性的破缺与恢复机制。

Method: 在 Ricci 平坦（Ricci-flat）几何中，选取主导曲率不变量——Kretschmann 标量，将其与希格斯场通过一个受普朗克尺度抑制的耦合项引入，以此修改希格斯有效势，并解析求解曲率对真空期望值的影响。

Result: 推导出使希格斯真空期望值归零的临界曲率（critical curvature）解析表达式；对于 Schwarzschild 黑洞，获得了对应的相变半径（phase-transition radius）以及标志量子引力效应（quantum-gravity effects）开始的普朗克曲率半径（Planck-curvature radius）。

Conclusion: 在 Schwarzschild 黑洞事件视界内部存在一个自洽区域，该区域内电弱对称性得以恢复，同时有效描述仍然适用；该模型通过一个无量纲的曲率-希格斯耦合（dimensionless curvature-Higgs coupling）为强引力场与希格斯真空之间的联系提供了一个简洁的解析框架。

Abstract: I investigate the behaviour of the Higgs field in Schwarzschild spacetime by introducing a Planck-suppressed coupling between the Higgs field and the Kretschmann scalar, the leading curvature invariant in Ricci-flat geometries. I show that spacetime curvature modifies the Higgs effective potential, causing the vacuum expectation value to decrease continuously with increasing curvature. A critical curvature is derived at which the Higgs vacuum expectation value vanishes, signalling a curvature-induced restoration of electroweak symmetry. For Schwarzschild black holes, we obtain analytical expressions for the critical curvature, the corresponding phase-transition radius, and the Planck-curvature radius that marks the onset of quantum-gravity effects. These results identify a self-consistent region inside the event horizon in which electroweak symmetry is restored while the effective description remains applicable. The model provides a simple analytical framework linking strong gravitational fields to the Higgs vacuum through a single dimensionless curvature-Higgs coupling.

</details>


### [103] [Test-particle dynamics in a noncommutative deformation of Einstein-Rosen waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24654)
*Diego Henrique Carvalho dos Santos,José André Lourenço,Davi C. Rodrigues,Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非交换曲面黎曼几何框架下，研究了爱因斯坦-罗森引力波的非交换变形引起的现象学效应，发现变形在径向与纵向之间产生耦合，纵向响应由引力波剖面的谱加权泛函控制，表现出对紫外结构的增强敏感性，并在单色波和Weber-Wheeler脉冲两种情形下分别给出了准周期运动和脉冲式纵向记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）变形对爱因斯坦-罗森引力波（Einstein–Rosen gravitational-waves）产生的现象学效应，特别是通过非交换曲面黎曼几何（Riemannian geometry of noncommutative surfaces）框架来揭示径向与轴向坐标之间的非交换结构如何影响测试粒子的运动。

Method: 在Chaichian等人发展的非交换曲面黎曼几何框架下，对径向与轴向坐标引入非交换结构（noncommutative structure），采用微扰论（perturbation theory）研究非相对论性测试粒子（nonrelativistic test particles）在该变形几何中的运动。

Result: 变形在径向与纵向扇区之间产生耦合，而径向动力学在考虑的微扰阶次下不受非交换变形影响；纵向响应由引力波剖面的谱加权泛函（spectrally weighted functional）控制，对紫外结构（ultraviolet structure）具有增强敏感性。单色波情形下非交换修正诱导准周期纵向运动；Weber-Wheeler脉冲情形下产生类似于引力速度记忆效应（gravitational velocity memory effect）的脉冲式纵向响应。此外，在交换广义相对论中推导了Weber-Wheeler脉冲在径向远处产生的残余径向速度的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 非交换变形使得纵向观测量的高阶谱矩信息显著影响系统行为，从而增强对引力波高频成分的敏感性；不同类型的引力波源在非交换框架下可能表现出可区分的纵向响应特征。

Abstract: We investigate phenomenological effects induced by a noncommutative deformation of Einstein--Rosen gravitational-waves within the framework of the Riemannian geometry of noncommutative surfaces developed by Chaichian et al. Considering a noncommutative structure between the radial and axial coordinates, we study perturbatively the motion of nonrelativistic test particles in the corresponding deformed geometry. We show that the deformation generates a coupling between the radial and longitudinal sectors, while the radial dynamics remains unaffected by the noncommutative deformation at the perturbative order considered. The induced longitudinal response is governed by a spectrally weighted functional of the gravitational-wave profile, implying that longitudinal observables depend on higher-order spectral moments than their commutative radial counterparts. As a consequence, noncommutative effects exhibit enhanced sensitivity to the ultraviolet structure of the gravitational configuration. We analyze separately monochromatic Einstein--Rosen waves and the localized Weber--Wheeler pulse. In the monochromatic case, the noncommutative correction induces a quasi-periodic longitudinal motion, whereas for the Weber--Wheeler pulse it produces an impulsive longitudinal response analogous to a gravitational velocity memory effect. In addition, we derive an explicit analytical expression in commutative General Relativity for the residual radial velocity generated by the Weber--Wheeler pulse at large radial distances in the perturbative regime.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond-eikonal diffraction integral in gravitational lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24723)
*Emma Bruyère,Giulia Cusin,Cyril Pitrou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了引力透镜中衍射积分（diffraction integral）的推导过程，提出一种超越几何光学程函近似（beyond-eikonal expansion）的系统方法来评估基尔霍夫积分（Kirchhoff integral），并证明衍射效应与超越程函修正是同一物理的等价描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中用于引力透镜唯象应用的衍射积分是基尔霍夫积分的近似评估，但其推导过程中所涉及的近似条件尚未被系统性追踪与澄清。本文旨在建立一种系统框架，明确各近似步骤的适用范围，并揭示衍射现象在引力透镜中的物理起源。

Method: 采用超越程函展开（beyond-eikonal expansion）的方法系统评估基尔霍夫积分，仔细追踪标准推导中涉及的所有近似步骤。在该框架下，将通常的衍射积分作为领头阶贡献恢复，并显式给出一个在真实透镜场景中通常很小的修正项。进一步通过对所有几何光学像求和，证明了该评估等价于波传播的超越程函展开。

Result: 恢复衍射积分作为领头阶贡献，并获得一个在实际透镜场景中通常较小的修正项。证明基尔霍夫积分的评估结果与对所有几何光学像（geometric-optics images）求和后的超越程函展开等价。

Conclusion: 衍射效应（diffraction effects）与超越程函修正（beyond-eikonal corrections）并非两种不同的现象，而是同一基本物理过程的不同但等价的描述。这一发现澄清了引力透镜中衍射的物理起源。

Abstract: We revisit the derivation of the diffraction integral, which is an approximate evaluation of the Kirchhoff integral, widely used in the literature for phenomenological applications in gravitational lensing. We propose a systematic approach to evaluate the Kirchhoff integral within a beyond-eikonal expansion, carefully tracking all approximations involved in its standard derivation. In this framework, we recover the usual diffraction integral as the leading contribution, together with a correction term that is typically small in realistic lensing scenarios. We further show that our evaluation of the Kirchhoff integral is equivalent to a beyond-eikonal expansion of wave propagation, after summing over all geometric-optics images. This result clarifies the physical origin of diffraction in gravitational lensing and demonstrates that diffraction effects and beyond-eikonal corrections are not distinct phenomena, but rather different but equivalent descriptions of the same underlying physics.

</details>

### [105] [Tunable mesoscopic numerical model for bacterial biofilms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23677)
*J. Martin-Roca,B. Wu-Zhang,J. Oller,J. Ramirez,C. Valeriani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可调中尺度模型（tunable mesoscale model），用于细菌生物膜（bacterial biofilm）的流变学计算，该模型通过耗散粒子动力学（Dissipative Particle Dynamics, DPD）框架结合Gillespie启发的算法，显式模拟胞外聚合物（EPS）基质中聚合物-细菌间可逆交联的动态形成与断裂，揭示了生物膜结构由聚合物-聚合物与聚合物-细菌交联的竞争所主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来细菌生物膜的流变学计算提供一个明确的建模基础，特别是需要显式纳入胞外聚合物（EPS）基质中可逆交联（reversible crosslinking）的动态演化特征。

Method: 采用可调中尺度模型，基于耗散粒子动力学（DPD）框架，并结合Gillespie启发的算法，使聚合物和细菌之间的键合（bonds）能够动态形成与断裂，从而捕捉网络结构的内在演化特性。

Result: 生物膜结构由聚合物-聚合物交联与聚合物-细菌交联之间的竞争所决定，这一竞争受结合能（binding energy）、连接子可用性（linker availability）和键刚度（bond stiffness）的调控。

Conclusion: 提供了一个最小模型（minimal model），有助于理解两类物种（聚合物与细菌）之间的竞争机制，为生物膜结构形成的物理规律提供了简洁的理论框架。

Abstract: We present a tunable mesoscale model to provide a basis for future rheological calculations of of bacterial biofilms, explicitly incorporating reversible crosslinking within the extracellular polymeric substance (EPS) matrix. Using a Dissipative Particle Dynamics framework combined with a Gillespie-inspired algorithm, bonds between polymers and bacteria dynamically form and break, capturing the intrinsically evolving nature of the network. We show that biofilm structure is governed by a competition between polymer-polymer and polymer-bacteria crosslinks, controlled by binding energy, linker availability, and bond stiffness and provide a minimal model that helps to understand the competition between both species.

</details>


### [106] [Stochastic Counterdiabatic Driving via Biorthogonal Liouvillian Eigenmodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24393)
*Sandeep Suresh Cranganore,Sebastian Lehner,Johannes Brandstetter,Max Welling*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于规范型变换（gauge-type transforms）的数值框架，通过福克-普朗克生成元（Fokker-Planck generator）的精确谱分解构造反非绝热修正（counterdiabatic correction），在有限时间驱动随机系统中完美消除非绝热滞后（non-adiabatic lag），使得伴随自由能模拟（escorted free energy simulations）在任意驱动速度下均能精确满足轨迹等式 $\mathcal{W}_\mathbf{u} = \Delta\mathcal{F}$，数值验证表明非绝热滞后被抑制约12–16个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间驱动随机系统会产生过量耗散（excess dissipation），导致演化概率分布滞后于瞬时平衡分布，从而降低基于Jarzynski等式的非平衡自由能估计器的收敛性。现有构造伴随场的方法（如流场法、靶向自由能微扰、学习微分同胚）仍难以获得闭合解析形式，亟需一种通用的数值框架来解决这一挑战。

Method: 基于规范型变换替代广义坐标变换，利用含时福克-普朗克生成元（time-dependent Fokker-Planck generator）的精确谱分解构建伴随自由能模拟的数值框架。通过Liouville算符的双正交分解（biorthogonal decomposition）直接导出反非绝热修正，该修正作用于瞬时平衡分布，可在任意驱动速度下精确抵消非绝热滞后，形式上类似于量子系统中的捷径到绝热（shortcuts-to-adiabaticity）技术如Berry过渡驱动（transitionless driving）。

Result: 在含时双阱势和谐波陷阱中的过阻尼粒子模拟验证表明，反非绝热条件精确满足至机器精度；非绝热滞后在总变差距离（total variation distance）上被抑制约12个数量级，在KL散度（KL divergence）上被抑制约16个数量级。确定性传播的福克-普朗克密度在所有协议速度下均呈现耗散功 $\mathcal{W}_{\text{diss}}(t) \approx 0$。

Conclusion: 该数值框架成功实现了完美伴随自由能模拟，在任意驱动速度下精确消除非绝热滞后并达成零方差估计，为统计力学中的自由能计算和随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）提供了高精度计算工具。

Abstract: Finite-time driving of stochastic systems generates excess dissipation, causing the evolving probability distribution to lag behind the instantaneous equilibrium, and consequently degrading the convergence of nonequilibrium free energy estimators based on the Jarzynski equality. Escorted free energy simulations address the non-adiabatic lag by engineering control fields $\mathbf{u}$ that eliminate the lag, enforcing the trajectory-wise equality $\mathcal{W}_\mathbf{u} = Δ\mathcal{F}$, and yielding zero-variance estimators. However, constructing the escorting field in closed form remains a challenge, approached variously through flow-field methods, targeted free energy perturbation, or learned diffeomorphisms. In this work, we construct a complementary numerical framework based on gauge-type transforms instead of generalized coordinate transforms for perfect escorting based on the exact spectral decomposition of the time-dependent Fokker-Planck generator. The biorthogonal decomposition of the Liouville operator directly yields a counterdiabatic correction whose action on the instantaneous equilibrium distribution exactly cancels the non-adiabatic lag at arbitrary driving speed in formal analogy with shortcuts-to-adiabaticity techniques such as Berry's transitionless driving for quantum systems. Numerical verification for simulations of an overdamped particle in a time-varying double-well potential and harmonic traps confirms that the counterdiabatic condition is satisfied to machine precision, with the non-adiabatic lag suppressed by roughly twelve orders of magnitude in total variation distance and sixteen orders in KL divergence relative to the unescorted dynamics. As a diagnostic, we demonstrate vanishing dissipated work $\mathcal{W}_{\text{diss}}(t) \approx 0$ for the deterministically propagated Fokker-Planck density across all protocol speeds.

</details>


### [107] [Reframing the coupling force of adaptive resolution simulation in terms of the Liouville-type hierarchy for open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24574)
*Julian F. Hille,Rupert Klein*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究为自适应分辨率模拟（AdResS）中用于补偿界面伪影的耦合力（即“热动力”）提供了一种新视角，证明其大部分可归入开放系统的刘维尔型层级理论模型框架，并由此导出了一维积分表达式，进而分析了该力对模拟参数的依赖关系，为简化其数值计算和建立新的界面验证标准提供了理论依据，最后通过超临界Lennard-Jones流体的模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放多粒子系统的自适应分辨率模拟（AdResS）中，不同分辨率区域之间的界面会引入伪影，需要通过施加耦合力（即“热动力”，thermodynamic force）进行补偿。然而，该力的理论基础尚不够清晰，其数值计算和验证方法也缺乏与界面特性相匹配的系统性准则。本文旨在为这一耦合力提供新的理论视角。

Method: 本文从开放系统的刘维尔型层级理论（Liouville-type hierarchy）出发，将热动力（thermodynamic force）的大部分贡献纳入该理论模型框架。针对简单原子流体这一具体情形，推导出了一维积分表达式，从而分析了热动力对关键模拟参数的依赖关系。

Result: 导出了适用于简单原子流体的一维积分表达式，阐明了热动力对模拟参数的依赖关系。在此基础上，提出了简化热动力数值计算的新方法，以及适用于界面特性（interfacial nature）的新的验证标准。对处于不同热力学状态的超临界Lennard-Jones流体的模拟验证了上述理论分析。

Conclusion: 本文建立的热动力理论视角不仅为理解AdResS中的界面补偿机制提供了更坚实的理论基础，还为实际模拟中热动力的计算与验证提供了更具物理依据的简化策略和评估准则。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel perspective on the coupling force employed to compensate the interface artifacts prevalent in adaptive resolution simulations (AdResS) of open many-particle systems. We show that a substantial part of this "thermodynamic force" can be framed in terms of the theoretical model of the Liouville-type hierarchy for open systems. The correspondence is made explicit for the case of a simple atomistic fluid, for which a one-dimensional integral expression is derived. This enables the analysis for dependencies of the thermodynamic force on important simulation parameters, which is taken to inspire both simplifications for the numerical calculation of the thermodynamic force and new criteria for its validation that are adequate to the interfacial nature of the problem. The theoretical claims are then verified in a simulation study of the atomistic supercritical Lennard-Jones fluid at different thermodynamic states.

</details>

### [108] [Exact Holomorphic Cayley Lumps in Eight Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22775)
*Graeme Donald Robertson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了八维sigma模型（描述四维欧氏空间嵌入）的一类精确自对偶解。该理论具有Spin(7)不变的Cayley四形式，可导出Nambu-Goto作用的Bogomolny型下界；其饱和产生一阶自对偶方程。四元数多项式尝试因符号残余失配而系统性失效，表明存在固有代数障碍；而C²到C⁴的全纯嵌入恒满足自对偶方程。计算机代数验证了480种八元数基中仅少数能校准给定全纯嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维sigma模型中的自对偶解结构，特别是描述四维欧氏空间嵌入的八维模型，并揭示该构造与早期Corrigan等人关于高维自对偶性以及Gauntlett等人关于校准膜（calibrated branes）工作的联系。

Method: 基于Spin(7)不变的Cayley四形式导出Bogomolny型下界，从而得到一阶自对偶方程；分析四元数多项式（quaternionic polynomial）解的存在性；引入全纯嵌入（holomorphic embedding）作为替代方案；并利用计算机代数对所有480种可容许八元数基（octonionic bases）进行系统计算。

Result: 四元数多项式尝试因残余符号失配而系统性失败，表明存在固有代数障碍；而C²到C⁴的全纯嵌入恒满足自对偶方程；在全部480种八元数基中，仅有一小部分基能够校准给定的全纯嵌入。

Conclusion: 本文提出的构造与Corrigan等人及Gauntlett等人的早期工作存在内在联系；全纯嵌入为构建一类精确自对偶解提供了有效路径，而四元数多项式方法的失败揭示了该问题中代数结构的限制性。

Abstract: A class of exact self-dual solutions is constructed for an eight-dimensional sigma model describing four-dimensional embeddings in Euclidean space. The theory admits a Spin(7)-invariant Cayley four-form that generates a Bogomolny-type bound for the Nambu-Goto action. The saturation of this bound leads to a first-order self-duality equation for embedding coordinates. A quaternionic polynomial ansatz has been shown to fail systematically by a residual sign mismatch, indicating an intrinsic algebraic obstruction. By contrast, the holomorphic embeddings of C^2 into C^4 satisfy the self-duality equation identically. The relationship between the present construction and the earlier work of Corrigan et al. on higher-dimensional self-duality and Gauntlett et al. on calibrated branes is discussed. Computer algebraic calculations using all 480 admissible octonionic bases show that only a small subset calibrates a given holomorphic embedding.

</details>


### [109] [Comments on deformed phase-space cosmology, SUSY and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22791)
*O. López-Aguayo,J. C. López-Domínguez,G. E. Pérez-Cuéllar,M. Sabido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了描述史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）内部的时间依赖度规的超对称（supersymmetric, SUSY）变形版本。通过极小超空间（minisuperspace）坐标及其正则动量的非对易变形构建玻色子模型，随后利用超对称量子力学（SUSY quantum mechanics）方法构造超荷（supercharges）并导出变形的SUSY Wheeler-DeWitt方程，最后通过WKB近似获得经典动力学并讨论变形对相空间宇宙学和黑洞的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超对称与非对易变形引入黑洞内部度规的量子引力描述中，从而探索经典奇点问题的可能解决途径及其在变形相空间宇宙学中的后果。

Method: 首先在极小超空间坐标及正则动量上引入非对易变形，构建玻色子模型；随后运用超对称量子力学方法构造变形模型的超荷，并据此导出变形的超对称Wheeler-DeWitt方程；最后采用WKB近似获得经典解及哈密顿约束条件。

Result: 成功构造了变形的超对称Wheeler-DeWitt方程；通过WKB近似找到了超对称情形与玻色子情形下的经典解，并确定了满足哈密顿约束的条件。

Conclusion: 变形（deformation）在相空间宇宙学和黑洞物理中产生显著效应，为理解量子引力框架下黑洞内部的经典动力学与奇点规避提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This paper presents the construction of the supersymmetric (SUSY) deformed version of the time dependent metric that describes the interior of the Schwarzschild BH. First, the noncommutative bosonic model is constructed by inducing the deformation in the minisuperspace coordinates and the associated canonical momentum. Following the methods of SUSY quantum mechanics, the supercharges of the deformed models are constructed and the deformed SUSY Wheeler-DeWitt equation is obtained. The classical dynamics are also obtained using the WKB approximation, we find the classical solutions and the conditions to satisfy the Hamiltonian constraint for both the SUSY and bosonic cases. Finally, the effects of the deformation in the context of deformed phase space cosmology and black holes are discussed.

</details>


### [110] [Tensor Catalyzed Decoherence of Primordial Scalar Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22914)
*C. P. Burgess,D. Dineen,R. Holman,Greg Kaplanek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次完整计算了最简单单时钟暴胀模型中标量度规扰动的退相干主导贡献，发现该贡献不受慢滚压制，与张量模退相干同量级，并验证了其紫外有限性及超哈勃极限下的局域高斯特性的退化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中关于暴胀原始涨落退相干（decoherence）的计算未能完整考虑所有主导阶相互作用，导致得到的结果受到慢滚压制；本文旨在填补这一空白，提供首个完整的主导阶退相干计算。

Method: 在单时钟暴胀模型（single-clock inflationary model）中，假设退相干源于度规涨落的标量部分与由未测量的短波长标量模和张量模（tensor modes）构成的环境之间的引力相互作用；在 $(H/M_p)^2$ 和慢滚参数 $\epsilon_1$ 的领先阶下，纳入所有贡献性相互作用，包括同时涉及张量和标量环境的交叉相互作用。

Result: 主导退相干贡献不受慢滚压制，其量级与张量模退相干相同；该贡献源于同时涉及张量和标量环境的交叉相互作用，这在以往分开处理时被遗漏；验证了主导贡献的紫外有限性（UV finite）；确认在超哈勃（super-Hubble）暴胀深层区域，驱动退相干的相互作用退化为时间局域（time-local）高斯形式。

Conclusion: 该结果修正了此前文献中关于退相干受慢滚压制的结论；时间局域高斯退化行为可用于可靠计算观测相关的晚期纯度演化，克服了朴素微扰论（naive perturbation theory）在该区域失效的问题；同时表明破坏退相干微扰论的效应并不会影响暴胀对原始涨落振幅的基本预言。

Abstract: We provide the first complete calculation of the leading contribution to the decoherence of primordial fluctuations in the scalar part of the metric fluctuations within simplest, single-clock, inflationary models, assuming decoherence comes from gravitational interactions with an environment made up of the other, unmeasured, short-wavelength scalar and tensor modes. We include the contributions from {\it all} of the contributing interactions at leading order in powers of $(H/M_p)^2$ and of the slow-roll parameter $ε_1$. Unlike all extant calculations in the literature we find a result that is {\it not} slow-roll suppressed (and so is the same order of magnitude as tensor-mode decoherence). The difference arises because the dominant decoherence comes from interactions that involve {\it both} the tensor and scalar environments simultaneously (and so are missed when they are examined separately). We verify that the leading contributions to decoherence are UV finite (as they must be). We confirm that the interactions driving decoherence become time-local and Gaussian in the deep super-Hubble inflationary regime and show how this can be used to reliably compute the evolution of the purity in the late-time regime relevant for observations (where naive perturbation theory is known to break down). The same derivation shows explicitly why the effects that undermine perturbation theory for decoherence do not also undermine the basic inflationary prediction for the amplitude of primordial fluctuations (in agreement with general arguments).2

</details>


### [111] [Mass/electric versus NUT/magnetic charges: duality from scattering amplitudes in $D\geq4$ and for all bosonic spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22938)
*Ricardo Monteiro,Lecheng Ren,Daniel Siretanu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了 Kerr-NUT 度规及其在 D≥4 维中的自旋-s 场，揭示了质量与多重 NUT 荷在旋转变形径向方程中的不同角色，建立了高维“电磁”对偶性（electric-magnetic duality），并通过动量空间中的三点散射振幅展示了其对偶结构，最终指出高维自对偶引力（self-dual gravity）不具备与 D=4 情形类似的可积性（integrability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Kerr-NUT 度规及相关自旋场推广到任意维度 D≥4，澄清质量与多重 NUT 荷在旋转背景下的物理解释，并探索高维电磁对偶性及其在散射振幅中的体现。

Method: 采用 Cartesian 多 Kerr-Schild 坐标明确写出旋转变形径向方程，将质量与 NUT 荷视为该方程的不同解；在动量空间中通过三点散射振幅构建解，使用自旋提升算子（spin-raising operator）S_μ 作用于标量种子，标量种子的旋转依赖分别重整为贝塞尔函数 J_{(D-5)/2}（电扇区）和 J_{-(D-5)/2}（磁扇区）。

Result: 建立了高维“电磁”对偶性：对 D≥6，多重磁荷需取相等值；电扇区与磁扇区的三点散射振幅通过贝塞尔函数指标的正负对称性相联系；对 2→2 经典散射振幅的分析未发现 D≥5 中自对偶引力的可积性。

Conclusion: 高维 Kerr-NUT 度规与自旋场存在自然的电磁对偶结构，但高维自对偶引力不具 D=4 情况下的可积性，说明该对偶性在更高维度中的动力学性质有本质不同。

Abstract: We revisit Kerr-NUT metrics and related spin-$s$ fields in $D\geq4$, and study their associated scattering amplitudes. Starting in position space, we highlight the interpretation of mass and (multiple) NUT charges as being associated to distinct solutions of the rotation-deformed radial equation, which is made explicit in Cartesian multi-Kerr-Schild coordinates. This interpretation extends to electromagnetism with magnetic-type charges, and also extends to higher-spin counterparts, in accordance with the classical double or multi copy. We then establish a notion of ``electric-magnetic" duality in higher dimensions, relating mass/electric to NUT/magnetic charges, which generalises the $D=4$ case in a novel manner. For $D\geq6$, this involves the choice where the multiple magnetic charges are equal. The duality is revealed in momentum space, by the 3-point scattering amplitudes that generate the solutions. For all spins, these amplitudes are constructed from a spin-raising operator $\mathcal S_μ$ and take the form $\varepsilon^{μ_1\cdotsμ_s}{\mathcal S}_{μ_1}\cdots {\mathcal S}_{μ_s}$ acting on a scalar seed. The scalar seed of the electric sector is dual to that of the magnetic sector: where the former's rotation dependence resums to a Bessel function $J_{\frac{D-5}{2}}$, the latter resums to a Bessel function $J_{-\frac{D-5}{2}}$. Finally, we explore the notion of self-duality that arises from this picture. Studying the classical $2\!\mapsto\!2$ amplitudes that determine leading-order scattering, we find no evidence of a higher-dimensional analogue of the integrability of $D=4$ self-dual gravity.

</details>


### [112] [Overcrowding and the Finite-$N$ Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23025)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Anik Rudra,Augustine Larweh Mahu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Hironaka分解研究有限N下规范不变算子的Hilbert空间结构，证明主不变量可选取为齐次单迹算子，并发现非平凡次级不变量必然在远低于通用迹恒等式的标度出现，揭示了矩阵模型中的“拥挤效应”与快速 scrambler 时间尺度的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大N极限自由描述无法捕捉有限N下规范不变算子Hilbert空间的完整结构，特别是有限N迹关系（finite-N trace relations）带来的重整化效应。本文旨在建立一种系统性代数框架来理解这一结构。

Method: 采用d个厄米N×N矩阵的不变环的Hironaka分解（Hironaka decomposition）作为核心工具，分析主不变量（primary invariants）与次级不变量（secondary invariants）的代数结构，并研究其标度行为。

Result: 证明了主不变量总可选取为齐次单迹算子（homogeneous single-trace operators）；非平凡次级不变量必然在度数L_{N,d}=2log_d N+log_d log_d N+O_d(1)出现，该标度远低于通用迹恒等式（universal trace identity）的度数N+1。这一“全局拥挤效应”（global overcrowding effect）导致指数级增长的独立短单迹算子竞争仅1+(d-1)N^2个代数独立坐标，且拥挤标度与快速 scrambler（fast scramblers）的最快加扰时间一致。

Conclusion: 拥挤效应与加扰时间标度的一致性并非偶然，它为矩阵模型中的加扰（scrambling）提供了微观代数图像。Hironaka分解可作为规范理论集体描述中组织微扰和固有有限N信息的自然框架。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ trace relations reorganize the Hilbert space of gauge-invariant operators beyond the freely generated large-$N$ description. We study this structure using the Hironaka decomposition of the invariant ring of $d$ Hermitian $N\times N$ matrices. We first prove that the primary invariants may always be chosen to be homogeneous single-trace operators. We then show that, for any such choice, a nontrivial secondary invariant must appear by degree $L_{N,d}=2\log_d N+\log_d\log_d N+\mathcal{O}_d(1)$, which is parametrically below the first universal trace identity at degree $N+1$. This is a global overcrowding effect: exponentially many independent short single traces compete for only $1+(d-1)N^2$ algebraically independent coordinates. The overcrowding scale matches the fastest scrambling times expected for fast scramblers. We argue that this agreement of scales is not accidental: overcrowding provides a microscopic algebraic picture of scrambling in matrix models. Low-rank examples show that secondary invariants can distinguish configurations with identical primary data and, for suitable dynamics, label semiclassical sectors connected by instantons. These results identify the Hironaka decomposition as a natural framework for organizing perturbative and intrinsically finite-$N$ information in collective descriptions of gauge theories.

</details>


### [113] [Horava Stars Revisited: New Phases of Incompressible Stars and Black Holes, and Buchdahl's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23172)
*Mu-In Park*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非投射型Horava引力（non-projectable Horava gravity）中研究了不可压缩静态恒星的精确解，推导了修正的Buchdahl界，发现了满足所有标准能量条件的超致密天体（UCOs）与规则黑洞解，并证明了Buchdahl定理在该引力理论中的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Horava引力作为一种通过各向异性标度（anisotropic scaling with z>1）放弃爱因斯坦对时空平等处理的重整化引力模型，能够避免鬼场问题（ghost problem）。本文旨在该理论框架下寻找静态恒星的精确解，并探索其致密性边界及可能存在的规则黑洞解。

Method: 以z=3 Horava引力中球对称静态黑洞解作为恒星外部时空，针对不可压缩（均匀密度）静态恒星，在任意宇宙学常数和各向同性压强条件下求解精确解，并设定lambda=1使得Birkhoff定理成立。在此基础上分析宇宙学常数为零时的致密性上限，以及负压强情形下的超致密天体解。

Result: 当宇宙学常数为零时，均匀密度恒星的修正Buchdahl界取值范围为4/9到1。具有负压强的超致密天体（UCOs）的致密性C>1，且同时满足全部四项标准能量条件。规则（非奇点）黑洞解存在于质量超过极值黑洞质量的情形下，物质局域于内视界（inner horizon）包围的类时核（timelike core）区域，外部度规不受核心物质影响。此外还发现了具有正压强但违反所有标准能量条件的负质量恒星。

Conclusion: 本文证明Buchdahl定理在非投射型Horava引力中成立，证明过程依赖于平均密度的权重单调性条件（weight-monotonicity condition）和Birkhoff定理，以及平均密度非增的常规假设。规则黑洞解是Birkhoff定理在Horava引力中的重要体现。

Abstract: I study a particular exact solution for static stars in four-dimensional non-projectable Horava gravity, which has been proposed as a renormalizable gravity model without the ghost problem by abandoning Einstein's equal-footing treatment of space and time through anisotropic scaling with z > 1. Considering the spherically symmetric static black-hole solutions in z = 3 Horava gravity as the exterior spacetimes of stars, I obtain an exact solution for incompressible (i.e., uniform-density) static stars with an arbitrary cosmological constant and isotropic pressure, and lambda=1, in which Birkhoff's theorem holds. For a vanishing cosmological constant, I obtain a modified Buchdahl bound on the maximum compactness for uniform-density stars, which ranges from 4/9 to 1. By contrast, I find that Ultra-Compact Objects (UCOs) with compactness C > 1 also exist with negative pressure while, surprisingly, satisfying all four standard energy conditions. UCOs include the regular (non-singular) black-hole solutions with masses above the extremal black-hole mass. In these solutions, the matter is localized at the timelike core region bounded by the inner horizon, while their exterior metrics are unaffected by the core matter and identical to the corresponding vacuum Horava black-hole solutions. These long-sought regular black-hole solutions are essential manifestations of Birkhoff's theorem in Horava gravity. I also find negative-mass stars with positive pressure that violate all standard energy conditions. Finally, I prove Buchdahl's theorem in non-projectable Horava gravity. The proof uses a weight-monotonicity condition on the average density and Birkhoff's theorem, together with the usual assumption that average density is non-increasing.

</details>


### [114] [On the Nucleon Effective Mass in Neutron Stars Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23253)
*Yuuki Sugiyama,Akira Dohi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了核子有效质量（Dirac质量与Landau质量）对中子星冷却性质的影响，指出两种质量的区分在以往模拟中被忽略，并通过Walecka模型与相对论平均场（RMF）理论下的真实状态方程进行冷却模拟，证明有效质量的处理可能是中子星冷却不确定性的重要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的冷却强烈依赖于其内部超高密度核物质的状态方程（equation of state），而有效核子质量（effective nucleon masses）在超新星爆发和原中子星冷却中的影响已被近期研究关注，但其本身的不确定性来源——标量介子吸引相互作用的差异以及Dirac质量与Landau质量的定义区别——尚未在中子星冷却模拟中被充分区分和评估。

Method: 首先采用Walecka模型，该模型允许在统一框架内引入Dirac质量和Landau质量；随后基于该模型计算中子的发射率（emissivity）和直接Urca过程（direct Urca process）及相应冷却曲线；进一步采用基于相对论平均场（RMF）理论的两种真实状态方程进行冷却模拟，仅通过有效质量特性区分二者。

Result: 研究表明，Dirac质量和Landau质量对高质量中子星的冷却过程有显著影响，而大多数先前冷却模拟未区分这两种质量；基于RMF理论的冷却模拟结果与Walecka模型的结论大体一致。

Conclusion: 有效核子质量的处理可能是中子星冷却模拟中一个重要的不确定性来源。

Abstract: The cooling of neutrons stars, which are composed of ultra-high-density nuclear matter, strongly depends on the equation of state because the dominant cooling sources are neutrinos produced in their interiors. Here, we investigate the cooling properties of neutron stars, focusing on effective nucleon masses, whose impacts have been recently examined in supernova explosion and proto-neutron star cooling. The uncertainties of effective masses originate from the difference in the attractive interaction of scalar meson and the definition of the Dirac and Landau masses. At first, we employ so-called Walecka models that allow these two masses to be introduced within a unified framework and evaluate the resulting cooling curves. Based on the Walecka model, we compute the emissivity of the neutrons and the direct Urca process and their cooling curves. We demonstrate that the Dirac and Landau masses affect cooling of massive neutron stars, although most previous cooling simulations do not distinguish them. We also perform cooling simulations with two realistic equation of states based on relativistic mean-field (RMF) theory, whose differences arise from properties of only effective masses among characteristic parameters. These results are broadly consistent with those obtained from the Walecka models, indicating that the treatment of effective nucleon masses may be an important source of uncertainty in neutron-star cooling.

</details>


### [115] [Effects of Curvature-Scalar Coupling on Vacuum Energy in Flat (3+1)-Dimensional Space-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23331)
*Volodymyr Gorkavenko,Oleh Barabash,Pavlo Nakaznyi,Mariia Tsarenkova,Nazar Yakovenko,Andrii Zaporozhchenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本研究在平坦(3+1)维时空中，研究了磁性拓扑缺陷（magnetic topological defect）对带电有质量标量场（charged massive scalar field）真空极化（vacuum polarization）的影响。缺陷被建模为不可穿透的有限厚度磁通管，并在其表面施加最一般的Robin边界条件（Robin boundary condition）。结果表明，在平坦时空中，磁性拓扑缺陷产生的总真空能量对曲率耦合参数ξ的依赖仅在一般Robin边界条件下显著，而在Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件下则不然，凸显了边界条件在真空极化现象中的非平凡作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索磁性拓扑缺陷如何影响真空极化，特别关注边界条件在其中的角色，以揭示平坦时空中曲率耦合（curvature coupling）与真空涨落之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 将磁性拓扑缺陷建模为一个带有内部磁通、对物质场不可穿透的有限厚度管状结构，并在其表面施加最一般形式的Robin边界条件，以此对带电有质量标量场的真空极化进行解析分析。

Result: 在平坦时空中，总真空能量对曲率耦合ξ的依赖仅出现于一般Robin边界条件下，而Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件对应例外情况。研究详细分析了该效应随边界条件参数的变化关系。

Conclusion: 边界条件在真空极化现象中扮演了非平凡且关键的角色：即使是在平坦时空背景下，最一般的Robin边界条件也能使诱导真空能量显著依赖于曲率耦合，这一发现深化了对拓扑缺陷附近量子场涨落行为的理解。

Abstract: We investigated how a magnetic topological defect affects the vacuum polarization of a charged massive scalar field in a flat $(3+1)$-dimensional space-time. The defect was modeled as an impenetrable to matter field finite-thickness tube with magnetic flux inside. We implemented the most general form of the Robin boundary condition on the surface of the magnetic tube, which enables a fully general analysis of the problem. We have found that in flat spacetime, the total vacuum energy generated by a magnetic topological defect depends on the curvature $ξ$, except for special cases corresponding to the Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions. By contrast, when Robin's general boundary conditions are imposed, the induced vacuum energy acquires an explicit dependence on the curvature coupling $ξ$, which is significant even in flat space-time. A detailed study of the dependence of the effect on the boundary condition parameter has been carried out. The obtained results highlight the nontrivial role played by boundary conditions in vacuum polarization phenomena.

</details>


### [116] [Standard Model Effective Field Theory and Oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23616)
*Z. Drogosz,E. I. Sfakianakis,K. Slawinska,A. Wereszczynski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明希格斯势能中引入一个维度-6算符（dimension-six operator）$O_6 = (Φ^\dagger Φ)^3$，能够在标准模型$SU(2)$玻色子扇区中将振荡子（oscillons）的寿命延长数个数量级，该效应在希格斯与W玻色子质量的物理比值$m_H/m_W=1.556$下成立，且算符耦合常数低于当前实验上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡子（oscillons）作为标准模型中非拓扑孤子（non-topological solitons）的候选对象，其稳定性与寿命是理解早期宇宙玻色子动力学和超出标准模型新物理的重要切入点。文中指出，此前对振荡子在标准模型中稳定性的研究尚未系统考虑有效场论（effective field theory）框架下高维算符的修正效应。

Method: 采用有效场论方法，在标准模型希格斯势能中显式加入维度-6算符$O_6 = (Φ^\dagger Φ)^3$，并在$SU(2)$玻色子扇区中数值求解含时运动方程，系统扫描耦合常数参数空间，检验振荡子寿命的变化行为。

Result: 在物理质量比$m_H/m_W=1.556$下，维度-6算符的引入可使振荡子寿命延长数个数量级。该增强效应在算符耦合常数低于当前实验上限的整个允许区域内均可观测到，表明高维算符对振荡子稳定性的影响不可忽略。

Conclusion: 标准模型中的振荡子对新的物理尺度（new physics scale）高度敏感，其寿命可作为探测维度-6算符耦合强度的灵敏探针（sensitive probe）。该发现可能对早期宇宙的玻色子动力学和相变（phase transition）过程产生重要影响。

Abstract: We show that the inclusion of a dimension-six operator in the Higgs potential has a dramatic impact on the stability of oscillons in the $SU(2)$ bosonic sector of the Standard Model, extending their lifetime by orders of magnitude. This happens for the physical value of the ratio between the Higgs and W boson masses, $m_H/m_W=1.556$ and for the dimension-six operator $O_6 = (Φ^\dagger Φ)^3$ whose coupling constant is below the current upper bound.

</details>


### [117] [$\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills thermodynamics from effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23620)
*Ubaid Tantary,Qianqian Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用有效场论（EFT）方法，首次计算了四维纯 $\mathcal{N}=2$ 超对称杨-米尔斯（SYM）理论在有限温度下、弱耦合展开至't Hooft耦合常数 $\lambda$ 二阶的自由能密度。其中 $T$ 尺度贡献来自完整理论的三圈真空图，$\sqrt\lambda T$ 尺度贡献来自降维三维有效理论的二圈计算，最终得到了全新的 $\lambda^2$ 阶项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 热力学性质的基础上，进一步计算渐近自由（asymptotically free）的 $\mathcal{N}=2$ SYM 在有限温度下的弱耦合自由能密度，以获取更高的 $\lambda^2$ 阶修正。

Method: 采用有效场论方法分层处理：硬尺度（hard scale）$T$ 的贡献由完整理论中的无质量三圈真空图计算；电尺度（electric scale）$\sqrt\lambda\,T$ 的贡献则来自降维后的三维有效理论的二圈计算。通过耦合重整化抵消项和 EFT 单位算符（unit-operator）抵消项消除 $1/\varepsilon$ 极点，剩余标度依赖由单圈 $\beta$ 函数（beta function）固定。

Result: 展开结果重现了此前已知的 $\lambda^{3/2}$ 阶及更低阶项，并首次得到了 $\lambda^2$ 阶的新项。耦合重整化抵消项与 EFT 单位算符抵消项共同消除了所有 $1/\varepsilon$ 极点，验证了理论的自洽性。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{N}=2$ SYM 在有限温度下的自由能密度弱耦合展开已推进至 $\lambda^2$ 阶，该结果对理解渐近自由超对称规范理论的热力学性质具有参考价值。

Abstract: We compute the weak-coupling free energy density of pure four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills (SYM) theory through second order in the 't Hooft coupling $λ$ at finite temperature using effective-field-theory methods. The contribution from the hard scale $T$ is obtained from massless three-loop vacuum diagrams in the full theory, and that from the electric scale $\sqrtλ\,T$ from a two-loop calculation in the dimensionally reduced three-dimensional effective theory. In contrast to $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM, the theory is asymptotically free: the coupling-renormalization counterterm, together with the EFT unit-operator counterterm, cancels all $1/ε$ poles. The remaining renormalization-scale dependence is fixed by the one-loop beta function. The expansion reproduces earlier results through order $λ^{3/2}$, and the order-$λ^2$ term is new.

</details>


### [118] [Stability and Chaotic Dynamics in a Nonlinearly Confined ghost-sector Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23659)
*Zahra Molaee,Soleyman Fatholahzadeh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含鬼自由度（ghost degree of freedom）的哈密顿系统，通过引入四次和六次非线性项（quartic and sextic nonlinearities）来约束系统的大振幅行为。结果表明，六次非线性主导的动力学能够将鬼自由度引发的局部不稳定性限制在相空间（phase space）的紧致区域内，并观察到从准周期运动到有界混沌（bounded chaos）的转变，为经典层次上鬼动力学的正则化提供了一个可控框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统包含鬼自由度的哈密顿系统由于动能的不定性（indefinite nature of kinetic energy）通常表现出逃逸（runaway）轨迹。本文旨在通过引入高阶非线性自相互作用来克服这一不稳定性，实现系统动力学的有界演化。

Method: 模型包含一个正能量模式与一个负能量模式，并引入四次和六次非线性项以正则化大振幅行为。分析手段包括运动方程推导、哈密顿流的几何性质分析、数值积分、庞加莱截面（Poincaré surfaces of section）以及李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）分析。

Result: 六次非线性项主导渐近动力学，阻止系统逃逸至无穷远。系统展现出从规则准周期运动到混沌动力学的转变，表现为正的最大李雅普诺夫指数（positive maximal Lyapunov exponents）。混沌轨迹被限制在相空间的紧致区域内，实现了鬼哈密顿系统中的有界混沌运动。

Conclusion: 该结果为非线性自相互作用如何在经典层次上正则化鬼动力学提供了一个具体例证，并表明该模型可作为研究非标准哈密顿理论中稳定性、约束与混沌的有用原型。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear dynamics of a Hamiltonian system containing a ghost degree of freedom coupled to a canonical sector through nonlinear interactions. The model consists of one positive-energy and one negative-energy mode, augmented by quartic and sextic nonlinearities that regularize the large-amplitude behavior of the system. Unlike conventional ghost models, which typically exhibit runaway trajectories due to the indefinite nature of the kinetic energy, the present Hamiltonian possesses a confining nonlinear structure that renders the accessible energy surfaces compact for finite total energy.
  We derive the equations of motion and analyze the geometric properties of the Hamiltonian flow. Particular attention is devoted to the interplay between local ghost-induced instability and global nonlinear confinement. We show that the sextic contribution dominates the asymptotic dynamics and prevents escape to infinity despite the presence of a negative-energy sector. As a consequence, the model provides a controlled framework for studying bounded dynamics in ghost-coupled systems.
  The phase-space structure is investigated through numerical integration, Poincaré surfaces of section, and Lyapunov analysis. Depending on the interaction strength and nonlinear couplings, the system exhibits a transition from regular quasi-periodic motion to chaotic dynamics characterized by positive maximal Lyapunov exponents. Remarkably, chaotic trajectories remain confined within compact regions of phase space, yielding a realization of bounded chaotic motion in a ghost-containing Hamiltonian system.
  These results provide a concrete example of how nonlinear self-interactions can regularize ghost dynamics at the classical level and indicate a useful prototype for studying stability, confinement, and chaos in nonstandard Hamiltonian theories.

</details>


### [119] [Freezing Swampland: A Krylov Complexity Criterion for the Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23747)
*Amin Faraji Astaneh,Reza Ghomi Shurkaie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从量子信息理论（quantum information theory）出发，通过Krylov spread complexity（Krylov扩散复杂度）的行为对弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture）提出新视角：带电热场双态（thermofield double state）在极端黑洞极限下Krylov扩散被有效冻结，而放电通道的开启会解除这一冻结，从而为弱引力猜想提供了一种基于复杂度的解释。该结果连接了沼泽地纲领（swampland program）、黑洞物理与量子信息理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture）这一沼泽地纲领中的核心对偶性约束，与量子信息理论中的复杂度概念联系起来，探索其是否具有基于复杂度的新解释。

Method: 采用量子信息理论视角，分析全息对偶于AdS Reissner–Nordström黑洞的带电热场双态（thermofield double state）的Krylov spread complexity（Krylov扩散复杂度）。引入带电物质，在半经典近极端（near-extremal）框架下研究视界附近的Schwinger pair production（Schwinger对产生）效应。

Result: 在黑洞极端极限（extremal limit）下，Krylov扩散被有效冻结，回归振幅（return amplitude）变为纯相位。Schwinger对产生开启放电通道后，冻结行为被解除，动力学恢复非平凡行为。

Conclusion: 弱引力猜想可能具有基于复杂度的解释：在有可用放电通道时，不存在精确的量子态冻结。这一结果建立了沼泽地纲领、黑洞物理与量子信息理论之间的新联系。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-information-theoretic perspective on the Weak Gravity Conjecture through the behavior of Krylov spread complexity. For a charged thermofield double state holographically dual to an AdS Reissner--Nordström black hole, we show that the extremal limit on the black-hole side is accompanied by an effective freezing of Krylov spreading. In this regime, the return amplitude becomes effectively a pure phase, and the dual quantum state ceases to spread nontrivially in Krylov space. We then incorporate charged matter and study the effect of Schwinger pair production in the near-horizon region. Within this semiclassical near-extremal analysis, the frozen behavior is lifted once the discharge channel is opened, and the dynamics become nontrivial again. This suggests that the Weak Gravity Conjecture may admit a complexity-based interpretation in terms of the absence of exact freezing in the presence of an available discharge channel. More broadly, our results point to a new connection between the swampland program, black-hole physics, and quantum information theory.

</details>


### [120] [The extremal Reissner-Nordström throat from non extremal near horizon expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23760)
*Anirudhda Shinde,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极端黑洞近极端极限下的弦卡罗尔展开（String Carroll expansion）行为。对于Reissner-Nordström (RN)黑洞，弦卡罗尔展开能正确描述近视界Rindler区域，但仅靠该展开不足以恢复极端极限下的AdS₂×S²喉区几何；在Eddington-Finkelstein坐标下，二阶贡献恢复了AdS₂所需的径向依赖性，而在静态坐标下，径向扇区需要无穷级数展开才能获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非极端黑洞的近视界区域具有普遍的Rindler形式，而极端黑洞的近视界几何则呈现为Lorentzian AdS₂×S²喉区。本文旨在研究从非极端到极端极限的过渡过程中，弦卡罗尔展开（String Carroll expansion）能否以及如何捕捉这两种截然不同的近视界几何结构。

Method: 采用弦卡罗尔展开（String Carroll expansion）对非极端Reissner-Nordström (RN)黑洞的近视界度规进行展开，并在Eddington-Finkelstein坐标和静态坐标两种坐标系下分别分析近极端极限的行为，考察不同阶数展开项的贡献。

Result: 弦卡罗尔展开能正确捕捉四维非极端RN黑洞的近视界Rindler区域，但不足以恢复极端极限下的AdS₂×S²喉区。在Eddington-Finkelstein坐标下，二阶展开项恢复了AdS₂几何所需的径向依赖性；在静态坐标下，时间扇区可由二阶近视界展开获得，但径向扇区需要所有阶数的贡献，无法通过任何有限阶截断得到。

Conclusion: 弦卡罗尔展开（String Carroll expansion）在非极端情形下有效，但在极端极限下高阶项变得不可忽略，不同坐标系下获取极端喉区几何所需的展开阶数不同，表明极端极限对近视界展开结构有本质性的影响。

Abstract: The near horizon region of a non extremal black hole has a universal Rindler form, but the strict horizon limit is not an ordinary Lorentzian geometry. In the String Carroll expansion of this non extremal near horizon metric,the transverse angular directions form a base and the time-radial Rindler directions appear as a distinguished two-dimensional longitudinal sector fibered over this base. We study how this String Carroll expansion behaves for the Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole as the extremal limit is approached, where the expected near horizon geometry is the Lorentzian $AdS_2 \times S^2$ throat. We show that while for the four-dimensional non extremal RN geometry, the String Carroll expansion correctly captures the near horizon Rindler region, it is not sufficient to recover the extremal $AdS_2 \times S^2$ throat. The reason is that the higher order terms, which are suppressed in the String Carroll expansion, become essential in the extremal limit. We show that in Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, the required contribution appears at second order and restores the radial dependence needed for the $AdS_2$ geometry. The extremal $AdS_2 \times S^2$ throat is then obtained as the zero-temperature limit of the scaled throat geometry. In static coordinates, the temporal sector of the extremal throat geometry can be obtained from a second order near horizon expansion, but the radial sector cannot be obtained from any finite order truncation and requires contributions from all orders in the near horizon expansion.

</details>


### [121] [R-Twisting, Fibered Knots, and Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24037)
*Shi Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证R-扭转（R-twisting）意味着4d Argyres-Douglas理论的Seiberg-Witten曲线可与R-扭转圆结合形成环面扭结（torus knots）的映射环面（mapping tori），从而生成标记该映射环面的3d规范理论，该理论在红外（IR）捕获4d BPS谱和突变，并解决了《Cecotti:2011iy》中畴壁方法的边界问题，展现出超对称增强，且与3d秩零（rank zero）理论相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Milnor纤维化定理（Milnor fibration theorem）启发，旨在将4d Argyres-Douglas理论的Seiberg-Witten曲线与R-扭转圆相结合，以解决《Cecotti:2011iy》中畴壁方法（domain wall approach）的边界问题（boundary problem）。

Method: 通过R-扭转构造将Seiberg-Witten曲线与R-扭转圆组合为环面扭结的映射环面，进而生成由这些映射环面标记的3d规范理论。

Result: 生成的3d理论在红外捕获了4d BPS谱（BPS spectrum）和突变（mutations），展现出超对称增强（supersymmetry enhancement），并与3d秩零理论（rank zero theories）相关。

Conclusion: 该构造有效解决了《Cecotti:2011iy》中畴壁方法的边界问题，并为4d BPS谱和突变提供了3d红外描述。

Abstract: We argue that R-twisting implies the Seiberg-Witten curves of 4d Argyres-Douglas theories could be combined with the R-twisted circle to form the mapping tori of torus knots, inspired by Milnor fibration theorem. Then 3d gauge theories labeled by mapping tori are generated, which in the IR capture 4d BPS spectrum and mutations. This construction addresses the boundary problem of the domain wall approach in \cite{Cecotti:2011iy}. These 3d theories show a supersymmetry enhancement, and are related to 3d rank zero theories.

</details>


### [122] [Light-like retarded correlators and the horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24242)
*Justin R. David,Leonard Schwarze*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现共形场论（CFT）中标量初级算符的延迟关联函数（retarded correlator）在有限温度下、大类光动量（large light like momenta）时会出现反常标度行为（anomalous scaling），该行为依赖于视界曲率。通过 AdS/CFT 对偶，在平面、球面和双曲视界黑洞背景下分别推导了不同的反常标度指数，并利用 d=2 精确解、AdS_5 平面黑洞的 Heun 方程数值分析以及一般维度下的 WKB 近似予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限温度下共形场论（CFT）中延迟关联函数在大类光动量（large light like momenta）区域的标度行为，发现其偏离通常量纲分析（dimensional analysis）的预期，且该反常标度依赖于黑洞视界（horizon）的曲率。

Method: 利用 AdS/CFT 对偶（AdS/CFT correspondence）将 CFT 延迟关联函数的计算转化为黑洞背景下场方程的求解；在 d=2 时获得精确结果；对 AdS_5 平面黑洞采用 Heun 方程数值分析；在一般维度下使用 WKB 近似（WKB approximation）进行解析分析；并通过双曲视界黑洞的精确解以及大 d 极限分析提供额外验证。

Result: 平面视界黑洞的延迟关联函数标度行为为 ω^{2/(d+2)(2Δ-d)}，而球面与双曲视界黑洞则在频率平方等于视界 Casimir 时标度为 ω^{(2Δ-d)/2}；此外还计算了应力张量（stress tensor）关联函数在所有三个通道中的反常标度指数。

Conclusion: 有限温度 CFT 延迟关联函数在大类光动量下存在依赖于视界曲率的反常标度行为，该结论得到了精确解、数值分析和 WKB 近似的一致支持。

Abstract: We show that retarded correlators in conformal field theories of scalar primary operators evaluated at finite temperature using $AdS/CFT$ scale anomalously at large light like momenta. The anomalous scaling depends on the curvature of the horizon. Setting the momenta equal to the frequency $ω$, the retarded correlator for black holes with planar horizons scales as $ω^{\frac{2}{d+2} (2Δ- d) }$ as opposed to the scaling behaviour of $ω^{2Δ- d}$ expected by dimensional analysis at generic fixed momenta and large frequencies. $Δ$ is the dimension of the primary and $d$, the number of space-time dimensions. For black holes with spherical and hyperbolic horizons when the frequency squared equals the Casimir along the horizon, the retarded correlator scales as $ω^{\frac{2Δ-d}{2} }$. We establish this using exact results in $d=2$, and a numerical analysis of the Heun equation for the $AdS_5$ planar black hole and finally using the WKB approximation in general $d$. The exact result for black holes with hyperbolic horizon as well as the analysis at large $d$ provides additional checks for the anomalous scaling behaviour. Finally we evaluate the anomalous scaling exponent for the stress tensor correlator in all its 3 channels.

</details>


### [123] [Dynamical Quarks in the Ensemble of Center Vortices with Monopole Defects: Color Confinement Beyond External Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24266)
*Luis E. Oxman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动力学夸克（dynamical quarks）与有向/无向中心涡旋（center vortices）系综耦合的理论框架，通过玻色化色流（bosonized color currents）描述夸克部分。在该流体图像中，红外涡旋-单极凝聚体（vortex–monopole condensate）将有限能量构型限制为色中性，而嵌入凝聚体的中性色密度分布会产生受阻区域（frustrated regions），导致其局域化为有限尺度构型。该结果与中心涡旋主导强子谱形成的格点证据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯杨-米尔斯理论（pure Yang-Mills theory）中，中心涡旋已被用于描述夸克探针间的通量管（flux-tube）形成，但动力学夸克如何与该涡旋系综相互作用尚未被充分研究。本工作旨在将动力学夸克纳入该中心涡旋框架，以更完整地理解强子谱的形成机制。

Method: 采用玻色化色流来描述夸克部分，将夸克-涡旋相互作用纳入一个流体（fluid-like）框架；在此框架中，红外涡旋-单极凝聚体被用于分析有限能量构型的色量子数约束以及色密度局域化行为。

Result: 红外涡旋-单极凝聚体将有限能量构型限制为色中性（color neutral）；此外，中性分布的组分色密度（constituent color densities）在嵌入该凝聚体后会产生受阻区域，从而驱动其局域化为有限尺度的构型。

Conclusion: 该理论图像与格点QCD（lattice QCD）中关于中心涡旋在塑造强子谱（hadron spectrum）中发挥核心作用的证据一致，表明所提出的框架能够有效描述动力学夸克与中心涡旋系综的相互作用。

Abstract: We consider the interaction of dynamical quarks with the ensemble of oriented and nonoriented center vortices proposed to describe flux-tube formation between quark probes in pure Yang-Mills theory. The quark sector is described in terms of bosonized color currents. Within this fluid-like framework, the infrared vortex--monopole condensate restricts finite-energy configurations to be color neutral. Furthermore, neutral distributions of constituent color densities embedded in the condensate generate frustrated regions, providing a mechanism for their localization into finite-size configurations. The resulting picture is consistent with lattice evidence indicating that center vortices play a central role in shaping the hadron spectrum.

</details>


### [124] [General Properties of the Thermo-Metric for CV event manifolds and the magnetization combinatorial scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24342)
*Pietro Fre,Alexander S. Sorin,Mario Trigiante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在前期研究基础上，分析了Calabi–Vesentini流形上扩展Souriau Gibbs分布对应热力学度量（thermo-metrics）的微分几何结构，揭示了热力学空间（thermo-space）在无约束时为平坦空间，而冻结磁场会使系统演化到具有特定曲率结构的弯曲子流形上，其无穷远边界呈现超立方体结构，并表现出与Lorentz时空因果结构及Penrose图相似的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期关于信息几何（information geometry）与几何热力学（geometrical thermodynamics）的对应关系，以及Calabi–Vesentini（CV）流形上扩展Souriau Gibbs分布配分函数精确计算的基础上，进一步研究相应热力学度量（thermo-metrics）的微分几何性质。

Method: 采用微分几何方法分析热力学空间的度量结构，研究磁场冻结条件下热力学系统的弯曲子流形演化，并通过Riemann曲率张量（Riemann tensor）的分量行为及对称矩阵编码来刻画空间结构，同时利用平坦嵌入将弯曲子流形追溯到$\mathbb{R}^{2n-1}$上的$\mathfrak{a}_{2n-1}$型单李代数Cartan矩阵（Cartan matrix）。

Result: 发现微观CV流形在偶数维和奇数维情形下存在细微差异；无约束时完整热力学空间为平坦空间；冻结磁场使系统演化到弯曲子流形上，其结构仅依赖于冻结的连续磁场链长度；弯曲子流形嵌入$\mathbb{R}^{2n-1}$的平坦度量等价于$\mathfrak{a}_{2n-1}$型Cartan矩阵，或可解释为广义平移超曲面（generalized translation hypersurface）；无穷远边界具有超立方体结构，其面心点和顶点是所有测地线（geodesics）的终点，仅依赖于起始角斜率。

Conclusion: 该弯曲子流形上Riemann张量的行为由具有特殊对称性的矩阵编码，可能在对称排列的子流形上产生曲率墙（curvature walls）并导致热力学空间的分区化（categorical partitioning）；无穷远边界的超立方体结构及其测地线终点性质与Lorentz时空的无穷远因果结构及Penrose图（Penrose diagrams）具有相似性。

Abstract: Following previous results recently obtained by us, on Information Geometry versus Geometrical Thermodynamics and on the exact calculation of partition functions for extended Souriau Gibbs distributions on Calabi Vesentini manifolds, we study the differential geometry of the corresponding thermo-metrics. A general intriguing scheme is discovered and put into evidence. A small yet significant difference, distinguishes the even from the odd dimensional instance of the microscopic CV manifolds. Apart from that the complete thermo-space is flat when no constraint is introduced. Freezing the magnetic fields, which can be done according to complicated combinatorials, forces the thermo-system to evolve on curved submanifolds of the thermo--space that have a structure depending only on the length of the $n-1$ chain of frozen contiguous magnetic fields. The behavior of Riemann tensor components for such spaces is codified by a symmetric matrix with peculiar behavior along special symmetrically arranged submanifolds that, might be responsible for the generation of curvature walls and for the categorical partitioning of the thermo space. The embedding of this curved submanifold into $\mathbb{R}^{2n-1}$ can be traced back to the vanishing of magnetic fields and, in this case, the flat metric on $\mathbb{R}^{2n-1}$ is the $\mathfrak{a}_{2n-1}$ simple Lie algebra Cartan matrix. In another version the flat embedding reveals the geometric interpretation of the $n$-manifold as a generalized translation hypersurface. The boundary at infinity has a hypercube structure whose face central points and vertices appear, numerically, to be the end-points of all geodesics depending only on their angular slope at the start. This general feature is reminiscent of the causal structure at infinity of Lorentzian space-times and of Penrose diagrams.

</details>


### [125] [Supersymmetric Moduli Space and Vacua with Vector Fields in $D=4$ Gauged $\mathcal{N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24477)
*Andrés Anabalón,Stefano Maurelli,Marcelo Oyarzo,Mario Trigiante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在M理论于S^7上紧化得到的纯膨胀STU模型中，研究了由AdS孤子型时空描述的费米子反周期边界条件下的超对称真空，系统刻画了这些正规超对称真空的模空间，并建立了它们与三维强耦合“禁闭”规范场论的全息对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当费米子在$AdS_4$中的一个类空$S^1$上取反周期边界条件时，超引力理论中由AdS孤子型时空描述的基态结构，并理解其全息对偶的物理含义。

Method: 在M理论于$S^7$上紧化并存在非平凡规范场（gauge fields）的背景下，聚焦于纯膨胀STU模型（purely dilatonic STU model），通过构造和分析AdS孤子型时空几何来研究超对称真空态。

Result: 得到了一族处处正规的超对称真空（supersymmetric vacua），并给出了这些解的超对称模空间（moduli space）的完整刻画，该模空间由截断中维度为一的算符的真空期望值（vacuum expectation values）参数化。

Conclusion: 所构造的几何体在全息对偶（holographic duality）框架下描述了三维强耦合“禁闭”规范场论（confining gauge theories），为研究低维禁闭系统的全息实现提供了新的理论平台。

Abstract: When fermions are taken to be anti-periodic along a spacelike $S^1$ in $AdS_4$, certain ground states of the supergravity theory are described by AdS-soliton-like spacetimes. We study these geometries within the purely dilatonic STU model obtained from the compactification of M-theory on $S^7$ in the presence of non-trivial gauge fields. The resulting configurations define a rich family of everywhere regular supersymmetric vacua that holographically describe strongly coupled ``confining'' gauge theories in three dimensions. We provide a complete characterization of the moduli space of supersymmetric solutions in terms of the vacuum expectation values of the dimension-one operators of the truncation.

</details>


### [126] [Spin-resolved double-trace thermal coefficients in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24499)
*Ilija Burić,Ivan Gusev,Andrei Parnachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本研究将全息热两点函数的构造从零空间分离推广到非零空间分离，利用零频率体波动方程（以Heun函数解析求解）固定KMS条件残留的歧义，从而高效计算按自旋分解的双迹算符热系数，并揭示了应力张量扇区类空分离处的复体锥奇异性被双迹贡献所消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，OPE中的应力张量扇区（stress-tensor sector）结合KMS条件（KMS condition）可确定零空间分离下的全息热两点函数（holographic thermal two-point functions）。本文旨在将该构造推广至非零空间分离情形，解决KMS条件在该情形下残留的依赖于空间分离的歧义性问题。

Method: 通过引入零频率体波动方程（zero-frequency bulk wave equation）来固定KMS条件残留的歧义项，并利用Heun函数（Heun functions）对方程进行解析求解。基于此方法，计算按自旋分解的双迹算符（double-trace operators）的热系数。

Result: 得到了一个高效计算按自旋分解的双迹算符热系数的方法。在Lorentzian解析结构分析中发现，应力张量扇区在类空分离处出现的复体锥奇异性（complex bulk-cone singularities）在全两点函数中并不持续存在，这些奇异性被双迹贡献（double-trace contribution）所消解。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将全息热两点函数的构造从零空间分离推广至非零空间分离，为计算双迹算符热系数提供了高效解析方法，并揭示了全息热两点函数中奇异性结构的消除机制。

Abstract: It was previously shown that the stress-tensor sector of the OPE, together with the KMS condition, fixes holographic thermal two-point functions at vanishing spatial separation. We extend this construction to nonzero spatial separation, where the KMS condition leaves a residual ambiguity that depends only on the spatial separation. We show that this ambiguity is fixed by the zero-frequency bulk wave equation, which can be solved analytically in terms of Heun functions. This gives an efficient method for computing thermal coefficients of double-trace operators resolved by spin. We also study the Lorentzian analytic structure of the resulting correlator and show that complex bulk-cone singularities which appear at spacelike separation in the stress-tensor sector do not persist in the full two-point function; they are resolved by the double-trace contribution.

</details>


### [127] [Near-Light-Cone Nonhydrodynamic Structure from Boosted Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24576)
*Navid Abbasi,Jewel Kumar Ghosh,Dirk H. Rischke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，在流体力学（hydrodynamics）的长波展开中，外部的洛伦兹助推（boost）可以改变限制展开收敛的非流体力学奇点的来源。在大助推下，主导收敛的奇点来自静止系谱（rest-frame spectrum）中大动量、近光锥（near-light-cone）区域；移除洛伦兹因子增长后，重标度后的收敛尺度由理论的微观谱结构控制。作者在弛豫时间动力学理论（relaxation-time kinetic theory）和全息对偶（holography）两个实例中明确验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学作为多体系统在长波长下的普适有效理论，其流体力学模式（hydrodynamic modes）的长波展开的收敛性由热平衡中守恒量推迟关联函数（retarded correlators）的解析结构决定。本文旨在揭示外部助推如何改变限制该展开的非流体力学奇点的来源，并阐明其与微观谱结构的关系。

Method: 通过解析分析热平衡中守恒量推迟关联函数的解析结构，研究洛伦兹助推对流体力学长波展开收敛半径的影响。在弛豫时间动力学理论和全息对偶两个具体模型中，显式计算并验证助推下奇点来源的转移以及重标度收敛尺度与微观谱结构之间的关联。

Result: 研究发现，外部助推可以改变限制流体力学长波展开的非流体力学奇点：在大助推下，主导奇点来源于静止系谱中大动量、近光锥区域的非流体力学激发。移除洛伦兹因子增长后，重标度后的收敛尺度不再依赖于助推，而是由理论的微观谱结构唯一控制。

Conclusion: 外部洛伦兹助推会改变流体力学长波展开的收敛性质，将主导奇点的来源从静止系谱的低能区域转移到高能、近光锥区域；重标度后的收敛尺度反映了理论的微观谱结构。这一发现在弛豫时间动力学理论和全息对偶中均得到验证，表明其具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: Hydrodynamics provides a universal description of many-body systems at long wavelengths. Its long-lived collective excitations, known as hydrodynamic modes, admit long-wavelength expansions whose convergence is encoded in the analytic structure of retarded correlators of conserved quantities in thermal equilibrium. We show that a boost can change which nonhydrodynamic singularity limits such an expansion. At large boost, the relevant singularity originates in the large-momentum, near-light-cone region of the rest-frame spectrum. After removing the Lorentz-factor growth, the rescaled convergence scale is controlled by the microscopic spectral structure of the theory. We explicitly demonstrate this in two examples: relaxation-time kinetic theory and holography.

</details>


### [128] [Krylov complexity and spectral density of BMN matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24632)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以BMN矩阵模型为背景，在质量隙（mass gap）较大的情况下，利用Krylov复杂性（Krylov complexity）作为量子多体动力学的诊断工具，计算了矩（moments）、返回振幅（return amplitudes）、谱函数（spectral function）与态密度（density of states）等多个物理量，分析了谱函数共振附近的红外发散（IR divergences），发现集体模式（collective modes）实际上是非传播的扩散模式或弛豫模式（diffusion modes or relaxation modes），并通过Krylov熵（Krylov entropy）的早期线性增长确认了该模型在高质量隙下的量子混沌（quantum chaos）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Krylov复杂性作为典型诊断工具，研究BMN矩阵模型在大质量隙（large mass gap）下的量子多体动力学行为，特别是为了解析集体模式的本质以及验证量子混沌的出现。

Method: 计算了大质量形变下的矩与返回振幅，涵盖态的展开复杂性（spread complexity）和算符的Krylov增长（Krylov growth of operators），提出了任意阶矩的一般表达式，讨论了对应的正交多项式；进一步计算了算符Krylov增长中的谱函数与态密度，诊断了共振附近的红外发散，并计算了Krylov方差（Krylov variance）与Krylov熵。

Result: 谱函数共振附近的红外发散揭示了集体模式实际上是非传播的扩散模式或弛豫模式，且具有无限弛豫时间；这些模式因大质量隙而异常稳定，而紫外区域则因高散射率而极度阻尼；Krylov熵在早期的线性增长确认了该模型在大质量隙下的量子混沌行为。

Conclusion: 在大质量隙条件下，BMN矩阵模型的集体模式本质上是非传播的扩散/弛豫模式，早期Krylov熵的线性增长为量子混沌的出现提供了有力证据。

Abstract: We use Krylov complexity as a typical diagnostic of quantum many body dynamics in the context of BMN matrix model at large mass gap. We calculate several physical entities, for example, the moments and return amplitudes at large mass deformation. We calculate them for both spread complexity of states as well as Krylov growth of operators in the matrix model. We propose a general expression for the moments at any given order $n$. We also discuss orthogonal polynomials for spread as well as operator Krylov complexity. In the context of the Krylov operator growth we further compute the spectral function and the density of states. A careful analysis of the spectral function near the resonance reveals various IR divergences in addition to the physical collective modes. These collective modes appear to be exceptionally stable due to large mass gap. On the other hand, the UV of the theory appears to be extremely damped due to higher scattering rates. We carefully diagnose these IR divergences near resonance which reveals that these collective modes are in fact non-propagating and should be thought of as diffusion modes or relaxation modes with infinite relaxation time. We also calculate the Krylov variance and Krylov entropy for the matrix model and in particular at early time. The linear early time growth of the Krylov entropy confirms the onset of quantum chaos in the matrix model at large mass gap.

</details>

### [129] [Effective Parameters, Real Behavior: Renormalization for Robotics -- From Infinite Electron Mass to Sim-to-Real Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24079)
*Youran Sun,Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重整化（renormalization）思想的跨仿真-现实差距（sim-to-real gap）方法：通过引入依赖于分辨率的有效参数（effective parameters）来吸收仿真器遗漏的细节，从而在仿真中复现真实行为。作者在比例-微分（PD）控制中解析证明了该机制，并通过绳索操控和水下游泳案例加以阐释，最后给出了选取可观测量、识别遗漏物理及确定有效参数的实用流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨仿真-现实差距（sim-to-real gap）是机器人学的核心问题，现有主流方法依赖构建越来越精确的仿真器。本文希望探索另一条路径：不追求仿真器的绝对精确，而是通过重整化（renormalization）思想引入有效参数来补偿仿真器遗漏的物理细节。

Method: 作者首先在有限仿真频率下的比例-微分（proportional-derivative, PD）控制中通过解析推导展示了重整化机制——比例反馈改变有效微分增益，微分反馈改变有效惯性。随后将同一视角扩展到动态绳索操控和水下游泳两个案例，并最终给出一个包含可观测量选取、遗漏物理识别以及有效参数确定的实用流程（practical procedure）。

Result: 解析结果表明，在PD控制中，比例反馈与微分反馈会分别改变有效微分增益和有效惯性，从而补偿有限仿真频率带来的误差。绳索操控与水下游泳的案例进一步说明该重整化视角具有跨领域适用性。

Conclusion: 重整化（renormalization）为机器人学提供了一条跨越仿真-现实差距的互补路径：使用有效参数（effective parameters）来复现真实行为，而非一味追求仿真器精度的提升。

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a central problem in robotics, and the prevailing approach is to build increasingly accurate simulators. Here, we propose another approach based on renormalization: using effective, resolution-dependent parameters to absorb details omitted by the simulator and reproduce real behavior. These parameters may differ from measured physical values because they compensate for what the simulator leaves out. We demonstrate this mechanism analytically for proportional--derivative (PD) control at finite simulation frequency, where proportional feedback changes the effective derivative gain and derivative feedback changes the effective inertia. We then interpret dynamic rope manipulation and underwater swimming through the same perspective. Finally, we present a practical procedure for choosing observables, identifying omitted physics, and determining effective parameters. Renormalization offers robotics a complementary path across the sim-to-real gap: effective parameters, real behavior.

</details>
