### [130] [DualityCert: Verifier-Gated Language-Model Repair of Broken Duality Claims in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23614)
*Xingyang Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个名为 DualityCert 的符号验证器，用于检验四维 N=1 夸克规范理论（quiver gauge theories）中的 Seiberg 对偶（Seiberg duality）声明的正确性，并将其用作语言模型智能体（language-model agents）的修复环境。实验表明，验证器门控重试（verifier-gated retry）与带类别级反馈（category-level verifier feedback）的策略能提升修复成功率，但不同模型间的最优策略存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Seiberg 对偶声称的自动验证与自动修复面临挑战，尤其是在理论物理中语言模型（language model）的输出可能包含非平凡错误。本文旨在开发一种轻量但严格的符号验证器，用于自动检测候选对偶声明中的不一致性，并探索如何利用该验证器作为修复引擎以提升语言模型的输出质量。

Method: 构建 DualityCert，一个符号验证器（symbolic verifier），用于评估四维 N=1 夸克规范理论候选 Seiberg 对偶声明的四个一致性条件：'t Hooft 反常匹配（'t Hooft anomaly matching）、超势 R-荷一致性（superpotential R-charge consistency）、中心荷匹配（central-charge matching）以及一个有界的弦环代理（bounded chiral-ring proxy）。将验证器集成到语言模型智能体的修复流程中：智能体接收一个有意的错误声明并逐步编辑，直到验证器通过。在预注册的 145 个错误声明基准上，在第一个确认性模型调用（confirmatory model call）之前即固定分析方案，比较了验证器门控重试（verifier-gated retry）、停止优先策略组合（stop-first strategy portfolio）与独立验证器过滤重采样（independent verifier-filtered resampling）等不同策略，并在 deepseek-chat 和 qwen-plus 两个模型上进行确认性测试。

Result: 验证器门控重试在 deepseek-chat 上提升修复成功率 +8.3 个百分点（pp），在 qwen-plus 上提升 +7.1 pp（Holm 校正后 p<0.002）。在 11 次尝试的同等预算下，停止优先策略组合在 deepseek-chat 上低于独立验证器过滤重采样 10.3 pp，但在 qwen-plus 上反而领先 14.7 pp，两种策略在不同模型上排名反转。在 qwen-plus 上，类别级验证器反馈比无内容重试提升 +8.7 pp，可解释的义务恒等式（interpretable obligation identities）比结构相同的遮蔽反馈提升 +6.4 pp；但在 deepseek-chat 上未检测到这两种效应。单独的 MiniMax-M2.5 扩展实验再次观察到迭代增益，且独立验证器过滤重采样优于策略组合。

Conclusion: 不同语言模型上的最优验证器利用策略不同，但所有获胜策略均使用相同的低成本证书（cheap certificate）。验证器、基准、实验协议及所有逐次尝试记录均已公开发布。

Abstract: We present DualityCert, a symbolic verifier for candidate Seiberg-duality claims in four-dimensional N=1 quiver gauge theories. The verifier evaluates 't Hooft anomaly matching, superpotential R-charge consistency, central-charge matching, and a bounded chiral-ring proxy. A claim that passes receives a consistency certificate, which states that no tested inconsistency was found, not that the duality is proven. We use the verifier as a repair environment for language-model agents, which receive a deliberately broken claim and must edit it until it certifies. On a preregistered benchmark of 145 broken claims, with the analysis fixed before the first confirmatory model call, verifier-gated retry improves final repair success over a single attempt by +8.3 percentage points (pp) on deepseek-chat and +7.1 pp on qwen-plus (Holm-adjusted p<0.002). Under an equal budget of eleven attempts, the stop-first strategy portfolio underperforms independent verifier-filtered resampling by 10.3 percentage points on deepseek-chat but outperforms it by 14.7 points on qwen-plus, reversing the ordering of the two tested verifier-exploitation policies across the two confirmatory models. On qwen-plus, category-level verifier feedback is worth +8.7 pp over content-free retry, and interpretable obligation identities alone are worth +6.4 pp over structurally identical masked feedback. Neither effect is detected on deepseek-chat. Separately, a preregistered MiniMax-M2.5 extension again finds an iteration gain and independent verifier-filtered resampling outperforming the strategy portfolio. Which policy is better thus differs between the two models, while every winning policy uses the same cheap certificate. The verifier, benchmark, protocol, and all per-attempt records are released.

</details>

### [131] [Investigating planar Proca metamaterials in nonlinear (2+1)-Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23013)
*Widervan de Deus Morais,Stamatios Strikos,Ronaldo Thibes,José Abdalla Helayël-Neto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含非线性效应、德布罗意-普罗卡（de Broglie-Proca）质量项和Chern-Simons质量项的平面电磁系统，揭示了由非线性产生的空间色散（spatial dispersion）轮廓，并将三维QED真空表征为非局域超材料（nonlocal metamaterial）。研究发现，本构关系（constitutive relations）与介电函数（dielectric function）均受Chern-Simons质量影响而产生空间色散；仅在同时存在电场和磁场时背景磁场才对该色散特性产生贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索包含非线性效应以及de Broglie-Proca和Chern-Simons质量项的平面电磁系统的物理特性，以描述可用于技术应用的新型平面超材料（planar metamaterials）。

Method: 通过推导本构关系和介电函数，分析由Chern-Simons质量项引起的空间色散；并基于施加的外部电磁场以及de Broglie-Proca和Chern-Simons质量参数来解析色散关系（dispersion relations）。

Result: 非线性效应产生了空间色散轮廓，使三维QED真空表现为非局域超材料；介电函数中出现的空间色散源自Chern-Simons质量项。在仅存在背景磁场的特殊情形下，该磁场对介电函数和空间色散轮廓均无贡献，只有当电场和磁场同时存在时才产生贡献。

Conclusion: 该平面电磁系统通过非线性效应与Chern-Simons质量项的结合，展现出可作为非局域超材料的行为，具有潜在技术应用价值；背景磁场的贡献依赖于电场与磁场的共存条件。

Abstract: The present work pursues the investigation of the physics of a planar electromagnetic system incorporating nonlinear effects, as well as de Broglie-Proca and Chern-Simons mass terms in vacuum, which can be tuned to describe new planar metamaterials (MTMs) potentially suitable for technological applications. As a result, we observe interesting effects generated by a spatially dispersive profile that emerges from non-linearity. This characterizes the three-dimensional QED vacuum as a nonlocal metamaterial. The constitutive relations are derived, and the dielectric function of the metamaterial (MTM) exhibits spatial dispersion due to the Chern-Simons mass. The dispersion relations are analyzed in terms of applied external electromagnetic fields and both the de Broglie-Proca and Chern-Simons mass parameters. We also notice that, in the special case in which only a background magnetic field is present, this field gives no contribution neither to the dielectric function nor to the spatial dispersion profile, as is the case when both the electric and magnetic fields are present.

</details>


### [132] [Architecture-agnostic analysis of partially coherent light with programmable photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24104)
*Kevin Zelaya,Matthew Markowitz,Jonathan Friedman,Mohammad-Ali Miri*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与架构无关的方法，利用肖尔-霍恩定理（Schur-Horn theorem）和可编程光子酉电路，仅通过输出功率测量即可直接提取辐射场的空间相干矩阵（coherence matrix）特征值，从而避免了传统干涉测量方案的相位敏感性和噪声问题。该方法在多种通用拓扑及欠参数化非通用架构中均得到数值验证，并对光学损耗和器件偏差具有内在鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场的空间相干度（spatial degree of coherence）的精确表征对于光学通信、先进成像和量子信息处理等应用至关重要。然而，传统的全相干矩阵测量依赖相位敏感的干涉装置，易受噪声干扰且难以在集成平台上扩展。

Method: 提出一种架构无关的方法，兼容任何通用可编程光子酉电路（universal programmable photonic unitary circuit）。基于肖尔-霍恩定理（Schur-Horn theorem），对输出相干矩阵进行对角化，从而仅通过输出功率测量即可直接提取其特征值。

Result: 在多种通用拓扑结构上进行了数值验证，并证明即使在欠参数化（under-parameterized）、非通用的架构中，该方法也仅带来微小的精度损失。此外，黑箱优化方法对任意光学损耗和器件偏差具有内在鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为开发鲁棒、低深度且可编程的空间相干分析器（spatial coherence analyzer）铺平了道路。

Abstract: The precise characterization of the spatial degree of coherence of a radiation field is important for assessing its suitability for specific applications in optical communications, advanced imaging, and quantum information processing. However, measuring the full coherence matrix traditionally requires complex, phase-sensitive interferometric setups that are highly susceptible to noise and difficult to scale on integrated platforms. To address this, we propose an architecture-agnostic approach for analyzing partially coherent light that is compatible with any universal programmable photonic unitary circuit, regardless of its internal topology. Leveraging the Schur-Horn theorem, our method diagonalizes the output coherence matrix, enabling direct extraction of its eigenvalues from output power measurements alone. We numerically validate this framework across various universal topologies and demonstrate its efficacy even in under-parameterized, non-universal architectures with only minor loss in precision. Finally, our black-box optimization approach proves inherently resilient to arbitrary optical losses and component deviations, paving the way for robust, lower-depth, and programmable spatial coherence analyzers.

</details>


### [133] [Combined Cavity Alignment and Mode-Mismatch Sensing using RF-QPD Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22907)
*Mitchell Schiworski,Stefan Ballmer*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用象限光电探测器（QPD）与柱面透镜、古伊相位望远镜（Gouy phase telescope）来同时感知光学腔模式失配（mode mismatch）和对准（alignment）的方案，无需定制传感器，可与PDH锁腔方案简单集成，并通过解析分析与光学仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腔模式失配与对准感知方法通常需要定制传感器，实现复杂；作者希望开发一种简洁、低成本且易于与现有锁腔方案（如Pound-Drever-Hall，PDH）集成的通用诊断技术。

Method: 方法基于柱面透镜和精心设计的古伊相位望远镜（Gouy phase telescope），利用三个象限光电探测器（Quadrant Photo-Detectors, QPDs）分别感知每个自由度，提升灵敏度并提供冗余；该方案无需射频（RF）调制，也可提供一般性诊断信息。

Result: 提供了该技术的解析概览以及古伊相位望远镜的通用设计方程；通过详细光学仿真验证了该技术方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该技术简单、易于实现，能为复杂光学实验提供模式失配与对准的通用诊断，且具备冗余和无需RF调制的优势。

Abstract: In this article we describe a scheme for sensing both the mode mismatch and alignment of a cavity which does not require custom sensors and uses only Quadrant Photo-Detectors (QPDs). The technique is simple relying upon cylindrical lenses and thoughtful Gouy phase telescope design, and is trivial to implement alongside a Pound-Drever-Hall (PDH) locking scheme. The Gouy phase telescope design utilizes all three of the QPDs to sense each degree of freedom which also increases sensitivity and provides redundancy. The technique we show also works without RF modulation, which is useful for providing other general diagnostic information in complex optical experiments. An analytical overview of the technique as well as generic design equations for the Gouy phase telescope is provided. Finally, a detailed optical simulation is presented to verify the technique.

</details>


### [134] [Dispersion models describing coupled systems in doped crystals. II. Infrared phonon excitations in GaAs single crystals and phonon-plasmon coupling in Zn-doped GaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23868)
*Daniel Franta,Beata Hroncova,Mihai-George Muresan,Stefan Zollner*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一个广义色散框架（generalized dispersion framework），通过同时拟合反射率、透射率和椭偏数据，对300–440 K温度范围内本征与锌掺杂砷化镓（GaAs）中耦合的声子与自由载流子激发进行了红外光谱分析，并成功描述了掺杂与温度对声子-等离子体激元耦合（phonon-plasmon coupling）的影响及重掺杂样品中TO声子共振的Fano型不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的红外光谱模型难以自洽地描述掺杂半导体中晶格振动（声子）与自由载流子（电子或空穴）之间的耦合效应，尤其在高掺杂和变温条件下，传统的处理方法往往无法同时兼顾反射、透射和椭偏数据的联合拟合。

Method: 采用广义色散框架，对单声子模使用高斯展宽分布（Gaussian-broadened distributions），对多声子吸收带使用三角-德尔塔-三角表示（triangle-delta-triangle representations），并在300–440 K温度范围内同时对反射率、透射率和椭偏数据进行拟合。

Result: 该框架自洽地描述了晶格与载流子对介电响应（dielectric response）的贡献，再现了声子-等离子体激元耦合随掺杂和温度演化的趋势。在重掺杂样品中观测到TO声子共振的显著Fano型不对称性（Fano-type asymmetry），并将其归因于声子激发与轻、重空穴Drude背景之间的干涉。

Conclusion: 所得光学常数在远红外至中红外波段保持物理自洽性，证明该框架适用于掺杂半导体中束缚与自由激发的耦合问题，为GaAs及相关光子材料的红外光学性质建模提供了定量工具。

Abstract: We present a temperature-dependent infrared study of intrinsic and Zn-doped GaAs within a generalized dispersion framework describing coupled phonon and free-carrier excitations. Reflectance, transmittance, and ellipsometric data measured from 300 to 440 K are fitted simultaneously using Gaussian-broadened distributions for single-phonon modes and triangle-delta-triangle representations for multiphonon absorption bands. The formalism provides a self-consistent description of lattice and carrier contributions to the dielectric response and reproduces the evolution of phonon-plasmon coupling with doping and temperature. In heavily doped samples, a pronounced Fano-type asymmetry of the TO-phonon resonance is observed and attributed to interference between phonon excitations and the Drude background of light and heavy holes. The resulting optical constants remain physically consistent across the far- and mid-infrared range and demonstrate the applicability of the framework to coupled bound and free excitations in doped semiconductors. The approach provides a quantitative tool for modeling infrared optical properties of GaAs and related photonic materials.

</details>


### [135] [Non-Centrosymmetric $γ$-Phase GaS Nanobelts for On-Chip Nonlinear Photonic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23939)
*Yukihiro Endo,Masato Takiguchi,Yoshiaki Sekine,Hisashi Sumikura,Yoshitaka Taniyasu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过自催化气-液-固生长合成的GaS纳米带，发现其主要结晶为非中心对称的γ相堆叠，消除了传统β相的中心对称性限制，展现出与GaSe相当的二次谐波强度，并成功集成到SiN波导上实现了高效的片上倍频与和频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华（van der Waals, vdW）半导体中的二阶非线性光学过程为实现紧凑、可集成的光子转换平台提供了可能。GaS因宽带隙可抑制近红外激光激发下的双光子吸收而具有吸引力，但块体GaS通常结晶为中心对称的β相，这消除了其二阶非线性，严重限制了其在非线性光子学中的应用。

Method: 通过自催化气-液-固（self-catalyzed vapor-liquid-solid）生长法合成GaS纳米带；利用边缘选择性生长动力学（edge-selective growth kinetics）在Ga催化剂界面稳定非中心对称的γ相堆叠；将纳米带集成到SiN波导上实现片上倍频与和频生成。

Result: GaS纳米带主要结晶为非中心对称的γ相，表现出强烈的二次谐波生成（second-harmonic generation, SHG），强度与广泛使用的非线性光学材料GaSe相当。集成到SiN波导后，实现了高效的片上倍频（SHG）与和频生成（sum-frequency generation）。

Conclusion: γ-GaS纳米带作为一种可转移的一维非线性材料，非常适合片上光子集成，并在非线性光学应用中展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Second-order nonlinear optical processes in van der Waals (vdW) semiconductors offer a compelling route toward compact, integrable photon-conversion platforms. Among III-VI vdW semiconductors, GaS is particularly attractive owing to its wide bandgap suppressing two-photon absorption under near-infrared laser excitation. However, bulk GaS typically crystallizes in the centrosymmetric $β$ phase, which eliminates second-order nonlinearity and severely limits its application in nonlinear photonics. Here, we demonstrate that GaS nanobelts synthesized via self-catalyzed vapor-liquid-solid growth predominantly crystallize in non-centrosymmetric $γ$-phase stacking. This behavior originates from edge-selective growth kinetics at the Ga catalyst interface, which stabilizes the $γ$ phase and enables deterministic in-plane dipole moment alignment. The GaS nanobelts exhibit strong second-harmonic generation (SHG) with intensities comparable to those of GaSe, a widely used nonlinear optical material. Furthermore, we integrate the nanobelts onto SiN waveguides and demonstrate efficient on-chip SHG and sum-frequency generation. These results establish $γ$-GaS nanobelts as a transferable one-dimensional nonlinear materials well suited for on-chip photonic integration and indicate their strong potential for nonlinear optical applications.

</details>


### [136] [Quantitative infrared nanoscopy: Probe-cavity eigenmodes and nano-gap polaritons for strongly coupled nanoscale optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23950)
*Benjamin Allen,Brayden Lukaskawcez,Alyssa Bragg,Nitzan Hirshberg,Leo Fan Bowen Lo,Michael Fogler,Dimitri N. Basov,Stephanie Gilbert-Corder,Hans Bechtel,Alexander S. McLeod*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出“EigenProbe”形式化框架，将纳米光学探针与样品之间的近场相互作用描述为纳米间隙极化激元（nano-gap polaritons）的本征模式，从而精准预测非微扰条件下的近场显微镜和光谱学实验，并实现局域光学常数的可靠反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解析模型难以定量描述探针尖端与介电环境之间的强光学耦合条件，限制了纳米光学显微镜和光谱学的灵敏度与场增强效应。

Method: 建立探针-腔本征模（probe-cavity eigenmodes）的形式化理论，将探针与环境的相互近场作用归结为复合系统的本征激发——纳米间隙极化激元（nano-gap polaritons）；结合定量校准与半解析腔本征模散射描述，实现对非微扰相互作用的有效建模。

Result: 通过极声子（polar phonons）与分子振动（molecular vibrations）的受控纳米光学实验验证，证明纳米间隙极化激元可以被实际实现并用于局域光学常数的可靠、快速反演。

Conclusion: EigenProbe 形式化为将多样化的光学纳米显微镜技术发展为纳米尺度光学环境的高精度计量学奠定了基础，并指导纳米间隙腔在量子材料局域激发操控与光子发射器强耦合中的未来应用。

Abstract: Optical nanoscopies including near-field optical microscopy and spectroscopy circumvent the diffraction limit of conventional optics thanks to the nanoscale light focus emerging at the apex of a sharp irradiated probe. However, while strong optical coupling between the apex and its dielectric environment affords both enhanced nanoscopic measurement sensitivity and potentially ultra-strong fields within a nano-gap cavity, the conditions for this coupling remain poorly quantified by prevailing analytic models. Here we present a robust formalism of probe-cavity eigenmodes that fully describes how mutual near-field interactions between probe and environment produce a composite response to external fields qualitatively distinct from that of its distinct components. This "EigenProbe" model identifies the fundamental excitations of realistic optical nanoscopies as nano-gap polaritons, which provide an elegant basis to accurately predict near-field microscopy and spectroscopy experiments especially when probe-sample interactions are non-perturbative. Through comparison to carefully controlled nanoscopies of polar phonons and molecular vibrations alike, we show how nano-gap polaritons are both realized and utilized for reliable and rapid "inversion" of local optical constants. This advance demands both a careful understanding of the probe response through quantitative calibration, and our efficient semi-analytic description of cavity eigenmode scattering at the probe apex. Our EigenProbe formalism sets the stage for maturing diverse and proliferating optical nanoscopies into precision metrologies of nano-scale optical environments, and guides future use of nano-gap cavities to manipulate local excitations of quantum materials and to achieve strong coupling over photonic emitters.

</details>


### [137] [Lead nanoparticles, the deep-ultraviolet to near-infrared plasmonic platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24340)
*Michael Foltýn,Michal Kvapil,Kristýna Bukvišová,Tomáš Šikola,Michal Horák*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过实验首次研究了化学合成的球形铅（lead, Pb）纳米颗粒的等离激元性能，发现其支持的局域表面等离激元共振（localized surface plasmon resonances）覆盖从近红外到深紫外（低于200纳米）的整个光谱范围，是等离激元元素金属中范围最广的，因此有望作为一种多光谱等离激元平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铅作为一种非贵金属，在深紫外（deep ultraviolet）光谱区域的等离激元应用方面具有特殊潜力，但此前尚未有实验研究对其等离激元性能进行探讨。

Method: 通过化学合成制备球形铅纳米颗粒，并利用扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy）与电子能量损失谱（electron energy loss spectroscopy）相结合的方法，在单颗粒水平上评估其等离激元性能。

Result: 球形铅纳米颗粒的光学性质表现出直径依赖性；它们支持的局域表面等离激元共振（localized surface plasmon resonances）覆盖从近红外到深紫外的整个光谱范围，延伸至波长低于200纳米，该范围在所有等离激元元素金属中最为广泛。

Conclusion: 铅纳米颗粒在极宽波长范围内表现出稳定的等离激元性能，证实了其作为多光谱等离激元平台（multispectral plasmonic platform）的潜力。

Abstract: Among the other non-noble metals, lead (Pb) is a material of particular interest for plasmonic applications in the deep ultraviolet spectral region. However, experimental studies on its plasmonic performance have not yet been conducted. In this work, the dependence of the optical properties of spherical lead nanoparticles on their diameter is demonstrated. The plasmonic performance of chemically synthesized lead nanoparticles is evaluated at the single-particle level by means of a combination of scanning transmission electron microscopy and electron energy loss spectroscopy. Our findings demonstrate that these nanoparticles support localized surface plasmon resonances across the entire spectrum from near-infrared to deep-ultraviolet. This range was identified as the most extensive among all plasmonic elemental metals, extending to wavelengths below 200 nanometers. Consequently, lead nanoparticles exhibit stable plasmonic performance over a remarkably broad wavelength range, thereby substantiating their potential as a multispectral plasmonic platform.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

### [138] [Fourth-order closure obstruction and chiral nonlocality in circular kinetic magnetotransport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22730)
*P. Shubham Parashar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文指出在应力层级截断角动量层级会缺失高阶费米面谐波的反向作用，推导出缺失的四阶项（第四阶闭合障碍）并分析了其梯度展开条件、磁场效应及与完整层级的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维圆形费米面上，角动量层级在应力层级截断会忽略来自高阶费米面谐波（higher Fermi-surface harmonics）的确定反向作用（back-action），需定量描述这一缺失项及其物理后果。

Method: 通过分析圆形二维费米面上流变（streaming）过程角动量逐次变化的完整序列（1→2→3→2→1），推导出电流本征值的四阶修正项Λ(q)=γ₁+νq²-κ₄q⁴+⋯，其中包含系数ν=v_F²/(4γ₂)和κ₄=ν²/γ₃，并将此缺失项定义为第四阶闭合障碍（fourth-order closure obstruction）。

Result: 该四阶项的梯度展开在νq²/γ₃≪1时受控。圆形对称性将相同系数引入每个守恒角动量块内的径向双拉普拉斯算子（radial bi-Laplacian）；精确保留m=3谐波（不作梯度展开）会单调放大高阶径向模式。在零场下正碰撞率排除实波数极点和响应零点；等速率尾端给出平方根补全。磁场使系数变为手性（chiral），产生霍尔符号反转（Hall sign reversal），并在m=3谐波长寿命下增强该系数。在无碰撞高场极限下，完整层级成为贝塞尔极零点阶梯（Bessel pole–zero ladder），而有限截断层级构成其有理逼近。

Conclusion: 结果将受控的低梯度系数与其依赖于几何和波数的高阶补全分离开来，为理解角动量层级截断的适用范围和修正提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Closing an angular moment hierarchy at the stress level omits a definite back-action from higher Fermi-surface harmonics. For a circular two-dimensional Fermi surface, streaming changes angular momentum by one, so the shortest omitted sequence, $1\!\to\!2\!\to\!3\!\to\!2\!\to\!1$, adds a fourth-order term to the current eigenvalue, $Λ(q)=γ_1+νq^2-κ_4q^4+\cdots$, with $ν=v_F^2/(4γ_2)$ and $κ_4=ν^2/γ_3$. We call this missing operator term the fourth-order closure obstruction. Its gradient expansion is controlled when $νq^2/γ_3\ll1$. Circular symmetry carries the same coefficient into a radial bi-Laplacian within each conserved angular-momentum block, and retaining $m=3$ exactly, without a gradient expansion, amplifies higher radial modes monotonically. At zero field, positive collision rates exclude real-wave-number poles and response zeros; an equal-rate tail gives a square-root completion. A magnetic field makes the coefficient chiral, produces a Hall sign reversal, and enhances it when the $m=3$ harmonic is long lived. In the collisionless high-field limit, the complete hierarchy becomes a Bessel pole--zero ladder, while finite closures form rational approximants to it. The result separates a controlled low-gradient coefficient from its geometry- and field-dependent finite-wave-number completion.

</details>


### [139] [Theory of Andreev reflection spectroscopy with anisotropic spin-dependent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22747)
*Zhiyue Li,Guoping Zhang,Tingyong Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文针对传统Andreev反射理论忽略界面散射各向异性的缺陷，提出一个包含自旋依赖各向异性散射的广义模型，拓展了BTK与CTC理论框架，能够统一描述从正常金属到半金属的输运行为。模型揭示了界面各向异性可通过透射自旋滤波产生自旋极化电流，并非线性调控极化率与Andreev反射强度。在Co薄膜实验中得到验证，为利用非磁性平台构建抗干扰自旋源提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学（spintronics）需要高效产生与控制自旋极化电流，但传统铁磁方法易受外磁场干扰。Andreev反射谱是测量自旋极化率与超导能隙的关键手段，但现有理论假设各向同性界面散射，忽略了异质结中普遍存在的各向异性，导致材料性质的误判。因此需要建立一个考虑自旋依赖各向异性散射的广义模型来修正现有理论。

Method: 引入自旋向上与自旋向下电子各自独立的界面势垒，将Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk（BTK）形式论及其Chen-Tesanovic-Chien（CTC）扩展推广至各向异性情形。通过修改边界条件求解Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）方程，推导出包含透射几率判断函数（transmission probability judgment function）的电流公式，该函数用于识别主导自旋通道。

Result: 在非磁性金属中，界面各向异性可通过透射自旋滤波（transmission spin filtering）产生可观的自旋极化电流，同时抑制Andreev反射并降低子隙电导。对于正极化率的铁磁体，各向异性对极化率进行非线性调控，先增强Andreev反射至一个阈值而后抑制；负极化率材料则呈现逆反谱，通过电导比较可明确判定极化率符号。外延Co薄膜测量验证了模型的有效性，解析出微弱的各向异性。

Conclusion: 该模型精炼了Andreev谱的解读方法，支持利用非磁性平台构建抗干扰自旋源，对磁阻器件（magnetoresistive devices）和超导量子技术具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Spintronic technologies require efficient generation and control of spin-polarized currents. Conventional ferromagnet-based methods suffer from sensitivity to external magnetic fields. Andreev reflection spectroscopy is vital for measuring spin polarization and superconducting gaps, yet prevailing theories assume isotropic interface scattering. This neglects ubiquitous anisotropy in heterostructures, causing misinterpretation of material properties. To resolve this, we develop a generalized model incorporating spin-dependent anisotropic scattering. Introducing distinct interface barriers for spin-up and spin-down electrons extends both the Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk formalism and the Chen-Tesanovic-Chien extension. This unified framework describes transport from normal metals to half-metals. Solving the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations with modified boundary conditions yields current formulae with a transmission probability judgment function identifying dominant spin channels. In non-magnetic metals, interfacial anisotropy generates sizable spin-polarized currents via transmission spin filtering, suppressing Andreev reflection and reducing sub-gap conductance. For positively polarized ferromagnets, anisotropy nonlinearly modulates polarization, enhancing Andreev reflection to a threshold before suppression. Negatively polarized materials exhibit inverse spectra, enabling unambiguous polarization sign determination via conductance comparisons. Epitaxial Co film measurements validate the model, resolving subtle anisotropies. This refines Andreev spectrum interpretation and supports interference-resistant spin sources using non-magnetic platforms, benefiting magnetoresistive devices and superconducting quantum technologies.

</details>


### [140] [Radiative Spin Caloritronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22845)
*Philippe Ben-Abdallah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测了非互易磁光多体系统中的自旋热霍尔效应（spin thermal Hall effect），其中纵向辐射热流可产生横向光子自旋角动量累积；该效应与逆自旋热霍尔效应构成Onsager-Casimir互易对，从而在非互易光子系统中将热流与光子自旋耦合为输运通道。热力学第二定律对自旋-热耦合施加了基本界限，导出了量化辐射自旋-热转换效率的热-自旋品质因子（thermal-spin figure of merit）。该工作为光子自旋热电子学（photon spin caloritronics）建立了完整的热力学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未系统揭示非互易（nonreciprocal）磁光多体体系中热辐射与光子自旋角动量之间的耦合输运机制，缺乏描述该体系中自旋热霍尔效应的完整热力学理论框架。

Method: 通过理论分析建立非互易磁光多体系统中纵向辐射热流驱动横向光子自旋角动量累积的自旋热霍尔效应模型；基于Onsager-Casimir互易关系推导热流与光子自旋的耦合输运方程，并应用热力学第二定律限定自旋-热耦合强度的理论上限。

Result: 发现并预测了非互易磁光多体系统中的自旋热霍尔效应及其逆效应，二者构成Onsager-Casimir互易对；导出了热力学第二定律所施加的自旋-热耦合基本界限以及热-自旋品质因子。

Conclusion: 该研究为光子自旋热电子学建立了完整的热力学框架，为自旋调控热辐射（spin-controlled thermal radiation）和非互易光子热器件（nonreciprocal photonic thermal devices）奠定了概念基础。

Abstract: We predict the spin thermal Hall effect in nonreciprocal magneto-optical many-body systems, in which a longitudinal radiative heat current generates a transverse accumulation of the spin angular momentum carried by thermal photons. We show that this effect and the inverse spin thermal Hall effect constitute an Onsager-Casimir reciprocal pair, thereby establishing heat and photon spin as coupled transport channels in nonreciprocal photonic systems. The second law of thermodynamics imposes fundamental bounds on the spin-heat coupling, leading to a thermal-spin figure of merit that quantifies the efficiency of radiative spin-heat conversion. Our results establish a complete thermodynamic framework for photon spin caloritronics and lay the conceptual foundations for spin-controlled thermal radiation and nonreciprocal photonic thermal devices.

</details>


### [141] [Spectral Topology and Non-Bloch Band Theory for Domain-Wall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22976)
*Mingtao Xu,Rui Wang,Tian-Shu Deng,Wei Yi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了环形排列的畴壁（domain-wall）构型下一维非厄米模型（non-Hermitian models）的谱拓扑（spectral topology），发现本征态在界面处的局域化源于相邻畴之间谱绕数（spectral winding numbers）的差异，并利用Ronkin函数形式将广义布里渊区（generalized Brillouin zone, GBZ）推广至畴壁体系。除了传统的趋肤模（skin modes），还识别出一种需遍历所有畴的新型行波式趋肤模，二者在空间分布和GBZ条件上均存在本质区别，且行波式模携带有限通量谱绕数（flux spectral winding number），表现出边界敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注开边界条件下非厄米体系的趋肤效应，但对于畴壁构型（不同域环状排列）下的谱拓扑行为尚缺乏系统理解，尤其是不问畴之间界面局域化的拓扑起源、以及广义布里渊区在该构型下的适用性问题有待澄清。

Method: 将Ronkin函数形式（Ronkin-function formalism）拓展至畴壁构型，以导出复动量空间中的广义布里渊区（GBZ）条件；通过分析相邻畴之间关于对应本征能量的谱绕数之差，解释本征态界面局域化的拓扑起源。

Result: 发现两类趋肤模：传统型对应各畴在开边界条件下的驻波（standing waves），以及一种涉及全部畴的新型行波式趋肤模（traveling-wave-like skin modes）。这两类模服从不同的GBZ条件，行波式模还携带有限通量谱绕数，体现出边界敏感性。

Conclusion: 畴壁构型下非厄米体系的两类趋肤模在空间轮廓与GBZ条件上均可区分，行波式趋肤模的有限通量谱绕数揭示了其与边界的强耦合特性，为理解非厄米谱拓扑中的局域化行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the spectral topology of one-dimensional non-Hermitian models in a domain-wall configuration, where different domains are arranged in a ring geometry. While eigenstates can localize near an interface under the non-Hermitian skin effect, we show that the localization of an eigenstate originates from the difference in the spectral winding numbers, with respect to the corresponding eigenenergy, between the two adjacent domains. We then obtain the conditions for the generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) in the complex momentum space, by extending the Ronkin-function formalism to the domain-wall configuration. In addition to the conventional skin modes that correspond to standing waves on individual domains under the open boundary condition, a unique type of traveling-wave-like skin modes emerges, whose construction involves all domains. Besides their difference in the spatial profiles, these two types of modes obey distinct GBZ conditions, making them differentiable on the GBZ. Interestingly, the traveling-wave-like modes further carry a finite flux spectral winding number, indicating their boundary sensitivity.

</details>


### [142] [Janus-induced atomic reconstruction amplifies twist-angle modulation of interlayer thermal transport in moiré bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23103)
*Bin Xu,Rulei Guo,Jie Sun,Hiroo Suzuki,Takuma Yoshida,Ichiro Nakaya,Koki Sawasaki,Yasuhiko Hayashi,Shohei Chiashi,Tomoki Machida,Junichiro Shiomi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维摩尔双层（moiré bilayers）中，通过引入Janus诱导的镜面对称破缺（mirror-symmetry breaking）来调控原子重构（atomic reconstruction），从而显著改变层间热导对扭转角（twist angle）的依赖性，在MoSSe/MoS2双层中观察到异常强的热导扭转角依赖性和极小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维摩尔双层中的原子重构能够显著改变局部堆叠和层间耦合，为调控声子介导（phonon-mediated）性质提供了可能性。本文旨在探索如何通过引入Janus诱导的镜面对称破缺来修饰层间热导的扭转角依赖性。

Method: 在MoSSe/MoS2双层中引入Janus结构，利用其本征面外偶极矩（out-of-plane dipole）产生无摩擦界面（frictionless interface），降低晶格形变能，从而促进原子重构形成局部扭曲的非周期摩尔图案（aperiodic moiré patterns）。

Result: MoSSe/MoS2双层展现出异常强的热导扭转角依赖性，在小扭转角处出现显著的最小值，其降低速率（reduction rate）比扭转双层MoS2大近一个数量级。这些现象源于弱化的层间耦合和抑制的界面声子传输。

Conclusion: 层间热输运（interlayer thermal transport）受到原子重构的修饰，Janus诱导的镜面对称破缺是促进二维摩尔结构中声子工程（phonon engineering）的有效途径。

Abstract: In two-dimensional moiré bilayers, atomic reconstruction, the spontaneous structural relaxation toward energy-minimizing stacking registries in the near-commensurate regime, can strongly modify local stacking and interlayer coupling, providing a possibility to significantly control phonon-mediated properties. Here we show that the twist-angle dependence of interlayer thermal conductance can be modified by introducing Janus-induced mirror-symmetry breaking into bilayer MoS2. The intrinsic out-of-plane dipole in MoSSe/MoS2 bilayers leads to frictionless interface, and reduces the lattice deformation energy, thereby promoting atomic-reconstruction into locally distorted aperiodic moiré patterns. These features weaken interlayer coupling and suppress phonon transmission across the interface, leading to an anomalously strong twist-angle dependence of thermal conductance, with a pronounced minimum at small twist angles and a reduction rate nearly one order of magnitude larger than that of twisted bilayer MoS2. Our results demonstrate that interlayer thermal transport is modified by atomic reconstruction, highlighting Janus-induced mirror-symmetry breaking as an effective way to promoting phonon engineering in two-dimensional moiré structures.

</details>


### [143] [Electrical control of the metal-insulator transition in a one dimensional device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23138)
*J. Craquelin,L. Jarjat,B. Hue,A. Théry,C. Fruy,N. Struchkov,D. Stefani,M. M. Desjardins,A. Cottet,M. R. Delbecq,T. Kontos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过空间调制悬浮碳纳米管的局域势，利用与凝聚态系统的类比，实现了一个大且均匀的能量间隙，该间隙可在约两个数量级范围内连续调控，从而使电子系统在低温下从绝缘态转变到近金属态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳米尺度上利用外部物理参数控制低能谱是量子器件的关键资源，其中能量间隙（energy gap）的出现与退相干（decoherence）的抑制密切相关，而退相干的缓解对于量子信息处理至关重要。实现在有限参数空间区域内持续有效的退相干对抗措施，尤其是考虑到近期自顶向下构建超导拓扑链（superconducting topological chains）的努力，成为本文的研究动机。

Method: 本文采用空间调制悬浮碳纳米管（suspended carbon nanotube）局域势的方法，并利用与凝聚态系统（condensed matter systems）的物理类比，来实现对电子系统能隙的调控。

Result: 实验展示了一个大的能量间隙，该间隙在碳纳米管上具有均匀性，并且可在约两个数量级的范围内进行连续调节，从而使电子系统在低温下从绝缘态（insulating state）转变为近金属态（near-metallic state）。

Conclusion: 利用空间调制局域势的方法可以有效地在悬浮碳纳米管中实现可大范围调谐的能量间隙，这一成果为在有限参数空间内持续抑制退相干、推动超导拓扑链等量子器件的实际构建提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Controlling the low energy spectrum at the nanoscale with an external physical parameter has become an important resource for quantum devices. The emergence of an energy gap is one such key feature, linked to the mitigation of decoherence needed for quantum information processing. Indeed, the detrimental effects of high-energy uncontrolled excitations can only be cured at some specific tuning points in general. Achieving an energy gap is a natural way to extend decoherence countermeasures over a finite region of parameter space. This would be particularly useful in view of the recent efforts to build superconducting topological chains in a top-down approach. In this work, we demonstrate a large energy gap by spatially modulating the local potential of a suspended carbon nanotube, exploiting an analogy with condensed matter systems. This gap is homogeneous on the nanotube and tunable by about two orders of magnitude, bringing the electronic system from an insulating state to a near-metallic state at low temperatures.

</details>


### [144] [Signatures of Topological Magnon Edge States in THz Spectroscopy and Cavity Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23170)
*Ipsika Mohanty,Johannes Knolle,Silvia Viola Kusminskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全光学路径，通过太赫兹泵浦-探测光谱技术，利用参量放大和磁电耦合机制，检测铁磁拓扑磁振子绝缘体中的手性边缘模，从而突破传统探针对拓扑边缘态灵敏度不足的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁振子绝缘体（topological magnon insulators, TMIs）因其非平庸的体态磁振子拓扑结构和无耗散输运的手性边缘模（chiral edge modes），成为低能耗自旋信息处理的有前景平台。然而，理论模型虽预测了这些边缘态，传统实验探针对其灵敏度有限，直接实验检测仍面临挑战。

Method: 该方法基于磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling）机制，通过共振电磁驱动实现边缘磁振子的参量放大（parametric amplification）。研究聚焦于具有六角蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）的二维范德华铁磁材料，其磁振子带隙位于太赫兹（terahertz, THz）范围。源自动态电荷涨落并与最近邻及次近邻相互作用的晶格对称性一致的自旋依赖有效电偶极矩（spin-dependent effective electric dipole moment），可诱导单光子-双磁振子过程（one-photon-two-magnon processes），进而产生参量放大。基于此，提出了一种太赫兹泵浦-探测光谱协议（THz pump-probe spectroscopy protocol），选择性放大并吸收检测边缘模，并讨论了利用太赫兹腔体选择性耦合边缘模并滤除体态贡献的可能性。

Result: 证明了自旋依赖有效电偶极矩能够驱动单光子-双磁振子过程，实现边缘磁振子的参量放大。所提出的太赫兹泵浦-探测光谱协议能够选择性放大并检测边缘模，且太赫兹腔体可进一步增强对边缘模的选择性耦合，同时抑制体态信号的干扰。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条探测拓扑磁体的实验路径，为磁性量子材料中奇异拓扑现象的实验探索开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Topological magnon insulators (TMIs) have emerged as promising platforms for low-energy spin-based information processing, due to their non-trivial bulk magnon topology and robust, chiral edge modes that support dissipationless transport. Although theoretical models predict these edge states, direct experimental detection remains challenging due to their limited sensitivity to conventional probes. In this work, we propose an all-optical pathway to detect topological magnon edge modes in ferromagnetic TMIs. Our approach harnesses parametric amplification of edge magnons via resonant electromagnetic driving, enabled by magnetoelectric coupling mechanisms. We concentrate on two-dimensional van der Waals ferromagnetic materials on the honeycomb lattice with magnonic band gaps in the terahertz (THz) range. We show that a spin-dependent effective electric dipole moment, arising from dynamic charge fluctuations and consistent with the lattice symmetry up to next-nearest-neighbor interactions, gives rise to one-photon-two-magnon processes leading to parametric amplification. On this basis, we propose a THz pump-probe spectroscopy protocol in which edge modes are selectively amplified and subsequently detected in absorption. Furthermore, we discuss the possibility of using THz cavities, enabling selective coupling to edge modes while filtering out bulk contributions. These findings establish a route for probing topological magnets and open new avenues for experimental exploration of exotic topological phenomena in magnetic quantum materials.

</details>


### [145] [Current- and field-driven domain wall dynamics and chirality switching in a planar helimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23252)
*Roman Teslia,Oleksiy Kolezhuk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强易平面各向异性的螺旋磁体（helimagnet）中，电流和磁场对手性动力学（chirality dynamics）的影响。基于准一维受挫铁磁体在Lifshitz点附近的连续统理论，证明了畴壁（domain wall）可通过自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque）的耗散（非绝热）分量由电流驱动；同时，磁场中的绝热部分会破缺手性相关的有效磁势能对称性，并推导了手性翻转条件，数值模拟支持了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并调控螺旋磁体中手性（chirality）的动力学行为，特别是利用电流和磁场实现对手性的可控驱动与翻转。

Method: 基于准一维受挫铁磁体（frustrated ferromagnet）在Lifshitz点附近导出的连续统理论（continuum theory），分析自旋转移矩的耗散（非绝热）分量与绝热分量在电流和磁场下的作用，并结合数值自旋-晶格模拟（spin-lattice simulations）进行验证。

Result: 1) 通过自旋转移矩的耗散（非绝热）分量，电流可以驱动连接相反手性畴的畴壁；2) 磁场下的绝热自旋转移矩破缺了有效磁势能关于手性的对称性；3) 主导的对称性破缺项出现在磁化梯度的三阶，并据此推导出手性翻转的条件。

Conclusion: 电流和磁场可有效调控强易平面各向异性螺旋磁体中的手性动力学与翻转，理论分析结果得到了数值模拟的支持。

Abstract: We study the effect of electric current and magnetic field on the dynamics of chirality in a helimagnet with a strong easy-plane anisotropy. Using a continuum theory, derived for a quasi-one-dimensional frustrated ferromagnet close to the Lifshitz point, we show that a domain wall connecting domains with opposite chiralities can be driven by the current via the dissipative (non-adiabatic) component of the spin-transfer torque. Further, it is demonstrated that the adiabatic part of the torque in the presence of a magnetic field breaks the symmetry of the effective magnetic potential energy with respect to the chirality. We show that the leading symmetry-breaking term arises in the third order in the magnetization gradient, and derive the conditions of the chirality switching. Our conclusions are supported by numerical spin-lattice simulations.

</details>


### [146] [Current cross-correlations as probes for poor man's Majorana states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23498)
*Saatwik Patnaik,Aditya Saran,Himadri S Dhar,Pertti Hakonen,Thierry Martin,Bhaskaran Muralidharan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用电流-电流互关联（current-current cross-correlations）作为超越微分电导谱（differential conductance spectroscopy）的测量框架，以验证三量子点最小Kitaev链中贫人马拉约纳模（poor man's Majorana modes, PMM）的真正非局域性（non-locality），并指出该特征在短链极限下仍具稳定性，可作为诊断真正PMM的可靠手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖微分电导谱来表征PMM模，但该方法不足以验证其真正的非局域性。本文旨在引入电流互关联测量框架，为确认纠缠态及拓扑保护态的真实非局域性提供更可靠的诊断工具。

Method: 基于三量子点最小Kitaev链模型，通过理论分析电流互关联（current cross-correlations）的符号和幅度特征，研究PMM模在偏离甜点（sweet spot）时的退定域化（delocalization）行为，并与微分电导谱方法进行对比。

Result: 电流互关联在PMM甜点附近表现出显著稳定性，尤其对外点失谐（detuning of an outer dot）不敏感。这种稳定性源于非局域输运过程（nonlocal transport processes）相对幅度的内在结构，可作为短链极限下真正PMM的突出特征和诊断标志。

Conclusion: 电流互关联测量应作为验证真正非局域性及拓扑保护态（topologically protected states）的不可或缺的诊断框架，为实验上明确确认PMM的存在提供了超越传统电导谱的新途径。

Abstract: The minimal Kitaev chain that emulates a topological superconductor with three quantum dots offers a tunable platform for potentially hosting poor man's Majorana (PMM) modes. Asserting the need to go beyond differential conductance spectroscopy, we introduce current-current correlations as a viable framework for verifying their true non-locality. The robustness of the PMM modes, specifically with respect to delocalization as the system is tuned away from sweet spots, we show, is embedded in the relative magnitudes of the nonlocal transport processes. This aspect is adeptly captured by current cross-correlations, whose features show remarkable stability around the PMM sweet spot, specifically with respect to the detuning of an outer dot. We establish this as a prominent feature and a diagnostic for true PMMs even in the short chain limit. Our results accentuate the need for current cross-correlation measurements as a diagnostic framework for unambiguously verifying true non-locality of entangled states as well as topologically protected states.

</details>


### [147] [Spin-Hall devices: spin relaxation spatially separates current injection from Joule dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23589)
*Yanbo Qiao,Sariah Al Saati,Jean-Eric Wegrowe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于最小耗散原理的变分方法，研究连接外部负载电路的自旋霍尔棒（spin Hall bar）的稳态，发现当负载电阻与自旋弛豫长度（spin-relaxation length）相比放置足够远时，焦耳耗散（Joule dissipation）消失，这一全局稳态是纯自旋流注入（pure spin current injection）相对于常规电流注入的显著特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连接外部负载电路的自旋霍尔棒（spin Hall bar）的稳态行为，探讨纯自旋流注入（pure spin current injection）在该系统中特有的全局稳态特性。

Method: 采用基于最小耗散原理（principle of minimum power dissipation）的变分方法（variational approach），并将其推广至双自旋通道模型（two-spin-channel model），推导纵向和横向电流密度的自洽分布，以及相应的自旋和电荷累积与耗散功率。

Result: 当负载电阻与自旋弛豫长度（spin-relaxation length）相比放置足够远时，焦耳耗散（Joule dissipation）消失，呈现一个高度非平凡的全局稳态。

Conclusion: 纯自旋流注入（pure spin current injection）与常规电流注入相比，其最显著的特征是能够实现焦耳耗散（Joule dissipation）消失这一高度非平凡的全局稳态。

Abstract: The stationary state of a spin Hall bar connected to an external load circuit is investigated through a variational approach based on the principle of minimum power dissipation generalized to the two-spin-channel model. The self-consistent distributions of longitudinal and transverse current densities, alongside the corresponding spin and charge accumulations and dissipation power in the resistance, are derived. Surprisingly, it is shown that the Joule dissipation vanishes when the load resistance is placed at a sufficiently large distance compared with the spin-relaxation length. Such a highly non-trivial global stationary state appears as the most striking characteristic of the injection of pure spin current compared with more usual current injection.

</details>


### [148] [Hidden topology and strong quantum metric bounds in trivial systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24208)
*Chang-An Li,Yulin Qin,Bo Fu,Jian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过发展一种维度约化（dimension-reduction）框架，在二维系统中建立了量子度量积分（quantum metric integral, QMI）非零下界与拓扑不变量之间的推广关系，即使传统陈数（Chern number）为零或贝里曲率（Berry curvature）恒为零时，该下界依然由一维拓扑障碍（one-dimensional topological obstructions）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，二维系统的量子度量积分（QMI）由陈数（Chern number）给出下界，但该下界在陈数为零或贝里曲率（Berry curvature）恒为零时退化为平凡下界，无法提供有效的几何约束。因此，需要建立更一般的下界关系，以涵盖这些情形。

Method: 发展了一种维度约化（dimension-reduction）框架，通过嵌套循环（nested-loop）的方式将二维量子度量积分（QMI）分解为低维分量。在此基础上，利用嵌套威尔逊环（nested Wilson loop）引入万尼尔带基（Wannier-band basis），进一步分析高阶拓扑相（higher-order topological phases）中的量子几何。

Result: 在倾斜二维Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型和具有手征对称性（chiral symmetry）的各向异性Wilson-Dirac模型中，QMI的非零下界由两个不同方向上的量子化万尼尔带（quantized Wannier bands）决定。在Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes模型中，万尼尔带QMI的下界由高阶拓扑不变量（higher-order topological invariant），即四极矩（quadrupole moment），给出。

Conclusion: 该维度约化框架为QMI建立了非零下界，将量子几何（quantum geometry）与拓扑（topology）之间的基本关系推广到了更广泛的系统，包括传统二维拓扑平凡（trivial）的情形以及高阶拓扑相。

Abstract: The quantum metric integral (QMI) in two-dimensional (2D) systems is conventionally bounded from below by the Chern number. For systems with zero Chern number or identically vanishing Berry curvature, however, this bound becomes trivial and provides no useful geometric constraints. Here, we develop a dimension-reduction framework that decomposes the 2D QMI into lower-dimensional components in a nested-loop way. With this method, we establish a nonzero lower bound on the QMI arising from one-dimensional topological obstructions even when the conventional 2D topology is trivial. We explicitly demonstrate this mechanism in a tilted 2D Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model and an anisotropic Wilson-Dirac model with chiral symmetry. The resulting lower bounds of QMI are determined by the quantized Wannier bands along two different directions. We further investigate the quantum geometry in higher-order topological phases following the same strategy. By introducing Wannier-band basis obtained from the nested Wilson loop, we demonstrate that the Wannier-band QMI is bounded from below by the higher-order topological invariant, e.g. the quadrupole moment in Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes model. Our results establish nonzero lower bounds on QMI from a dimension-reduction framework, thereby generalizing the fundamental relation between quantum geometry and topology.

</details>


### [149] [Chiral thermal fluctuations and enhanced refrigeration in a nonreciprocal nanomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24209)
*Jesse J. Slim,Javier del Pino,Sander A. Mann,Ewold Verhagen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量了纳米机械谐振子网络中的热流与制冷过程，利用光诱导合成磁通（synthetic magnetic flux）控制非互易性（nonreciprocity），展示了合成磁场如何通过手性（chirality）影响热涨落，并证明非互易性可在强耦合（strong coupling）条件下增强制冷效果，使其温度低于时间反演对称（time-reversal symmetric）网络的理论下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究广泛关注互易性破缺（breaking of reciprocity）如何影响热流以及微观热力学过程（如制冷和能量转换），但缺乏直接的实验测量和机理验证。

Method: 实验采用纳米机械谐振子网络，通过光诱导合成磁通引入可控的非互易性；利用时间调制的光力学相互作用（time-modulated optomechanical interactions）实现可控耦合、规范场（gauge fields）及频率转换驱动的制冷过程；通过测量涨落关联（correlations of fluctuations）量化非平衡热流并成像网络中的热和有效温度（effective temperature）。

Result: 合成磁通在谐振子环路中赋予热涨落手性特征，在不同频带产生不同手性的显著手性流（chiral flows）；非平衡系统中的热流可由合成磁通调谐，从而在热稳态（thermal steady-state）中重新分配能量；非互易性在强耦合条件下增强了谐振子的制冷效果，使其温度突破时间反演对称网络的约束下限。

Conclusion: 实验结果从实验上证明了非互易性对热力学机器的影响，并为在微观层面表征此类系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Understanding how the breaking of reciprocity influences thermal flows and microscopic thermodynamic processes such as refrigeration and energy conversion is of broad current interest. We report experimental measurements of thermal flows and refrigeration in nanomechanical resonator networks in which nonreciprocity is controlled through an optically-induced synthetic magnetic flux. Time-modulated optomechanical interactions allow controlled coupling, gauge fields, and refrigeration processes through nanomechanical frequency conversion. We quantify nonequilibrium heat flows between resonators coupled to dissipative baths of different occupation and image heat and effective temperature in networks through measuring correlations of fluctuations. Synthetic magnetism is shown to imprint chirality on thermal fluctuations in a loop of resonators, leading to pronounced chiral flows with different handedness in distinct frequency bands. We find that the heat flows in a non-equilibrium system are tuned by the synthetic magnetic flux, which redistributes energy in the thermal steady-state. Specifically, we illustrate how nonreciprocity enhances the refrigeration of a resonator in the strong coupling regime, reducing its temperature below the bound that applies to time-reversal symmetric networks. These results experimentally demonstrate the impact of nonreciprocity on thermodynamic machines, and provide new methods to characterize them at the microscopic level.

</details>


### [150] [Large-area CVD Graphene for Photogating-Based Photodetection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24344)
*D. A. Matienko,D. P. Borisenko,A. Yu. Kuntsevich*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了大面积化学气相沉积（CVD）石墨烯作为可扩展光电探测器平台，揭示了衬底诱导的光栅控（photogating）机制在石墨烯/二氧化硅/硅结构中对单层、双层及三层CVD石墨烯的光电导信号起主导作用。单层石墨烯灵敏度最高（995 A/W），增加层数会降低灵敏度但可改善探测率；器件响应取决于光刻工艺路径，展示了CVD石墨烯在光栅控光电探测器中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大面积CVD石墨烯作为可扩展的光电探测器平台，验证其在实际工业应用中的可行性。

Method: 制备单层、双层及三层CVD石墨烯/二氧化硅/硅结构器件，通过栅压依赖的电阻率测量（gate voltage-dependent resistivity measurements）和电容性再充电电流（capacitive recharging current）对比，直接证明光栅控（photogating）的主导作用；同时比较不同光刻工艺路径对器件性能的影响。

Result: 器件对光子能量高于硅禁带宽度（Si band gap）的照明有响应，对通信波段（telecom frequency）无响应，表明光电导机制为光栅控。单层石墨烯灵敏度最高（995 A/W）；增加层数因屏蔽（screening）和并联电导（parallel conduction）降低灵敏度，但可降低噪声提升探测率（detectivity）。金属接触在石墨烯转移前制备的器件光响应更高。

Conclusion: CVD石墨烯具备工业相关性和高灵敏度，适用于基于光栅控的光电探测器。

Abstract: Our work explores large-area CVD graphene as a scalable platform for photodetectors. We show that substrate-induced photogating in graphene/SiO$_2$/Si structures enables a photoconductivity signal in mono-, bi-, and trilayer CVD graphene. The devices respond to illumination with photon energies above the Si band gap and do not respond to telecom frequency, suggesting the photoconductivity mechanism through the photogating. Large sample area and gate voltage-dependent resistivity measurements allow us to prove the dominant role of the photogating directly, demonstrating similarity of the capacitive recharging current and the photoresponse. The sensitivity is the highest (995 A/W) for the monolayer graphene. Increasing the number of graphene layers reduces the sensitivity due to screening and parallel conduction, but can improve detectivity by lowering the noise level. The sensitivity of CVD graphene device depends on fabrication route of photolitography: device with metal contacts fabricated before graphene transfer show higher photoresponce. Thus, our work demonstrate the potential of industrially relevant CVD graphene for sensitive photogating-based photodetectors.

</details>


### [151] [Anomalous Localization in Magnetically Doped Two-Dimensional Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24373)
*Felippe Amorim,Washington F. dos Santos,Mauro S. Ferreira,Alexandre Reily Rocha,Caio Lewenkopf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过解析框架和大规模数值模拟，研究了二维拓扑绝缘体（2DTI）中高浓度磁杂质导致的边缘态传输行为，预测了安德森局域化（Anderson localization）及一种亚指数衰减的异常局域化（anomalous localization）标度律，并发现传输行为仅由有效杂质浓度决定的普适标度行为（universal scaling behavior），在Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱中与实验数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立稀磁杂质对二维拓扑绝缘体（two-dimensional topological insulator, 2DTI）边缘态的影响已较为清楚，但在密集、无序的磁性杂质系综下，边缘输运性质仍缺乏理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立理论框架预测此类体系中的局域化行为。

Method: 本文发展了一个解析分析框架，并结合大规模数值模拟（extensive numerical simulations），描述有限浓度磁杂质存在时二维拓扑绝缘体边缘态的输运行为。

Result: 预测了安德森局域化（Anderson localization）的发生，并发现一种异常局域化（anomalous localization）机制，其电导随系统长度L呈亚指数衰减，标度律为$\ln {\cal G} \propto -\sqrt{L}$。传输行为表现出仅由有效杂质浓度（effective impurity concentration）决定的普适标度行为（universal scaling behavior）。将模型应用于Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱，与实验数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解磁性掺杂拓扑相（magnetically doped topological phases）中的异常局域化现象提供了理论基础，揭示了高浓度磁杂质下边缘态输运的普适标度律。

Abstract: Two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) harbor spin-polarized edge states that are topologically protected by time-reversal symmetry against non-magnetic structural disorder. However, coupling to magnetic impurities breaks this symmetry, inducing backscattering and destroying perfect quantization. While the impact of isolated dilute magnetic impurities is well understood, the transport properties in the presence of dense, disordered ensembles of magnetic moments remain poorly understood. In this work, we develop an analytical framework, supported by extensive numerical simulations, that captures the behavior of edge transport in two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) with a finite concentration of magnetic impurities. We predict the onset of Anderson localization and uncover an anomalous localization regime characterized by a sub-exponential decay of the conductance, scaling as $\ln {\cal G} \propto -\sqrt{L}$, where $L$ is the system length. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the transport exhibits a universal scaling behavior governed solely by the effective impurity concentration. Applying our model to Mn-doped HgTe quantum wells, we find excellent agreement with experimental data. These findings provide a theoretical foundation for understanding anomalous localization phenomena in magnetically doped topological phases.

</details>


### [152] [Electron Hydrodynamics and Bernoulli Effect in Venturi-Shaped 2D Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24386)
*C. A. Monari,A. D. Levin,A. S. Jaroshevich,Z. D. Kvon,V. A. Chitta,D. V. Dmitriev,A. K. Bakarov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过设计文丘里（Venturi）形状的楔形器件，在高迁移率二维电子系统中直接观测到强非线性输运（transport）现象，并用量子点（quantum dot）理论模型（Bernoulli效应类似）解释这一非线性响应，证实了电子流体动力学（electron hydrodynamics）框架在二维电子输运中的适用性，并为固态系统中非线性与预湍流（preturbulent）现象的研究开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子流体动力学为理解超清洁导体中的输运提供了有力框架，但迄今实验证据主要局限于线性响应（linear-response）区域。本文旨在探索并直接观测二维电子系统（2D electron system）中的强非线性输运行为。

Method: 设计并制备了具有文丘里（Venturi）形状楔形几何结构的器件，该结构专门用于增强对流非线性（convective nonlinearities）。通过对器件施加电流并测量电流-电压（current-voltage, I-V）特性，获得非线性电压响应和显著的二极管效应（diodicity）数据。

Result: 实验观测到明显的非线性电压响应和大的二极管效应（diodicity）。实验结果与一个将非线性归因于电子流体对流加速度（convective acceleration）的理论模型（类比伯努利效应，Bernoulli effect）定量吻合。

Conclusion: 这些结果为流体动力学框架在二维电子输运中的适用性提供了有力证据，并为探索固态系统中的非线性及预湍流（preturbulent）现象开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The study of electron hydrodynamics provides a powerful framework for understanding transport in ultraclean conductors, yet experimental evidence has thus far been largely restricted to the linear-response regime. Here, we report the direct observation of a strongly nonlinear transport regime in a high-mobility two-dimensional electron system. By engineering devices with a Venturi-shaped wedge geometry specifically designed to enhance convective nonlinearities, we uncover a pronounced nonlinear voltage response and large diodicity in the current-voltage characteristics. Our experimental findings show quantitative agreement with a theoretical model that attributes the observed nonlinearity to the convective acceleration of the electron fluid, analogous to the Bernoulli effect. These results provide compelling evidence for the applicability of the hydrodynamic framework to two-dimensional electron transport and open new avenues for exploring nonlinear and preturbulent phenomena in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [153] [Testing edge chirality with a three-path fractional quantum Hall interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24451)
*Eugene V. Sukhorukov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平均流干涉仪（average-current interferometer），通过三个相干量子点接触（QPC）形成的磁通环绕回路，测量分数量子霍尔（FQH）边缘态的方向性因果响应（directional causal response），并实现准粒子电荷（quasiparticle charge）、标度维度（scaling dimension）与交换角（exchange angle）的独立提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有干涉实验难以区分分数量子霍尔边缘态中下游（downstream）与上游（upstream）传播模式，且无法同时提取准粒子的电荷、标度维度和交换统计（exchange statistics）等关键参数。本文旨在设计一种新型干涉仪，通过方向性因果响应灵敏地解析边缘态传播方向，并为一般阿贝尔（Abelian）边缘态提供完整的参数提取方案。

Method: 构建由三个相干量子点接触（coherent quantum point contacts, QPCs）组成的磁通环绕回路，主导阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）谐波为隧穿（tunneling）的三次方项。利用直接转移（direct transfer）与相干两步路径（coherent two-step path）之间的干涉区分下游和上游传播。对局域Laughlin边缘（$ν=1/m$）及一般阿贝尔边缘，系统分析弱有限程非局域密度相互作用（finite-range nonlocal density interaction）和循环电压排序（cyclic voltage ordering）对方向性振幅的影响。

Result: 对于$ν=1/m$的局域Laughlin边缘，上游系数严格为零，下游振幅标度为$E^{3ν-2}$。引入弱有限程非局域密度相互作用后，上游系数激活且振幅标度为$E^{2ν-1}$，其相对于下游参考的相位为$-π(1-ν)/2+χ_a$（$χ_a=0$或$π$）。对于一般阿贝尔边缘，方向性振幅标度为$E^{3Δ_ℓ-2}$（$Δ_ℓ=δ_++δ_-$），上下游振幅之比$A^u_ℓ/A^d_ℓ=|\sin(πδ_-)/\sin(πδ_+)|$。在相同顶点（same-vertex）相干未分辨飞行（unresolved-flight）机制下，标度维度与方向性比值确定交换角$θ_ℓ=π(δ_+-δ_-)$。阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆磁通频率额外给出电荷信息。

Conclusion: 该平均流干涉仪能够独立提取准粒子的电荷、标度维度和交换角，提供了一种完整刻画分数量子霍尔边缘态方向性因果响应及其统计性质的实验方案。

Abstract: We propose an average-current interferometer for the directional causal response of fractional quantum Hall edges. Three coherent quantum point contacts (QPCs) form a flux-enclosing loop, so the leading Aharonov-Bohm harmonic is cubic in tunneling. Interference between a direct transfer and a coherent two-step path resolves downstream and upstream propagation. For a local Laughlin edge at $ν=1/m$, the upstream coefficient vanishes exactly, while the downstream amplitude scales as $E^{3ν-2}$. A weak finite-range nonlocal density interaction spanning the tunneling points activates the upstream coefficient without creating an upstream mode. At low temperature and unresolved delay, its amplitude scales as $E^{2ν-1}$ and its aligned phase relative to the downstream reference is $-π(1-ν)/2+χ_a$ modulo $2π$, with $χ_a=0$ or $π$. Opposite cyclic voltage orderings isolate the two directions, while a folded same-filling Laughlin edge with a neutral weak link realizes a sign-tunable bridge. For a general Abelian edge, the device probes the directional content of the selected tunneling vertex. If $δ_\pm$ are its downstream and upstream weights, its nonzero directional amplitudes scale as $E^{3Δ_\ell-2}$, with $Δ_\ell=δ_++δ_-$, and obey $A^u_\ell/A^d_\ell=|\sin(πδ_-)/\sin(πδ_+)|$. When both are nonzero, positive-flux alignment gives, after removal of a known fixed sign, the relative phase $πΔ_\ell=2πh_\ell$. In the same-vertex coherent unresolved-flight regime, the scaling dimension and directional ratio determine the exchange angle $θ_\ell=π(δ_+-δ_-)$ modulo $2π$. The Aharonov-Bohm flux frequency additionally gives the charge, enabling separate extraction of quasiparticle charge, scaling dimension, and exchange angle.

</details>


### [154] [Exceptional Cones from an Indefinite Bogoliubov Metric in Hyperbolic Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24456)
*Junhui Cao,Kirill Bazarov,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示双曲极化激元凝聚体（hyperbolic polariton condensate）中玻戈留波夫（Bogoliubov）准粒子服从一种由能带曲率符号决定的不定度规（indefinite metric），该度规将声学光锥转变为双曲稳定性楔形，并在驱动-耗散系统中导出非厄米（non-Hermitian）玻戈留波夫色散和例外锥（exceptional cone）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通凝聚体中的长波玻戈留波夫声子实现标准的声学洛伦兹度规（acoustic Lorentz metric）。本文旨在探索当凝聚体形成于双曲极化激元能带时，其集体激发是否展现出更丰富的几何结构。

Method: 基于双曲极化激元能带中的玻戈留波夫理论，分析能带曲率符号对集体激发度规（metric）的影响；引入驱动-耗散（driven-dissipative）条件和增益饱和（gain saturation），导出非厄米玻戈留波夫色散关系。

Result: 发现该度规的空间部分继承了能带曲率的反号特征，将声学光锥转换为双曲稳定性楔形（hyperbolic stability wedge）。零判别式面（zero-discriminant surface）在参数空间$(q_x,q_y,Δ_{\rm NH})$中构成例外锥，在固定增益饱和下表现为例外双曲线（exceptional hyperbola）。跨越该面时玻戈留波夫支合并，双正交相位刚性（biorthogonal phase rigidity）消失，谱从传播变为放大或过阻尼动力学。

Conclusion: 双曲极化激元凝聚体为可控平台，其中非厄米例外简并（non-Hermitian exceptional degeneracies）由有效的玻戈留波夫度规组织而成。

Abstract: Long-wavelength Bogoliubov phonons in an ordinary condensate realize the standard acoustic Lorentz metric. We show that a condensate formed in a hyperbolic polariton band realizes a different collective geometry with an indefinite Bogoliubov metric whose spatial signature is inherited from the opposite signs of the band curvatures. This metric converts the acoustic light cone into a hyperbolic stability wedge, separating propagating quasiparticles from dynamically unstable ones. In a driven-dissipative condensate, gain saturation turns this metric relation into a non-Hermitian Bogoliubov dispersion. The zero-discriminant surface becomes an exceptional cone in the parametric space $(q_x,q_y,Δ_{\rm NH})$, appearing as an exceptional hyperbola at fixed gain saturation. Across this surface the Bogoliubov branches coalesce, the biorthogonal phase rigidity collapses, and the spectrum changes from propagation to amplified or overdamped dynamics. Our results identify hyperbolic polariton condensates as a controllable setting where non-Hermitian exceptional degeneracies are organized by an effective Bogoliubov metric.

</details>


### [155] [Skew scattering induced contribution to orbital Hall response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24671)
*Dayana Joy,Vivek Pandey,Rhonald Burgos Atencia,Pankaj Bhalla*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究系统地分析了时间反演对称性破缺的三维Weyl半金属中，由无序（disorder）诱导的轨道霍尔电导率（orbital Hall conductivity）的贡献。利用量子动力学方法，作者揭示了侧跳（side-jump）和斜散射（skew scattering）的作用，并证明高阶无序贡献（尤其是无序势的三次方项）在振荡电场下主导轨道霍尔电导率，抑制了其他散射机制。通过调节无序势强度、外加能量及Weyl节点间距，可增强外禀轨道霍尔电导率，数值估计结果支持了该机制在轨道电子学（orbitronics）器件中的潜在实验相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间反演对称性破缺的三维Weyl半金属中，无序对轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect）的贡献尚不明确。本研究旨在利用量子动力学方法，系统量化侧跳和斜散射等无序诱导机制对轨道霍尔电导率的影响，并探索其调控手段与实验可行性。

Method: 采用量子动力学方法（quantum kinetic approach），分析无序势的侧跳与斜散射贡献；通过解析推导轨道霍尔电导率随无序势强度、费米能量及Weyl节点间距的依赖关系，并结合数值估计验证理论结果。

Result: 明确展示了轨道霍尔电导率对无序势和费米能量的依赖性；在时间反演对称性破缺的Weyl半金属中，高阶无序贡献（尤其是无序势的三次方项）在振荡电场下占主导，压制了侧跳等其他散射机制；通过调节无序势强度、外加能量及Weyl节点间距，可有效增强外禀轨道霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了无序诱导的轨道霍尔电导率在Weyl半金属中的主导机制与调控途径，数值估计结果凸显了其在推进轨道电子学器件技术方面的实验可操作性。

Abstract: Our study provides the disorder-induced contribution to the orbital Hall conductivity in three-dimensional Weyl semimetals with broken time-reversal symmetry. Using the quantum kinetic approach, we analyse the impact of side-jump and skew scattering contributions to the system. The dependence of the orbital Hall conductivity on both disorder potential and the Fermi energy is explicitly demonstrated. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the higher-order disorder contribution, especially from the third power of disorder potential, dominates the orbital Hall conductivity under an oscillating electric field in a time-reversal symmetry broken Weyl semimetal, suppressing other scattering mechanisms, including the side jump contributions. We can enhance the extrinsic orbital Hall conductivity by tuning the strength of the disorder potential, applied energy, and choosing the system with appropriate Weyl node separation. Finally, our results are supported by numerical estimations and highlight potential experimental relevance for advancing orbitronics device technologies.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [The loop-nilpotent cohomological Hall algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23544)
*Shivang Jindal,Andrei Neguţ*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为具有典型立方势的三重箭图（tripled quiver）的圈幂零上同调Hall代数（CoHA）构建了一个显式的shuffle代数模型，并将其与量子化Coulomb分支代数、Kac多项式及Yangian的Drinfeld-Gavarini对偶等建立联系，同时证明了一个关于局部化shuffle代数的球形生成猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为圈幂零上同调Hall代数（loop-nilpotent cohomological Hall algebra）提供一个显式的shuffle代数模型，并以此建立该代数与量子化Coulomb分支代数、Kac多项式以及Yangian结构之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过构建显式的shuffle代数模型来刻画圈幂零上同调Hall代数，并利用该模型推导出该代数在ℏ=0时的超交换性、生成元以及BPS Lie代数（BPS Lie algebra）的显式刻画。

Result: (1) 将圈幂零CoHA与相应箭图规范理论的量子化Coulomb分支代数（quantized Coulomb branch algebra）联系起来；(2) 证明该代数在ℏ=0时是超交换的；(3) 给出圈幂零CoHA和完整preprojective CoHA的生成元；(4) 通过度数和可整除性条件显式刻画了完整preprojective CoHA的BPS Lie代数，并给出了Kac多项式的新公式。此外，还证明了关于局部化shuffle代数球形生成的猜想，并指出对于ADE型箭图，圈幂零CoHA是Yangian的Drinfeld-Gavarini对偶的正半部分。

Conclusion: 所构建的shuffle代数模型为理解圈幂零CoHA及其相关结构（如Coulomb分支、Kac多项式和Yangian对偶）提供了一个统一的代数框架，并验证了关于球形生成的重要猜想。

Abstract: We give an explicit shuffle algebra model for the loop-nilpotent cohomological Hall algebra (CoHA) of a tripled quiver with canonical cubic potential. As consequences, we (1) relate the loop-nilpotent CoHA to the quantized Coulomb branch algebra of the corresponding quiver gauge theory, (2) show that the loop-nilpotent CoHA is supercommutative after specialization at $\hbar=0$, (3) give generators for both the loop-nilpotent CoHA and the full preprojective CoHA, and (4) obtain an explicit characterization of the BPS Lie algebra of the full preprojective CoHA via certain degree and divisibility conditions. This gives a new formula for the Kac polynomials of the quiver in terms of the dimensions of certain vector spaces of polynomials. We also prove a conjecture on the spherical generation of the localized shuffle algebra and show that for ADE quivers, the loop-nilpotent CoHA is the positive half of Drinfeld-Gavarini dual of the Yangian.

</details>


### [157] [Coulomb branches, quantized zastavas, Kac polynomials, and shuffle algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24711)
*Dinakar Muthiah,Alex Weekes*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类从Kac-Moody仿射格拉斯曼截面（Kac-Moody affine Grassmannian slices）到zastava空间的闭嵌入（closed embeddings）是可量子化的（quantize），由此构造了任意箭图（quiver）的极限量子化zastava代数$\mathcal{A}$（扮演Borel Yangian角色）及其正部$\mathcal{A}^+$（扮演unipotent Yangian角色），证明了其Hilbert级数（Hilbert series）由Kac多项式的Hua公式给出，并证明了$\mathcal{A}^+$同构于某个shuffle代数，进而证明了Negut关于局部化shuffle代数的球面生成（spherical generation）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作中构造的Kac-Moody仿射格拉斯曼截面的闭嵌入在一般情况下不可量子化，本研究的动机是寻找该嵌入可量子化的变体，并利用量子化构造来研究极限量子化代数的结构性质，最终证明Negut关于局部化shuffle代数球面生成的猜想。

Method: 采用Braverman-Finkelberg-Nakajima（BFN）构造的变体（zastava空间）代替原Coulomb分支构造；通过取极限构造极限量子化zastava代数$\mathcal{A}$和正部$\mathcal{A}^+$；利用单极子公式（monopole formula）的极限计算Hilbert级数；通过shuffle代数同构刻画$\mathcal{A}^+$的代数结构。

Result: 证明了闭嵌入在zastava空间情形下是可量子化的；构造了极限量子化zastava代数$\mathcal{A}$（Borel Yangian）和$\mathcal{A}^+$（unipotent Yangian）；证明了$\mathcal{A}$和$\mathcal{A}^+$的Hilbert级数由Kac多项式的Hua公式给出；证明了$\mathcal{A}^+$同构于某个shuffle代数；基于这些结果获得了Negut猜想的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了zastava空间的量子化闭嵌入与Yangian型代数结构之间的深刻联系，并通过shuffle代数同构和Hilbert级数公式，为Negut关于局部化shuffle代数球面生成的猜想提供了完整证明。

Abstract: We previously constructed closed embeddings of Kac-Moody affine Grassmannian slices using fundamental monopole operators. These spaces are defined via the Braverman-Finkelberg-Nakajima construction of Coulomb branches for quiver gauge theories, and the embeddings do not quantize in general. However, there is variant of the BFN construction that produces zastava spaces, and we show that the closed embeddings do quantize in that case. This allows us to take a limit and construct the limit quantized zastava $\mathcal{A}$ for an arbitrary quiver. This algebra plays the role of the Borel Yangian. We also construct a positive part $\mathcal{A}^+$, which plays the role of the unipotent Yangian. By taking the limit of the monopole formula, we show that both $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{A}^+$ have Hilbert series given by a version for Hua's formula for Kac polynomials. We also show that $\mathcal{A}^+$ is isomorphic to a certain shuffle algebra. Finally, using these results we obtain a proof of Negut's conjecture on the spherical generation of localized shuffle algebras via the second author's work on generators of Coulomb branches.

</details>

### [158] [Dirac geometry, deformation theory and shifted symplectic geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23826)
*Mohamed Moussadek Maiza*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种狄拉克变形（Dirac deformation）理论，在扭曲狄拉克几何（twisted Dirac geometry）与泊松几何（Poisson geometry）之间建立插值，并证明该变形与狄拉克几何的主要结构运算（约化、积分、Morita等价）相容。主要实例包括紧致李群上的Cartan–Dirac结构变形为李代数对偶上的KKS泊松结构，以及相应的拟辛群胚变形为辛群胚。该理论统一涵盖了从拟哈密顿约化到哈密顿约化等多种经典变形的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何框架中，扭曲狄拉克几何与泊松几何之间存在概念上的间隙，缺乏一个统一的变形理论来联系两者及其相关的约化、积分与等价结构。本文旨在填补这一空白，并揭示拟哈密顿约化（quasi-Hamiltonian reduction）与哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction）等经典构造之间深层的几何联系。

Method: 提出狄拉克变形（Dirac deformation）理论，系统建立从扭曲狄拉克结构到泊松结构的连续变形，并验证该变形对三种核心结构运算的相容性：沿强狄拉克映射（strong Dirac maps）的约化、到拟辛群胚（quasi-symplectic groupoid）的积分，以及李代数胚（Lie algebroids）的Morita等价（Morita equivalence）。关键实例为紧致李群G上的Cartan–Dirac结构L_G变形为李代数对偶g*上的KKS泊松结构，并提升至群胚层面的变形。

Result: 该理论作为特例恢复了一系列经典变形：从拟哈密顿约化到哈密顿约化（沿共轭类）；Steinberg与Sevostyanov切片到其加法对应（Kostant、Slodowy）切片；乘法抛物约化与单幺约化；拟哈密顿内爆（quasi-Hamiltonian implosion）到辛内爆（symplectic implosion）；乘法Moore–Tachikawa簇到其加法类比。在1-平移辛叠（1-shifted symplectic stack）的拟辛群胚展示语言下，主要约化定理给出对应约化空间的光滑变形。

Conclusion: 狄拉克变形理论提供了一个统一框架，将拟哈密顿几何与标准哈密顿几何联系起来，并为多个独立发展的变形构造给出了系统的几何解释。该理论在约化空间的光滑变形方面具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We develop a Dirac deformation theory that interpolates between twisted Dirac geometry and Poisson geometry, and prove that this deformation is compatible with the principal structural operations of Dirac geometry: reduction along strong Dirac maps, integration to quasi-symplectic groupoids, and Morita equivalence of Lie algebroids. The fundamental example is the deformation of the Cartan--Dirac structure $L_G$ on a compact Lie group~$G$ to the Kirillov--Kostant--Souriau Poisson structure on $\mathfrak{g}^*$, and its lift to a deformation of the quasi-symplectic groupoid $D(G)\rightrightarrows G$ to the symplectic groupoid $T^*G\rightrightarrows\mathfrak{g}^*$. As applications, we obtain a uniform deformation theory recovering, as special cases, the deformation of quasi-Hamiltonian to Hamiltonian reduction along conjugacy classes, the Steinberg and Sevostyanov slices to their additive (Kostant, Slodowy) counterparts, the multiplicative parabolic and unipotent reductions, the quasi-Hamiltonian implosion to symplectic implosion, and the multiplicative Moore--Tachikawa varieties to their additive analogues. In the language of quasi-symplectic groupoid presentations of $1$-shifted symplectic stacks, our main reduction theorem yields a smooth deformation of the corresponding reduced spaces.

</details>
