### [159] [Bimodal colloids highlight the structural mirror of rigidity percolation and yielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22901)
*Robert A. Campbell,Ziye Zhuang,Ali Mohraz,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过粒子模拟发现，亚稳颗粒凝胶（metastable particulate gels）中，刚性渗透（rigidity percolation）导致的流体-固体相变与屈服（yielding）导致的固体-流体相变并非完全对称的镜像过程，而是由相同的介观结构——簇间单连接桥（singly-connected intercluster bridges）所主导：这些瓶颈结构既是刚性的来源，也是屈服时刚性优先丧失的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管亚稳颗粒凝胶中流体-固体与固体-流体的非线性相变在动力学上表现出相似性，但其多尺度特性使研究者难以判断这些过程是否遵循统一的结构路径。本文旨在探究刚性建立与丧失是否共享相同的介观结构基础。

Method: 采用粒子模拟（particle simulations）分析不同类别的颗粒级键合（particle-level bonds）及其对整体力学响应（bulk mechanical response）的贡献。具体考察了单连接桥（singly-connected bridges）和边介数中心性（edge-betweenness centrality, EBC）两类结构特征。

Result: 刚性过度依赖于簇间单连接桥（singly connected intercluster bridges），而屈服则局域化在高边介数中心性（high EBC）的键合上。令人瞩目的是，这两个独立识别的结构群体存在显著重叠，且发挥相似的力学作用；双峰性（bimodality）通过将大颗粒接触集中在这两个群体中，暴露出这种对应关系，从而为共同骨架提供了成分标签。

Conclusion: 刚性与屈服是同一介观结构——簇间瓶颈——在力学上的对立表现：建立刚性的瓶颈结构也正是刚性优先丧失的位点。因此，流体-固体与固体-流体转变并非简单的镜像关系，而是共享同一结构通路。

Abstract: In metastable particulate gels, it is tempting to believe that the dynamic similarities between the fluid-to-solid non-linear phase transition of rigidity percolation and the solid-to-fluid transition that occurs during yielding represent mirror images of the same continuous process. Even though these behaviors are clearly dynamically similar, their multi-scale nature makes it difficult to determine if they could also follow a unified structural pathway. We know from model monodisperse colloidal gels that both yielding and the elastic modulus seem to be heavily influenced by a small subset of topologically distinct singly-connected bridges linking mesoscale features. Here we use particle simulations to examine the participation of different classes of particle-level bonds and their contributions to the bulk mechanical response. We find that rigidity is disproportionately supported by singly connected intercluster bridges, whereas yielding localizes at bonds with high edge-betweenness centrality (EBC); strikingly, these independently identified populations substantially overlap and perform comparable mechanical roles. Bimodality exposes this correspondence by concentrating large-particle contacts in both populations, thereby providing a compositional label for the common backbone. Thus, rigidity and yielding are opposing mechanical manifestations of the same mesoscale structure: the intercluster bottlenecks that establish rigidity are also the sites at which rigidity is preferentially lost.

</details>


### [160] [From Local Structure to Thermodynamics and Transport of Water with Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22903)
*Andreas Kretschmer,Florian Altmann,Nader Nour,Alper T. Celebi,Markus Valtiner*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用六维对关联函数、三体结构描述符、过量熵和输运性质，系统评估了基于不同密度泛函理论交换关联泛函训练的机器学习力场；结果显示，底层泛函的选择对水分子的微观结构与动力学预测有显著影响，其中RPBE-D3在结构、热力学和输运性质上与实验最为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在液态水的模拟中，机器学习力场（machine learning force field）的预测准确度高度依赖于训练时采用的密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）交换关联泛函。然而，不同泛函如何影响力场所预测的微观结构、热力学和动力学性质，缺乏系统且定量化的比较分析。

Method: 采用完整六维对关联函数（six-dimensional pair correlation function）、三体结构描述符（three-body structural descriptor）、过量熵（excess entropy）及输运性质（transport property）作为评估指标，对源自不同DFT交换关联泛函的机器学习力场进行对比分析，并以经典SPC/E模型作为额外参照。

Result: 忽略色散修正的泛函会导致显著的过度结构化（overstructuring）、过负的过量熵以及被抑制的扩散。平动熵与取向熵贡献紧密耦合，二者共同与约化自扩散系数呈现明确关系。在所有测试模型中，RPBE-D3在结构、热力学和输运性质上与实验最为一致；SPC/E模型与RPBE-D3表现出显著相似性，这与两者具有相近的Born有效电荷和部分电荷一致。

Conclusion: 机器学习力场预测的液态水微观结构与动力学对底层DFT泛函高度敏感；RPBE-D3是目前测试范围内综合表现最佳的选择。不同力场的平动与取向熵的耦合关系以及其与自扩散系数的关联，为理解水动力学行为提供了结构-熵关系视角。

Abstract: We evaluate machine learning force fields derived from different density functional theory exchange correlation functionals using the full six-dimensional pair correlation function of liquid water, three-body structural descriptors, excess entropy, and transport properties. The predicted microscopic structure and dynamics depend strongly on the underlying functional: neglecting dispersion produces pronounced overstructuring, overly negative excess entropy, and suppressed diffusion. Translational and orientational entropy contributions are tightly coupled and together exhibit a clear relationship with the reduced selfdiffusion coefficient. Among the tested models, RPBE-D3 provides the most consistent agreement with experiment across structural, thermodynamic, and transport properties. The classical SPC/E model serves as an additional reference and displays notable similarities to RPBE-D3, consistent with the comparable Born effective and partial charges of the two models.

</details>


### [161] [Active flows drive anchoring of nematics at rigid walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23164)
*Michael Fang,Ioannis Hadjifrangiskou,Sumesh P. Thampi,Julia M. Yeomans,Jan Rozman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过连续介质模拟（continuum simulations）和近壁简化解析分析，研究了主动向列相（active nematics）在刚性边界上的排列行为。结果表明，在流动-翻滚（flow-tumbling）区，伸展型（extensile）系统平行于边界排列，收缩型（contractile）系统垂直于边界排列；而在流动-排列（flow-aligning）区，取向依赖于活性和流动排列参数的符号。该框架统一解释了刚性壁上由活性诱导的锚定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管约束（confinement）对活性材料中的流动有显著影响，但目前尚不清楚当没有热力学锚定（thermodynamic anchoring）时，活性粒子在刚性边界上如何定向排列。本文旨在填补这一理解空白。

Method: 采用主动向列相的连续介质模拟（continuum simulations）以及基于约化的近壁描述（reduced near-wall description）的解析论证。

Result: 在流动-翻滚（flow-tumbling）区，伸展型（extensile）系统平行于边界排列，收缩型（contractile）系统垂直于边界排列。在流动-排列（flow-aligning）区，取向取决于活性（activity）和流动排列参数（flow aligning parameter）的符号：要么类剪切流（shear-like flow）选择莱斯利角（Leslie angle），要么无法建立唯一排列。

Conclusion: 该研究为刚性壁上活性诱导锚定（activity-induced anchoring）提供了统一框架，表明致密活性物质中的边界排列可以仅由自生流动（self-generated flows）与取向动力学（orientational dynamics）的相互作用产生。

Abstract: Although confinement strongly influences flows in active materials, it remains unclear how active particles align at rigid boundaries when no thermodynamic anchoring is imposed. We address this question using continuum simulations of active nematics, together with analytical arguments based on a reduced near-wall description. In the flow-tumbling regime, extensile systems align parallel to the boundary, whereas contractile systems align perpendicular to it, consistent with active anchoring observed at active-passive interfaces. In the flow-aligning regime, the preferred orientation depends on the sign of activity and of the flow aligning parameter: either the shear-like flow generated near the wall selects the Leslie angle, or no unique alignment is established. These results provide a unified framework for activity-induced anchoring at rigid walls, demonstrating that boundary alignment in dense active matter can emerge solely from the interplay between self-generated flows and orientational dynamics.

</details>


### [162] [Universal Sign Reversal of Magnetic Response in Transmembrane Ionic Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23239)
*Tina Arabi,Ehsan Noruzifar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个介观平衡框架（mesoscopic equilibrium framework），揭示弱磁场通过改变亚稳态输运状态（metastable transport states）的平衡占据概率来调控非平衡离子电流，而非直接作用于离子微观轨迹。该理论统一解释了磁场增强与抑制离子流的矛盾现象，并预测了由单一平衡协方差（equilibrium covariance）控制的磁信号反转普适判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱磁场已被长期报道可增强或抑制跨膜离子电流，但这些看似矛盾的响应（contradictory responses）的物理起源尚未得到解决。

Method: 发展了一个介观平衡框架（mesoscopic equilibrium framework），将弱磁场对输运的影响归结为对亚稳态输运状态（metastable transport states）平衡占据概率（equilibrium occupation probabilities）的修正，进而改变非平衡离子电流（nonequilibrium ionic current）。

Result: 该理论在一个统一的理论框架（unified theoretical framework）内自然解释了磁增强与磁抑制两种效应，并预测了由单一平衡协方差（single equilibrium covariance）控制的磁信号反转（magnetic sign reversal）普适判据。此外，还识别出特征磁场依赖性（characteristic magnetic-field dependence）和状态依赖的输运调制（state-dependent transport modulation）等可实验检验的特征。

Conclusion: 该理论为解释生物离子输运中的弱磁场效应提供了定量框架（quantitative framework）。

Abstract: Weak magnetic fields have long been reported to either enhance or suppress transmembrane ionic currents, yet the physical origin of these apparently contradictory responses remains unresolved. Here, we develop a mesoscopic equilibrium framework showing that weak magnetic fields primarily modify the equilibrium occupation probabilities of metastable transport states, thereby altering the resulting nonequilibrium ionic current. Rather than acting directly on microscopic ionic trajectories, magnetic fields regulate transport through the statistical redistribution of conducting states. This mechanism naturally explains both magnetic enhancement and suppression within a unified theoretical framework and predicts a universal criterion for magnetic sign reversal governed by a single equilibrium covariance. The theory further identifies experimentally testable signatures, including characteristic magnetic-field dependence and state-dependent transport modulation, providing a quantitative framework for interpreting weak-field magnetic effects in biological ionic transport.

</details>


### [163] [Microphase Separation in Quorum-Sensing Active Particles with Competing Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23259)
*Michele Antonioli,Nicoletta Gnan,Claudio Maggi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受微生物通信启发的竞争性群体感应（quorum-sensing）模型，通过引入多重感知范围间的竞争，将主动物质（active matter）中传统的宏观相分离转变为具有有限特征长度的自组织微相（self-organized microphases），并通过粗粒化场论和数值模拟系统验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准主动物质群体感应模型仅能产生宏观相分离等集体现象；作者希望探索一种更简单的微观机制——竞争性感知范围——能否导致更丰富的微相行为，从而弥合微生物通信现象与主动物质相行为之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 从微观动力学出发，推导出粗粒化场论（coarse-grained field theory），其系数与微观感知函数的矩（moments）显式关联；通过粒子基模拟与连续统理论（continuum theory）的直接对比，预测特征调制长度（modulation length）和关联长度（correlation length）；在二维数值模拟中验证预测，并使用高阶梯度展开（higher-order gradient expansion）捕捉更强竞争下的团簇相（cluster phase）。

Result: 二维数值模拟证实了理论预测：随着感知尺度之间竞争增强，体系从宏观相分离（macrophase separation）转变为有限波长密度调制（finite-wavelength density modulations）；在更强的竞争相互作用下，体系出现一种带有间隙渗流网络（interstitial percolating network）的奇特团簇相。

Conclusion: 竞争性群体感应相互作用是一种简单、可调的微观机制，可用于生成可调谐的主动微相，从而扩展了主动物质中相分离的理论框架。

Abstract: Standard quorum-sensing models in active matter exhibit collective phenomena such as motility-induced phase separation. Here, we show that incorporating competing sensing ranges-- a minimal ingredient inspired by microbial communication --qualitatively changes this behavior, replacing macroscopic phase separation with self-organized microphases characterized by an emergent finite length scale. Starting from the microscopic dynamics, we derive a coarse-grained field theory whose coefficients are explicitly related to the moments of the microscopic sensing function. This mapping enables a direct comparison between particle-based simulations and continuum theory, allowing the characteristic modulation and correlation lengths to be predicted directly from the microscopic interaction parameters. Two-dimensional numerical simulations confirm these predictions and reveal a transition from macrophase separation to finite-wavelength density modulations as the competition between sensing scales increases. For stronger competing interactions, the system develops a peculiar cluster phase with an interstitial percolating network, which is captured by a higher-order gradient expansion. Our results identify competing quorum-sensing interactions as a simple microscopic mechanism for generating tunable active microphases.

</details>


### [164] [Shear-mode Direct Piezoelectric Response of Ferroelectric Nematic Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23297)
*Péter Salamon,Marcell Tibor Máthé,Hiroya Nishikawa,Fumito Araoka,Antal Jákli*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究首次定量测量了铁电向列相液晶的剪切模式正压电耦合常数，通过周期剪切诱导电流和振荡流变学实验，揭示了流动取向诱发正压电响应的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅在固态晶体中观察到压电效应（piezoelectricity），近期虽发现液态铁电向列相液晶也具有压电性，但仅定量测量了逆压电信号（施加电压诱导的机械形变）。本研究旨在填补对正压电效应（机械形变诱导电信号）定量测量的空白。

Method: 对两种典型铁电向列化合物 RM734 和 DIO 进行周期剪切诱导电流（periodic shear-induced electric current）和振荡流变学（oscillatory rheology）测量，分析温度、频率和应变依赖的一阶与二阶谐波电流信号，并结合振荡流变学数据定量确定剪切模式正压电耦合常数（shear-mode direct piezoelectric coupling constants）。

Result: 成功定量获得了两种材料的剪切模式正压电耦合常数，数值相近，并与先前逆压电测量结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: 提出铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）的流动取向（flow alignment）是产生正压电响应的物理机制。

Abstract: Piezoelectricity (linear coupling between mechanical deformation and electric signal) was originally observed only in solid crystals. Recently, it was discovered that liquid ferroelectric nematic liquid crystals are also piezoelectric. However, so far only their converse piezoelectric signals (applied voltage-induced mechanical deformation) were measured quantitatively. In this work, we have carried out periodic shear-induced electric current and oscillatory rheology measurements on the two archetypic ferroelectric nematic compounds, RM734 and DIO. From temperature, frequency and strain dependent results of the first and second harmonic current signals together with the results from oscillatory rheometry, we were able to quantitatively determine the shear-mode direct piezoelectric coupling constants. These values are similar for both materials and are compared to results of previous converse piezoelectric measurements. We propose a physical mechanism in which the flow alignment of ferroelectric polarization leads to the direct piezoelectric response.

</details>


### [165] [Transient fluid removal at soft interfaces: Stationary squeeze-out and dynamic scraping in a block-on-flat contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23405)
*R. Xu,T. Tada,D. Ferré Sentis,B. N. J. Persson*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验分析了在不同润滑剂（水、甘油、泥浆、硅脂）条件下，矩形橡胶块与瓷砖/玻璃界面间的瞬态摩擦行为，发现高黏度流体的排出主要依赖滑动引起的刮擦作用，缩短接触块可加速流体排出，为橡胶胎面块的多尺度排水通道设计提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清除橡胶基底界面的流体对于维持行走和车辆制动在受污染表面上的摩擦至关重要，因此需要理解不同黏度流体在瞬态滑动过程中的排出机制。

Method: 使用矩形橡胶块在润滑的瓷砖和玻璃表面进行滑动摩擦实验，测试两种不同滑动方向长度的块配置，并设置不同的静态等待时间；润滑剂包括水、甘油、泥浆和硅脂。基于实测表面粗糙度进行静态挤压排出（stationary squeeze-out）计算，并与甘油实验结果对比。

Result: 水润滑下，静态挤压排出在滑动开始前基本完成；甘油润滑下，静态挤压排出与滑动诱导的流体排出均重要；泥浆和硅脂等极高黏度物质条件下，滑动距离达到与块长度相当的量级时进入稳态滑动，且滑动诱导的刮擦（scraping）主导流体排出。将接触块分为更短的块可缩短排水距离并增加前缘数量，从而加速流体排出。

Conclusion: 静态挤压排出计算与甘油实验吻合较好。研究结果为具有多尺度排水通道和足够柔顺性以促进瞬态流体排出的橡胶胎面块提供了设计指南。

Abstract: Fluid removal from rubber-substrate interfaces is crucial for maintaining friction during walking and vehicle braking on contaminated surfaces. We study the transient friction of rectangular rubber blocks sliding against tile and glass surfaces lubricated with water, glycerol, mud, or silicone grease. Two block configurations with different lengths in the sliding direction were tested after different stationary waiting times. For water, stationary squeeze-out is nearly complete before sliding begins. For glycerol, both stationary squeeze-out and sliding-induced fluid removal are important. For mud and grease, the steady-sliding state is reached after a sliding distance of the order of the block length, with little dependence on the preceding waiting time, showing that sliding-induced scraping dominates fluid removal for highly viscous substances. Dividing the contact into shorter blocks accelerates fluid removal by reducing the drainage distance and increasing the number of leading edges. Stationary squeeze-out calculations based on the measured surface roughness are in reasonably good agreement with the glycerol experiments. The results provide design guidelines for rubber tread blocks with multiscale drainage channels and sufficient compliance to promote transient fluid removal.

</details>


### [166] [The Polymer Physics of Kinetoplast DNA as a Polymerised Membrane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23410)
*Takahiro Sakaue,Davide Michieletto*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文分析了动基体DNA（kDNA）的构象与动力学特性，提出其大尺度行为可用聚合膜（polymerized membrane）的物理来描述，并证明了水动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）对其动力学的重要性，所预测的动力学标度（dynamical scaling）与实验数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动基体DNA（kDNA）是一种由数千个环状DNA分子通过拓扑连接（topological linking）构成的巨大片状结构，可视为天然存在的二维奥运凝胶（Olympic gels），其物理性质尚不明确，亟需理论理解。

Method: 利用聚合膜（polymerized membrane）的物理框架描述kDNA的大尺度行为，并引入水动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）分析其在水溶液中的动力学特性。

Result: 所预测的动力学标度（dynamical scaling）能统一解释近期体外成像实验（in vitro imaging experiments）的多项数据；给出了kDNA面内弹性模量（in-plane elastic modulus）的估计值，其大小与近期测量结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: kDNA的构象与动力学特性可用聚合膜物理结合水动力学相互作用有效描述，理论标度与实验数据一致，弹性模量估计可靠。

Abstract: We analyze the conformational and dynamical properties of the kinetoplast DNA (kDNA), a massive sheet-like structure made from thousands of circular DNA molecules, found in the mitochondrion of certain parasites. The connectivity between circular DNA molecules is achieved by topological linking, hence, the kDNA may be regarded as a naturally occurring two-dimensional version of Olympic gels, whose physical properties are yet to be understood. We propose that the basic aspects in the large scale behaviors of kDNA could be described by the physics of polymerized membrane. Our analysis indicates the relevance of the hydrodynamic interactions in the dynamics of kDNA in aqueous solution. We demonstrate that the predicted dynamical scaling scenario captures various experimental data recently obtained from {\it in vitro} imaging experiments in a unified manner. We also provide an estimate for the in-plane elastic modulus of kDNA, whose magnitude agrees well with recent measurements.

</details>


### [167] [On the flash temperature in sliding rubber contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23562)
*B. N. J. Persson*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于预测在刚性随机粗糙表面上滑动的粘弹性固体闪温（flash temperature）的解析理论，该理论考虑了所有相关长度尺度上的表面粗糙度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性固体（viscoelastic solids）在粗糙表面上滑动时，摩擦生热导致的闪温对材料磨损和性能具有重要影响，而现有理论尚未充分结合表面粗糙度在多尺度上的效应。

Method: 采用解析理论方法，构建了粘弹性固体在刚性随机粗糙表面上滑动的闪温模型，并将所有相关长度尺度上的表面粗糙度纳入理论框架。

Result: 该理论能够定量描述闪温与表面粗糙度多尺度特征之间的关系，为摩擦热效应分析提供了解析基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析理论有效预测了粘弹性固体在粗糙表面滑动时的闪温，为理解和控制摩擦热导致的材料行为提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We present an analytical theory for the flash temperature for viscoelastic solids sliding on rigid and randomly rough surfaces. The theory takes into account the surface roughness on all relevant length scales.

</details>


### [168] [Electrolytes confined between polarizable surfaces in slit pores with anisotropic permittivity tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23812)
*Alexandre P. dos Santos,Yan Levin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出了一种在窄狭缝孔隙内对各向异性介电张量的粗粒化电解质溶液进行高效模拟的方法，采用二维周期格林函数与各向异性三维Ewald求和相结合求解静电方程。蒙特卡洛模拟表明，介电各向异性会显著重塑双电层结构，增强面内离子-离子关联，并使得介电与金属边界在强各向异性下具有几乎一致的结构轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以高效模拟具有各向异性介电张量的受限电解质溶液，特别是窄狭缝孔隙中极化表面的静电相互作用。

Method: 采用二维周期格林函数法（2D periodic Green's function method）结合平板修正的各向异性三维Ewald求和（slab-corrected anisotropic 3D Ewald summation）求解极化表面的静电方程组；并将该方法应用于蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），研究1:1电解质在极化介电表面与金属表面之间的受限行为。

Result: 介电各向异性（dielectric anisotropy）显著重塑双电层结构；坐标拉伸变换表明，单个离子-镜像相互作用严格依赖于体相的平行介电常数（parallel permittivity）；垂直介电常数（perpendicular permittivity）的抑制会大幅增强直接的面内离子-离子关联（in-plane ion-ion correlations）。在强各向异性下，这些横向关联完全主导热力学，使得介电表面与金属表面的结构轮廓几乎相同，驱使较小的阳离子直接进入较大阴离子的接触平面（contact plane）。

Conclusion: 介电各向异性在受限电解质中起着关键作用，不仅改变双电层结构，还通过增强面内关联使不同边界条件下的行为趋同，这为理解各向异性介电环境中的静电效应提供了新的物理图像。

Abstract: We present a method that enables efficient simulations of coarse-grained electrolyte solutions inside a narrow slit pore with an anisotropic dielectric permittivity tensor. The electrostatic equations for polarizable surfaces are solved using a 2D periodic Green's function method combined with a slab-corrected anisotropic 3D Ewald summation. We apply this approach in Monte Carlo simulations to study 1:1 electrolytes confined between both polarizable dielectric and metallic surfaces. Our results show that dielectric anisotropy aggressively reshapes the double-layer structure. While a coordinate stretching transformation demonstrates that individual ion-image interactions depend strictly on the bulk-like parallel permittivity, the suppression of the perpendicular permittivity dramatically amplifies direct in-plane ion-ion correlations. Under strong anisotropy, these lateral correlations dominate the thermodynamics completely, rendering the structural profiles of mutually opposing dielectric and metallic boundaries practically identical by forcing the smaller cations directly into the contact plane of the larger anions.

</details>


### [169] [Growth and remodeling control shape memory in morphogenetic rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23907)
*Nicolas Romeo,David B. Brückner,Noah P. Mitchell*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 研究通过粘-弹-塑性杆模型的理论与数值模拟，揭示了重塑在形态发生中的双重作用：低塑性导致图案粗化（coarsening），高塑性引入几何无序（geometric disorder），从而在形状复杂度与可重复性之间存在权衡；而生长可以打破这一权衡，使复杂形状得以可重复生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长结构中的弹性屈曲（elastic buckling）倾向于简化而非保留形状的复杂度，而生物系统却能在持续重塑材料的过程中构建复杂且可重复的形态。该研究旨在理解重塑与生长如何调控形态发生的复杂度与可重复性之间的平衡。

Method: 采用解析理论与数值模拟，构建了一个生长中的粘-弹-塑性杆（visco-elasto-plastic rod）的最小模型，系统分析不同塑性程度和生长速率下形状演化行为。

Result: 低塑性下图案通过弹性松弛（elastic relaxation）发生粗化，高塑性下涨落（fluctuations）被转化为几何无序；存在一个最优中间塑性值，能够保护初始图案的复杂度与可重复性。生长通过同时抑制这两种失效模式，打破了这一权衡。

Conclusion: 重塑（remodeling）和生长（growth）速率是控制编码图案（encoded pattern）得以记忆、退化或转变的两个关键调控参数。

Abstract: Mechanical instabilities provide a general design principle for shaping developing organs and engineering soft materials. However, in slender structures, simple elastic buckling tends to erase rather than preserve shape complexity: structures relax to the simplest possible shape, erasing finer detail. Living systems nonetheless build complex, reproducible morphologies from continually remodeling material, while remaining robust to noise arising across scales. Using analytical theory and numerical simulations of a minimal model of growing visco-elasto-plastic rods, we show that remodeling plays two opposing roles: At low plasticity, patterns coarsen through elastic relaxation, while high plasticity converts fluctuations into geometric disorder. This sets a trade-off between shape complexity and reproducibility with an optimal intermediate plasticity, which protects initial patterns. Growth breaks this trade-off by suppressing both failure modes, enabling complex shapes to be reproducibly generated. Our results identify remodeling and growth rates as two knobs governing whether an encoded pattern is remembered, degraded, or transformed.

</details>


### [170] [Dynamically enabled transition pathways in multistable systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24070)
*Franco Nicolas Piñan Basualdo,Benjamin Gorissen*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究耗散耦合的双稳态元件网络在动态加载下的状态可达性。通过脉冲驱动，系统能够实现准静态加载下无法访问的跃迁路径，提高跃迁图连通性，并使单个迟滞单元可通过单标量输入独立寻址。实验使用气动迟滞单元验证了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准静态加载下，由双稳态（bistable）元件组成的系统的状态可达性受限于平衡结构，导致跃迁图（transition graph）稀疏且存在不可达的稳定态。作者旨在通过动态加载突破这一限制，提升系统的可达性与可控性。

Method: 作者引入耗散耦合（dissipative coupling）的迟滞子网络（hysteron network），采用脉冲驱动（pulse actuation）替代准静态加载，推导了脉冲振幅与时长到状态跃迁的控制映射（control map），并利用气动迟滞单元进行实验验证。

Result: 动态加载使准静态下被禁止的跃迁路径变得可行，跃迁图连通性增强，单个迟滞单元可通过单一标量输入独立寻址，从而访问原先不可达的状态。实验结果与理论预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于流体、机械和电气领域中耗散耦合的双稳态元件网络，为增强多稳态系统的状态可达性与可控性提供了一般性方法。

Abstract: Systems composed of interacting bistable elements are commonly described by transition graphs that determine which state changes are accessible under an external drive. Under quasistatic loading, accessibility is constrained by the equilibrium structure of the system, often resulting in sparse transition networks and unreachable stable states. Here, we show that dynamic loading of dissipatively-coupled hysteron networks enhances accessibility by enabling transition pathways that are forbidden under quasistatic driving while preserving the underlying equilibrium states. In particular, we consider pulse actuation and derive a control map linking pulse amplitude and duration to state transitions. For suitable dissipative couplings, individual hysterons become independently addressable using a single scalar input, increasing transition-graph connectivity and enabling access to otherwise unreachable states. We validate the framework experimentally using pneumatic hysterons and find good agreement with theory. More generally, the framework applies to dissipatively coupled networks of bistable elements across fluidic, mechanical, and electrical domains.

</details>


### [171] [Response-Selected Hidden Hyperuniformity in Hydrodynamic Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24102)
*Liyu Zhong,Yang Jiao*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了“响应选择超均匀性”（response-selected hyperuniformity）的概念，指出活性物质（active matter）中长波长有序并非单纯由密度或活性大小决定，而是由源-响应配对中横向驱动力（transverse forcing）所选择的活性力矩（active-moment）扇区决定。研究表明，局域中性簇筛选有符号活性力矩，产生二次消失的低波数谱；未筛选力矩导致红外平台，横向力谱呈现从四阶到六阶标度的普适交叉，且微观交换不必然破坏超均匀流有序，但稀有未筛选力矩决定了静流区的生存范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将活性物质中的超均匀性（hyperuniformity）视为预设密度场或连续场的一种属性，忽略了不可压缩流体（incompressible fluid）对不同微观力的非对称响应：纵向力（longitudinal forcing）被压力吸收，而只有横向力（transverse forcing）驱动流动。因此，需要一个新的框架来回答哪种活性力扇区被物理响应选择，而不仅仅是粒子排列是否均匀或总活性（total activity）是否小。

Method: 论文引入响应选择超均匀性（response-selected hyperuniformity）概念，将长波长有序定义为源-响应（source-response）配对的性质。通过分析可逆单价流体（reversible valence-one fluid）中局域中性簇对符号活性力矩扇区的筛选效应，研究一阶力矩谱在低波数区的二次消失行为，以及未筛选力矩产生的红外平台和横向力谱从四阶到六阶标度的普适交叉。

Result: 局域中性簇筛选有符号活性力矩扇区，使一阶力矩谱在低波数区二次消失；未筛选力矩则产生非零红外平台。横向力谱呈现从四阶到六阶标度的普适交叉，交叉位置由未筛选残差与筛选解析贡献的比值决定。残差为零时严格超均匀，有限残差导致缺陷控制的红外泄漏和有限筛选长度。

Conclusion: 微观交换（exchange）不必然破坏隐蔽的超均匀流有序，但稀有未筛选力矩（unscreened moments）决定静流区（quiet-flow regime）的生存范围。完全伙伴更新（complete partner renewal）保持规范形式，确立了可交换多极继承（exchangeable multipole inheritance），而周转（turnover）通过独立测量的局域缺陷密度（local defect density）调节残差。

Abstract: Hyperuniformity in active matter is usually treated as a property of a prescribed density or continuum field. This view misses a basic feature of hydrodynamic active matter: an incompressible fluid does not respond equally to every microscopic force. Longitudinal forcing is absorbed into pressure, whereas transverse forcing drives flow. The relevant question is therefore not only whether particles are uniformly arranged or whether the total activity is small, but which sector of the active forcing is selected by the physical response. Here we introduce response-selected hyperuniformity, in which long-wavelength order is a property of a source-response pair. In a reversible valence-one fluid with no prescribed partners, locally neutral clusters screen the signed active-moment sector that controls transverse flow, producing a first-moment spectrum that vanishes quadratically at low wavenumber. Locally unscreened moments instead generate a nonzero infrared plateau. The resulting transverse-force spectrum has a universal crossover from fourth- to sixth-order scaling, with the crossover set by the ratio of the unscreened residual to the screened analytic contribution. Complete partner renewal preserves this normal form, establishing exchangeable multipole inheritance, while turnover tunes the residual through an independently measured local defect density. The zero-residual limit yields strictly hyperuniform velocity fluctuations; any finite residual causes defect-controlled infrared leakage and sets a finite screening length. Thus microscopic exchange need not destroy hidden hyperuniform flow order, but rare unscreened moments determine how far the quiet-flow regime survives.

</details>


### [172] [Steady base states in a two-dimensional chiral fluid. The chiral Stokes cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24279)
*Francisco Vega Reyes*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文从第一性原理出发，建立了二维手性流体（chiral fluid）的流体力学（hydrodynamics）理论。通过强制执行角动量守恒（angular-momentum conservation）而不要求应力张量（stress tensor）对称，推导出应力张量与自旋通量（spin flux）的完整形式，并发现整个手性响应可通过一个单一物理操作——90°旋转——从经典牛顿流体产生。该理论将每个经典力学通道映射到其手性对应物（如压力到手性压力、剪切黏度到奇黏度等），并给出了稳态基态（如静止态、边界驱动流）的解析解，其预测可与气浮手性圆盘实验直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维手性流体（即携带净微观角动量/自旋场的流体）的流体力学尚未从第一性原理得到严格推导。现有文献多采用现象学框架，缺乏从角动量守恒基本定律出发的统一理论，且对手性响应与经典牛顿流体之间的内在关系认识不足。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，强制执行角动量守恒而不预设应力张量对称性，推导应力张量与自旋通量的完整形式。通过将经典牛顿流体力学中的物理量进行90°旋转操作，生成对应的手性通道。利用不可约（偏量）分解（deviatoric decomposition），将每个经典系数（压力、体黏度、剪切黏度、自旋通量梯度）映射到其手性对应物。对稳态基态（静止态、非均匀活性驱动流、边界驱动受限流）进行解析求解。

Result: 静止态由全纯手性复势（holomorphic chiral complex potential）描述，力学压力与手性压力构成共轭调和对，受拓扑存在条件约束。非均匀活性力驱动方位角流动。边界驱动的手性Stokes腔（chiral Stokes cavity）满足修正的亥姆霍兹-泊松系统（Helmholtz-Poisson system），在圆形域中给出闭式解，在方形域中给出数值解。一个无量纲群控制两种几何构型，决定从屏蔽单涡旋区到符号反转涡旋结构序列的交叉。理论恢复了文献中现象学框架作为特例。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了定量预测，可直接与气浮手性圆盘实验（López-Castaño et al. 2022）进行比较，并统一了现有手性流体文献中的现象学框架。通过将手性效应归结为经典牛顿流体的90°旋转操作，揭示了手性流体力学的物理本质。

Abstract: We develop from first principles the hydrodynamics of a two-dimensional chiral fluid, i.e. one carrying a
  net microscopic angular-momentum (spin) field. Enforcing angular-momentum conservation without imposing
  stress-tensor symmetry, we derive the full form of the stress tensor and of the spin flux, and we show that
  the entire chiral response is generated from the classical Newtonian one by a single operation of direct
  physical origin --- the $90^{\circ}$ rotation through which chirality acts, mirrored at the particle level by
  transverse forces (Caprini & Marini Bettolo Marconi 2025). Applied to the irreducible (deviatoric) decomposition, the rotation
  assigns to each classical channel a chiral partner --- pressure to chiral pressure, bulk and shear
  viscosities to their odd counterparts, the spin-flux gradient to its rotated image --- one coefficient and
  one mechanical action per channel, with no further cross-couplings. In this representation the steady base
  states become elementary. Quiescent states are organised by a holomorphic chiral complex potential, the
  mechanical and chiral pressures forming a conjugate harmonic pair subject to a topological existence
  condition; inhomogeneous activity forces azimuthal flows; and a boundary-driven confined flow, the
  chiral Stokes cavity, obeys a modified Helmholtz--Poisson system, solved in closed form in a
  circular domain and numerically in a square one. A single dimensionless group controls both geometries and
  sets the crossover from a screened, single-vortex regime to a sequence of sign-reversing vortical
  structures. The theory yields quantitative predictions, amenable to direct comparison with experiments on
  air-fluidised chiral disks (López-Castaño et al. 2022), and recovers the phenomenological frameworks of the
  chiral-fluid literature as particular cases.

</details>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Component structure and percolation in block models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20719)
*Riccardo Franchi,M. E. J. Newman*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文利用概率生成函数（probability generating functions）的精确方法，研究了随机块模型（stochastic block model）及其变体生成网络的组分结构与渗流（percolation）特性，推导了巨连通分支（giant component）大小、小分支分布、渗流簇大小及渗流阈值等关键表达式，并建立了微正则与正则块模型生成函数之间的映射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机块模型是网络社群结构研究中广泛使用的模型，但对其生成网络的组分结构和渗流性质尚缺乏基于精确方法的系统分析。

Method: 采用概率生成函数（probability generating functions）的精确方法来分析随机块模型及其度修正版本（degree-corrected versions）的网络结构；同时建立了微正则块模型（microcanonical block model）与正则块模型（canonical block model）之间的生成函数映射。

Result: 推导出巨连通分支大小、小分支分布、渗流簇大小及渗流阈值（percolation threshold）的表达式，涵盖节点渗流（node percolation）与边渗流（edge percolation）两种情形，并适用于原始随机块模型及其度修正变体。

Conclusion: 所开发的生成函数映射方法使得微正则块模型的结果可以轻松推广到正则块模型，从而以最小代价扩展了理论适用范围。

Abstract: The stochastic block model is a widely studied model of community structure in networks. Here we study the component structure and percolation properties of networks generated from this model and its variants, using exact methods based on probability generating functions. In particular, we derive expressions for the size of the giant component and the distribution of small components in such networks and for the size of the percolating cluster and position of the percolation threshold for both node and edge percolation, for the original stochastic block model and for its degree-corrected versions. In passing, we also develop a mapping between generating functions for microcanonical and canonical block models that allows us to generalize results for the former to the latter with minimal effort.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [AEcroscopyWave: Towards Self-Driving Characterization Platforms for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22975)
*Yongtao Liu,Jawad Chowdhury,Ganesh Narasimha,Ralph Bulanadi,Liam Collins,Ruben Millan Solsona,Marti Checa,Asraful Haque,Sumner B. Harris,Stephen Jesse,Rama Vasudevan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AEcroscopyWave——一个面向智能体AI时代（agentic-AI era）的自研表征平台，通过统一控制扫描探针显微镜（scanning probe microscopes）及可编程外围仪器，弥合了高通量自动化检测与人工专家驱动研究两类表征体制之间的鸿沟。平台设计兼顾系统性能的最大化与人类及AI智能体的易用性，并通过测试案例展示了异构科学仪器的可访问性、可组合性与智能体可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子材料表征传统上分为两类：工业级自动化系统（高通量但灵活性有限）与高度定制化、依赖人类专家的操作员驱动系统（带宽受限但具备研究级发现能力）。两类体制之间存在鸿沟，亟需一种能够融合高通量能力与自主科研发现潜力的桥梁方案。

Method: 通过构建AEcroscopyWave平台，统一控制扫描探针显微镜及可编程外围仪器；创建应用程序接口（API，application program interfaces）以控制硬件，并集成人工智能（AI）方法实现流程中的自主性（autonomy）。设计选择侧重于最大化系统能力并兼顾人类与AI智能体的易用性。

Result: 通过测试案例，展示了使异构科学仪器（heterogeneous scientific instruments）变得可访问、可组合且可被智能体使用的实际收益。

Conclusion: AEcroscopyWave平台成功桥接了电子材料表征中高通量自动化与专家驱动研究两种体制，为智能体AI时代的自驱动表征工具（self-driving characterization tools）提供了可行的实现路径。

Abstract: The characterization of electronic materials has traditionally been stratified into two distinct regimens: industry-scale automated systems to inspect materials for defects and ensure quality (such as in the semiconductor industry), and highly customized, operator-driven systems requiring human experts. The former offers high throughput but limited flexibility, whereas the latter is heavily bandwidth-limited but provides research-grade discovery capabilities. Recent advances in "self-driving" characterization tools offer the potential to bridge the two stratified regimes, by the creation of application program interfaces (APIs) that can control hardware, and the integration of AI methods to incorporate autonomy into the process. Here, we discuss our latest developments in AEcroscopyWave, a custom-built characterization platform for the agentic-AI era, that provides unified control of scanning probe microscopes with programmable peripheral instrumentation, highlighting the design choices that are necessary for maximizing the capability of the system and the ease of use for both human and AI agents. The benefits of making heterogeneous scientific instruments accessible, composable and usable by agents is demonstrated by test cases.

</details>


### [175] [Role of $p$-$d$ Hybridization on Optical Properties of Chalcopyrite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22992)
*Neunghee Han,Harang Kim,Minjae Kim,Woonhyuk Baek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和光学光谱实验，揭示了黄铜矿半导体量子点中价带p-d杂化（p-d hybridization）是决定光学相干性的关键机制：强p-d杂化（如CuInS₂）导致非相干光响应，而弱p-d杂化（如AgInS₂）则维持相干性，为设计具有相干光学性质的量子点提供了指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点（semiconductor quantum dots）有望通过限域效应、可调带隙和激子结合能来控制相干光学性质，但其固有的结构和组分不均匀性会降低光谱相干性，这是当前量子技术中一个亟待解决的核心障碍。

Method: 采用第一性原理电子结构计算（first-principles electronic-structure calculations）和光学光谱（optical spectroscopy）实验方法，对比研究CuInS₂与AgInS₂以及Ag₁₋ₓCuₓIn₁₋yGa_yS₂系列量子点中的p-d杂化效应。

Result: CuInS₂中强的p-d杂化诱导Cu(d)库仑散射通道（Coulomb scattering channel），产生非相干的光掺杂空穴载流子；AgInS₂中弱的p-d杂化则产生具有S(p)轨道特征的离域光掺杂空穴。在Ag₁₋ₓCuₓIn₁₋yGa_yS₂量子点中，Cu比例增加会增强p-d杂化，激活Cu(d)库仑散射，导致非相干光学响应。

Conclusion: 要实现半导体量子点的相干光学光谱，应避免光掺杂载流子具有p-d杂化的轨道特征（orbital character）。该结果为设计具有相干光学性质的量子材料提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Designing quantum materials for coherent optical properties is a central agenda in quantum technology. Semiconductor quantum dots are an emerging approach for controlling coherent optical properties via confinement effects, tunable band gaps, and exciton binding energies, yet their inherent structural and compositional inhomogeneity degrades the coherence of the optical spectra, posing a major obstacle. We show that, for chalcopyrite semiconductors, hybridization between transition-metal $d$ and ligand $p$ electrons in the valence band is key to the coherence of the quantum dot optical spectrum. We demonstrate this using first-principles electronic-structure calculations and optical spectroscopy. The strong $p$-$d$ hybridization in CuInS$_{2}$ induces the Cu($d$) Coulomb scattering channel, giving rise to the incoherent photodoped hole carrier, while the weak $p$-$d$ hybridization in AgInS$_{2}$ induces the delocalized photodoped hole carrier having a predominant S($p$) orbital character. Our experimental results on optical spectra suggest that when the Cu ratio is enhanced in the Ag$_{1-x}$Cu$_{x}$In$_{1-y}$Ga$_{y}$S$_{2}$ quantum dot, Cu atoms at both Ag sites and defect sites experience enhanced $p$-$d$ hybridization, and a coupling begins to develop between the electrons in the quantum dot and the defect electrons at a small Cu ratio. This coupling activates Cu($d$) Coulomb scattering for photodoped holes traversing the defect sites, producing an incoherent optical response that naturally explains the long-standing absence of band-edge spectral signatures in CuIn$_{1-y}$Ga$_y$S$_2$ quantum dots. These results serve as a guideline for designing semiconductor quantum dots. To achieve a coherent optical spectrum, avoid $p$-$d$-hybridized orbital character in the photo-doped carrier.

</details>


### [176] [Uncertainty-quantified $J$-integral computation for quasicontinuum and finite element methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23003)
*Sai Harshith Badi,Stephen M. Whalen,Ronald E. Miller,Ellad B. Tadmor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在三维准连续（QC3D）方法中严格实现并验证了J积分，利用Cauchy-Born规则从原子间势导出连续场，并在三个不同验证体制下与线弹性断裂力学（LEFM）理论及虚拟裂纹扩展（VCE）方法取得优异一致性；同时引入马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）框架量化不确定性，并通过硅的三点弯曲实验展示了预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J积分是断裂力学（fracture mechanics）中量化裂纹扩展驱动力（能量释放率）的核心概念，虽已在有限元（finite element, FE）和原子尺度计算中广泛实现，但在桥接原子与连续统描述的多尺度框架（multiscale frameworks）中的应用尚未被探索。

Method: 在三维准连续（quasicontinuum, QC3D）方法中，基于平面应变假设，利用Cauchy-Born规则（Cauchy-Born rule）从原子间势（interatomic potential）导出连续场（应力、应变能密度），实现J积分计算；并引入马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）框架，对给定网格和积分域下的J积分结果进行统计不确定性量化。

Result: 在三个验证体制下均与LEFM理论和VCE方法取得优异一致性：小应变线弹性（施加各向异性K场位移）、经非线性Cauchy-Born本构关系评估相同场（无原子弛豫）、以及启用原子弛豫使裂纹尖端区域达到平衡。针对硅的三点弯曲试验，计算的临界能量释放率与Griffith准则（Griffith criterion）吻合良好。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了在多尺度断裂模拟中可靠评估裂纹驱动力（crack driving forces）且具备量化不确定性的框架，能够在解析裂纹尖端原子机制的同时实现大规模断裂模拟。

Abstract: The $J$-integral is a fundamental concept in fracture mechanics, quantifying the energy release rate that drives crack propagation. While extensively implemented in finite element (FE) codes and adapted for atomistic calculations, its application within multiscale frameworks bridging atomistic and continuum formulations remains unexplored. This work presents a rigorous implementation and validation of the $J$-integral within the three-dimensional quasicontinuum (QC3D) method, computed under plane strain assumptions, using continuum fields (stress, strain energy density) derived from the interatomic potential via the Cauchy-Born rule. The implementation is validated against linear elastic fracture mechanics (LEFM) theory and the virtual crack extension (VCE) method across three regimes: (1) small-strain linear elasticity, with a prescribed anisotropic $K$-field displacement applied throughout; (2) the same field evaluated through the nonlinear Cauchy-Born constitutive relation, without atomic relaxation; and (3) the same relation with atomic relaxation enabled, allowing the crack-tip region to equilibrate. Excellent agreement is shown throughout. We further introduce a Markov chain Monte Carlo framework to statistically quantify the uncertainty of $J$-integral results for a given mesh and integration domain, applicable to conventional FE methods as well. Predictive capability is demonstrated via a QC3D simulation of a three-point bending test of silicon, where the computed critical energy release rate agrees closely with the Griffith criterion. This work establishes a reliable framework for evaluating crack driving forces in multiscale fracture simulations with quantified uncertainty, enabling large-scale fracture simulations while resolving atomistic mechanisms at the crack tip.

</details>


### [177] [Ferroelastic exciton splitting in hybrid perovskite nanowalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23051)
*Afreen,J. Delgado-Alvarez,H. Krishna Mishra,J. Castillo-Seoane,Koustav Maiti,Pravrati Taank,A. Borras,A. Barranco,Surajit Saha,Priya Mahadevan,J. R. Sanchez-Valencia,K. V. Adarsh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高度取向的MAPbI3纳米墙实验，结合温度依赖光致发光、X射线衍射和偏振分辨超快瞬态吸收光谱，发现铁弹织构与相共存可以通过激子-晶格耦合（exciton-lattice coupling）修饰激子态，导致从各向异性分裂激子到晶格修饰态（lattice-dressed states）的演化，为软极性半导体中对称性选择性光学响应提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合金属卤化物钙钛矿是软半导体，其电子激发受晶格畸变和结构相变强烈影响，但铁弹对称性破缺（ferroelastic symmetry breaking）是仅展宽光学共振，还是通过激子-晶格耦合（exciton-lattice coupling）修饰激子态，仍是一个未解决的重要问题。

Method: 采用斜角沉积（glancing-angle deposition）制备高度取向的MAPbI3纳米墙，实现铁弹织构（ferroelastic texture）、结构各向异性与明确光学轴之间的对称性选择性耦合，并联合温度依赖光致发光（photoluminescence）、X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）和偏振分辨超快瞬态吸收光谱（polarization-resolved ultrafast transient absorption spectroscopy）进行表征。

Result: 在5 K正交相中观察到偏振选择性激子分裂，具有正交光学选择规则和45 meV能量分离；在约160 K的正交-四方相共存区，出现能量低于各向异性分裂激子中心58 meV的晶格耦合激发；高温下激发逐渐获得晶格修饰特征，光学各向异性降低。

Conclusion: 铁弹织构（ferroelastic texture）和相共存（phase coexistence）能够修饰激子-晶格耦合，为在软极性半导体中实现对称性选择性光学响应提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Hybrid metal-halide perovskites are soft semiconductors in which electronic excitations are strongly influenced by lattice distortions and structural phase transitions. An important open question is whether ferroelastic symmetry breaking merely broadens optical resonances or instead modifies excitonic states through exciton-lattice coupling. Here, we address this question using highly aligned MAPbI3 nanowalls fabricated by glancing-angle deposition, enabling symmetry-selective coupling between ferroelastic texture, structural anisotropy, and a well-defined optical axis. Combining temperature-dependent photoluminescence, X-ray diffraction and polarization-resolved ultrafast transient absorption spectroscopy, we observe a polarization-selective excitonic splitting in the orthorhombic phase at 5 K, characterized by orthogonal optical selection rules and a 45 meV energy separation. Near 160 K, where orthorhombic and tetragonal phases coexist, a lower-energy lattice-coupled excitation emerges 58 meV below the centre of the anisotropically split excitonic structure, consistent with coupling between excitonic and lattice-dressed states. At higher temperatures, these excitations progressively acquire lattice-dressed character accompanied by reduced optical anisotropy. A symmetry-guided effective Hamiltonian captures the evolution from anisotropically split excitons to coupled excitonic and lattice-dressed states across the structural transition. Our results show that ferroelastic texture and phase coexistence can modify exciton-lattice coupling, providing a route to symmetry-selective optical responses in soft polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [178] [Hydrogen-Induced Sign Reversal in Magnetic Hysteresis Evolution of CoPd Alloys and Co/Pd Multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23053)
*S. S. Das,A. Gerber*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过反常霍尔效应（EHE）系统研究氢在CoPd合金及多层膜纳米结构中的吸氢行为，发现氢对磁滞回线的影响高度依赖于成分和层厚，源于Pd相关电子效应与磁弹各向异性（magnetoelastic anisotropy）之间的竞争，并在超薄多层膜中观察到界面磁连通性和应变介导的磁弹各向异性的主导作用，以及EHE极性的氢致翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢吸收可调控磁性薄膜纳米结构的电子、磁性和输运性质，但成分与纳米结构化对电子效应和晶格应变这两种竞争机制的影响尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究以理解其微观物理机制。

Method: 采用反常霍尔效应（extraordinary Hall effect, EHE）表征，在空气和4% H₂/N₂混合气氛下，对CoₓPd₁₀₀₋ₓ合金、[Co(0.1 nm)/Pd(d)]₁₅和[Co(0.2 nm)/Pd(d)]₁₅多层膜进行氢致磁滞回线变化的系统测量。

Result: 氢致响应不具有普适性，强烈依赖成分和层厚。富Pd的CoPd合金和Co(0.2 nm)/Pd多层膜中，低Co含量下氢先收缩磁滞回线，x~40%以上后回线扩张；超薄[Co(0.1 nm)/Pd(d)]₁₅多层膜中，氢诱发弱但非单调的扩张行为，并在交叉区域观察到EHE环路极性的氢致翻转。

Conclusion: 氢可通过成分调控的电子效应和磁弹效应选择性调节CoPd纳米结构的磁性，为氢响应自旋电子学（spintronics）和传感器件设计提供物理基础。

Abstract: Hydrogen absorption in magnetic thin film nanostructures can modulate their electronic, magnetic, and transport properties by modifying the electronic structure and lattice strain. However, the influence of composition and nanostructuring on these two competing effects is not well understood. We systematically investigate hydrogen-induced magnetic hysteresis in Co$_x$Pd$_{100-x}$ alloys, [Co(0.1 nm)/Pd(d)]$_{15}$, and [Co(0.2 nm)/Pd(d)]$_{15}$ multilayers, using extraordinary Hall effect characterizations (EHE) in air and 4% H$_2$/N$_2$ mixture. We show that the hydrogen-induced response is not universal, but depends strongly on composition and layer thickness. This reflects competition between Pd-related electronic effects and magnetoelastic anisotropy. In Pd-rich CoPd alloys and Co(0.2 nm)/Pd multilayers, hydrogen initially contracts the hysteresis loops at low Co fractions, followed by loop expansion above x ~ 40%. This contrast results from competition between suppression of Pd-induced magnetization through Pd-4d band filling and hydrogen-driven anisotropic strain that strengthens magnetoelastic anisotropy in Co-rich samples. In contrast, in ultrathin [Co(0.1 nm)/Pd(d)]$_{15}$ multilayers, hydrogen induces a weak, non-monotonic but generally expanding loop behaviour across x = 15-60%, indicating a dominant role of interfacial magnetic connectivity and strain-mediated magnetoelastic anisotropy in the ultrathin limit. Furthermore, we observe a hydrogen-induced reversal of the EHE loop polarity near the crossover regime, reflecting a change in the dominant EHE scattering mechanisms, thus providing an additional degree of magnetic tunability by hydrogen. These results demonstrate that hydrogen can selectively tune the magnetism of CoPd nanostructures via composition-controlled electronic and magnetoelastic effects, offering insights for hydrogen-responsive spintronic and sensing devices.

</details>


### [179] [Imaging of van der Waals Materials via Standing-Wave Photoemission Microscopy: Depth-Resolved Electronic Structure of WS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23073)
*J. R. Paudel,R. Muzzio,M. E. Matzelle,S. Sheikh,A. Fadul,A. Tiwari,F. Salmassi,E. Gullikson,K. M. McCreary,B. T. Jonker,J. Nieminen,C. M. Schneider,F. Kronast,A. Bansil,J. Katoch,A. X. Gray*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了驻波光电子发射电子显微镜（SW-PEEM）技术，可在埃（Å）尺度上对单层WS₂进行深度分辨的光谱显微分析，通过X射线驻波调谐获得化学深度分布与价带调制，并观测到顶部与底部硫层之间约0.2 eV的价带谱权重偏移，该偏移与硫相关表面物种有关而非直接基底杂化，从而确立了SW-PEEM作为一种非破坏性深度分辨探针，可用于范德华（van der Waals）和莫尔（moiré）体系中的界面耦合与化学重构研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华（van der Waals）材料在电子、光电和量子技术中具有广阔前景，但其深度分辨（depth-resolved）表征仍然面临挑战，亟需一种能够非破坏性地获取材料垂直方向化学与电子结构信息的方法。

Method: 本研究采用驻波光电子发射电子显微镜（standing-wave photoemission electron microscopy, SW-PEEM），在W/C多层基底上的单层WS₂中，通过调谐X射线驻波穿过单层，实现埃（Ångstrom）尺度的光谱显微分析，并结合X射线光学建模确定结构与场分布。

Result: SW-PEEM获得了单层WS₂的化学深度分布与价带调制，对顶部和底部硫层具有增强灵敏度；测量发现，在分别增强顶部与底部硫层灵敏度的条件下，硫衍生的价带谱权重存在约0.2 eV的偏移；该偏移不太可能源于直接的强基底杂化，而是与硫相关表面物种一致，并得到代表性元素硫模型计算的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了SW-PEEM作为一种非破坏性深度分辨探针，突显了其在探测范德华（van der Waals）和莫尔（moiré）体系中的界面耦合（interfacial coupling）、化学重构（chemical reconstruction）及涌现态（emergent states）方面的潜力。

Abstract: Two-dimensional van der Waals materials promise electronic, optoelectronic, and quantum technologies, yet depth-resolved characterization remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate standing-wave photoemission electron microscopy (SW-PEEM) for Angstrom-scale spectromicroscopy of monolayer WS2 on a W/C multilayer substrate. Tuning the X-ray standing wave through the monolayer yields a chemical depth profile and valence-band modulation with enhanced sensitivity to the top and bottom sulfur layers. X-ray optical modeling determines the structure and field distribution. The measurements reveal an ~0.2 eV shift in sulfur-derived valence-band spectral weight between measurements with enhanced sensitivity to the top and bottom sulfur layers. This shift is unlikely to arise from strong direct substrate hybridization and is instead consistent with sulfur-related surface species, as supported by calculations using a representative elemental-sulfur model. These results establish SW-PEEM as a non-destructive depth-resolved probe, highlighting its potential to probe interfacial coupling, chemical reconstruction, and emergent states in van der Waals and moiré systems.

</details>


### [180] [A DFT and Machine Learning-Assisted Study on the Lattice Thermal Conductivity of LiCdSb for Thermoelectric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23203)
*R. Zosiamliana,Lalhriat Zuala,N. T. Tien,Vo Khuong Dien,A. Laref,D. P. Rai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）结合玻尔兹曼输运方程，并采用混合泛函HSE06与机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）分别计算电子性能与晶格热导率，分析了LiCdSb的热电性质，发现其在高温下热电优值（ZT）超过1，表明该材料适用于高温能量转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确计算电子输运性质需要精确的能带结构，而晶格热导率（Kl）是评估热电性能的关键参数，但其理论计算复杂且计算资源需求巨大，因此需要寻找高效替代方法。

Method: 使用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）和玻尔兹曼输运方程计算电子与热电性质；采用混合泛函HSE06进行精确带隙计算；利用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）替代传统方法计算晶格热导率（Kl）。

Result: 室温下Kl=0.24 Wm^-1K^-1，与已有理论和实验数据定性一致；基于Slack+TDEC估算的ZT（优值，figure of merit）为0.18，机器学习模型估算的ZT为0.17，均与实验报道值0.10吻合；在600K以上ZT值超过1。

Conclusion: LiCdSb在高温下ZT值超过基准值1，使其成为一种有前景的高温能量转换热电材料。

Abstract: By using first-principles density functional theory (DFT) and the Boltzmann transport equation, we have calculated the corresponding electronic and thermoelectric properties of LiCdSb. For calculating electron transport properties, accurate band-structure estimation is crucial. Hence, for the precise band gap calculation, we have implemented a hybrid functional HSE06, which is widely known for its high accuracy. To evaluate the thermoelectric performance of a material, the calculation of lattice thermal conductivity (Kl) is a key parameter. However, from a theoretical perspective, the calculation of lattice thermal conductivity is very complex and demands huge computational resources. Therefore, in this work, we have opted for an alternative method of machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for the calculation of Kl. Our result of Kl=0.24 Wm^-1K^-1 at room temperature is in qualitative agreement with the available theoretical and experimental data. The figure of merit (ZT) with Kl estimated from Slack+TDEC ZT is 0.18 at 300 K, and machine learning (ML) models ZT is 0.17 at 300K, combining with HSE06-based electronic transport properties agreed well with the available experimentally reported value of ZT is 0.10 at 300K. However, we report the ZT value well above the benchmark value of 1 beyond 600K. The ZT value exceeding 1 at higher temperatures makes LiCdSb a promising material for high-temperature energy conversion.

</details>


### [181] [Stoichiometric cluster learning for few-shot property prediction of multi-ionic integrated energetic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23208)
*Ming-Yu Guo,Wei-Jia Zou,Yu Shang,Wei-Xiong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）和多任务微调（MT-FT）的策略，利用化学计量离子团簇表示（非周期性、保持化学计量比的配方单元团簇）绕过完整晶体结构预测，实现数据稀缺的多离子材料（以多离子集成炸药MIXs为例）的合成前筛选。该方法仅需25个结构精选的钙钛矿型含能材料（PEMs）即可获得良好预测性能，并成功推广至三种新合成的ABX₄材料，平均绝对误差（MAE）为92 m·s⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多离子材料（multi-ionic materials）因其带电构建单元如何聚集形成特定组装体的复杂性，给机器学习驱动的材料设计带来了独特的表征挑战。传统方法难以在缺乏完整晶体结构信息的情况下对候选材料进行有效预筛选。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 化学计量离子团簇表示（stoichiometric ionic-cluster representation），即用非周期性但保持化学计量比的配方单元团簇表示每种候选材料；(2) 多任务微调（multi-task fine-tuning, MT-FT），在保持能量-力目标作为物理正则化的同时，适配预训练原子级骨干网络以拟合稀疏的爆速标签。

Result: 在仅25个结构精选的钙钛矿型含能材料（PEMs）上完成交叉验证筛选。表征探针表明学习到的描述符隐式保留了位点感知的离子组织、密度信息和粗略堆积兼容性。该方法成功推广至三种新合成的ABX₄材料（包含未见过的ABX₄化学计量比和未见过的乙二铵B位阳离子），与Kamlet–Jacobs（K–J）参考爆速的三点一致，MAE为92 m·s⁻¹且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了基于化学计量保持的团簇学习作为一种面向合成前筛选的策略，适用于数据稀缺的多离子材料体系。

Abstract: Multi-ionic materials pose a distinct representational challenge in machine learning-driven materials design. Different from single-molecule or composition-based materials, their properties arise from how charged building blocks aggregate into specific assemblies. Here, we show how pretrained machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) can bypass full crystal-structure prediction and support pre-synthesis screening from stoichiometric ionic clusters using multi-ionic integrated explosives (MIXs) as a synthesis-facing example. This strategy combines a stoichiometric ionic-cluster representation, which represents each candidate material by a non-periodic, stoichiometry-preserved formula-unit cluster, with multi-task fine-tuning (MT-FT), which adapts a pretrained atomistic backbone while retaining the energy--force objective as physical regularization for the sparse detonation-velocity labels. With the pretrained backbone regularized by MT-FT, this surrogate provides a cross-validated screen across only 25 structurally curated perovskite-type energetic materials (PEMs) with experimentally derived Kamlet--Jacobs (K--J) detonation velocities. Representation probes show that the learned descriptors implicitly retain site-aware ionic organization, density information, and coarse packing compatibility, implying why non-periodic clusters can remain predictive before full crystal structures are known. The surrogate extends known PEMs chemistry to three newly synthesized ABX$_4$ materials with both unseen ABX$_4$ stoichiometry and an unseen ethylenediammonium B-site cation, yielding three-point concordance with K--J reference velocities and a mean absolute error (MAE) of 92~m$\cdot$s$^{-1}$ without retraining. Together, these results establish stoichiometry-preserved cluster learning as a synthesis-facing screening strategy for data-scarce multi-ionic materials.

</details>


### [182] [Fermi-level mediated acceleration of flash sintering of oxide ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23383)
*Qin-Kun Li,Evgeni S. Penev,Boris I. Yakobson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算揭示了闪烧（flash sintering, FS）过程中电荷补偿如何改变能带结构以加速离子迁移的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管闪烧在材料加工中高效且通用，但其原子尺度机理仍停留在推测阶段，缺乏明确的物理图像。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算，以钇稳定立方氧化锆（Y-stabilized cubic ZrO₂, YSZ）为模型材料，系统分析多种缺陷的电荷补偿行为及费米能级（Fermi level, E_F）的演化。

Result: 闪烧过程中，非化学计量缺陷的电荷平衡使Zr空位（V_Zr^q）的体扩散势垒从闪烧前的V_Zr^0降低约2 eV至闪烧时的V_Zr^{-4}；Y_ZrV_OY_Zr缺陷复合体中O空位（V_O）湮灭释放的替代缺陷Y_Zr在闪烧前作为电子受体稳定V_Zr^0，而闪烧起始时热激发的过量V_O作为电子施主上移E_F，从而支持V_Zr^{-4}的形成。

Conclusion: 提出了费米能级介导的阳离子扩散机制，该机制可推广至其他闪烧陶瓷体系，对氧化物陶瓷闪烧技术的通用理论具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The atomistic understanding of flash sintering (FS) remains speculative, despite its efficiency and versatility in materials processing. Employing first-principles calculations we demonstrate how charge compensation of a range of defects in the prototypical Y-stabilized cubic ZrO$_2$ (YSZ) shifts Fermi level E$_F$ up during FS, thereby accelerating cation migration for fast mass transport. The charge transition of Zr vacancy, V$_{Zr}^q$, reduces its bulk diffusion barrier in V$_{Zr}^{-4}$ during flash by 2 eV, relative to V$_{Zr}^0$ before flash, which is triggered by the charge equilibrium of nonstoichiometric defects. The substituent defect Y$_{Zr}$, released by annihilating O vacancy, V$_O$, in Y$_{Zr}$V$_O$Y$_{Zr}$ defect complex, acts as electron acceptor and favors V$_{Zr}^0$ before flash whereas excess V$_O$, as electron donor thermally generated at the FS onset, upshift E$_F$ and thus support V$_{Zr}^{-4}$. The proposed mechanism of Fermi-level mediated cation diffusion for YSZ is generalized to other flash-sintered ceramics and has considerable bearing on the general theory of FS techniques in oxide ceramics.

</details>


### [183] [Tailoring the Frequency-Dependent Optical Response of Hematite through Mono- and Co-Doping: A First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23409)
*Abdul Ahad Mamun,Muhammad Anisuzzaman Talukder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算，系统研究了B掺杂、Y掺杂及(B, Y)共掺杂对赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）晶格动力学稳定性与光学性质的影响，发现B掺杂会引发晶格不稳定性（虚频声子模式），而Y共掺杂可抑制该不稳定性并恢复结构稳定性；同时揭示了掺杂对介电函数、光吸收谱及能带结构的调控机制，为光电子和光子技术应用提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂对半导体材料晶体结构与光学性质的影响是发展下一代半导体与光子技术的关键，然而关于掺杂赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）中动力学稳定性（dynamical stability）与光学性质之间关系的研究尚不充分，亟需系统性的理论探索。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算，同时评估了B掺杂、Y掺杂及(B, Y)共掺杂α-Fe₂O₃的声子色散（phonon dispersion）特性和频率依赖的光学响应（frequency-dependent optical response）；通过分析有限温度振动性质、介电函数（dielectric function）和光学特征来理解晶格动力学与光-物质相互作用（light-matter interactions）。

Result: 纯相与Y掺杂赤铁矿保持动力学稳定，而B掺杂赤铁矿因Fe–O骨架畸变出现虚频声子模式（imaginary phonon modes），表明晶格不稳定性；Y与B共掺杂可通过晶格弛豫（lattice relaxation）和改善原子间作用力抑制软模（soft modes），恢复结构稳定性。B掺杂在价带引入附加态增强低能吸收，Y掺杂改变轨道杂化（orbital hybridization）导致更宽的色散。掺杂赤铁矿在2 eV以下表现主导的带间跃迁，并在1.80 eV至4 eV区间呈现强吸收，(B, Y)共掺杂结合了低能优势与改善的光学响应轮廓。

Conclusion: 掺杂显著增强了晶格振动、光-物质相互作用和光学响应，为通过掺杂策略调控赤铁矿性质以应用于光活性、光电子和光子技术提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Understanding the effects of doping on the crystal structure and optical properties of semiconductor materials is crucial for advancing next-generation semiconductor and photonic technologies. Although various studies have focused on doped hematite ($α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$), the relationship between dynamical stability and optical properties remains insufficiently explored. This study presents a comprehensive first-principles investigation that simultaneously evaluates the phonon dispersion characteristics and frequency-dependent optical response of B-doped, Y-doped, and (B, Y)-co-doped $α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$, providing deeper insights into the underlying mechanisms. We examined the finite-temperature vibrational properties, dielectric function, and optical characteristics to comprehend the lattice dynamics and light-matter interactions under electromagnetic radiation. Vibrational thermodynamics reveal that pristine and Y-doped hematite maintain dynamic stability, while B-doped hematite exhibits imaginary phonon modes indicating lattice instability due to distortions in the Fe--O framework. Notably, Y co-doping with B helps suppress these soft modes, restoring structural stability through lattice relaxation and improved interatomic forces. B doping enhances low-energy absorption by introducing additional states in the valence band, while Y doping alters orbital hybridization, leading to a broader dispersion. In the optical regime, doped hematite displays dominant interband transitions below $2$ eV and strong absorption between 1.80 eV and 4 eV. The (B, Y) co-doping combines the low-energy benefits with an improved optical response profile. In summary, doping significantly enhances lattice vibrations, light-matter interactions, and optical responses, providing an effective strategy for tailoring hematite for diverse applications in photoactive, optoelectronic, and photonic technologies.

</details>


### [184] [Fe-doping-induced band structure modification and cryogenic phase stability in Cs2AgBiBr6 single crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23456)
*Yanan Li,Xuejiao Wu,Jidong Deng,Jinbao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过铁（Fe）掺杂策略，利用改进水热法合成Cs₂AgBi₀.₅Fe₀.₅Br₆单晶，在保持结构相变的前提下同时实现高温相带隙窄化和低温结构不稳定性抑制，提升了该材料在光电子与自旋电子学中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cs₂AgBiBr₆虽被视为无铅替代材料，但其宽带隙（wide band gap）与有害本征缺陷（intrinsic defects）严重限制了其在光电子（optoelectronics）领域的实际应用，亟需有效的调控手段。

Method: 采用改进水热法（modified hydrothermal method）合成了Fe掺杂的Cs₂AgBi₀.₅Fe₀.₅Br₆单晶。

Result: 纯相与Fe掺杂晶体均在125 K附近发生结构相变（structural phase transition），但Fe掺杂从根本上改变了相变的影响：一方面窄化了高温相的带隙，另一方面抑制了低温结构不稳定性（cryogenic structural instability）。

Conclusion: 光学与X射线结构研究证实，Fe掺杂是一种有效的性能调控策略，可推动Cs₂AgBiBr₆在高效低温光电子与自旋电子（spintronic）器件中的应用。

Abstract: Despite its promise as a lead-free alternative, the practical application of Cs2AgBiBr6 in optoelectronics is limited by its wide band gap and detrimental intrinsic defects. To overcome these challenges, we synthesized Cs2AgBi0.5Fe0.5Br6 single crystals via a modified hydrothermal method. While both pristine and Fe-doped crystals undergo a structural phase transition near 125 K, Fe incorporation fundamentally alters its impact. The dopant simultaneously narrows the band gap in the high-temperature phase and suppresses the associated cryogenic structural instability. Our optical and X-ray structural studies establish Fe doping as a powerful strategy for tailoring the properties of Cs2AgBiBr6 , advancing its potential for high-performance, low-temperature optoelectronic and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [185] [Molecular dynamics with a first-principles-validated universal machine-learning potential reveals dynamic elementary processes of growth-related adspecies on GaN(0001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23461)
*Yoshito Takaesu,Akira Kusaba,Junko Ishii,Shigenori Matsushima,Yoshihiro Kangawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过将第一性原理分子动力学（FPMD）与通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）UMA 相结合，追踪了 GaN(0001) 表面上生长相关吸附物种在纯第一性原理方法无法企及的时间尺度上的动力学行为。研究发现了一种此前未被认识的扩散模式，揭示了 H 原子在吸附位点间的跳跃、GaNH 单元的可逆解离等新现象，这代表了机器学习原子间势在 GaN 金属有机气相外延（MOVPE）分子动力学中的首次应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对 GaN 金属有机气相外延（MOVPE）表面基元过程的原子尺度理解局限于静态密度泛函理论（DFT）能量学以及仅能模拟数十皮秒的第一性原理分子动力学（FPMD），无法探索更长时间尺度上的动力学行为。

Method: 将第一性原理分子动力学（FPMD）与通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）UMA 相结合。首先运行 FPMD 模拟 GaNH 吸附分子与 H 吸附原子共存体系，然后在 FPMD 快照上执行单点 UMA 计算以验证能量一致性，最后开展基于 MLIP 的长时分子动力学（150 ps）模拟。

Result: FPMD 揭示了一种新的扩散模式：GaNH 单元中的 N 原子将 Ga 原子从表面层抬起，被抬起的 Ga 进一步从表面抽取一个 H 吸附原子，这些事件在静态 DFT 中不可见。UMA 在 FPMD 快照上的单点计算无需重新训练即可再现第一性原理相对能量（均方根误差约 8.5 meV/原子）。长时 MLIP 分子动力学揭示了 H 原子在吸附位点间的跳跃、生长单元迁移路径的门控效应，以及 GaNH 单元可逆解离为独立迁移的 Ga 和 NH 吸附物种等新动力学行为。

Conclusion: 本文代表了机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在 GaN 金属有机气相外延（MOVPE）分子动力学中的首次应用，证明了该组合方法能够发现纯第一性原理方法无法捕捉的表面动力学过程，为理解 MOVPE 生长机理提供了新的原子尺度视角。

Abstract: Atomic-scale understanding of the surface elementary processes in metalorganic vapor phase epitaxy (MOVPE) of GaN has so far relied on static density-functional-theory (DFT) energetics and on first-principles molecular dynamics (FPMD) limited to a few tens of picoseconds. Here we combine FPMD with a universal machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP), UMA, to follow the dynamics of growth-related adspecies on GaN(0001) over time scales inaccessible to purely first-principles approaches. FPMD simulations of a GaNH admolecule coexisting with H adatoms reveal a hitherto unrecognized diffusion mode, in which the N atom lifts the Ga atom of the GaNH unit off the surface layer during migration, and show that the lifted Ga abstracts an H adatom from the surface, events invisible to static DFT. Single-point UMA calculations on FPMD snapshots reproduce the first-principles relative energies along the trajectory (RMSE of about 8.5 meV/atom) without any retraining. Long-time MLIP-based MD (150 ps) then reveals dynamics never observed within the FPMD window: site-to-site H-adatom hopping, which gates the migration paths of the growth unit, and reversible dissociation of the GaNH unit into independently migrating Ga and NH adspecies. This work constitutes, to our knowledge, the first application of an MLIP to the molecular dynamics of GaN MOVPE.

</details>


### [186] [Universal temperature-dependent electrical resistivity in actinides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23484)
*E. F. Talantsev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对锕系元素（actinides）电阻率随温度变化的实验数据长期缺乏统一理论解释的问题，提出了一个基于双平行导电通道的简单模型，将Bloch-Grüneisen方程（描述经典电子-声子耗散机制）与Arrhenius方程（描述最近邻跳跃导电）结合，成功拟合了从钍到锔八种元素相的实验ρ(T)数据，并应用于δ相钚基合金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测得的锕系纯金属电阻率-温度曲线之间差异极大，缺乏统一的理论方法来理解和拟合其ρ(T)数据；第一性原理计算的结果与实验数据甚至存在定性上的差异，亟需一个简单有效的解析模型来破解这一长期难题。

Method: 提出一个基于双平行导电通道的模型：一条通道由Bloch-Grüneisen方程描述（对应经典电子-声子散射耗散机制），另一条通道由Arrhenius方程描述（对应最近邻跳跃导电机制），将两者并联拟合实验ρ(T)数据。

Result: 模型成功拟合了从钍（Th）到锔（Cm）共八种元素相的电阻率数据；由此导出的德拜温度Θ_D与通过比热容测量报告的值吻合良好；在镎（Np）中提取出所有锕系元素中最大的Arrhenius活化能E_a=15.9 meV；模型还成功应用于δ相钚基合金Pu-Ce和Pu-Ce-Ga的ρ(T)数据。

Conclusion: 该双通道模型能够准确拟合多种锕系元素相及钚基合金的电阻率数据，导出的德拜温度与独立热容测量结果一致，表明该模型为理解和分析锕系元素电阻率数据提供了一种统一且有效的理论工具。

Abstract: Temperature-dependent electrical resistivity $ρ(T)$ is one of the most common types of experimental data analyzed in condensed matter physics. For one group of pure metals, the actinides, experimental $ρ(T)$ curves differ radically from one another to the point that there is no unified theoretical approach to understanding and fitting $ρ(T)$ data in these elements. First-principles calculations result in $ρ(T)$ curves that differ from experimental data, even qualitatively. In an attempt to unravel this long-standing problem, here I propose a simple model that accurately fits the $ρ(T)$ data for eight phases of elemental actinides (from thorium (Th) to curium (Cm)) for which experimental data are publicly available to date. The model is based on the concept of two parallel conduction channels: one is described by the Bloch-Grüneisen equation, which is associated with the classical electron-phonon dissipation mechanism, and the other by the Arrhenius equation, which is associated with the nearest-neighbor hopping (NNH) conductivity. Debye temperatures $Θ_D$ derived from application of the model to $ρ(T)$ data for eight elemental actinide phases agree well with reported values deduced from heat capacity measurements. For neptunium (Np) a maximum Arrhenius activation energy (among all actinides) of $E_a=15.9$ $meV$ was derived. The model was also successfully applied to $ρ(T)$ data measured on $δ$-phase plutonium-based alloys Pu-Ce and Pu-Ce-Ga.

</details>


### [187] [Observation of correlation-driven topological transport and robust ferromagnetism in 2D CrS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23625)
*Sk Md Obaidulla,M. Nur Hasan,Dayal Das,Rafiqul Alam,Antonio Supina,Muhammad Awais Aslam,Sherif Kamal,Iva Šarić Jankovic,Aleksandar Matkovic,Christian Teichert,Atindra Nath Pal,Heike C. Herper,Marko Kralj*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次通过无催化剂化学气相沉积法生长出层状 1T-CrS₂，发现其是一种具有面外易轴各向异性、居里温度高于室温的稳定范德华铁磁体。低温下观测到拓扑霍尔效应，第一性原理计算表明自旋-轨道耦合与电子关联协同驱动了涌现的拓扑输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现兼具强铁磁性（ferromagnetism）和涌现拓扑输运（topological transport）的关联层状磁性材料是量子材料领域的核心挑战，当前仍缺乏理想候选体系。

Method: 采用无催化剂化学气相沉积（catalyst-free chemical vapour deposition）生长层状 1T-CrS₂；通过输运测量（transport measurements）表征电导与磁电阻行为；结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与海森伯交换计算（Heisenberg exchange calculations）分析电子结构和磁性机制。

Result: 1T-CrS₂ 具有面外易轴各向异性，居里温度高于室温；输运中出现 80 K 附近的半金属-绝缘体交叉（semimetal–insulator crossover）和高达 350 K 的显著负磁电阻（negative magnetoresistance）；30 K 以下出现拓扑霍尔效应（topological Hall effect）。计算表明自旋-轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）打开狄拉克交叉点的能隙，电子关联（electronic correlations）通过抑制电子口袋并降低载流子密度重构费米面。

Conclusion: 1T-CrS₂ 是一种稀有的关联 3d 层状铁磁体，其中电子关联和自旋-轨道耦合协同驱动了涌现拓扑输运行为，为量子材料研究提供了重要平台。

Abstract: The realization of correlated layered magnets hosting robust ferromagnetism with emergent topological transport remains a key challenge in quantum materials. Here we report the first catalyst-free chemical vapour deposition growth of layered 1T-CrS$_2$, establishing a highly stable vdWs ferromagnet with an out-of-plane easy-axis anisotropy and a Curie temperature above room temperature. Transport measurements reveal a semimetal--insulator crossover near 80 K and pronounced negative magnetoresistance up to 350 K. A topological Hall effect emerges below 30 K, a rare signature of correlated transport in layered transition-metal dichalcogenide ferromagnets. First-principles calculations show that spin--orbit coupling gaps Dirac-like crossings, while electronic correlations reconstruct the Fermi surface by suppressing electron pockets and reducing the carrier density, enhancing momentum-dependent out-of-plane spin polarization. Magnetic measurements, supported by Heisenberg exchange calculations, reveal strong nearest-neighbour ferromagnetic exchange that stabilizes long-range ferromagnetism. Our results establish 1T-CrS$_2$ as a rare correlated 3$d$ layered ferromagnet in which electronic correlations and spin--orbit coupling cooperatively drive emergent topological transport.

</details>


### [188] [Elemental Germanium Phase-Change Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23709)
*Till Zellweger,Marko Mladenović,Kevin Portner,Christoph Weilenmann,Michael Stiefel,Hanglin He,Klemens Bauer,Luiz Felipe Aguinsky,Mathieu Luisier,Alexandros Emboras*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将纯锗（elemental germanium, Ge）作为CMOS兼容的相变存储材料，替代传统的硫系化合物（如GST）。实验证明，Ge在垂直存储单元中实现了亚纳秒级结晶速度（240皮秒，比GST快40倍）、优异的热稳定性（110°C下保持10年）以及更低的电阻漂移系数，同时避免了硫系元素的交叉污染问题，使得相变存储器可在标准半导体产线中制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于硫系合金（chalcogenide alloys）如$\mathrm{Ge_2Sb_2Te_5}$（GST）的相变存储器（phase-change memory, PCM）在反复擦写过程中会发生Ge、Sb、Te原子的重新分布，导致操作随机性（stochastic operation）并最终造成器件失效。虽然元素锑（antimony）曾被提出作为替代，但其亚稳态非晶相（metastable amorphous state）无法保证可靠的数据保持（data retention）。此外，碲（tellurium）和锑（antimony）会污染CMOS产线或作为非预期掺杂剂（unintended dopants），迫使PCM只能在专用工厂中制造。因此，亟需一种兼具高性能与CMOS兼容性的相变存储材料。

Method: 本文提出将元素锗（Ge）——一种标准半导体材料——作为CMOS原生（CMOS-native）相变材料。在垂直PCM单元（vertical PCM cell）架构中，对纯Ge相变层的结晶速度、非易失性数据保持能力、热稳定性以及电阻漂移系数等关键性能指标进行实验表征，并与工业标准GST进行对比。

Result: Ge在垂直PCM单元中实现了亚纳秒级结晶（crystallization），仅需240皮秒（ps），比GST快40倍；可提供非易失性数据存储，热稳定性优异，在110°C下可保持10年（GST约为87°C）；电阻漂移系数（resistance drift coefficient）比GST低约60%。

Conclusion: 纯Ge作为一种标准半导体，成功克服了传统硫系相变材料在材料稳定性与制造兼容性方面的根本限制，在关键性能指标上优于GST，并使得相变存储器能够在标准半导体制造设施中生产。

Abstract: Phase-change memory (PCM) is a mature technology for fast, scalable, non-volatile data storage, with applications spanning embedded memory, as well as in-memory and neuromorphic computing. PCM predominantly relies on chalcogenide alloys, with $\mathrm{Ge_2Sb_2Te_5}$ (GST) as the industry standard. Yet in these alloys, the individual Ge, Sb, and Te atoms redistribute upon cycling, causing stochastic operation and ultimately device failure. To address this issue, elemental antimony was proposed as a PCM material, but it exhibits a metastable amorphous state that prevents reliable data retention. Moreover, tellurium and antimony can contaminate complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) production lines or act as unintended dopants, restricting manufacturing of PCM to dedicated fabs. Here we introduce elemental germanium (Ge) as a CMOS-native phase-change material that overcomes these fundamental limitations. In a vertical PCM cell architecture, Ge enables sub-nanosecond crystallization (240 ps, 40 times faster than GST), non-volatile data storage with excellent thermal stability ($>$110 °C for 10 years vs. $\sim$87 °C for GST), and a resistance drift coefficient approximately 60% lower than in GST. These results establish pure Ge, a standard semiconductor, as an alternative to chalcogenide phase-change materials, achieving superior performance in key metrics and enabling phase-change memory to be fabricated in standard semiconductor facilities.

</details>


### [189] [Harnessing X-ray Absorption Spectroscopy Data through Multimodal Mining of Battery Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23886)
*Tanjin He,Aikaterini Vriza,Logan Ward,Xu Huang,Yiming Chen,Anubhav Jain,Gerbrand Ceder,Rajeev S. Assary,Ian T. Foster,Maria K. Y. Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态（图像与文本）文献挖掘管线，从电池领域的全文学术论文中自动提取X射线吸收光谱（XAS）图像及其元数据，构建了一个包含13,740条光谱的开放数据集，涵盖66种吸收元素和多种电池化学体系，为大规模光谱数据分析、跨实验室比较和高通量材料发现提供了AI就绪的数据资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线吸收光谱（X-ray absorption spectroscopy, XAS）是理解材料局域电子与原子结构的核心手段，但已发表光谱大多以图像形式嵌入文献，辅以碎片化文本描述，导致难以被数据驱动分析所利用。

Method: 采用多模态（multimodal）文献挖掘方法，开发了可扩展的光谱数据数字化管线（digitization pipeline），该管线能够自动识别全文文章中的XAS图像、数字化光谱曲线，并将每条光谱链接到关于吸收边（edge）和材料（material）的元数据。

Result: 将该管线应用于电池领域文献，生成了一个包含13,740条XAS光谱的开放数据集，覆盖66种吸收元素和多种电池化学体系；专家验证确认了光谱提取和元数据信息的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过将文献中嵌入的光谱转化为结构化的数值数据，该数据集为大规模XAS分析、跨实验室比较、高通量表征（high-throughput characterization）以及先进材料的自主发现（autonomous discovery）奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) is central to understanding the local electronic and atomic structure of materials, yet most published spectra remain inaccessible to data-driven analysis because they are embedded in figures and described through fragmented textual context in the literature. Here, we use multimodal (image and text) literature mining to transform this dispersed knowledge into an AI-ready experimental data resource. We developed a scalable spectroscopy data digitization pipeline that identifies XAS figures in full-text articles, digitizes spectral curves, and links each spectrum to accompanying metadata on the measured edge and material. Applying this pipeline to the battery literature produced an open dataset of 13,740 XAS spectra, spanning 66 absorbing elements and diverse battery chemistries, with expert validation confirming accurate extraction of spectral and metadata information. By converting literature-embedded spectra into structured numerical data, this dataset provides a foundation for large-scale XAS analysis, cross-laboratory comparison, high-throughput characterization, and autonomous discovery of advanced materials.

</details>


### [190] [Nature of Cr Segregation in FCC to BCC quenched Fe-12Cr Alloy, with Post-Quenching Heat Treatment: A Positron Annihilation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23995)
*S. Hari Babu,V. R. Reddy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过正电子湮没寿命谱（positron lifetime spectroscopy）、透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy）和穆斯堡尔谱（Mössbauer spectroscopy）等手段，对比分析了Fe-12Cr与Fe-9Cr合金中Cr析出的性质与动力学。研究发现，淬火后高密度位错及亚晶结构会诱导形成四方金属间σ相（tetragonal intermetallic σ-phase），而非文献中常报道的BCC α'相；Fe-12Cr合金中Cr析出动力学更快，析出物尺寸更大但数密度更低，且首次实验报道了缺陷自由晶格中的正电子寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-Cr二元合金是铁素体/马氏体钢（ferritic/martensitic steels）的模型体系。Cr团簇（Cr clustering）引发的延展性下降是导致合金性能劣化的关键因素之一，其严重程度依赖于Cr浓度。本研究旨在理解Fe-12Cr合金中Cr析出的本质，并对比其与Fe-9Cr合金在析出浓度和尺寸上的差异。

Method: 采用正电子湮没寿命谱（positron lifetime spectroscopy）、透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy）和穆斯堡尔谱（Mössbauer spectroscopy）对Fe-12Cr合金进行表征，并与Fe-9Cr合金对比。对合金进行高温FCC相淬火及后续748 K热处理，并通过选区电子衍射（selected area diffraction）确定析出相结构。

Result: 淬火后的Fe-12Cr合金含有高密度位错（dislocations）和亚晶结构（sub-grain structure），在748 K热处理后诱发富Cr σ相（σ-phase）析出；而无位错与亚晶结构的合金仅在晶界（grain boundaries）处有析出，与Fe-9Cr合金一致。选区衍射证实析出物为四方金属间σ相（tetragonal intermetallic σ-phase），而非文献中常见的BCC α'相。穆斯堡尔谱显示基体中Cr贫化，表明析出物富Cr。Fe-12Cr合金中Cr析出动力学更快，析出物平均尺寸更大但数密度更低。此外，首次实验报道了Fe-(9,12)Cr合金缺陷自由晶格的正电子寿命。

Conclusion: Fe-12Cr合金中Cr析出的本质受位错和亚晶结构调控，析出相为四方σ相而非BCC α'相。与Fe-9Cr合金相比，Fe-12Cr合金的Cr析出动力学更快，析出物尺寸更大、数密度更低。这些发现对理解Fe-Cr合金的微观结构与性能退化机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: The Fe-Cr binary alloy is a model system for ferrtitic/martensitic steels. Cr clustering instigated loss of ductility, is one of the influential factors to deteriorate the alloys, and its severity depends on Cr concentration. This study aims at understanding (i) the nature of Cr precipitation in Fe-12Cr alloy using positron lifetime spectroscopy, transmission electron microscopy and Mössbauer spectroscopy studies and (ii) comparing the precipitation concentration and size with respect to Fe-9Cr alloy. The quenched alloys (from the high temperature FCC phase) are found to contain a high density of dislocations along with the sub-grain structure which lead to Cr rich σ-phase precipitation during post-quenching heat treatment at 748 K, while the alloy prepared with no dislocations and the sub-grain structure shows no precipitation, except at grain boundaries, consistent with observations in Fe-9Cr alloys. Selected area diffraction confirmed that the precipitates are tetragonal intermetallic σ-phase, contradicting the majority of the literature on Fe-Cr ferritic alloys which reported it as Cr rich BCC α'-phase. Mossbauer spectroscopy studies showed signatures of Cr depletion from the rest of the matrix, indicating that the precipitates are Cr rich compared to the alloy composition. The kinetics of Cr precipitation is found to be higher in the Fe-12Cr alloy compared to the Fe-9Cr alloy. The average precipitate size is observed to be higher and the number density is lower in the Fe-12Cr alloy compared to the Fe-9Cr alloy. In addition, the experimental positron lifetime in defect-free lattice of Fe-(9, 12)Cr alloys is reported for the first time with substantial evidence.

</details>


### [191] [Recrystallisation phenomena in an ultrafine-grained Al-Mg-Si alloy revealed by correlative in situ EBSD and TEM heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24085)
*Moritz Theissing,Sandra Gonzaga,Patrick Willenshofer,Thomas M. Kremmer,Stefan Pogatscher,Stefan Mitsche,Frank F. Sene,Matheus A. Tunes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过原位EBSD加热等实验手段，直接确定了超细晶AA6061铝合金再结晶起始温度约为198°C，并发现晶粒细化抑制GP区形成、降低析出温度，且再结晶过程中晶界湮灭效应（sink strength）显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超细晶（ultrafine-grained, UFG）铝合金因其高晶界密度可作为辐照缺陷的湮灭位点（sink），在空间应用中前景广阔，但其热稳定性是关键瓶颈；铝构件在近地轨道太阳辐照下温度可达~200°C，接近严重变形铝合金的再结晶温度。准确且具有体代表性的再结晶起始温度测定至关重要，但传统原位透射电镜（in situ TEM）加热受薄膜效应、晶界衬度模糊及小采样体积的限制，无法满足这一需求。

Method: 对高压扭转制备的UFG AA6061 (Al-Mg-Si) 合金，采用原位TEM加热与原位电子背散射衍射（in situ EBSD）加热进行直接对比，并结合差示扫描量热法（differential scanning calorimetry, DSC）、分析型扫描透射电镜能谱分析（STEM-EDX）及显微硬度测试。基于KAM（kernel average misorientation）信息与温度相关的内禀湮灭强度，重新审视Brailsford-Bullough-Hayns湮灭强度理论。

Result: 原位EBSD对体材料中约10³个晶粒进行采样，将微观结构演化解析为连续回复、再结晶和晶粒长大三个阶段，确定失稳起始温度约为198°C。量热、显微硬度和纳米尺度元素分布图显示：晶粒细化抑制了GP区（Guinier-Preston zones）形成，并将析出温度降至更低，且析出既未阻碍再结晶，也未在超细晶结构消失后恢复强度。理论分析表明，随着回复和再结晶进行，晶界湮灭强度显著下降。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了原位EBSD加热作为一种在操作（in operando）方法，可用于先进金属体系中微结构失稳的体代表性测定。

Abstract: Ultrafine-grained (UFG) aluminium alloys are promising lightweight structural materials for space applications, where a high grain-boundary density can act as sinks for irradiation-induced defects. Their deployment, however, is contingent on thermal stability: aluminium components in low-Earth orbit can reach $\sim$200 $^\circ$C under solar irradiation, close to where severely deformed aluminium alloys recrystallise. Accurate, bulk-representative determination of recrystallisation onset is therefore essential, yet conventional in situ transmission electron microscopy (TEM) heating is compromised by thin-film effects, ambiguous grain-boundary contrast, and small sampling volumes. Here, a UFG AA6061 (Al-Mg-Si) alloy produced by high-pressure torsion was studied by a direct comparison of in situ TEM heating and in situ electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) heating, complemented by differential scanning calorimetry (DSC), analytical scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM-EDX) and microhardness. In situ EBSD sampled $\sim 10^{3}$ grains from bulk material and resolved the microstructural evolution into sequential recovery, recrystallisation and grain-growth regimes, placing the onset of instability at $\sim$198 $^\circ$C. Calorimetry, microhardness and nanoscale elemental mapping showed that grain refinement suppresses GP-zones formation and shifts precipitation to lower temperatures, with precipitation neither retarding recrystallisation nor restoring strength once the UFG structure is consumed. Revisiting the Brailsford-Bullough-Hayns sink-strength theory with a KAM-informed, temperature-dependent internal sink strength, we show that the grain-boundary sink strength collapses as recovery and recrystallisation proceed. We establish in situ EBSD heating as an in operando method for bulk-representative determination of microstructural instabilities in advanced metallic systems.

</details>


### [192] [Interaction-driven electronic ferroelectricity in van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24087)
*Ziying Wang,Ana Vera Montoto,Mohammad Amini,Yuxiao Ding,Jose L. Lado,Robert Drost,Adolfo O. Fumega,Peter Liljeroth*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究报告了双层1T-TaSe₂中两种堆叠几何结构（A-C和A-C'）均实现了准一维相互作用链，但分别由不同的相互作用机制主导。A-C'堆叠中的层间库仑相互作用导致层间自发电荷不平衡，产生面外铁电极化，证明了相互作用驱动的电子铁电性（interaction-driven electronic ferroelectricity）的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管窄带系统中的强电子关联（strong electronic correlations）有望实现新兴量子相，且范德华（van der Waals）材料中的铁电性通常源于晶格畸变或莫尔重构等结构对称性破缺，但直接从电子相互作用产生铁电性的可能性尚缺乏系统探索。

Method: 本研究采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）、扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）和第一性原理计算（ab initio calculations）相结合的方法，系统研究了由耦合的大卫之星电荷密度波（Star-of-David charge density wave）相构成的双层1T-TaSe₂的两种堆叠几何结构。
