Result: A-C堆叠中强层间杂化（interlayer hybridization）导致二聚化（dimerization）并形成能带绝缘态；A-C'堆叠则由层间库仑相互作用（interlayer Coulomb interactions）主导，产生自发层间电荷不平衡，进而形成面外铁电极化。此外，铁电和反铁电链间构型可通过外场实现稳定和电学切换。

Conclusion: 双层1T-TaSe₂是一个实现相互作用驱动电子铁电性的平台，建立了1T-TaSe₂多层体系中一类被忽视的电荷有序关联态（charge-ordered correlated states）家族。

Abstract: Strong electronic correlations in narrow-band systems provide a promising route to realize emergent quantum phases. While ferroelectricity in van der Waals materials is typically associated with inversion symmetry breaking driven by lattice distortions, interlayer sliding, or moiré reconstruction, the possibility of generating ferroelectricity directly from electronic interactions remains largely unexplored. Here, using molecular beam epitaxy, scanning tunneling microscopy, and ab initio calculations, we investigate two stacking geometries of bilayer 1T-TaSe$_2$, A-C and A-C$'$, formed by coupled Star-of-David charge density wave phases. We show that both stackings realize quasi-one-dimensional interacting chains, but are governed by distinct interaction mechanisms. In the A-C stacking, strong interlayer hybridization leads to dimerization and the formation of a band insulating state. In contrast, the A-C$'$ stacking is dominated by interlayer Coulomb interactions, producing a spontaneous charge imbalance between layers that gives rise to an out-of-plane ferroelectric polarization. Furthermore, we demonstrate that ferroelectric and antiferroelectric interchain configurations can be stabilized and electrically switched by an external field. Our results prove that bilayer 1T-TaSe$_2$ is a platform for interaction-driven electronic ferroelectricity, establishing an overlooked family of charge-ordered correlated states in 1T-TaSe$_2$ multilayers.

</details>


### [193] [Atomic-scale phase-field modeling with dopants: Stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation with fractional site occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24107)
*Kairi Masuda,Yu Kumagai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将相场建模（phase-field modeling）概念扩展至原子尺度，基于随机自洽谐振近似（SSCHA）理论构建原子系统的自由能，允许分数占据（fractional site occupation），从而直接计算化学势并重现Ag在Cu体相中的分布及晶格膨胀，并成功识别晶界处Ag原子的择优偏析位点，实现了原子尺度图案形成的可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相场建模基于连续介质力学（continuum mechanics），难以直接应用于由化学势驱动的原子尺度图案形成（如掺杂剂偏析和空位有序化），因此需要将相场概念推广至离散原子系统。

Method: 在随机自洽谐振近似（SSCHA）理论框架内构建原子系统的自由能，引入分数占据来描述原子位点的部分占据，并直接计算自由能对占据数的导数以获得化学势。

Result: 成功重现了Ag在体相Cu中的分布以及由此导致的晶格膨胀。应用于Σ5(310)[001] Cu晶界掺杂Ag的研究发现，Ag原子优先偏析于晶界三角图案的顶点位置；随掺杂量增加，多余的Ag原子先在顶点附近、后在三角图案底部位置发生偏析。

Conclusion: 该方法将相场概念拓展至离散原子系统，能够识别掺杂剂的择优偏析位点，从而可视化原子尺度的图案形成过程。

Abstract: Phase-field modeling has achieved great success in predicting pattern formation in materials, such as the formation of ferroelectric domains. However, because it is typically based on continuum mechanics, conventional phase-field modeling cannot be straightforwardly applied to atomic-scale pattern formation, such as dopant segregation and vacancy ordering, which are driven by chemical potentials. Here, we extend the phase-field concept to the atomic scale by formulating the free energy of atomic systems within stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation (SSCHA) theory to allow fractional site occupations. Our methodology enables us to directly calculate the derivative of the free energy with respect to site occupation and thereby obtain the chemical potential, successfully reproducing Ag distributions in bulk Cu as well as the resulting lattice expansion. Furthermore, we applied our methodology to investigate dopant segregation around a Σ5(310)[001] Cu grain boundary doped with Ag atoms. We found that Ag atoms preferentially segregate at the vertices of the triangular motif of a grain boundary. As the number of dopants increases, excess Ag atoms segregate near the vertices and then at the bottom sites of the triangular motif. This study extends the phase-field concept to discrete atomic systems, enabling the identification of preferential dopant-segregation sites and thereby visualizing atomic-scale pattern formation.

</details>


### [194] [Square Net TaSiAs Nanowires with Topological Surface Conduction and Linear Magnetoresistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24244)
*Anand Roy,Ofek Goldreich,Guy Ohad,Barun Barick,Olga Brontvein,Ora Bitton,Katya Rechav,Yishay Feldman,Leeor Kronik,Ernesto Joselevich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了Si方格子（square-net）拓扑材料TaSiAs单晶纳米线的合成与性质。化学气相传输法（chemical-vapor-transport）生长的纳米线表面包裹SiO2介电壳层，具有良好的环境稳定性和洁净表面。实验观察到远低于块体材料的室温电阻率和非饱和线性磁阻（linear magnetoresistance），结合第一性原理计算揭示了宽范围线性能带色散和受对称性保护的狄拉克锥（Dirac cone），证实了低维方格子拓扑材料的独特量子输运特性及其在互连、自旋电子学和量子计算中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方块拓扑材料（square-net topological materials）因其结构多样性、拓扑保护能带及本征磁有序和超导等电子特性而备受关注，但其低维结构和性质尚不清楚。实现低维方块拓扑材料有望增强准一维几何和高表面体积比带来的量子行为，并促进其在功能器件中的集成。

Method: 采用化学气相传输法（chemical-vapor-transport）合成高质量TaSiAs单晶纳米线，纳米线表面自然形成SiO2介电壳层。利用原子分辨率结构分析表征核-壳界面和Si方格子晶格取向，结合电输运和磁输运测量以及第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行理论和实验验证。

Result: TaSiAs纳米线室温电阻率比块体类似物低4至11倍，并表现出非饱和线性磁阻（non-saturating linear magnetoresistance）。第一性原理计算显示宽范围（4 eV）线性能带色散，以及由同形对称性（symmorphic symmetry, C4）和非同形对称性（non-symmorphic symmetry）保护的多个狄拉克锥（Dirac cone）。

Conclusion: SiO2化学封装（chemical encapsulation）使低维方块拓扑材料具备环境稳定性和洁净表面，是观测稳健拓扑保护表面态的关键。该发现展示了低维方块拓扑材料的独特性质及其在下一代互连（interconnects）、自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）和量子计算（quantum computing）中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Square-net topological materials exhibit various interesting properties arising from the interplay of their structural diversity, topologically protected bands and different possible electronic features, including intrinsic magnetic ordering and superconductivity. However, little is known about the low-dimensional structures of these materials and their properties. Realization of low-dimensional topological square-net materials has the potential of enabling enhancement of their quantum behavior arising from quasi-compact 1D geometry and high surface-to-volume ratio, and their integration into functional devices. This work reports on the synthesis and properties of high-quality, single-crystal nanowires of Si square-net material TaSiAs. The chemical-vapor-transport produces TaSiAs nanowires that are chemically encapsulated with a thin dielectric shell of SiO2, enabling remarkable ambient stability and a pristine surface, which are critical for observing robust topologically protected surface states. Atomic-resolution structural analysis reveals a sharp core-shell interface, and a Si square-net lattice extending along the nanowire axis. These chemically protected nanowires allowed us to observe rich electrical and magnetotransport features, including 4 to 11 times lower room-temperature resistivity than the closest bulk analogues and non-saturating linear magnetoresistance, revealing topologically protected coherent surface transport. First-principles calculations show a wide-range (4 eV) linear band dispersion, alongside different Dirac cones that are protected by either symmorphic (C4) or non-symmorphic symmetries, predicting transport features consistent with our results. The findings demonstrate the unique properties of low-dimensional square-net topological materials and their potential applications, including next-generation interconnects, spintronic devices and quantum computing.

</details>


### [195] [Catalyst Diffusion Transformer: Generative Inverse Design of Heterogeneous Catalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24272)
*Hayoung Doo,Dong Hyeon Mok,Seoin Back,Jonggeol Na*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 Catalyst Diffusion Transformer (CatDiT)，一个统一的逆催化剂设计框架，通过学习压缩潜在表征，实现从金属间合金到氧化物表面的有效生成，支持同时基于吸附质类型、结合能和催化剂类别的多条件生成，并在氮还原反应中实现了目标活性窗口内候选材料的1.5倍富集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催化剂的化学设计空间巨大且设计变量复杂且相互依赖，使得面向目标性质的催化剂发现过程高度劳动密集和资源密集。现有生成模型通常局限于单条件控制或狭窄化学空间，缺乏能够处理多条件约束并覆盖广泛催化剂结构类型的统一框架。

Method: 提出 Catalyst Diffusion Transformer (CatDiT)，一种基于扩散变换器（diffusion transformer）的逆催化剂设计框架。该框架通过学习压缩的潜在表征（latent representations）实现高效训练与快速采样，并支持同时对吸附质类型（adsorbate type）、结合能（binding energy）和催化剂类别（catalyst class）进行条件控制，兼具离散性质的可控性与连续性质的方向性控制。

Result: 在氮还原反应（nitrogen reduction reaction, NRR）的多条件生成应用中，模型生成了28个经密度泛函理论（DFT）弛豫的合金候选结构，这些结构满足目标活性窗口并位于纯金属 *N-*H 标度线之上，相较源分布实现了约1.5倍的富集（enrichment）。

Conclusion: CatDiT 被验证为一种实用且可扩展的方法，适用于性质导向的催化剂逆设计（property-directed catalyst inverse design）与目标导向的催化剂生成，为反应特异性催化剂发现提供了丰富的候选池。

Abstract: The vast chemical design space and complex, interdependent design variables make catalyst discovery for targeted properties highly labor- and resource-intensive. Although generative models have emerged as a promising solution, existing approaches are generally limited to single-property conditioning or narrow chemical spaces. Here, we present Catalyst Diffusion Transformer (CatDiT), a unified framework for inverse catalyst design that generates valid and novel structures ranging from intermetallic alloys to oxide surfaces. By learning compressed latent representations, CatDiT enables efficient training and rapid sampling while supporting simultaneous conditioning on adsorbate type, binding energy, and catalyst class. The model provides reliable control of discrete properties and directional control of continuous properties, enriching candidate pools for reaction-specific catalyst discovery. As a representative application, multi-conditional generation for the nitrogen reduction reaction (NRR) yields 28 density functional theory (DFT)-relaxed alloy candidates that satisfy the target activity window and lie above the pure-metal *N-*H scaling line, corresponding to a ~1.5-fold enrichment over the source distribution. These results establish CatDiT as a practical and scalable approach for property-directed catalyst inverse design and targeted catalyst generation.

</details>


### [196] [Aligning Heterogeneous DFT Datasets: A Graph Neural Network Approach to Cross-Functional Formation Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24327)
*Yidong Huang,Tenglong Lu,Hanwen Kang,Junfeng Huang,Sheng Meng,Miao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络（graph neural network）的迁移学习方法（transfer learning），利用MatPES数据集中38万个PBE-r2SCAN结构配对数据训练模型，将传统PBE计算能量升级至r2SCAN精度（MAE: 14.3 meV/atom），解决了异质性密度泛函理论（heterogeneous DFT）计算中不同泛函方法间的能量尺度不一致问题，从而打破多源DFT数据的孤岛屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质性DFT计算中不同交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functionals）、截断能（kinetic energy cutoffs）、赝势（pseudopotentials）和色散校正（dispersion corrections）的选择会导致系统性形成能误差（formation energy errors），平均能量差异可达107 meV/atom（PBE vs r2SCAN）。这种方法依赖的性能差异阻碍了多源DFT数据的整合，严重限制了训练鲁棒材料AI模型的数据规模与质量。

Method: 采用基于图神经网络（graph neural network）的迁移学习方法，利用MatPES数据库中380,190个结构配对的PBE-r2SCAN数据训练模型，预测跨泛函能量残差（cross-functional energy residuals）并对齐不一致的DFT能量尺度。模型架构采用GPTFF（graph neural network for potential energy surface fitting），将常规PBE能量转换为r2SCAN级精度。

Result: 模型将PBE能量转换为r2SCAN精度，平均绝对误差（mean absolute error, MAE）为14.3 meV/atom，优于CHGNet的18.2 meV/atom。该方法能可靠预测相稳定性（phase stability）、电池电压曲线（battery voltage profiles）和反应热力学（reaction thermodynamics）。

Conclusion: 该图神经网络迁移学习方法可有效将大规模传统PBE数据集升级至r2SCAN高精度标准，实现多源DFT数据的整合，从而推动高性能材料基础模型（foundation models）的开发。

Abstract: Heterogeneous density functional theory (DFT) calculations, particularly plane-wave implementations, introduce systematic formation energy errors ranging from tens to hundreds of meV/atom, depending on the selection of exchange-correlation functionals, kinetic energy cutoffs, pseudopotentials, and dispersion corrections. As demonstrated by the MatPES dataset, identical structures can exhibit an average energy discrepancy of 107 meV/atom between PBE and r2SCAN calculations. Such method-dependent discrepancies hinder the integration of multi-source DFT data, greatly limiting the scale and quality of datasets for training robust materials AI models. Here, we resolve this fundamental data silo barrier via graph-based transfer learning. Leveraging 380,190 structurally paired PBE-r2SCAN entries from the MatPES database, we train a structure-aware graph neural network to predict cross-functional energy residuals and align inconsistent DFT energy scales. By adopting GPTFF model architecture, the model converts conventional PBE energies to r2SCAN-level accuracy with a mean absolute error of 14.3 meV/atom, compared with 18.2 meV/atom achieved by CHGNet. This versatile approach effectively upgrades massive legacy PBE datasets to high-precision r2SCAN standards. It enables reliable predictions of phase stability, battery voltage profiles, and reaction thermodynamics, while allowing the integration of multi-source DFT data to advance the development of high-performance materials foundation models.

</details>


### [197] [Hybrid-parity sliding multiferroics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24337)
*Zhenzhou Guo,Jiangtao Yu,Shibo Fang,Jin Cao,Xiaodong Zhou,Yee Sin Ang,Wenhong Wang,Zhenxiang Cheng,Xiaotian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新型混合宇称滑动多铁材料（hybrid-parity sliding multiferroics），其中滑动铁电性与非相对论性自旋劈裂（NSS）通过层间滑动实现耦合，并以双层VBr₂为例，通过第一性原理计算演示了面外铁电极化与奇偶两种宇称NSS分量的可逆协同翻转，为实现低能耗、非易失性逻辑器件提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动铁电体（sliding ferroelectrics）为通过可逆层间滑动实现非常规磁性的电控提供了非易失性平台，但滑动铁电性与混合宇称非相对论性自旋劈裂（nonrelativistic spin splitting, NSS）之间的耦合尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用对称性分析（symmetry analysis）识别共面磁体（coplanar magnets）是实现滑动多铁性的自然平台；结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），以双层VBr₂为代表体系进行验证。

Result: 在双层VBr₂中实现了面外铁电极化（±0.12 pC/m）与偶宇称和奇宇称NSS分量的耦合反转，滑动势垒仅为6 meV/f.u.；NSS分量的符号与滑动可切换的铁电极化锁定，并编码于自旋电流响应（spin-current responses）中。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了滑动多铁材料的范畴，并提升了滑动铁电体在低能耗、非易失性逻辑器件中的功能性。

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectrics provide a nonvolatile platform for the electrical control of unconventional magnetism through reversible interlayer sliding. However, the coupling between sliding ferroelectricity and hybrid-parity nonrelativistic spin splitting (NSS) remains largely unexplored. Here, we introduce a class of hybrid-parity sliding multiferroics in which the spontaneous ferroelectric polarization is coupled to certain NSS components through interlayer sliding, allowing these components to be reversibly switched in an electrical way. Symmetry analysis identifies coplanar magnets as natural platforms for realizing this form of sliding multiferroicity. First-principles calculations establish bilayer VBr$_2$ as a representative example, demonstrating the coupled reversal of the out-of-plane ferroelectric polarization ($\pm$0.12 pC/m) and the signs of both even- and odd-parity NSS components via an interlayer-sliding pathway with an ultralow barrier of 6 meV/f.u. The signs of these NSS components are locked to the sliding-switchable ferroelectric polarization and encoded in the spin-current responses, providing a signature of the coupled ferroic switching. Our findings expand the scope of sliding multiferroics and the functionality of sliding ferroelectrics for low-energy, nonvolatile logic devices.

</details>


### [198] [Neural RHEED alignment with limited training data during CdTe MBE growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24467)
*Bartłomiej Turowski,Jakub J. Meixner,Róża Dziewiątkowska,Wojciech Zaleszczyk,Tomasz Wojciechowski,Valentine V. Volobuev,Marcin M. Wysokiński,Tomasz Wojtowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的神经视觉辅助方法，用于在分子束外延（MBE）生长过程中自动完成晶体学对准。仅通过15个CdTe结构的反射高能电子衍射（RHEED）图案训练，结合物理感知后处理，模型能够可靠推断晶体学方向，替代人工逐帧检查。研究比较了2D和3D ResNet架构，并提供了可直接用于闭环控制的神经网络系统及可推广至新材料的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分子束外延（MBE）生长过程中，晶体学对准是影响薄膜质量的关键步骤。传统方法依赖人工逐帧检查反射高能电子衍射（RHEED）图案，效率低且难以规模化。因此，研究旨在开发一种数据高效、自动化的对准方法，减少对大量标注数据和人工干预的依赖。

Method: 采用神经视觉辅助方法，基于仅15个CdTe结构的RHEED图案训练深度学习模型。设计了基于2D和3D ResNet架构的神经网络，并进行比较。进一步引入物理感知后处理（physics-aware postprocessing），利用RHEED图像采集的物理约束提升推断准确性。

Result: 所训练的模型能够可靠地推断晶体学方向，替代人工逐帧检查。实验交付了两个主要成果：(i) 一个可直接用于未来CdTe生长实验闭环控制的完整神经网络系统；(ii) 一个可推广至数据有限的新材料体系的通用工作流程。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着向人工智能驱动的MBE生长迈进一步，展示了机器学习辅助自动化在薄膜合成领域的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a data-efficient neural-vision assisted method to automate crystallographic alignment during molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) growth. Trained on reflection high-energy electron diffraction (RHEED) patterns from only 15 CdTe structures, our model - enabled by physics-aware postprocessing - reliably infers crystallographic directions, replacing manual frame-by-frame inspection. To this end, we design, test, and critically compare neural-network architectures based on 2D and 3D ResNet configurations, both with and without postprocessing that leverages the physical constraints of RHEED image acquisition. Our work delivers (i) a fully trained neural system ready for closed-loop deployment in future CdTe growth experiments and (ii) a generalizable pipeline for new materials where access to diverse RHEED datasets is limited. More broadly, this study represents a step toward AI-driven MBE growth and demonstrates the potential of machine-learning-assisted automation in thin-film synthesis.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum oscillation fingerprints of altermagnetism in hole-doped RuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24599)
*Yuchi Yang,Yusheng Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），本研究利用量子振荡（quantum oscillations）方法研究空穴掺杂RuO₂，发现空穴掺杂可增强自旋分裂（spin splitting）并重构费米面（Fermi surface），Ru磁矩与量子振荡自旋分裂特征之间存在准线性关联，该关联可由最小二维d波交变磁性模型（d-wave altermagnetic model）描述，同时发现非磁态到交变磁态（altermagnetic state）经中间态的转变，为识别RuO₂中的交变磁态提供了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性（altermagnetism）兼具铁磁（ferromagnetism）和反铁磁（antiferromagnetism）特征，近年备受关注。尽管空穴掺杂可能促进争议中的交变磁候选材料RuO₂的磁性，但其电子与磁性质在空穴掺杂下的演化机制尚不清晰，亟需系统研究。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），采用量子振荡（quantum oscillations）方法研究空穴掺杂RuO₂的电子结构与磁性质，分析角度依赖的量子振荡频率（angle-dependent quantum oscillation frequency），并构建最小二维d波交变磁性模型（minimal two-dimensional d-wave altermagnetic model）以描述观测到的关联行为。

Result: 空穴掺杂可增强RuO₂的自旋分裂（spin splitting）并重构费米面（Fermi surface），该效应体现在角度依赖的量子振荡频率中。追踪一对闭合费米面费米口袋（Fermi-surface pockets）发现，Ru磁矩与基于量子振荡的自旋分裂特征间存在准线性关联（quasi-linear correlation），该关联在宽掺杂范围内有效。此外，空穴掺杂RuO₂展现出从非磁态（nonmagnetic state）经中间态（intermediate state）向交变磁态（altermagnetic state）的转变。

Conclusion: 量子振荡中的准线性关联以及稳定交变磁态（stable altermagnetic state）独特的量子振荡频率可作为识别RuO₂中交变磁态（altermagnetic state）的有效特征。研究结果为理解空穴掺杂RuO₂提供了全面框架，并为交变磁性转变（altermagnetic transitions）及其识别提供了新见解。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, characterized by its ferromagnetism-like spin-splitting band structure and antiferromagnetism-like magnetic order, has garnered considerable attention recently. Although hole doping may promote magnetism in the debated altermagnet candidate RuO2, the evolution of its electronic and magnetic properties under hole doping remains poorly understood. Based on first-principles calculations, we employ quantum oscillations to study hole-doped RuO2. We find that hole doping can enhance spin splitting and reconstruct the Fermi surface in RuO2, which is revealed by the angle-dependent quantum oscillation frequency. By tracking a pair of closed Fermi-surface pockets, we identify a meaningful correlation between the magnetic moment of Ru and a quantum-oscillation-based signature of spin splitting. This correlation follows a quasi-linear trend over a broad doping range, which can be captured by a minimal two-dimensional d-wave altermagnetic model. In addition, the hole-doped RuO2 exhibits a transition from a nonmagnetic to an altermagnetic state via an intermediate state. The quasi-linear correlation through quantum oscillation and the distinct quantum oscillation frequency of the stable altermagnetic state can serve as useful signatures for identifying the altermagnetic state in RuO2. Our results provide a comprehensive framework for understanding hole-doped RuO2, offering new insights into altermagnetic transitions and their identification.

</details>


### [200] [A General Model of Interfacial Chemical Equilibrium in Phase-Field Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24620)
*Yanzhou Ji,Long-Qing Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种相场法（phase-field method）中多溶液相界面平衡的新方法，采用辅助非守恒变量描述相间成分差异，以化学扩散势差驱动演化，可归约为WBM和KKS两种极限模型，并可直接集成热力学数据库，适用于WBM/KKS难以处理的复杂成分演化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相场法中的WBM模型（Wheeler-Boettinger-McFadden model）和KKS模型（Kim-Kim-Suzuki model）在处理某些界面平衡问题时存在局限，例如无法有效处理两个有序相之间不存在共同成分范围的互扩散问题。

Method: 引入辅助非守恒变量（auxiliary non-conserved variables）来描述不同相之间的成分差异，其时间演化由不同化学扩散势（chemical diffusional potentials）之间的差异驱动。该方法将WBM模型（界面化学成分相等）和KKS模型（界面化学扩散势相等）作为两种极限情况包含在内。

Result: 新方法可直接集成热力学数据库（thermodynamic databases），无需额外的近似和简化处理。该方法在WBM和KKS模型难以适用的场景下仍能正常工作。

Conclusion: 该方法具有广泛的通用性，适用于涉及成分演化（composition evolution）和化学平衡（chemical equilibria）的多种问题，特别是两个有序相之间不存在共同成分范围的互扩散过程。

Abstract: We report a new approach to interfacial equilibria among multiple solution phases in the phase-field method. It employs auxiliary non-conserved variables to describe the composition differences among different phases, and their temporal evolution is driven by the differences among different chemical diffusional potentials. It is reduced to the Wheeler-Boettinger-McFadden (WBM) model of equal interfacial chemical compositions and to the Kim-Kim-Suzuki (KKS) model of equal interfacial chemical diffusion potentials as two limiting cases. It can directly incorporate thermodynamic databases without any further approximations and simplifications. It is generally applicable to a wide range of problems involving composition evolution and chemical equilibria, including processes such as interdiffusion between two ordered phases not sharing any common composition range, which would pose difficulty to the existing treatments using WBM and KKS models.

</details>


### [201] [Growth and characterization of planar hexagonal Ge on CdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24685)
*Andrea Besana,Veronica Regazzoni,Marco Faverzani,Fabrizio Rovaris,Emiliano Bonera,Sonia Freddi,Elisa Brugaletta,Mohamed Zaghloul,Fabio Pezzoli,Francesco Montalenti,Monica Bollani,Daniel Chrastina,Anna Marzegalli,Antonio M. Mio,Emilio Scalise,Giovanni Isella*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过低能等离子体增强化学气相沉积（PECVD）在非基底 m 面 CdS 衬底上成功实现了六方相锗（hexagonal germanium）的平面外延生长，并系统研究了生长温度、应变弛豫机制及缺陷演化规律。X 射线衍射、扫描透射电子显微镜和偏振拉曼光谱证实了具有预期晶体对称性的六方 Ge 外延层，但光致发光谱未检测到 Ge 相关发射。透射电子显微镜与原子尺度建模表明，应变弛豫主要由有限位错机制在界面附近数纳米内完成，而远离界面时六方有序性因堆垛层错（以 I3 型缺陷为主）逐渐丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方相 IV 族半导体相较于立方金刚石相展现出独特的电子与光学性质，然而在平面异质结构中稳定这些亚稳同素异形体（metastable allotropes）仍是一个重大挑战。本研究旨在探索在非基底 m 面 CdS 衬底上实现六方锗的平面外延生长，并揭示其稳定化机制。

Method: 采用低能等离子体增强化学气相沉积（low-energy plasma-enhanced chemical vapor deposition, PECVD）在非基底 m 面 CdS 衬底上外延生长六方锗。通过调节生长温度研究其对六方相形成与稳定性的影响。利用 X 射线衍射（XRD）、扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）、偏振分辨拉曼光谱（polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy）和光致发光（PL）光谱进行表征，并结合透射电子显微镜观察与原子尺度建模（atomistic modeling）分析应变弛豫与缺陷机制。

Result: X 射线衍射、STEM 和偏振拉曼光谱证实形成了具有预期晶体对称性的外延六方锗，拉曼响应表现出六方 Ge 的 E₂g 声子模式特征偏振选择定则；然而光致发光谱未检测到 Ge 相关发射特征。透射电子显微镜与原子建模显示，应变弛豫主要依赖有限位错机制，在界面数纳米内有效缓解大部分失配应变，但可能涉及局域立方堆垛插入。远离界面处，六方有序性因堆垛层错（stacking-fault disorder）逐渐丧失，其中 I3 型缺陷占主导。

Conclusion: 研究确立了 CdS 作为六方锗平面稳定化的有前景模板，并深入揭示了亚稳 IV 族异质结构中控制应变弛豫的缺陷机制，为六方相 IV 族半导体的异质集成提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Hexagonal group-IV semiconductors have attracted increasing interest owing to their unconventional electronic and optical properties compared to the cubic diamond phase. However, the stabilization of these metastable allotropes in planar heterostructures remains a major challenge. In this work, we demonstrate the epitaxial growth of planar hexagonal germanium on non-basal $m$-plane CdS substrates by low-energy plasma-enhanced chemical vapor deposition. The role of growth temperature in the formation and stabilization of the hexagonal phase is investigated. X-ray diffraction, scanning transmission electron microscopy, and polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy reveal the formation of epitaxial hexagonal germanium with the expected crystal symmetry. In particular, the Raman response exhibits the characteristic polarization selection rules of the E$_\text{2g}$ phonon mode of hexagonal Ge. Conversely, photoluminescence spectroscopy does not reveal any Ge-related emission feature. Combined transmission electron microscopy observations and atomistic modeling show that strain relaxation is governed by a limited set of dislocation mechanisms, which efficiently relieve most of the mismatch strain within a few nanometers from the interface and can involve localized cubic stacking insertions. At greater distances from the interface, the progressive loss of hexagonal order is increasingly dominated by stacking-fault disorder, particularly I3-type defects. These results establish CdS as a promising template for the planar stabilization of hexagonal Ge and provide insight into the defect mechanisms governing strain relaxation in metastable group-IV heterostructures.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Probing an Intermediate-Mass Black Hole Companion of Sagittarius A* with Pulsar Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24201)
*Zexin Hu,Lijing Shao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用脉冲星计时（pulsar timing）观测来探测位于银河系中心（Galactic Center）人马座A*（Sgr A*）附近隐藏的中等质量黑洞（intermediate-mass black hole, IMBH）的可能性。研究发现，脉冲星-超大质量黑洞（SMBH）系统对第三体（IMBH）的扰动非常敏感，即使在复杂的银河系中心天体物理环境下，射电计时观测仍能对IMBH的存在提供严格的约束，并补充现有观测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心（Galactic Center）隐藏的中等质量黑洞（IMBH）可能解释围绕人马座A*（Sgr A*）的恒星分布异常，但现有观测手段对其存在与否缺乏足够约束，亟需新的探测方法。

Method: 基于下一代射电望远镜（如SKA）对环绕Sgr A*的脉冲星进行射电计时观测（radio timing observation），分析脉冲星-超大质量黑洞（SMBH）双星系统受到第三体（IMBH）引力扰动时的可探测性。

Result: 脉冲星-SMBH系统对第三体扰动高度敏感，可用来对IMBH的存在施加严格约束；即使在银河系中心复杂天体物理环境的强扰动下，计时观测仍能补充现有观测约束。

Conclusion: 下一代射电望远镜的脉冲星计时观测有望为银河系中心是否存在中等质量黑洞（IMBH）提供新的、强有力的探测手段，并弥补当前观测限制的不足。

Abstract: An intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) hidden in our Galactic Center (GC) may explain the puzzling observations of the stellar distribution around Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*), the supermassive black hole (SMBH) in the GC. Future observations with the next-generation radio telescopes, such as the SKA, are promising to discover pulsars orbiting around Sgr A*, and thus provide the possibility of constraining the hidden IMBH with pulsar timing. We study the detectability of a third-body, the IMBH, in the pulsar-SMBH system based on radio timing observation. We find that the pulsar-SMBH system is very sensitive to such a third-body perturbation and can be used to put stringent constraints on the existence of the IMBH. Even under strong perturbations caused by the complex GC astrophysical environments, timing observation will still complement the existing observational constraints.

</details>


### [203] [Transport Properties of the MRI in Differentially Rotating Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24457)
*Harshraj Bandyopadhyay,Peter Hammond,David Radice*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维高分辨率广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，研究了差转中子星中磁旋转不稳定性（MRI）驱动的湍流，测量了有效输运系数（剪切粘性、混合长度、平均场发电机参数）及其与平均流参数的相关性，发现这些系数与量纲分析预测存在约一个量级的偏差，且混合长度与密度无关，挑战了现有大涡模拟（LES）的常用假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差转中子星中的磁旋转不稳定性（MRI）驱动的湍流对中子星的演化、塌缩及角动量输运至关重要，但当前缺乏对其有效输运系数的精确测量及量纲分析预测的定量验证。

Method: 采用三维高分辨率广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，对高质量和低质量两类差转中子星模型进行MRI演化，并测量湍流的有效输运系数（剪切粘性$α_{\rm vis}$、混合长度$\ell_{\rm mix}$、平均场发电机参数$α_{\rm DYN}$）及其与平均流参数（静止质量密度$ρ$、MRI特征波长$λ_{\rm MRI}$、垂直阿尔芬速度$v_A$）的相关性。

Result: 饱和状态下，$\ell_{\rm mix}$和$α_{\rm DYN}$分别与$λ_{\rm MRI}$和$v_A$存在相关性，符合量纲分析预期，但两者数值偏差约一个量级；混合长度$\ell_{\rm mix}$与密度$ρ$基本无关，质疑了现有广义相对论大涡模拟（LES）中$\ell_{\rm mix}(ρ)$的假设；所有输运系数在时空上表现出高度间歇性。

Conclusion: 基于量纲分析选取输运系数的模拟可能定性重现MRI诱导湍流的效应，但定量预测仍需完全解析的GRMHD模拟。

Abstract: We perform three-dimensional, high-resolution, general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (GRMHD) simulations of the magnetorotational instability (MRI) in differentially rotating neutron stars. We consider both high-mass models which collapse either promptly, or due to the outward transport of angular momentum removing rotational support, and lower-mass models, which remain stable even after solid-body rotation has been achieved. We measure effective transport coefficients of the resulting turbulent flow, shear-viscosity $α_{\rm vis}$, mixing-length $\ell_{\rm mix}$, and mean-field dynamo $α_{\rm DYN}$-parameter, and their correlations with mean-flow parameters, such as rest-mass density $ρ$, characteristic wavelength of the MRI $λ_{\rm MRI}$, and vertical Alfvén velocity $v_A$. At saturation, $\ell_{\rm mix}$ and $α_{\rm DYN}$ correlate with $λ_{\rm MRI}$ and $v_A$ respectively, as expected from dimensional analysis. However, both quantities show deviations of roughly an order of magnitude from the values predicted on the basis of these correlations. They are also highly intermittent in space and time. The mixing length is found to be largely independent of density, calling into question the $\ell_{\rm mix}(ρ)$ ansatz used in many general-relativistic large-eddy simulations. Our results show that, while the effects of MRI-induced turbulence might be qualitatively reproduced by simulations that employ transport coefficients chosen using dimensional-analysis considerations, fully-resolved GRMHD simulations are needed to make quantitative predictions.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Nonlocal Cubic Density Gibbs Measures from Bosonic Gibbs States with Three-Body Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23041)
*Hao Liang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维和三维环面上研究了具有重整化非局部三体相互作用的巨正则玻色吉布斯态的高温平均场极限，构造了极限非线性经典吉布斯测度，并证明了相对自由能和约化密度矩阵的收敛性（三维情况下需对小相互作用施加条件）。证明结合了密度通道表示与基于自由吉布斯态上符号表示的相干态变分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有重整化非局部三体相互作用的玻色系统在高温平均场极限下的行为，特别是其严格的热力学极限和量子到经典的过渡。

Method: 采用密度通道表示（density-channel representation）将相互作用重新表述，并结合基于自由吉布斯态上符号表示（upper-symbol representation）的相干态变分方法（coherent-state variational method）进行证明。

Result: 在二维和三维中构造了极限非线性经典吉布斯测度（nonlinear classical Gibbs measure），证明了相对自由能（relative free energy）和任意固定阶的约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrices）的收敛性；三维结果依赖于对相互作用施加的小条件（smallness condition）。该框架作为特例涵盖了Lewin、Nam和Rougerie（2021）研究的均匀正定模型。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架成功推广了之前的工作，为具有非局部三体相互作用的玻色系统在高温平均场极限下的严格分析提供了统一的数学方法。

Abstract: We study the high-temperature mean-field limit of grand-canonical bosonic Gibbs states on the torus with renormalized nonlocal three-body interactions. In dimensions two and three, we construct the limiting nonlinear classical Gibbs measure and prove convergence of the relative free energy and of the reduced density matrices of every fixed order; in three dimensions, a smallness condition on the interaction is imposed. The proof combines the density-channel representation of the interaction with a coherent-state variational method based on the upper-symbol representation of the free Gibbs state. The same framework also contains, as a special case, the homogeneous positive-type model studied by Lewin, Nam, and Rougerie (2021).

</details>


### [205] [$\mathcal{P}(Φ)_2$ Theory from many-body quantum Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23084)
*Phan Thành Nam,Zhilin Yang,Xiangchan Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从多体量子吉布斯态（quantum Gibbs state）的严格极限出发，导出了二维单位环面上的 𝒫(Φ)₂ 测度，特别是 Φ^{2p}₂ 测度对应的量子模型。研究采用巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）描述，处理了具有一般对称性、无需因子化形式的 p-体相互作用势。与 Φ⁴₂ 情形不同，高阶相互作用下的 Wick 重整化（Wick renormalization）会产生一个完整的低阶相互作用层级，作者通过图形化形式体系（graphical formalism）对其进行了系统整理。分析的关键工具是一个对数稳定性估计（logarithmic stability estimate），该估计对非局域哈特里泛函（nonlocal Hartree functional）和多体哈密顿量均成立，且对相互作用范围一致（uniform），从而能够利用领头 p-体相互作用的 positivity 控制 Wick 反项（Wick counterterm）生成的所有低阶项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子场论中的 𝒫(Φ)₂ 测度作为多体量子系统的严格热力学极限进行构造，特别是将之前仅针对 Φ⁴₂ 情形的工作推广到一般 Φ^{2p}₂ 模型，以处理高阶相互作用带来的新挑战。

Method: 采用巨正则系综下的一般对称 p-体相互作用量子模型；使用图形化形式体系系统组织 Wick 重整化生成的低阶相互作用层级；引入对相互作用范围一致的对数稳定性估计，以控制 Wick 反项产生的低阶项。

Result: 成功将一般阶数的 Φ^{2p}₂ 测度严格推导为多体量子吉布斯态的极限；建立了对 Hartree 泛函和多体哈密顿量均一致成立的对数稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Fröhlich--Knowles--Schlein--Sohinger 针对 Φ⁴₂ 情形的结果推广到了任意阶的 𝒫(Φ)₂ 测度，为量子场论模型与多体量子统计力学（statistical mechanics）之间的严格联系提供了更一般的理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the $\mathcal{P}(Φ)_2$ measure on the two-dimensional unit torus as the rigorous limit of many-body quantum Gibbs states. In particular, the quantum model corresponding to the $Φ^{2p}_2$ measure is formulated in the grand-canonical ensemble with a general symmetric $p$-body interaction potential which is not required to have a factorized form. Unlike in the $Φ^4_2$ case investigated by Fröhlich--Knowles--Schlein--Sohinger in \cite{FKSS25}, in the treatment of higher-order interactions, Wick renormalization generates a full hierarchy of lower-order interactions which we organize systematically using a graphical formalism. One key ingredient of our analysis is a logarithmic stability estimate for both the nonlocal Hartree functional and the many-body Hamiltonian that is uniform in the interaction range, allowing us to use the positivity of the leading $p$-body interaction to control all lower-order terms generated by the Wick counterterms.

</details>


### [206] [Explicit higher order rational rogue waves of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23151)
*Robert Conte,ENS Paris-Saclay,The University of Hong Kong*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性薛定谔方程（NLS）的N阶有理怪波（rational rogue wave），提出了基于三项递推关系（three-term recurrence relation）的生成方法，仅需计算3个N-1阶行列式，相比传统方法需计算2个2N阶行列式效率大幅提升，并成功显式计算出带有六个任意复参数的七阶怪波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已证明无法通过非线性叠加公式（nonlinear superposition formula）生成依赖于2N-2个实参数的N阶有理怪波序列，需要更高效的构造方法。

Method: 采用三项递推关系生成该序列，每一步仅需计算关于2N-2个变量的3个N-1阶行列式，而先前方法需要计算关于2N个变量的2个2N阶行列式。

Result: 成功显式获得了七阶怪波及其六个任意复参数，表达式非常紧凑。

Conclusion: 这些紧凑的表达式为研究除已观察到的模式（同心环、多边形配置等）之外是否存在新模式提供了可能性。

Abstract: The $N$-th order rational rogue wave of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLS), which depends on $2 N-2$ real parameters, has been shown to be impossible to generate by the nonlinear superposition formula. We here generate this sequence by a three-term recurrence relation, each step only requiring the computation of three $N-1$-th order determinants of $2 N-2$ variables, while the previous method requires two determinants of order $2 N$ in $2 N$ variables. This allows us to obtain explicitly the seventh wave with its six arbitrary complex parameters. These very compact expressions open the possibility to investigate the possible existence of new patterns in addition to the already observed ones (concentric rings, polygonal configurations, \dots).

</details>


### [207] [Localization of quantum systems at Liouville tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23864)
*Ood Shabtai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在闭凯勒流形（closed Kähler manifold）的Berezin–Toeplitz量子化框架下，研究了一组两两交换的量子可观测量（pairwise commuting quantum observables），并假设其主符号满足Arnold–Liouville定理。通过联合本征截面（joint eigensections）将量子空间等距嵌入到固定刘维尔环面（Liouville torus）上的$L^2$空间，使得一大类量子可观测量（包括由不连续函数定义的）能作为$L^2(\Lambda_{a_0})$上的算子序列实现，并强收敛到乘法算子。讨论了该收敛的谱含义，并给出了在Lie代数表示收缩及量子可观测量谱投影对上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Berezin–Toeplitz量子化中，处理交换可观测量及其联合谱结构是一个基础问题。本文旨在建立一种统一的几何框架，将量子空间嵌入到经典环面（Liouville torus）上的$L^2$空间，从而将一大类量子算子（包括由间断函数定义的）实现为乘法算子的强极限，以揭示其谱行为并应用于表示论和谱投影。

Method: 假设一组两两交换的量子可观测量的主符号（principal symbols）满足Arnold–Liouville定理，利用这些可观测量的联合本征截面定义从量子空间到固定刘维尔环面$\Lambda_{a_0}$上的$L^2$空间的等距嵌入。在该嵌入下，将量子可观测量（包括由不连续函数定义的）构造为$L^2(\Lambda_{a_0})$上的算子序列。

Result: 所构造的量子算子序列强收敛到乘法算子（multiplication operators）。该收敛性具有谱含义，可用于分析量子可观测量的谱行为。进一步，该结果可应用于Lie代数表示收缩（contractions of Lie algebra representations）以及量子可观测量谱投影对（pairs of spectral projections）的研究。

Conclusion: 本文建立的嵌入框架将量子算子的强收敛性与经典环面上的乘法算子联系起来，为交换量子可观测量提供了统一的谱分析方法。该结果在Lie代数表示收缩和谱投影对中的应用展示了其理论价值。

Abstract: We consider a collection of pairwise commuting quantum observables in the setting of Berezin--Toeplitz quantization of a closed Kähler manifold and assume that the Arnold--Liouville theorem applies to their principal symbols. We use joint eigensections of these observables to define isometric embeddings of the quantum spaces into $L^2(Λ_{a_0})$, where $Λ_{a_0}$ is a fixed Liouville torus. These embeddings allow a broad class of quantum observables, including some defined by discontinuous functions, to be realized as sequences of operators on $L^2(Λ_{a_0})$ that converge strongly to multiplication operators. We discuss the spectral implications of this convergence and give applications to contractions of Lie algebra representations and to pairs of spectral projections of quantum observables.

</details>


### [208] [A phase transition for the hard sphere model on the hyperbolic plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23873)
*Lewis Bowen,Marcus Michelen,Will Perkins*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了双曲平面中硬球模型（hard sphere model）存在相变（phase transition），这一结果在欧氏平面和三维空间中仍是长期未解决的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬球模型是统计物理（statistical physics）中的经典模型，物理学文献预测其在二维和三维欧氏空间中存在相变，但这一结论长期缺乏严格数学证明。本文旨在严格证明该模型在双曲平面中确实发生相变。

Method: 基于统计物理中的硬球模型，粒子用等大小的球体表示，在双曲平面（hyperbolic plane）的几何框架下进行分析，利用双曲空间的负曲率（negative curvature）特性来建立相变存在的严格证明。

Result: 在双曲平面中成功证明了硬球模型存在相变，而在$\mathbb{R}^2$和$\mathbb{R}^3$中该问题仍是重大开放问题。

Conclusion: 双曲几何的负曲率性质使得硬球模型在双曲平面中能够严格证明相变的存在，为理解统计物理中相变现象的几何依赖性提供了新见解。

Abstract: The hard sphere model is a classical model from statistical physics in which particles are represented by equal-sized spheres. Longstanding predictions from the physics literature indicate that in $\mathbb{R}^2$ and $\mathbb{R}^3$ the system undergoes a phase transition, but it remains a major open problem to confirm this. We prove the existence of a phase transition for this model in the hyperbolic plane.

</details>


### [209] [Local Weyl law and length-minimising loops on hyperbolic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24060)
*Daniel Meriaz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定光滑能量窗口上，对大量Weil–Petersson双曲曲面取平均时，局部Weyl律的方差。结果与Berry随机波模型的预测一致，并引入了基于点的长度最小化测地环（length-minimising geodesic loops）等几何概念，结合Mirzakhani的工作与精确驻相法（stationary phase arguments），明确了方差渐近行为的主要项与误差项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证随机波模型（random wave model）对局部Weyl律方差的预测，并建立该方差在Weil–Petersson双曲曲面系综上的精确渐近行为。

Method: 利用Mirzakhani的工作并结合精确驻相法（stationary phase arguments）来识别主项与误差项；引入长度最小化测地环（length-minimising geodesic loops）及其序列的概念，并证明其拓扑完全特征（均为简单环），以此约束依赖于不同短本原测地环（primitive geodesic loops）长度的余项贡献。

Result: 局部Weyl律方差的渐近行为得以明确表达，且与Berry随机波模型的预测一致；长度最小化测地环被证明是简单的，这为简化余项估计提供了新的论证。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅验证了随机波模型的预测，还通过几何新概念为相关余项贡献提供了精简的刻划；文中进一步引入的“探索环”（exploring loops）族可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We study the variance of a local Weyl law over a fixed smooth energy window, when averaged over large Weil--Petersson hyperbolic surfaces. Our results are consistent with the predictions of Berry's random wave model. Our approach allows to explicitly integrate certain test functions which depend on lengths of based geodesic loops, and relate them to the associated lengths of the closed geodesics in their free-homotopy class. We thus utilise the work of Mirzakhani, with exact stationary phase arguments, to identify correct main and error terms, making explicit the asymptotic behaviour of the variance of the local Weyl law. Furthermore, we introduce the geometric notion of length-minimising geodesic loops and sequences, based at a point. We prove a complete characterisation of the topology of these, namely that they are simple. This forms a key ingredient in our study, and yields a new streamlined argument to bound the contributions of remainder terms which depend on lengths of pairs of different short primitive geodesic loops. To illustrate the generality of our results, we further introduce a family of "exploring" loops based at a point, which might be of independent interest.

</details>


### [210] [A doubled Gordon threshold for palindromic quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24188)
*Wencai Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一频准周期薛定谔算子（quasiperiodic Schrödinger operator），提出一种新的Gordon型方法，同时利用近似重复和回文对称性（palindromic symmetry），证明了在完全共振相位下，若Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）小于2β(α)，则E不能成为特征值。作为应用，对几乎马蒂厄算子（almost Mathieu operator）证明了在特定参数范围内谱为纯奇异连续谱（purely singular continuous spectrum），解决了Avila-Jitomirskaya猜想中关于完全共振相位下尖锐谱跃迁（sharp spectral transition）的剩余无特征值部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Avila和Jitomirskaya关于几乎马蒂厄算子（almost Mathieu operator）在完全共振相位下尖锐谱跃迁（sharp spectral transition）猜想中，尚未被证明的“无特征值（absence of eigenvalues）”部分。

Method: 发展了一种新的Gordon型方法（Gordon-type method），该方法同时利用了近似重复序列（approximate repetitions）和回文对称性（palindromic symmetries），并结合Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponent）L(E)与频率逼近指数β(α)之间的关系进行分析。

Result: 对一频准周期薛定谔算子（quasiperiodic Schrödinger operator），证明了在完全共振相位（completely resonant phase）2θ∈αℤ+ℤ下，若L(E)<2β(α)，则E不能成为特征值。对几乎马蒂厄算子（almost Mathieu operator），证明了当2θ∈αℤ+ℤ且1<|λ|<e^{2β(α)}时，该算子具有纯奇异连续谱（purely singular continuous spectrum）。

Conclusion: 该结果完成了Avila-Jitomirskaya猜想中关于完全共振相位下尖锐谱跃迁（sharp spectral transition）的证明，特别是填补了谱纯奇异连续区域中“无特征值”部分的空白。

Abstract: We consider one-frequency quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators \[ (H_{v,α,θ}u)(n) =
u(n+1)+u(n-1) +
v(θ+nα)u(n) \] acting on $\ell^2(\mathbb Z)$, where $α\notin\mathbb Q$ and $v\in C^2(\mathbb T,\mathbb R)$ is an even function. We develop a new Gordon-type method that exploits approximate repetitions and palindromic symmetries simultaneously. Denote by $L(E)$ the Lyapunov exponent and let
  \[β(α) =
\limsup_{|k|\to\infty} -\frac{\log\|kα\|_{\mathbb R/\mathbb Z}}{|k|}. \] We prove that, for every completely resonant phase $2θ\inα\mathbb Z+\mathbb Z$,
  $E$ cannot be an eigenvalue if $L(E)<2β(α)$.
  As an application, consider the almost Mathieu operator \[ (H_{λ,α,θ}u)(n) =
u(n+1)+u(n-1) +
2λ\cos\bigl(2π(θ+nα)\bigr)u(n). \] We show that if $2θ\inα\mathbb Z+\mathbb Z$ and $1<|λ|<e^{2β(α)}$, then $H_{λ,α,θ}$ has purely singular continuous spectrum. This resolves the remaining absence-of-eigenvalues part of a conjecture of Avila and Jitomirskaya concerning the sharp spectral transition for completely resonant phases.

</details>


### [211] [Milnor metric for Morse--Smale flows from field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24238)
*Giovanni Molinari,Michele Schiavina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究阿贝尔BF理论在Morse-Smale矢量场轴向规范固定条件下的配分函数，证明其恢复了流形扭曲上同调行列式线上的Milnor度量。利用Batalin-Vilkovisky形式论的两步推出到上同调的方法，该Milnor度量作为同时包含闭轨道和临界点的动力系统中Ruelle动力zeta函数在零点处取值的推广，结合Schwarz关于Lorenz规范下BF理论配分函数的经典结果，为Fried猜想提供了一个场论实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文试图从量子场论（quantum field theory）角度为Fried猜想（Fried's conjecture）提供场论实现。该猜想涉及规范固定后的阿贝尔BF理论配分函数与动力系统zeta函数之间的深层联系，但此前缺乏统一的场论框架。

Method: 采用Batalin-Vilkovisky（BV）形式论，特别是通过两步BV推出（BV pushforward）到上同调（cohomology）的技术。在轴向规范固定条件下，将配分函数与扭曲上同调行列式线上的Milnor metric（Milnor度量）建立对应关系。

Result: 证明了阿贝尔BF理论在Morse-Smale矢量场轴向规范下的配分函数恢复了Milnor度量；该Milnor度量被推广为同时包含闭轨道（closed orbits）和临界点（critical points）的动力系统中Ruelle动力zeta函数（Ruelle dynamical zeta function）在零点处的取值。

Conclusion: 该结果与Schwarz关于Lorenz规范下BF理论配分函数的经典结果相结合，共同构成了Fried猜想的场论实现。该猜想对Morse-Smale流（Morse-Smale flows）成立。

Abstract: We study the partition function of Abelian $BF$ theory with an axial gauge fixing condition given by a Morse--Smale vector field, and we show that it recovers the Milnor metric on the determinant line on the twisted cohomology of the manifold. To do this we use the Batalin--Vilkovisky formalism and, in particular, a BV pushforward to cohomology in two steps. In this context, the Milnor metric serves as the generalisation of the Ruelle dynamical zeta function evaluated at zero for systems containing both closed orbits and critical points. This, together with a classic result due to Schwarz on the partition function of $BF$ theory in the Lorenz gauge, provides a field-theoretic realisation of Fried's conjecture, which is true for Morse--Smale flows.

</details>


### [212] [A turning point principle for liquid Lane-Emden stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24530)
*King Ming Lam*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有“硬化气体”状态方程的液体系列-埃姆登星（liquid Lane-Emden stars），建立了与质量-半径曲线转折点（turning points）相联系的径向增长模式数变化定理（turning point principle），并分析了高中心密度极限下质量-半径曲线的螺旋行为与模式数有限/无限增长的条件，进而推广了非径向稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与气体情形不同，液体自由边界破坏了族的自相似性（self-similarity），使质量-半径曲线发生弯曲并在特定绝热指数γ条件下产生极值点。此前缺少针对此类液体系列的、类似Zel'dovich-Wheeler-Thorne及其他相对论性转折点原理的严格分析框架。

Method: 通过固定绝热指数，以中心密度κ为参数构造一参数族；利用线性化算子的负Morse指标（negative Morse index）定义径向增长模式（radial growing modes）数目；将增长模式数变化与质量-半径曲线转折点的弯曲方向相联系；在中心密度极限下，借助平面动力系统（planar dynamical system）分析气体尾部行为以判断曲线是否螺旋（spiral）。

Result: 径向增长模式数沿族局部恒定，仅可在质量-半径曲线转折点处发生变化，且弯曲方向决定增长模式的增益或损失。高中心密度极限下，气体尾部行为决定质量-半径曲线是否螺旋，从而决定增长模式数是否保持有界或趋向无穷。据此恢复并加强了已知的液体系列-埃姆登星径向（不）稳定性结果，并结合现有非径向分析给出了非径向稳定性的转折点判据。

Conclusion: 本文为液体系列-埃姆登星提供了严格的转折点原理，揭示了质量-半径曲线几何与线性稳定性谱之间的定量联系，并为非径向稳定性提供了统一的转折点判据。

Abstract: Upon fixing the adiabatic index, the spherically symmetric liquid Lane-Emden stars governed by the Euler-Poisson system with a "stiffened gas" equation of state $p=ρ^γ-1$ form a one-parameter family, naturally parametrised by the central density $κ=\barρ_κ(0)\in(1,\infty)$. In contrast to the gaseous case, the liquid free boundary breaks the self-similarity of the family and bends the mass-radius curve, creating extrema of the mass when $γ<2(d-1)/d$. We formulate a turning point principle in the spirit of Zel'dovich, Wheeler and Thorne, and of its rigorous relativistic counterpart from the works of Hadžić, Lin and Rein: the number of radial growing modes equals the negative Morse index of the linearised operator, is locally constant along the family, and can change only at the turning points of the mass-radius curve, the bending orientation there dictating whether a growing mode is gained or lost. We describe the large central density limit in which the gaseous tail (read off from a planar dynamical system) determines whether the mass-radius curve spirals, and hence whether the number of growing modes remains bounded or tends to infinity. As corollaries we recover and sharpen the known radial (in)stability results for liquid Lane-Emden stars and, in combination with existing non-radial analysis, obtain a turning point criterion for non-radial stability.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Measuring Cosmic Neutrino Masses Independently of Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24742)
*Frank J. Qu,Fei Ge,Hanyue Wang,Emmanuel Schaan,W. L. Kimmy Wu,Alexander Friedland,Blake D. Sherwin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了宇宙学中微子质量上限对暗能量（dark energy）模型的依赖性，指出传统ΛCDM框架下∑m_ν<0.056 eV（95% CL）的强约束在模型扩展后显著放松。作者提出了两种稳健路径：一是现有暗能量边缘化路径，在灵活暗能量历史下仍保持约束能力，得到∑m_ν<0.152 eV；二是新提出的“晚期宇宙无关”路径，仅依赖原初CMB和重构透镜谱，对任意暗能量模型均给出∑m_ν<0.41 eV。这些放松的上限恰好落在下一代实验室实验（如Project 8）的探测灵敏度范围内，凸显了宇宙学与地面实验协同的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡表明中微子有质量，这是超出标准模型（Standard Model）的唯一实验室证据。宇宙学在ΛCDM框架下对中微子质量总和给出极强约束（∑m_ν<0.056 eV），与反序（inverted-ordering）下限（0.10 eV）存在2-3σ的张力；然而数据同时暗示暗能量可能演化，因此需要评估中微子质量上限对暗能量模型依赖的程度。

Method: 系统解构每个宇宙学探针对晚期物理的敏感性，提出两种稳健路径：（i）暗能量边缘化路径，保留所有数据并对暗能量状态方程参数(w₀, wₐ)以及更灵活的binned和cubic w(a)历史进行边缘化；（ii）晚期宇宙无关路径，结合原初CMB（通过A_lens边缘化声学峰平滑效应）与重构透镜谱C_L^{κκ}，从构造上消除对晚期宇宙膨胀历史的依赖。

Result: 暗能量边缘化路径给出∑m_ν<0.152 eV，加入Simons Observatory透镜和Spec-S5 BAO后可收紧至σ(∑m_ν)≈0.043 eV。晚期宇宙无关路径在所有测试的暗能量模型下给出∑m_ν<0.41 eV，未来可收紧至0.31 eV（Simons Observatory）和0.28 eV（宇宙方差极限）。这些放松的上限用统计精度换取了模型独立性。

Conclusion: 放松后的中微子质量上限恰好落在下一代实验室实验如Project 8（m_{ν_e}~0.1 eV）的探测灵敏度范围内，这为未来宇宙学与地面中微子测量之间的协同研究提供了重要动机。

Abstract: Neutrino oscillations establish that neutrinos are massive, providing the only laboratory detection of physics beyond the Standard Model. Direct kinematic experiments bound the electron-neutrino mass to $m_{ν_e} < 0.45$ eV (KATRIN, 90% CL), implying $\sum m_ν\lesssim 1.3$ eV. Conversely, cosmology within $Λ$CDM is highly constraining: Planck CMB, CMB lensing, and DESI DR2 BAO yield $\sum m_ν< 0.056$ eV (95% CL), in 2-3$σ$ tension with the inverted-ordering floor (0.10 eV). However, this bound relies on $Λ$CDM, while data hint at an evolving dark energy. To determine the model dependence of cosmic neutrino mass bounds, we deconstruct each probe's sensitivity to late-time physics and pursue two robust routes to a $\sum m_ν$ bound: (i) The existing dark-energy-marginalized route, retaining all data and marginalizing over $(w_0, w_a)$, is shown to also be immune to flexible binned and cubic $w(a)$ histories, yielding $\sum m_ν< 0.152$ eV, sharpening to $σ(\sum m_ν) \approx 0.043$ eV with Simons Observatory lensing and Spec-S5 BAO. (ii) A new late-Universe-free route combines primary CMB, marginalizing over acoustic-peak smoothing via $A_{\rm lens}$, with the reconstructed lensing spectrum $C_L^{κκ}$, removing late-time expansion dependence by construction. This yields $\sum m_ν< 0.41$ eV today, tightening to 0.31 eV (Simons Observatory) and 0.28 eV (cosmic-variance limit) across all tested dark-energy models. These relaxed bounds trade statistical power for model independence. Interestingly, they land in the sensitivity range targeted by next-generation laboratory experiments like Project 8 ($m_{ν_e} \sim 0.1$ eV), motivating vital synergies between future cosmological and terrestrial neutrino measurements.

</details>


### [214] [Stability in Axion Inflation with Strong Backreaction from a Massive Vector Boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22893)
*Michael J. Baker,Joaquim Iguaz Juan,Lorenzo Sorbo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究具有有质量U(1)规范场的轴子暴涨（axion inflation）模型，发现当规范场质量足够大时，强反作用（strong backreaction）区域的转变过程是平滑的，避免了传统无质量规范场情形下出现的非线性不稳定性（instability），从而为实现可控制的强反作用暴涨提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无质量规范场耦合的轴子暴涨模型中，当规范场对暴胀子（inflaton）产生强烈反作用时，系统会出现一种不稳定性，其非线性演化和最终状态至今难以理解。本文旨在探索通过引入有质量规范场来规避这一不稳定性，从而实现可控的强反作用暴涨。

Method: 本文对耦合了有质量U(1)规范场的轴子暴涨模型进行修改研究，通过比较有质量与无质量规范场情形下强反作用区域的转变行为，分析质量参数如何影响系统的动力学稳定性。

Result: 研究表明，当规范场具有足够大的质量时，系统向强反作用区域的转变是平滑的，从而避免了无质量情形下出现的不稳定性。这表明有质量规范场的轴子暴涨模型存在一个可控的强反作用区域。

Conclusion: 该发现意味着，在有质量规范场的轴子暴涨模型中，可以实现可控的强反作用暴涨，从而可能使得此类模型在现象学上成为暴涨的可行实现方案。

Abstract: We study a modification of the model of axion inflation coupled to a $U(1)$ gauge field where the vector field is massive. In the conventional scenario with a massless gauge field, the onset of the regime where the gauge field strongly backreacts on the inflaton displays an instability whose nonlinear evolution and endpoint remain poorly understood. We argue that, if the gauge field is massive enough, the transition to the strong backreaction regime instead occurs smoothly, avoiding this instability. This observation suggests that axion inflation with massive gauge fields admits a controllable strong backreaction regime, so that a phenomenologically viable realization of inflation might be possible in this class of models.

</details>


### [215] [Probing Dark Matter Substructure with Wave-Optics Distortions of Strongly Lensed LISA Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22787)
*Tonghua Liu,Kai Liao,Marek Biesiada,Jieci Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究强引力透镜效应（strong lensing）中引力波信号的相位变化能否区分三种暗物质结构（NFW晕、自相互作用晕SIDM、模糊暗物质场FDM）。基于LISA任务四年的可探测透镜化大质量黑洞双星种群，模拟生成了波形信号，并以光滑奇异等温椭球透镜加外部剪切进行拟合。结果表明，NFW和SIDM的缓慢变化部分与光滑透镜的常数、梯度和曲率高度简并；而FDM的相干密度涨落留下更大的频率依赖残差，使得NFW-FDM和SIDM-FDM种群分别需要约60和110个可分辨像波形即可区分，证明重复透镜化LISA信号能够探测透镜星系中暗物质的空间形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强引力透镜研究主要利用振幅和到达时间信息，本工作探索引力波相位变化是否能够区分透镜星系中不同类型的暗物质结构（NFW、SIDM、FDM），从而超越仅测量总透镜质量的局限。

Method: 生成了LISA四年任务中可探测的透镜化大质量黑洞双星种群的波形，对每个信号用相同的具有外部剪切的光滑奇异等温椭球透镜模型进行拟合，并分析拟合残差。

Result: 在132个透镜系统中，有311个像在时间上被分辨为独立信号。NFW和SIDM的缓慢变化部分与光滑透镜的常数、梯度和曲率项高度简并；FDM的相干密度涨落则在拟合后留下更大的频率依赖残差。NFW-FDM和SIDM-FDM种群分别需要约60和110个可分辨像波形即可区分。

Conclusion: 重复透镜化的LISA信号能够探测透镜星系中暗物质的空间形态，而不仅仅是总透镜质量，这为利用引力波相位信息进行暗物质结构区分提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Strong lensing changes the phase of a gravitational-wave signal as well as its amplitude and arrival time. We study whether this phase information can distinguish three dark-matter structures in the lens: a Navarro--Frenk--White halo (NFW), a self-interacting halo (SIDM), and a fuzzy-dark-matter field (FDM). We generate waveforms for the detectable lensed massive-black-hole-binary population of a four-year LISA mission and fit every signal with the same smooth singular-isothermal-ellipsoid lens with external shear. In 132 lens systems, 311 images are resolved as separate signals in time. NFW and SIDM produce real waveform changes, but their slowly varying part is largely degenerate with the constant, gradient, and curvature of a smooth lens. The coherent density fluctuations of FDM leave a larger frequency-dependent residual after this fit. The NFW--FDM and SIDM--FDM populations become distinguishable with about 60 and 110 resolved image waveforms, respectively. These results show that repeated lensed LISA signals can probe the spatial form of dark matter in lens galaxies, rather than only the total lensing mass.

</details>


### [216] [ALAverse: A falsifiable anthropic model from the string landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24100)
*Zhiqi Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文结合轴子景观（axiverse）与负宇宙学常数（cosmological constant），引入观测时间加权的人择原理（observation-time-weighted anthropic argument），构建了一个人择Lambda-轴子宇宙（anthropic Lambda-axion universe, ALAverse）模型，自然解决了暗能量的精细调节和巧合问题。该模型预测中等滚动（moderate-roll）动力学，并在当前观测数据中以2.7σ显著度拒绝了ΛCDM模型，同时对未来高精度哈勃图观测具有可证伪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗能量的精细调节问题（fine-tuning problem）和巧合问题（coincidence problem）是当前宇宙学的核心难题。现有标量场暗能量模型通常需要人为调节初始条件，而本文旨在利用弦论景观（string landscape）自然提供的轴子景观与负宇宙学常数，通过人择原理给出一个无需精细调节的暗能量理论框架。

Method: 采用均匀先验（uniform prior）假设宇宙学常数$Λ$的分布，并将轴子衰变常数（axion decay constant）设定为约化普朗克尺度（reduced Planck scale）量级，结合观测时间加权的人择论证（observation-time-weighted anthropic argument），对暗能量动力学进行分类筛选。推导出一个双参数（$δ_Ω$和$|\varepsilon_s|$）参数化方案，涵盖ALAverse解及广泛的典型场（canonical field）和幻像场（phantom field）模型。

Result: 该框架预测观测到$0.1 < Ω_m < 0.9$的概率约为40%。负宇宙学常数使得快滚动（fast-roll）情形在人择上不利，慢滚动（slow-roll）因需要初始场位移的精细调节而被统计上排斥，故自然导向中等滚动。当前观测数据给出$δ_Ω= -0.0498 \pm 0.0186$，对应ΛCDM模型（$δ_Ω= 0$）和幻像模型（$δ_Ω>0$）分别被以2.7σ拒绝；数据还显示出对ALAverse优于慢滚动精质场（slow-roll quintessence）的微弱偏好。

Conclusion: ALAverse模型为暗能量的精细调节与巧合问题提供了一种自然的弦论人择解释，并成功预测了中等滚动动力学。未来对哈勃图（Hubble diagram）的高精度测量有望对模型预测进行决定性检验。

Abstract: We combine the axiverse with a negative cosmological constant $Λ$, both originating from the string landscape, and apply an observation-time-weighted anthropic argument. Adopting a uniform prior on $Λ$ and a typical string-motivated axion decay constant comparable to the reduced Planck scale, this framework predicts a $\sim 40\%$ probability of observing $0.1 < Ω_m < 0.9$, thereby naturally resolving the long-standing fine-tuning and coincidence problems of dark energy. Unlike many scalar-field dark energy models, the anthropic Lambda-axion universe (ALAverse) statistically disfavors slow-roll dynamics, as slow-roll requires fine-tuning of the initial field displacement. Moreover, the negative cosmological constant renders fast-roll scenarios anthropically unfavorable, since they typically yield only a very brief observational window with positive dark energy density. Having ruled out both extremes, the ALAverse characteristically predicts a moderate-roll dynamics. We derive a two-parameter parametrization in terms of $δ_Ω$ and $|\varepsilon_s|$ that covers ALAverse solutions as well as a broad class of canonical and phantom field models. Current observational data yield $δ_Ω= -0.0498 \pm 0.0186$, corresponding to a $2.7σ$ rejection of $Λ$CDM ($δ_Ω= 0$) and phantom models ($δ_Ω> 0$). The data also show a mild preference for the ALAverse over slow-roll quintessence, a trend that can be conclusively tested with future high-precision measurements of the Hubble diagram.

</details>


### [217] [Cosmic microwave background temperature cosmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23535)
*C. J. A. P. Martins*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文运用宇宙学（cosmography）这一模型独立的方法，结合Sunyaev-Zeldovich效应与高分辨率光谱观测数据（红移范围0≤z≤6.34），系统约束了标准温度-红移关系T(z)=T₀(1+z)的偏离，发现偏离被限制在百分比量级，并比较了不同宇宙学方法及典型绝热拓展模型（Lima模型）的约束效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学模型中的温度-红移关系T(z)=T₀(1+z)在诸多物理拓展模型（如光子数不守恒情形）中会被破坏，需要借助模型无关的方法对该关系进行精确检验，以甄别超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 采用宇宙学方法（cosmography）这一模型无关（model-independent）途径，使用天体物理的Sunyaev-Zeldovich效应与高分辨率光谱测量数据（红移范围0≤z≤6.34），并对比不同宇宙学展开变量及Lima绝热拓展（adiabatic extension）模型下的约束结果。

Result: 标准温度-红移关系的偏离被约束在百分比量级，单一非标准参数即可描述这些偏离；直接拟合的温度-红移关系斜率与FIRAS推断结果一致但略低；标准宇宙学中基于尺度因子（scale factor）展开的惯用方法不适用于该观测量，Padé近似相对于普通泰勒级数并无显著优势。

Conclusion: 宇宙学方法可以有效约束温度-红移关系的偏离，且偏离程度很小，表明标准关系在现有数据下高度成立；不同宇宙学展开的选择对结果有一定影响，但整体约束稳健。

Abstract: The temperature-redshift relation, $T(z)=T_0(1+z)$, is a cornerstone of the standard cosmological model, but it is violated in many physically motivated extensions thereof, e.g. when photon number is not conserved. We apply the methodology of cosmography, a model-independent approach to cosmology, to this observable, and use astrophysical Sunyaev-Zeldovich and high-resolution spectroscopic measurements spanning the redshift range $0\le z\le6.34$, to constrain violations of the standard relation. We explore the impact of different cosmographic approaches and expansion variable choices and also compare the cosmographic constraints with those obtained, with the same data, in the canonical adiabatic extension model of Lima. While these choices have some impact, overall we find that such violations are constrained to percent level, and that a single non-standard parameter suffices to describe them. We also show that the directly fitted slope of the temperature-redshift relation is compatible with (but slightly smaller than) the one inferred from FIRAS, and that standard cosmography lore, based on scale factor expansions, does not apply to this observable, e.g. Padé approximants have no meaningful advantage over plain Taylor series.

</details>


### [218] [Gravitational waves from super-Hubble bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24734)
*Pulkit S. Ghoderao,Arttu Rajantie,Danièle Steer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究暴胀期间产生但直到辐射主导时期才发生碰撞与渗流的宇宙学一级相变气泡，发现超哈勃尺度的气泡碰撞产生的引力波峰值频率更低、振幅更强，可被LIGO、NANOGrav、LISA等现有及未来引力波实验探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴胀期间成核的真真空气泡通常在暴胀结束时碰撞，产生的引力波频率位于MHz量级且振幅有限。本文旨在探索气泡尺寸远超哈勃长度（Hubble length）时对引力波观测信号的影响，并评估其被现有及未来引力波探测器捕捉的可能性。

Method: 采用一个简单的说明性模型，在理论上分析暴胀期间成核但直到辐射主导时期（radiation-dominated era）才发生碰撞的气泡的演化行为，并计算这些超哈勃气泡（super-Hubble bubbles）碰撞所产生的引力波频谱特征。

Result: 超哈勃气泡的共动半径决定了峰值频率可远低于MHz频段；在相同碰撞起始时间下，其引力波振幅相比哈勃尺度或更小的气泡显著增强。综合效应使得这类引力波信号落入LIGO、NANOGrav、LISA等探测器的可观测频率与振幅范围。

Conclusion: 暴胀后不久发生的超哈勃气泡碰撞所产生的引力波，在频段和振幅上均可被现有及未来的引力波实验探测到，为研究早期宇宙的一级相变提供了新的观测窗口。

Abstract: We consider a cosmological first-order phase transition in which bubbles of true vacuum nucleate during inflation but do not collide and percolate until the Universe has entered the radiation-dominated era. If the collisions take place soon after the end of inflation, the size of these bubbles can be significantly greater than the Hubble length. This has two important consequences for the gravitational waves produced by the bubble collisions: First, as the peak frequency is determined by the comoving bubble radius, it can be well below the MHz frequencies typical for bubble collisions at the end of inflation. Second, when comparing collisions beginning at the same time, the amplitude of the gravitational waves is greatly enhanced relative to Hubble-sized and smaller bubbles. Together, these two effects mean that gravitational waves produced by super-Hubble bubbles soon after the end of inflation can be within the observable frequency and amplitude ranges of LIGO and NANOGrav, as well as LISA and other future gravitational wave experiments. We demonstrate this with a simple illustrative model.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>
