### [219] [Effects of flavor-mixings on charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23497)
*Fabio L. Braghin,Parada T. P. Hutauruk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在U(3) Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型中，引入一种源于真空极化的新型味道混合（flavor-mixing）相互作用，采用适当时正则化方案（proper-time regularization scheme），计算了带电K介子和π介子的部分子分布函数（PDFs），并发现混合效应强度与夸克有效质量及其差异成正比，在特定x区间对PDFs产生显著影响，改善了理论结果与实验数据的吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多采用瞬子诱导的't Hooft相互作用处理味道混合效应，但该方法存在局限性；本文旨在探索由真空极化产生的不同味道混合相互作用对介子PDFs的影响，以更精确地描述带电K介子和π介子的夸克分布。

Method: 采用U(3) Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型，引入源于真空极化的新型味道混合相互作用（flavor-mixing interaction）；使用适当时正则化方案（proper-time regularization scheme）以有效计入夸克禁闭（quark confinement）；通过求解间隙方程（gap equations）、介子质量和介子-夸克耦合常数，计算带电K介子和π介子的价夸克分布（valence-quark distributions），并在标度μ² = 4和27 GeV²下与实验数据及JAM分析结果进行对比。

Result: 味道混合效应强度与夸克有效质量及其差异成正比；π介子和K介子的价夸克分布中，混合效应分别在x ≃ 0.3和x ≃ 0.8（π介子）以及x ≃ 0.2和x ≃ 0.7（K介子）处最为显著；两种标度下的混合效应强度相似，但整体改善了π介子和K介子的PDFs与实验数据的吻合度。

Conclusion: 新型味道混合相互作用能够有效改进π介子和K介子部分子分布函数的理论描述，其影响在特定动量分数x区间集中体现，且与夸克有效质量差异密切相关。

Abstract: We investigate the charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions (PDFs) in the U(3) Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, considering a novel flavor-mixing interaction arising from vacuum polarization that differs from the instanton-induced 't~Hooft interaction. In this work, the proper-time regularization scheme is employed, and, effectively, it might take quark confinement into account. The gap equations, the meson masses, and the meson--quark coupling constants are calculated in the presence of flavor mixing interactions. The valence-quark distributions of the charged kaon and pion are calculated and compared with existing experimental data and JAM analysis results at scales $μ^{2} =$ 4 and 27 GeV$^{2}$. The strength of mixing effects on the PDFs is found to be proportional to the quark effective masses and their differences. We further find that the dominant flavor mixing effects in the pion valence up-quark distribution at scales $μ^{2} =$ 27 and 4 GeV$^2$ are around $x \simeq 0.3$ and $x \simeq 0.8$, while for the kaon, the effects are shown at around $x \simeq 0.2$ and $x \simeq 0.7$. We also find similar strength of mixing effects on the PDFs at both scales $μ^{2} =4$ and 27 GeV$^{2}$; however, it certainly improves the pion and kaon PDFs.

</details>


### [220] [Electromagnetic form factors of singly charmed baryons $Σ_c$ and $Λ_c$ in a covariant quark-diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23943)
*Ye Cao,Cheng Chen,Dongyan Fu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变夸克-双夸克模型（covariant quark-diquark model）中系统研究了基态单粲重子（singly charmed baryons）$\Sigma_c$ 和 $\Lambda_c^+$ 的类空电磁形状因子，获得了磁矩、电荷半径与磁矩半径，并基于解析渐近关系预测了 $e^+e^-\to\Sigma_c\bar\Sigma_c$ 的总截面。关键发现包括：粲重子的电荷形状因子随动量转移 $Q^2$ 衰减远慢于质子，表明其电荷分布更紧凑；$\Sigma_c$ 的磁形状因子由轻轴矢量双夸克主导，而 $\Lambda_c^+$ 的磁形状因子则由粲夸克主导，揭示了轻双夸克自旋构型（spin configuration）对磁性质的決定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单粲重子的内部结构与内部动力学（inner dynamics），需要通过电磁形状因子（electromagnetic form factors）、磁矩（magnetic moments）以及电荷/磁矩半径等可观测量来揭示其物理机制。

Method: 采用协变夸克-双夸克模型（covariant quark-diquark model）对 $\Sigma_c$（$\Sigma_c^{++},\Sigma_c^+,\Sigma_c^0$）和 $\Lambda_c^+$ 的类空电磁形状因子进行系统计算，并利用连接类空与类时区域的解析渐近关系（empirical asymptotic relation）预测 $e^+e^-\to\Sigma_c\bar\Sigma_c$ 的总截面。

Result: 理论计算与已有格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）对 $\Sigma_c^{++}$ 和 $\Sigma_c^0$ 的结果定性一致。单粲重子的电形状因子随 $Q^2$ 衰减远慢于质子，表明其电荷分布更紧凑；$\Sigma_c$ 的磁形状因子由轻轴矢量双夸克主导，$\Lambda_c^+$ 的磁形状因子则由粲夸克主导。

Conclusion: 轻双夸克的自旋构型（spin configuration）在决定磁性质中起到决定性作用。预测的 $e^+e^-\to\Sigma_c\bar\Sigma_c$ 总截面可在 BESIII、Belle II、LHCb 以及拟议中的 Super Tau-Charm Facility 等现有或未来设施中进行检验。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the spacelike electromagnetic form factors of the ground-state singly charmed baryons, $Σ_c$ ($Σ_c^{++},Σ_c^+,Σ_c^0$) and $Λ_c^+$, within a covariant quark-diquark model. Based on this framework, we obtain their magnetic moments as well as electric charge and magnetic moment radii. Such observables are important to understand the internal structure and the inner dynamics of these heavy baryon states. Our theoretical calculations are in qualitative agreement with available lattice QCD results of $Σ_c^{++}$ and $Σ_c^0$ baryons. We also discuss the mechanism behind the dependence of the numerical results on the charm quark and light diquark. A key finding is that the electric form factors of the singly charmed baryons fall off much more slowly with momentum transfer $Q^2$ than that of the proton, indicating a more compact electric charge distribution, which is attributed to the heavy charm quark acting as a localized core. More importantly, we observe a striking difference in the magnetic structure: while the magnetic form factors of $Σ_c$ are dominated by the light axial-vector diquark, those of $Λ_c^+$ are unexpectedly governed by the charm quark due to the vanishing contribution of the scalar diquark. This highlights the decisive role of the light-diquark spin configuration in determining the magnetic properties. Finally, using an empirical asymptotic relation connecting the spacelike and timelike regions, we predict the total cross section for $e^+e^-\toΣ_c\barΣ_c$. Our findings can be tested at existing facilities including the BESIII, Belle II and LHCb, as well as the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility.

</details>


### [221] [Prospects for observing the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24203)
*Yueling Yang,Bingbing Yang,Junfeng Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于LHC上赝标量顶夸克偶素（pseudoscalar toponium）η_t的观测，系统研究了其衰变过程η_t → W⁺W⁻，通过两种衰变常数方案估计了分支比，并指出在HL-LHC阶段通过双轻子级联衰变通道可实现数十个可观测事例，该无b喷注（b-jet）的干净双轻子（dilepton）信号可作为互补且更灵敏的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LHC上观测到赝标量顶夸克偶素η_t及其性质研究的持续关注驱动，本文旨在对η_t的特征衰变过程η_t → W⁺W⁻进行唯象学研究，以深化对这一新粒子衰变行为的理解。

Method: 采用两种衰变常数f_η_t方案来估算η_t → W⁺W⁻衰变的分支比（branching ratio），并结合未来高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）阶段超过3×10⁷个η_t事例的预期产额，考虑带电轻子（charged lepton）的真实鉴别效率，对双轻子级联衰变过程η_t → W⁺W⁻ → ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓ ℓ′⁺ν_ℓ′进行信号事例数估计。

Result: 在两种衰变常数方案下，η_t → W⁺W⁻衰变分支比被量化估计；考虑到HL-LHC中3×10⁷以上的η_t事例及带电轻子鉴别效率后，级联衰变通道可产生数十个双轻子事件，具备实验可观测性。

Conclusion: 本文提出通过η_t → W⁺W⁻ → ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓ ℓ′⁺ν_ℓ′通道搜索η_t，该通道无b喷注且具有干净的双轻子特征，可作为现有搜索方法的互补方案，并可能提供更灵敏的探测途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of the pseudoscalar toponium $η_{t}$ at the LHC and by the ongoing interest in its properties, we phenomenologically investigate the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ process, one of the characteristic decays of toponium $η_{t}$. The branching ratio for the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay is estimated with two scenarios of the decay constant $f_{η_{t}}$. It is found that tens of dilepton events from the cascade decays, $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ ${\to}$ ${\ell}^{-}\barν_{\ell}{\ell}^{{\prime}+}ν_{{\ell}^{\prime}}$ with ${\ell}$, ${\ell}^{\prime}$ $=$ $e$ and $μ$, could be experimentally observed, when considering the realistic identification efficiency of the charged lepton and assuming more than $3{\times}10^{7}$ $η_{t}$ events available at the future HL-LHC. We propose searching for $η_{t}$ via the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ ${\to}$ ${\ell}^{-}\barν_{\ell}{\ell}^{{\prime}+}ν_{{\ell}^{\prime}}$ channel, which features no $b$-jets and clean dilepton signatures, thus offering a complementary and potentially more sensitive probe.

</details>


### [222] [A High-Precision Numerical Framework for Time-Varying Solar Neutrino Flux with Full Earth Matter Oscillation Corrections for Global Underground Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24421)
*Keyu Han,Isabella Yin,Kaoru Yagi,Kevin Yifan Jiang,Xiangpan Ji,Shaomin Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一且计算高效的框架，用于高精度预测太阳中微子通量，该框架整合了时变日地距离、一维与三维地球电子密度剖面以及基于Strang分裂的快速MSW传播机制，并提供了针对中国锦屏地下实验室等多个实验地点的具体预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳中微子（solar neutrino）研究已持续半个多世纪，用于检验标准太阳模型（Standard Solar Model）和粒子物理标准模型（Standard Model）的电弱部分。当前实验进入高精度测量时代，要求理论预测达到亚百分比精度以进行有意义的比较，因此亟需建立高保真度的计算框架。

Method: 论文识别并分析了计算太阳中微子通量所需的核心物理与计算组件，并提出一个统一的计算框架。该框架包含：(i) 时变日地距离；(ii) 基于一维（1D）和三维（3D）地球电子密度剖面建模的地球物质效应（Earth matter effects）；(iii) 基于Strang分裂（Strang splitting）的快速Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein（MSW）中微子传播形式化实现，支持对中微子轨迹和能量网格进行快速大规模扫描。

Result: 该框架提供了针对中国锦屏地下实验室（China Jinping Underground Laboratory, CJPL）以及其他积极参与太阳中微子项目的地下实验室的特定站点预测。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够高效生成高精度太阳中微子通量预测，满足当前实验对亚百分比精度的要求，并为不同实验室提供位置特异性的理论参考。

Abstract: Solar neutrinos have been studied for over half a century to test both the Standard Solar Model and the electroweak sector of the Standard Model of particle physics. Contemporary experiments are now entering an era of high-precision measurements, demanding corresponding theoretical predictions with sub-percent accuracy to enable meaningful comparison. In this paper, we identify and analyze the essential physical and computational components required to compute solar neutrino fluxes with high fidelity, and present a unified, computationally efficient framework. This framework incorporates: (i) the time-varying Earth-Sun distance; (ii) Earth matter effects modeled using both one-dimensional (1D) and three-dimensional (3D) Earth electron-density profiles; and (iii) a fast, Strang-splitting-based implementation of the Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein (MSW) neutrino propagation formalism, enabling rapid, large-scale scans over neutrino trajectories and energy grids. We deliver site-specific predictions for the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL) and other underground laboratories actively engaged in solar neutrino programs.

</details>


### [223] [Light Dark Matter Discovery Potential and Model Selection at LDMX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24524)
*Adam Berger,Riccardo Catena,Jan Conrad,Taylor R. Gray*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在似然统计框架下，系统评估了LDMX实验对轻暗物质（light dark matter）的发现潜力、参数推断能力及暗扇区（dark sector）模型区分能力，结果表明LDMX沿热 relic 目标具有强发现潜力，且二维反冲电子分布分析比一维分析具有更强的模型区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻暗物质是解释观测到的 relic 丰度的重要候选，而加速器实验是探测轻暗物质的强有力手段。即将开展的LDMX实验需要通过精确测量反冲电子的能量和横动量来探测轻暗物质，但尚需系统评估其在各种背景假设下的发现潜力和模型区分能力。

Method: 基于似然的统计框架（likelihood-based statistical framework），结合信号与背景建模及其系统不确定性，同时采用频率学派（frequentist）和贝叶斯方法（Bayesian methods）；在四个基准点（benchmark points）上评估发现潜力，利用贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor）进行模型比较，并在二维反冲电子分布上进行参数估计。

Result: LDMX在沿 relic 目标的多个基准点上具有强大的5σ发现潜力，并能给出90%置信水平上的排除限（exclusion limits）。二维反冲电子分布的归一化和形状分别编码了耦合常数和暗光子质量（dark photon mass），参数估计可在不确定性范围内恢复这两个参数。基于贝叶斯因子的模型比较能够统计地区分不同暗扇区假设，且二维分析比一维分析具有显著更强的区分能力。

Conclusion: LDMX实验在探测轻暗物质方面具有优异的发现潜力、参数推断能力和模型选择能力，所发展的似然统计框架可直接应用于实际LDMX实验数据。

Abstract: Light dark matter (DM) is a compelling scenario for the observed relic abundance, with accelerator-based searches as a powerful discovery strategy. The upcoming Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX) is designed to probe light DM by measuring the energy and transverse momentum of recoil electrons in high-intensity electron-nucleus collisions. We evaluate the discovery potential of LDMX to light DM at four benchmark points along the thermal target for complex scalar DM mediated by a dark photon, for different background assumptions, and assess its model selection power at a representative benchmark. We find that LDMX has strong projected 5$σ$ discovery potential along the relic target across both background scenarios for certain benchmarks, and we further compute projected 90% C.L. exclusion limits assuming no measured signal events. The normalization and shape of the two-dimensional recoil electron distribution encodes the coupling and dark photon mass, respectively, enabling parameter inference in the event of a signal excess. We perform parameter estimation on simulated data and find that both parameters are recovered within their uncertainties along the relic target. We assess whether the data can distinguish between competing dark sector hypotheses, in particular, dark photons with additional higher electromagnetic moment interactions. We demonstrate that model comparison using the Bayes factor allows dark sector hypotheses to be statistically distinguished, with the two-dimensional analysis affording substantially greater discriminating power than the one-dimensional analysis. These results are obtained within a likelihood-based statistical framework, incorporating signal and background modelling with their associated systematic uncertainties and employing both frequentist and Bayesian methods. The framework is designed for direct application to real LDMX data.

</details>


### [224] [Global analyses of helicity-dependent parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24613)
*Emanuele R. Nocera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了螺旋性依赖（helicity-dependent）或极化（polarised）部分子分布函数（PDFs）的定义、物理诠释、微扰QCD提取框架、实验约束及全局分析方法，并展示了极化PDF确定的最新进展以及未来电子-离子对撞机（Electron-Ion Collider）的预期影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化部分子分布函数是理解量子色动力学（Quantum Chromodynamics, QCD）中核子自旋结构的关键，它们编码了快速运动质子的自旋如何在部分子（parton）层面共享，因此需要对其定义、提取方法及现状进行系统性综述。

Method: 方法基于全局QCD分析（global QCD analyses），通过拟合极化硬散射实验数据（包括包含性深度非弹散射（inclusive deep-inelastic scattering）、半包含性深度非弹散射（semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering）以及极化质子-质子对撞（polarised proton-proton collisions））来提取螺旋性依赖PDFs。

Result: 呈现了极化PDF确定的最新进展（state of the art），并指出未来电子-离子对撞机（Electron-Ion Collider）的测量将带来重要影响。

Conclusion: 该综述系统总结了极化PDF的物理意义、提取框架及当前水平，同时指出了未来实验对进一步完善核子自旋结构理解的推动作用。

Abstract: Helicity-dependent, or polarised, parton distribution functions describe the longitudinal polarisation of quarks, antiquarks, and gluons inside a polarised nucleon. They encode how the spin of a fast-moving proton or neutron is shared among its partonic constituents and are therefore central to the modern understanding of nucleon spin structure in Quantum Chromodynamics. Helicity PDFs are extracted from global QCD analyses of polarised hard-scattering data, including inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, and polarised proton-proton collisions. This article reviews the definition and physical interpretation of polarised PDFs, the perturbative QCD framework used to determine them, the experimental observables that constrain the different parton flavours, and the statistical methodology of global analyses. The state of the art in polarised PDF determination is presented, together with the impact expected from future measurements at the Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [225] [Next-to-leading order FsQED corrections to radiative pion pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24642)
*Carlo M. Carloni Calame,Marco Ghilardi,Andrea Gurgone,Guido Montagna,Mauro Moretti,Oreste Nicrosini,Fulvio Piccinini,Francesco P. Ucci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在FsQED框架下计算了辐射返回过程e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻γ的次领头阶修正，并将结果与因式化标量QED方法及广义矢量介子主导模型进行对比，同时包含了FsQED之外的首阶修正以及φ介子共振区的辐射衰变贡献，最终将这些新特征集成到蒙卡事件产生器BabaYaga@NLO中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在味工厂（flavour factories）的辐射返回实验中，π⁺π⁻γ过程是测量π介子电磁形状因子的重要途径，而结构依赖修正（structure-dependent corrections）对观测量的数值影响需要精确计算，以提升实验与理论比较的精度。

Method: 采用FsQED方法（一种将介子形状因子嵌入圈积分的有效场论方案）计算次领头阶（next-to-leading order）修正；与因式化标量QED（factorised scalar QED）方法和广义矢量介子主导（generalised vector meson dominance）模型的结果进行对比；还纳入文献建议的超越FsQED的首阶修正，并研究了φ介子共振区（center-of-mass energies around the φ-meson resonance）的额外末态辐射机制（如辐射φ衰变）。

Result: 给出了结构依赖修正对多种可观测量影响的数值结果；首次估计了超越FsQED的修正贡献；提供了与KLOE实验相关的φ共振区辐射衰变数值结果。

Conclusion: 这些新特征已集成到蒙卡事件产生器（Monte Carlo event generator）BabaYaga@NLO中，该工具可用于评估辐射返回测量中π介子-光子相互作用建模的影响。

Abstract: We compute the next-to-leading order corrections to the radiative return process $e^+ e^- \to π^+ π^- γ$ within the FsQED approach to embed the pion form factor in the calculation of loop integrals. We compare our results with those of the factorised scalar QED approach, as well as with previous predictions obtained by us in the generalised vector meson dominance model. We show the numerical impact of the structure-dependent corrections on various observables of interest for radiative return experiments at flavour factories. Following recent input from the literature, we include in our calculation also leading corrections beyond FsQED and we provide a first estimate of such contributions. We also investigate additional mechanisms contributing to final-state radiation at center-of-mass energies around the $φ$-meson resonance, such as radiative $φ$ decays, presenting numerical results that are relevant for the KLOE experiment. These novel features are implemented in the Monte Carlo event generator BabaYaga@NLO, which can now be used to evaluate the impact of the modelling of pion-photon interaction in radiative return measurements.

</details>


### [226] [Application of flavor moonshine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22744)
*Hirotaka Sugawara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在先前工作的基础上引入超对称性（supersymmetry）和模对称性（modular symmetry），利用不同阶的两值模函数（two valued modular function）计算各代轻子、夸克及其超伴子（super partner）的质量，并指出三个可在现有对撞机上测量的超粒子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前基于Monstrous Moonshine的味物理（flavor physics）工作扩展到包含超对称性和模对称性的框架，以统一计算标准模型粒子及其超伴子的质量谱。

Method: 对每一代（family）仅使用一个输入粒子质量，分别采用阶数K=1、2、3的两值模函数计算带电轻子、中微子/上型夸克、下型夸克及其超伴子的质量。

Result: 给出超电子质量约为847 MeV（略低于质子质量）；超d、s、b夸克质量分别为0 GeV、5.56 GeV和2193.4 GeV；指出超奇异夸克可在KEKB和LHC上测量，超底夸克仅可在LHC上测量。

Conclusion: 该理论预言了三个可在当前加速器（如KEK B factory和CERN LHC）上检验的超粒子质量，为超对称模型的实验验证提供了具体可测目标。

Abstract: In our previous paper [1], we presented the application of Moonshine to the flavor physics based on the work of Cohn and Deutsch [2] and Bagger and West. Conway and S. Norton [3] and T.Eguchi, H.Ooguri and Y.Tachikawa[4]. We present here the extension of our previous paper [1] by including the supersymmetry and the modular symmetry. We calculate all the lepton masses and the quark masses and their super partner masses by using the one input particle mass for each family. The two valued modular function is defined in equation(7). We define the two valued modular function of degree K in equation (7). We use K = 1 two valued modular function for the charged leptons and k = 2 two valued modular function for the neutrino and for the up quarks and k = 3 two valued modular function for the down quarks. We emphasize that we have three particle masses which can be measured by the existing accelerators such as KEK B factory or CERN LHC. One is super electron mass "Me,s =847 MeV" which is slightly lower than the proton mass. The Super d, s and b quark masses are 0 GeV, 5.56 GeV, and 2193.4 GeV. Super down quark mass is 0 and super strange quark mass can be measured both in KEKB and in CERN LHC. The super bottom quark mass can be measured only in LHC.

</details>


### [227] [A Chromomagnetic Mechanism for the Rotational Phase Transition of Gluonic Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23199)
*Zhibin Li,Yidian Chen,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于全息框架下的旋转-磁对应关系，研究了纯胶子物质在旋转下的响应行为。通过用晶格QCD（lattice QCD）在虚角速度下的数据校准，发现真实旋转会增强色磁弦张力（chromomagnetic string tension）并提高退禁闭温度。研究发现温度依赖的色磁弦张力主导了系统的Barnett响应：低温弱张力导致负Barnett效应，相变点附近自旋贡献占优产生反常负总转动惯量。该反常区域仅在弱真实旋转下存在，大角速度下会消失。研究确立了色磁诱导惯性反转机制（CII mechanism）作为该反常旋转响应的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转是QCD物质的关键控制参数，但有效模型与格点QCD（lattice QCD）对其在退禁闭相变中的作用给出了相互矛盾的预测。本文旨在通过全息框架解决这一争议，揭示旋转对纯胶子物质影响的微观机制。

Method: 采用全息框架（holographic framework）下的旋转-磁对应关系（rotation-magnetic correspondence），并利用晶格QCD在虚角速度下的数据进行校准，研究纯胶子物质在真实旋转下的响应。

Result: 真实旋转增强色磁弦张力并提高退禁闭温度，与晶格QCD解析延拓预测一致。温度依赖的色磁弦张力主导Barnett响应：低温弱张力产生负Barnett效应，相变附近自旋贡献占优导致反常负总转动惯量。该反常区域仅在弱旋转下存在，大角速度下消失。高温下强弦张力恢复常规Barnett行为。

Conclusion: 反常旋转响应的微观起源在于非微扰色磁通量管（nonperturbative chromomagnetic flux tubes）的熔化与热恢复，由此确立色磁诱导惯性反转机制（CII mechanism）为该反常现象的解释。

Abstract: Rotation serves as a pivotal control parameter for QCD matter, yet effective models and lattice QCD yield conflicting predictions regarding its effect on the deconfinement transition. Using a rotation-magnetic correspondence within a holographic framework, we investigate the rotational response of pure gluonic matter. Calibrated against lattice QCD data at imaginary angular velocity, we find real rotation enhances chromomagnetic string tension and raises deconfinement temperature, consistent with lattice QCD analytic-continuation predictions. The temperature dependence of chromomagnetic string tension dominates the system's Barnett response: weak low-temperature tension induces the negative Barnett effect, and slightly above the transition, spin contributions prevail to generate an anomalous negative total moment of inertia. Since growing angular velocity further strengthens chromomagnetic string tension and suppresses spin-dominated inversion, this anomalous regime only survives at weak real rotation and vanishes at large angular velocity. At high temperature, fully restored strong string tension stabilizes conventional Barnett behavior. Stemming from the melting and thermal restoration of nonperturbative chromomagnetic flux tubes, our results establish the chromomagnetic-induced inertia inversion (CII) mechanism as the microscopic origin of this anomalous rotational response.

</details>


### [228] [Status of light inflaton: from inflation to laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23200)
*Niloy Mondal,Shashwat Sharma,Mathew Thomas Arun,Basabendu Barman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在最新宇宙微波背景观测（ACT）及对撞机与强度前沿实验的约束下，检验了亚GeV质量轻暴胀子（light inflaton）场景的可行性，并探讨了再加热期间暗物质产生的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最新宇宙学观测（ACT）与粒子物理实验的多重约束下，检验轻暴胀子场景是否仍然成立，并探索其与暗物质产生的关联。

Method: 假设四次暴胀子势（quartic inflaton potential），利用ACT对暴胀观测的最新约束，结合NA62、KOTO、BaBar、Belle、LHCb、MATHUSLA、FASER2、SHiP等实验及中性介子振荡数据，限制暴胀子质量与暴胀子-希格斯混合（inflaton-Higgs mixing）的参数空间。

Result: 识别出与ACT观测一致的参数空间区域，并获得了暴胀子质量和暴胀子-希格斯混合的约束范围。

Conclusion: 轻暴胀子场景在同时满足ACT宇宙学约束和强度前沿实验限制的情况下仍然可行，且在此框架下再加热期间的暗物质产生具有可探索的前景。

Abstract: We investigate the viability of the light inflaton scenario in light of the latest inflationary constraints from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT), together with bounds from collider and intensity-frontier experiments searching for a feebly coupled light scalar with a sub-GeV mass. Assuming a quartic inflaton potential, we identify the region of parameter space consistent with the ACT observations and derive constraints on the inflaton mass and inflaton-Higgs mixing using results from NA62, KOTO, BaBar, Belle, LHCb, MATHUSLA, FASER2, SHiP, and neutral meson oscillations. We also explore the prospects for dark matter production during reheating within this framework, while remaining consistent with the inflationary observables.

</details>


### [229] [Axion isocurvature and the model-building problem of low-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22809)
*Sarunas Verner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了预暴胀QCD轴子（pre-inflationary QCD axion）的一般性等曲率（isocurvature）界限，将暴胀归一化标度$f_I$与晚期衰变常数$f_a$独立处理。结合等曲率和张量谱得出轴子-张量（axion-tensor）和轴子条件化的Lyth界限（axion-conditioned Lyth bound），限制暴胀子行程（inflaton excursion）$\Delta\phi_{\rm CMB}/M_{\rm Pl}\lesssim10^{-7}\text{-}10^{-4}$，并导出了势导数间的层级关系，支持混合暴胀（hybrid）、跑动质量（running-mass）和拐点（inflection-point）等模型。再加热（reheating）和Peccei-Quinn对称性未恢复条件进一步强化了这些约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预暴胀QCD轴子通常被认为需要低标度暴胀（low-scale inflation），但现有工作缺乏对暴胀归一化与晚期衰变常数独立处理、以及任意轴子暗物质占比等一般情形的系统分析。本文旨在推导更一般的等曲率界限，并探索其对暴胀模型参数空间的约束。

Method: 作者推导了一个质量较轻轴子（light axion）的通用等曲率界限，独立处理暴胀归一化标度$f_I$与晚期衰变常数$f_a$，并允许任意轴子暗物质分数、非平凡扰动传递和非简谐丰度响应。结合等曲率与张量谱，进一步导出轴子-张量和轴子条件化Lyth界限。

Result: 最小基准情形（axions构成全部暗物质，$f_I=f_a\leq M_{\rm Pl}$，角扰动守恒）下，最宽松的CMB限制给出暴胀哈勃标度（inflationary Hubble scale）$H_I<1.25\times10^{10}$ GeV（95% C.L.）；若初始倾角（initial misalignment angle）$\theta_i=1$，约束加强为$H_I<2.2\times10^7$ GeV，山顶（hilltop）附近约束更强。由此得到暴胀子行程$\Delta\phi_{\rm CMB}/M_{\rm Pl}\lesssim10^{-7}\text{-}10^{-4}$，势一阶导数$|M_{\rm Pl}V'/V|\sim10^{-8}\text{-}10^{-5}$，二阶导数$M_{\rm Pl}^2V''/V\sim-0.01$。

Conclusion: 所得参数层级关系倾向于混合暴胀、跑动质量模型和拐点模型等具有暴胀标度、势导数、退出（exit）和再加热独立控制机制的模型。再加热条件和Peccei-Quinn对称性未恢复要求会强化约束，而非最小情景（nonminimal scenarios）可通过修改原初扰动、扰动传递或残余丰度来放松这些条件。

Abstract: The pre-inflationary QCD axion is often said to require low-scale inflation. We derive a general isocurvature bound for a light axion with a scale-invariant spectrum, treating its inflationary normalization $f_I$ independently of the late-time decay constant $f_a$ and allowing for an arbitrary axion dark matter fraction, nontrivial perturbation transfer, and the full anharmonic abundance response. In the minimal benchmark, where axions constitute all of the dark matter, $f_I=f_a\leq M_{\rm Pl}$, and the angular perturbation is conserved, the least restrictive CMB limit considered gives the 95% C.L. upper bound $H_I<1.25\times10^{10}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ on the inflationary Hubble scale. For an initial misalignment angle $θ_i=1$, the bound strengthens to $H_I<2.2\times10^7\,\mathrm{GeV}$, with still stronger constraints near the hilltop. Combining the isocurvature and tensor spectra yields axion-tensor and axion-conditioned Lyth bounds. If the observed curvature perturbation is generated by a canonical cold single-field slow-roll inflaton and the tensor amplitude varies slowly across the observable CMB window, these relations limit the inflaton excursion to $Δφ_{\rm CMB}/M_{\rm Pl}\lesssim10^{-7}\text{-}10^{-4}$ and require $|M_{\rm Pl}V'/V|\sim10^{-8}\text{-}10^{-5}$ while $M_{\rm Pl}^2V''/V\sim-10^{-2}$. This hierarchy favors models with independent control of the inflationary scale, potential derivatives, exit, and reheating, including hybrid, running-mass, and inflection-point models. Reheating and the requirement of Peccei-Quinn non-restoration sharpen these conditions, while nonminimal scenarios can relax them by modifying the primordial fluctuation, its transfer, or the relic abundance.

</details>


### [230] [Electroweak Baryogenesis: Advances in Sphaleron Rate Calculations and Implications of Thermal Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24026)
*Yanda Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了sphaleron速率计算的最新进展，重点聚焦于电弱重子生成（electroweak baryogenesis）。文章还提供了关于瞬子（instanton）与sphaleron诱导的重子数破坏、非阿贝尔规范理论（non-Abelian gauge theory）真空结构等拓扑场构型的教学性介绍，并建议将“sphaleron”译为“an zi”。此外，讨论了一级相变（first-order phase transition）对对撞机搜索和原初黑洞形成（primordial black hole formation）的更广泛意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sphaleron速率是理解电弱重子生成机制的关键量，其精确计算对于宇宙重子数不对称（baryon asymmetry）的起源研究至关重要。同时，该领域涉及非阿贝尔规范理论的拓扑结构，需要为读者提供从基础概念到前沿进展的系统梳理。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析（review）方法，梳理了sphaleron速率计算的最新进展，并以教学性（pedagogical）方式介绍了相关的拓扑场构型理论框架。

Result: 总结了电弱重子生成背景下sphaleron速率计算的最新成果，并提出了“sphaleron”的中文译名建议（“an zi”）。指出了一级相变在对撞机物理与宇宙学（原初黑洞形成）中的潜在影响。

Conclusion: sphaleron过程在电弱重子生成中扮演核心角色，其速率的精确计算对理解早期宇宙重子数不对称具有重要意义。一级相变的相关研究有助于打通粒子物理对撞机实验与宇宙学观测之间的联系。

Abstract: This thesis reviews recent advances in calculating the sphaleron rate, with particular emphasis on electroweak baryogenesis. It also provides pedagogical introductions to sphaleron- and instanton-induced baryon-number violation, the vacuum structure of non-Abelian gauge theories, and other topological field configurations, and suggests "an zi" as a possible Chinese term for "sphaleron" (see p. 3). Broader implications of first-order phase transitions for collider searches and primordial black hole formation are also discussed.

</details>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Dispersive estimates of Dirac equations with a domain wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22879)
*M. Burak Erdogan,Joseph Kraisler,Amir Sagiv*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了具有有界和无界畴壁的二维狄拉克方程（Dirac equation）的色散衰减估计（dispersive decay estimate），以及相应一维问题的估计，并发现拓扑等价模型可能展现出性质不同的动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些模型是拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）中体-边对应（bulk-edge correspondence）的范例，但其色散动力学（dispersive dynamics）此前尚未被研究过。

Method: 建立了一种新方法，能够分析超出常系数狄拉克算子局部扰动这一常规框架的模型。

Result: 建立了二维和有界/无界畴壁的狄拉克方程的色散衰减估计，以及一维情形的对应估计；证明了拓扑等价模型可能具有性质不同的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 提出的新方法拓展了可分析模型的范畴，揭示了拓扑等价性与动力学行为之间的非平凡关系。

Abstract: We establish dispersive decay estimates for two-dimensional Dirac equations with bounded and unbounded domain walls, together with estimates for the analogous one-dimensional problem. These are paradigmatic models of bulk-edge correspondence in topological insulators, yet their dispersive dynamics have not been previously studied. We show that topologically equivalent models may exhibit qualitatively different dynamics. Our new approach allows us to analyze models which are beyond the usual framework of localized perturbations of constant-coefficient Dirac operators.

</details>


### [232] [Continuous Data Assimilation for the 2D Navier-Stokes Equations from Partial Tangential Boundary Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23707)
*Gianmarco Del Sarto,Buddhika Priyasad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维Navier-Stokes方程在Navier滑移边界条件下的连续数据同化问题，仅利用边界子集上的切向速度有限维测量，证明足够强的边界反馈可在同化误差中产生强制谱隙，并给出了指数同步的充分判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续数据同化（continuous data assimilation）中，现有理论多假设内部观测，而实际应用中测量往往仅在边界可行。本文旨在探索仅利用边界（boundary）子集的有限维测量，是否仍能实现二维Navier-Stokes方程解与观测数据之间的指数同步。

Method: 采用边界反馈控制方法，在边界子集Γ上施加基于切向速度有限维观测的反馈项；通过分析混合边界问题（mixed-boundary problem）中的谱隙（spectral gap），证明反馈可诱导同化误差的强制单调性（coercivity），并结合非线性误差产生项的长期时间平均对称梯度能量（symmetric-gradient energy）估计，导出指数同步的充分条件。

Result: 证明了足够强且空间分辨足够细的边界反馈可在误差方程中产生量级为ν的强制谱隙。在无外力情形、小外力且未受扰动系统存在L²谱隙时、大黏性且无切向刚体运动（tangential rigid motions）的域上、以及存在正边界摩擦（positive boundary friction）的大黏性情形下，均验证了指数同步判据成立。对于允许切向刚体运动的完美滑移（perfect slip）域，在非刚体散装分量满足小条件时也实现了同步。

Conclusion: 仅依靠边界子集上的切向速度有限维测量，通过足够强的边界反馈即可实现二维Navier-Stokes方程解的指数同步；同步的可行性依赖于黏性、外力、边界摩擦和域几何性质等因素，研究为边界数据同化提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study continuous data assimilation for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations on a smooth, bounded, connected domain with Navier-slip boundary conditions, using no interior observations. The available data consist only of finite-dimensional measurements of the tangential velocity on a non-empty relatively open subset $Γ\subset\partialΩ$. We prove that sufficiently strong boundary feedback, constructed from sufficiently fine observations, generates a coercive spectral gap for the assimilation error. The limiting gap is identified with that of a mixed-boundary problem obtained by imposing a homogeneous Dirichlet condition on $Γ$, and is shown to be of order $ν$. Combining this feedback-induced coercivity with an estimate of the non-linear error production in terms of the long-time averaged symmetric-gradient energy of the reference solution, we obtain a sufficient criterion for exponential synchronisation. We verify this criterion in the unforced case, for sufficiently small forcing when the unnudged Navier-slip form has an $\mathrm{L}^2$ spectral gap, for sufficiently large viscosity on domains without tangential rigid motions, and for sufficiently large viscosity in the presence of positive boundary friction. We also treat perfect slip on domains admitting tangential rigid motions, where synchronisation follows under a smallness condition on the non-rigid solenoidal component of the forcing.

</details>


### [233] [On a free boundary problem of the Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24053)
*Shengchuang Chang,Renjun Duan,Shuangqian Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）与移动活塞耦合的自由边界问题（free boundary problem），通过共形变换（conformal transformation）和耦合能量结构（coupled energy structure）两种新工具，建立了全局非线性理论，首次证明了该完全耦合系统在全局麦克斯韦分布（global Maxwellian）附近的全局存在性、唯一性、非线性稳定性（nonlinear stability）以及指数收敛到平衡态（exponential convergence to equilibrium）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄气体与移动壁面相互作用的自由边界问题（即动理论中的经典活塞问题）是动理论（kinetic theory）中的核心模型，但其数学处理面临严重困难：动力学方程与移动边界之间存在强耦合（strong coupling），且解的正则性（regularity）过低导致经典拉格朗日表述（Lagrangian formulation）不可用。因此，需要发展全新的分析框架来建立该完全耦合系统的全局适定性（global well-posedness）理论。

Method: 本文提出了两项关键技术：一是引入共形变换（conformal transformation），将移动区域（moving domain）转化为固定区域（fixed domain）以简化边界处理；二是揭示了动能分布（kinetic distribution）与自由边界变量（free boundary variables）之间耦合的能量结构（coupled energy structure），从而发现界面处的内在耗散（intrinsic dissipation）与抵消机制（cancellation mechanisms）。

Result: 基于上述结构观察，本文建立了完全耦合系统的全局非线性理论（global nonlinear theory），证明了在全局麦克斯韦分布（global Maxwellian）附近解的全局存在性（global existence）、唯一性（uniqueness）、非线性稳定性（nonlinear stability）以及指数收敛到平衡态（exponential convergence to equilibrium）。

Conclusion: 本文首次为活塞型（piston type）完全耦合玻尔兹曼自由边界问题建立了严格的全局适定性（global well-posedness）理论，填补了该领域的重要空白。

Abstract: This paper studies a free boundary problem for the Boltzmann equation that models the interaction of rarefied gas with a moving wall---a classical piston problem in kinetic theory. The piston motion is governed by Newton's law under the drag force exerted by the gas, with or without an additional Hookean restoring force. A central challenge for such a problem is the strong coupling between the kinetic equation and the free moving boundary, for which the classical Lagrangian formulation is unavailable due to low-regularity of solutions. Our approach relies on two new ingredients: a conformal transformation is introduced to reduce the moving domain to a fixed domain, and a coupled energy structure linking the kinetic distribution and free boundary variables is uncovered to reveal intrinsic dissipation and cancellation mechanisms at the interface. These structural observations lead to a global nonlinear theory for the fully coupled system. As a result, we establish the global existence, uniqueness, nonlinear stability, and exponential convergence to equilibrium of solutions near a global Maxwellian. This provides the first rigorous global well-posedness theory for a fully coupled Boltzmann free boundary problem of piston type.

</details>


### [234] [Supersolution Construction and Optimal Hardy Inequality for Fractional Laplacians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24169)
*Philipp Hake,Matthias Keller,Felix Pogorzelski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超解构造（supersolution construction）的新判据，用于证明图上一般算子的哈代权（Hardy weight）最优性。对于无消灭项的图拉普拉斯算子（Laplacian），该方法通过格林函数（Green's function）无需额外假设即可给出最优哈代权；且该结果不限于局部有限图，从而可应用于一般图上的分数阶拉普拉斯算子（fractional Laplacian），并在欧几里得格点上得到了与连续情形一致的常数和渐近行为的最优哈代权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于图（graph）上哈代权最优性的判据通常局限于局部有限图，且依赖额外假设。本文旨在提出一个适用范围更广的新判据，以处理无消灭项拉普拉斯算子以及分数阶拉普拉斯算子等更一般的情形。

Method: 通过超解构造（supersolution construction）这一技术手段，建立一个新的判据来证明图上一般算子哈代权的最优性。该方法无需对图的局部有限性作出限制，并利用格林函数（Green's function）构造最优哈代权。

Result: 对于无消灭项的图上拉普拉斯算子，该方法始终能通过格林函数得到最优哈代权，无需额外假设。该结果突破了局部有限图的限制，并可推广到一般图上的分数阶拉普拉斯算子。在欧几里得格点（Euclidean lattice）上，获得了与连续设定（continuous setting）中期望的常数和渐近行为一致的最优哈代权。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新判据扩展了哈代权最优性证明的适用范围，覆盖了更一般的图结构（包括非局部有限图）和更广泛的算子类型（包括分数阶拉普拉斯算子），且所得结果在离散格点上与连续情形高度吻合。

Abstract: We give a new criterion to show optimality of Hardy weights for general operators on graphs via the supersolution construction. For Laplacians on graphs without killing terms this always gives rise to an optimal Hardy weight via the Green's function without any further assumptions. Furthermore, in contrast to earlier results, our result is not restricted to locally finite graphs. This allows us in particular to obtain optimal Hardy weights for the fractional Laplacian on general graphs. For the fractional Laplacian on the Euclidean lattice, we then obtain an optimal Hardy weight with the constant and asymptotics as it is expected from the continuous setting.

</details>


### [235] [Non-singular hotspots between closely spaced high-index nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24204)
*Konstantinos Alexopoulos,Bryn Davies,Pierre Millien*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中两个近乎接触的高折射率介电谐振器之间的场集中现象。通过标量亥姆霍兹透射问题（scalar Helmholtz transmission problem）模型，发现尽管场梯度有界，但在中间距离范围内仍会出现随间距倒数增长并最终饱和的预渐近集中效应，该效应可通过弱耦合机制（weak-coupling regime）加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有模拟与实验表明，两个接近的高折射率介电谐振器之间的场梯度（field gradient）会出现集中现象，但标准的数学理论预测该梯度保持有界，两者之间存在矛盾。本文旨在为这一预渐近（pre-asymptotic）集中效应提供理论解释。

Method: 采用三维标量亥姆霍兹透射问题作为模型，在谐振区域（resonant regime）内分析场上方程中的材料对比仅出现在低阶项中。通过弱耦合机制将谐振对视为孤立粒子谐振模式的微扰，利用均值论证（mean value argument）跨越间隙解释梯度增长，并将谱微扰估计（spectral perturbation estimates）从平均控制加强至逐点控制（pointwise control）。

Result: 当一对谐振模式在两个朝向的边界之间保持非零对比度时，场梯度随粒子间距的倒数增长；增长在粒子间相互作用不再微弱、微扰理论失效时停止。数值实验确认了从放大（amplification）到饱和（saturation）的转变。

Conclusion: 该预渐近集中效应源于弱耦合谐振模式的残余边界对比，其梯度增长受限于弱耦合机制的失效。研究为解释介电谐振器近场集中现象提供了理论框架，并强调了谱估计需要从平均控制加强到逐点控制的必要性。

Abstract: We study the concentration of the field between two nearly touching high-index dielectric resonators in three dimensions. The model is a scalar Helmholtz transmission problem in the resonant regime, wherein the wavelength inside the resonators of the same order as their typical diameter. The material contrast enters only a lower-order term of the equation and not its principal part. As a consequence, the gradient of the field stays bounded independently of the distance separating the particles, and does not blow up. Nevertheless, simulations and experiments show that the gradient still concentrates in the gap: as the particles approach, it grows like the inverse of their separation over an intermediate range of distances and then saturates once they are very close. We explain this pre-asymptotic effect through a weak-coupling regime, in which the resonant modes of the pair are perturbations of the modes of each isolated particle. When such a mode keeps a nonzero contrast between the two facing boundaries, a mean value argument across the gap accounts for the growth of the gradient; this requires strengthening the standard spectral perturbation estimates from an average to a pointwise control. The growth stops once the interaction between the particles is no longer weak and the perturbation theory fails. Numerical experiments confirm the transition from amplification to saturation.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [The sharp curl-Sobolev inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23827)
*Guofang Wang,Mingwei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当 n ≡ 3 (mod 4) 时球面 S^n 上的尖锐 curl-Sobolev 不等式，分类了所有极值函数（即 Killing 形式），并给出了几何与数学物理中的四个重要应用，包括证实了 Rivière 猜想以及 Faddeev-Skyrme 能量中 Hopf 映射的全局极小性等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可追溯到 Rivière 1998 年的长期遗留问题（即使在物理最相关的 n=3 情形也未解决），并最终证实了数学物理中若干悬而未决的猜想，包括 Rivière 关于 Hopf 映射 3-能量极小性的猜想、Ward 关于 Faddeev-Skyrme 能量全局极小性的预期，以及 Frank-Loss 关于 Dirac 零模式存在性的问题。

Method: 在 n ≡ 3 (mod 4) 的球面 S^n 上证明了一个共形不变的尖锐 curl-Sobolev 不等式，其商的分母为正；通过共形不变性，该结果同样适用于 R^n。随后利用该不等式，结合 Killing 形式对极值函数进行了完全分类，并以此推导出四个几何与变分应用。

Result: 1) 球面 S^n 上的标准度量是共形不变量 μ([g_st]) 的唯一优化子；2) 在 S^3 上，Hopf 映射同伦类中 3-能量 ∫|du|^3 的唯一极小化子恰好是 π∘Φ（Φ∈Conf^+(S^3)），证实了 Rivière 猜想；3) 在 S^3 上，对于 Faddeev-Skyrme 能量 FS_ρ，当耦合常数 ρ ≤ √2 时，同伦类中的唯一全局极小化子是 π∘R（R∈SO(4)），符合 Ward 的预期；4) 在 S^3 上存在 Dirac 零模时，磁场满足尖锐下界 ||curl A||_{3/2} ≥ 3ω_3^{2/3}，等号成立条件由 Killing 旋量刻画，回答了 Frank-Loss 关于 n=3 的情形的提问。

Conclusion: 本文建立的尖锐 curl-Sobolev 不等式及其极值分类是一致性很强的分析工具，能统一解决几何与数学物理中多个长期未决的猜想，展示了变分方法与共形几何在分析问题中的深刻联系。

Abstract: We solve a longstanding problem, going back at least to Rivière 1998 and open even in the physically most relevant case $n=3$, by proving a sharp curl-Sobolev inequality on $\mathbb{S}^n$ when $n\equiv 3\pmod 4$: for every $\frac{n-1}{2}$-form $α$, the conformally invariant quotient satisfies (with positive denominator) \[ \frac{\Big(\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}|{\rm curl}α|^{\frac{2n}{n+1}}\,{\rm dV}\Big)^{\frac{n+1}{n}}}{\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}\langle{\rm curl}α,α\rangle\,{\rm dV}}
  \ge \frac{n+1}{2}\,ω_n^{\frac1n}. \] We also classify all extremals in terms of Killing forms. By conformal invariance, the same result holds on $\mathbb{R}^n$.
  We then give geometric and variational applications that settle several open conjectures in geometry and mathematical physics. First, we show that on $\mathbb{S}^n$ the round metric is the unique optimizer for the conformal invariant $μ([g_{\rm st}])$. Second, we prove that the unique minimizers of the $3$-energy $\int_{\mathbb{S}^3}|{\rm d} u|^3$ in the homotopy class of the Hopf map $π:\mathbb{S}^3\to\mathbb{S}^2$ are exactly $π\circΦ$ with $Φ\in{\rm Conf}^+(\mathbb{S}^3)$, confirming a conjecture of Rivière. Third, for the Faddeev-Skyrme energy $\mathcal{FS}_ρ$ on $\mathbb{S}^3$, we establish global minimality of the Hopf map in the full predicted range: for every coupling constant $ρ\le \sqrt{2}$, the unique global minimizers in its homotopy class are precisely $π\circ R$ with $R\in\mathrm{SO}(4)$, as expected since Ward 1999. Fourth, in the presence of Dirac zero modes on $\mathbb{S}^3$, we prove the sharp lower bound $\|{\rm curl} A\|_{3/2} \ge 3ω_3^{\frac 2 3}$ for the magnetic field and characterize equality in terms of Killing spinors; in particular, this yields a sharp criterion for the existence of zero modes and answers a question of Frank-Loss for $n=3$.

</details>

### [237] [Charge-dependent atmospheric muon flux at 17 GV geomagnetic cutoff measured with the mini-ICAL prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23330)
*S. Pethuraj,Raj Shah,G. Majumder,J. M. John*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于印度中微子观测站（INO）的缩小版原型探测器mini-ICAL（约85吨，质量约为完整探测器ICAL的1/600），在2018至2022年间于地磁赤道附近（垂直截止刚度17 GV）采集的数据，测量了电荷依赖的宇宙线缪子（muon）微分通量谱（动量范围约1–5 GeV/c）。测量结果系统地低于具有更低地磁截止刚度的站点，且与CORSIKA模拟中不同强子相互作用模型的预言进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建INI的ICAL探测器旨在作为地下实验测量大气中微子振荡参数，而缩小版原型mini-ICAL的搭建与运行则是为了验证探测器性能、重建算法以及模拟框架，从而为全尺寸实验提供保障。

Method: 研究基于位于印度马杜赖（Madurai）、地磁纬度1.44°N、垂直截止刚度17 GV的mini-ICAL原型探测器，在2018至2022年间运行并采集宇宙线缪子数据，测量了电荷依赖的缪子（μ⁻和μ⁺）的微分通量谱，覆盖动量范围约1–5 GeV/c。测量结果与使用不同强子相互作用模型（hadronic interaction models）的CORSIKA模拟预言进行了比较。

Result: 在17 GV截止刚度处测得的缪子动量谱系统地低于具有更小地磁截止刚度（geomagnetic cutoff rigidity）的站点所报告的结果，这与低、中等刚度初级宇宙线（primary cosmic rays）在该截止值条件受到抑制的预期一致。

Conclusion: mini-ICAL原型成功验证了ICAL实验的探测器性能、重建算法和模拟框架，其测量的电荷依赖宇宙线缪子谱为地磁赤道附近的高截止刚度环境提供了独特的实验数据，有助于检验不同强子相互作用模型。

Abstract: The Iron CALorimeter (ICAL) detector at the India-Based Neutrino Observatory (INO) was conceived as an underground experiment designed to measure atmospheric neutrino oscillation parameters. As part of the R\&D programme, a scaled prototype (mini-ICAL), 85\,ton, approximately 1/600$^{\mathrm{th}}$ the mass of the full detector, was constructed at the IICHEP Transit Campus, Madurai (altitude 150\,m; latitude 9.9372$^\circ$\,N; longitude 78.013$^\circ$\,E; geomagnetic latitude 1.44$^\circ$\,N; vertical cutoff rigidity 17\,GV) and operated between 2018 and 2022. The prototype enabled measurements of charge-dependent cosmic muon spectra in the vicinity of the geomagnetic equator and provided an important validation of detector performance, reconstruction algorithms, and simulation frameworks for the ICAL experiment. Differential fluxes of $μ^{-}$ and $μ^{+}$ were measured over the momentum range $\sim$\,1--5\,GeV/c. The obtained momentum spectra are systematically lower than those reported at sites with smaller geomagnetic cutoff rigidities, consistent with the suppression of low- and intermediate-rigidity primary cosmic rays at the 17\,GV cutoff. The measurements are compared with predictions from different hadronic interaction models available in CORSIKA simulations.

</details>


### [238] [Off-shell Higgs boson measurements: Yukawa couplings, self-coupling, compositeness, and width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23352)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于CMS实验在LHC上采集的质子-质子对撞数据（13 TeV，138 fb⁻¹），利用四轻子衰变道测量希格斯玻色子在离壳区域（off-shell region）的产生。首次直接检验了复合希格斯玻色子模型（composite Higgs models），设定了复合能标Λ_H在95%置信水平下不低于870 GeV的下限。在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下检验了胶子融合产生过程，首次获得了离壳区域对希格斯玻色子自耦合（self-coupling）的约束。通过结合在壳（on-shell）与离壳测量，对轻夸克汤川耦合（light-quark Yukawa couplings）给出了迄今最严格的限制，并放松了对矢量玻色子耦合的假设以获得更模型无关的结果。联合H→ZZ与H→WW两个衰变道分析，将希格斯玻色子宽度约束为Γ_H = 5.1⁺²·⁰₋₁·₈ MeV，并以超过5倍标准差的置信水平排除了无离壳希格斯产生的情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子的离壳产生过程对超出标准模型（BSM）的新物理信号非常敏感，但此前在离壳区域对复合希格斯模型、希格斯自耦合以及轻夸克汤川耦合的约束仍较为缺乏。本文旨在利用CMS实验的高统计量数据填补这一空白，并通过联合在壳与离壳测量提升约束的模型无关性。

Method: 利用CMS探测器收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），通过四轻子（4ℓ）衰变道测量希格斯玻色子在离壳区域的产生截面。在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下对胶子融合产生过程进行检验，并联合H→ZZ与H→WW两个衰变道以提高对希格斯玻色子宽度（Γ_H）的敏感性。对复合希格斯模型、希格斯自耦合、轻夸克汤川耦合及宽度等参数进行统计推断。

Result: 首次直接检验复合希格斯模型，给出复合能标Λ_H在95%置信水平下不低于870 GeV的下限。首次获得离壳区域对希格斯自耦合的约束。通过结合在壳与离壳测量，给出了对轻夸克汤川耦合迄今最严格的限制。联合H→ZZ和H→WW信道分析得到希格斯宽度Γ_H = 5.1⁺²·⁰₋₁·₈ MeV，并以超过5倍标准差的置信水平排除了无离壳希格斯产生的情景。

Conclusion: 本文在离壳区域对希格斯玻色子产生进行的多项检验显著扩展了BSM物理的约束范围，尤其是在复合希格斯模型、自耦合和轻夸克汤川耦合方面取得了迄今最好或首次的结果。联合H→ZZ与H→WW通道的宽度测量以高置信度确认了离壳希格斯产生的存在，进一步验证了标准模型在电弱对称性破缺机制（electroweak symmetry breaking）中的基本描述。

Abstract: Measurements of Higgs boson production in the off-shell region are presented, using the four-lepton decay channel. Data from proton-proton collisions at the CERN LHC, collected by the CMS experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$ at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, are utilized. The first direct test of composite Higgs boson models is performed, with a lower limit on the compositeness scale $Λ_\mathrm{H}$ set at 870 GeV at the 95% confidence level. Tests of gluon-fusion production within the standard model effective field theory framework are performed. The first constraint on the Higgs boson self-coupling in the off-shell region is obtained. By combining on- and off-shell measurements, the analysis sets the tightest constraints to date on light-quark Yukawa couplings, while relaxing assumptions such as the bound on the Higgs boson coupling to vector bosons, thereby providing more model-independent results. Constraints on the Higgs boson width are provided while accounting for a range of beyond-the-standard-model effects, including both light and heavy particles in the gluon-fusion production loop, as well as modified couplings to vector bosons. A combined analysis of the H$\to$ZZ and PH$\to$WW channels is performed to improve sensitivity to the Higgs boson width, yielding $Γ_\mathrm{H}$ = 5.1$^{+2.0}_{-1.8}$ MeV. The scenario of no off-shell Higgs boson production is excluded at a confidence level exceeding 5 standard deviations.

</details>


### [239] [Predict before you train: Scaling Laws for particle physics foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23377)
*Jan-Lucas Uslu,Benjamin Nachman,Christopher Re*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文发现，针对对撞机喷注（jets）预训练的通用Transformer模型，其性能可通过仅基于小模型拟合的联合模型与数据缩放定律（scaling law）进行预测：在跨越三个数量级训练计算量的小模型上拟合缩放定律，可预测后续使用超百倍计算量训练的大模型损失，误差在1%以内；并将预测损失与下游物理性能（fine-tuning损失、背景抑制能力）联系起来，实现了在训练前将计算预算转化为预期物理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理中最大的机器学习模型训练成本极高，但在投入计算资源前无法估计给定架构的缩放回报。现有针对喷注（jets）的缩放定律尚未能预测未参与拟合的模型性能，因此需要一种可提前预测大模型性能的方法。

Method: 采用通用Transformer架构在对撞机喷注数据上进行预训练；仅基于小模型（覆盖三个数量级的训练计算量）拟合联合模型与数据缩放定律（joint model-and-data scaling law），然后预测后续训练的、计算量超出百倍以上的大模型损失。

Result: 预测的大模型损失与真实值偏差在1%以内；更低预训练损失在下游微调中系统性地导致更低的微调损失和更高的背景抑制能力；最终前沿（frontier）模型在准确率、AUC和夸克/胶子（quark/gluon）区分能力上与当前最先进的物理感知基础模型（physics-aware foundation models）一致，仅在top标记（top tagging）的高纯度尾部保留微弱优势。

Conclusion: 在此模型族和任务范围内，可在训练任何大模型之前将计算预算（compute budget）转化为预期的物理性能。该缩放定律方法为粒子物理中大模型的高效资源分配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The largest machine learning models in particle physics are also the most expensive to train, yet the return on scaling a given architecture cannot be estimated before that compute is spent. Scaling laws have been fit for jets, but none has yet been shown to predict the performance of models it was not fit on. We show that, for a generic transformer pretrained on collider jets, it can be forecast. Fitting a joint model-and-data scaling law on small models alone, spanning three orders of magnitude of training compute, we predict the loss of models trained afterward with more than one hundred times more compute to within one percent. We then connect the forecast to downstream physics performance: across two standard tagging benchmarks, lower pretraining loss yields systematically lower fine-tuning loss and higher background rejection after fine-tuning. Within this model family and these tasks, a compute budget can therefore be translated into expected physics performance before any large model is trained. The final frontier model is consistent with the published numbers for current state-of-the-art physics-aware foundation models trained on the same corpus, on accuracy, AUC, and quark/gluon rejection, with a residual edge for the physics-aware model only in the high-purity tail of top tagging. We release five pretrained models spanning multiple sizes, together with the complete training recipe and code.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文利用BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV下收集的20.3 fb⁻¹电子-正电子（e⁺e⁻）对撞数据，精确测量了四种D介子半轻衰变（D⁰→π⁻ℓ⁺ν_ℓ和D⁺→π⁰ℓ⁺ν_ℓ，ℓ=e,μ）的分支比，并首次确定了其前后不对称性。通过联合拟合，提取了强子跃迁形状因子f^{D→π}_+(0)与CKM矩阵元|V_{cd}|的乘积，并分别给出两者的最佳精度值。此外，还搜寻了标量流贡献并检验了轻子味普适性（lepton flavor universality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量D介子半轻衰变（semileptonic decay）的分支比和相关物理量，是检验标准模型（Standard Model）中Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa（CKM）矩阵元|V_{cd}|、强子跃迁形状因子以及轻子味普适性的重要途径。此前这些衰变过程的测量精度有限，且前后不对称性（forward-backward asymmetry）尚未被测定。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV处采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，对D⁰→π⁻e⁺ν_e、D⁰→π⁻μ⁺ν_μ、D⁺→π⁰e⁺ν_e和D⁺→π⁰μ⁺ν_μ四种衰变过程进行事件重建与分析。采用同时拟合（simultaneous fit）方法提取f^{D→π}_+(0)|V_{cd}|乘积，并分别利用标准模型全局拟合提供的|V_{cd}|和格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）计算的f^{D→π}_+(0)作为输入，解耦得到各自的独立值。同时，通过分析衰变率在ℓ⁺ν_ℓ四动量转移（four-momentum transfer）全范围及若干子区间的比值，检验轻子味普适性。

Result: 四种衰变的分支比分别为(2.950±0.017_stat.±0.017_syst.)×10⁻³、(2.817±0.037_stat.±0.019_syst.)×10⁻³、(3.622±0.034_stat.±0.018_syst.)×10⁻³和(3.507±0.043_stat.±0.026_syst.)×10⁻³。f^{D→π}_+(0)|V_{cd}|=0.1425±0.0005_stat.±0.0003_syst.。解耦得到f^{D→π}_+(0)=0.6339±0.0024_stat.±0.0014_syst.，|V_{cd}|=0.2262±0.0008_stat.±0.0005_syst.±0.0018_LQCD。标量流耦合常数的实部Re(C_S^μ)=0.022±0.023_stat.±0.003_syst.，虚部模|Im(C_S^μ)|=0.000±0.038_stat.±0.012_syst.。

Conclusion: 上述结果达到了目前最佳精度，首次测量了前后不对称性，并对c→d ℓ⁺ν_ℓ跃迁中的标量流贡献给出了上限约束。轻子味普适性检验未见显著偏离标准模型的迹象。

Abstract: The branching fractions of $D^0\to π^-e^+ν_e$, $D^0\to π^-μ^+ν_μ$, $D^+\to π^0e^+ν_e$, and $D^+\to π^0μ^+ν_μ$ are measured to be $(2.950\pm0.017_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.017_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, $(2.817\pm0.037_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.019_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, $(3.622\pm0.034_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.018_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$, and $(3.507\pm0.043_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.026_{\rm syst.})\times10^{-3}$ using $e^+e^-$ collision data with an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ collected at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector. The partial decay rates of these four decays are measured with the best precision to date and their forward-backward asymmetries are determined for the first time. By performing a simultaneous fit to these results, the product of the hadronic transition form factor $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ and the modulus of the $c\to d$ Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa matrix element $|V_{cd}|$ is given by $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)|V_{cd}|=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$. Taking the $|V_{cd}|$ provided by the standard model global fit and the $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ calculated from the lattice quantum chromodynamics as input, we obtain $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)=0.6339\pm0.0024_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0014_{\rm syst.}$ and $|V_{cd}|=0.2262\pm0.0008_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0005_{\rm syst.}\pm0.0018_{\rm LQCD.}$, respectively. The reported results have the best precision to date. We also search for the scalar current contribution in the $c\to d \ell^+ν_{\ell}$ transition and determine Re$(C_S^μ)=$ $0.022 \pm 0.023_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|{\rm Im}(C_S^μ)|=0.000 \pm $ $0.038_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.012_{\rm syst.}$. In addition, the lepton flavor universality is tested with the ratios of the decay rates between semimuonic and semielectronic decays in full and several $\ell^+ν_\ell$ four-momentum transfer ranges.

</details>


### [241] [Precision Measurement of Decay Dynamics in $D^{0(+)}\to π^{-(0)}\ell^+ν_\ell$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23949)

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV处采集的20.3 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，精确测量了四个D介子半轻衰变的分支比，未发现轻子味普适性（lepton flavor universality）破坏，并以空前精度提取了强子跃迁形状因子（hadronic transition form factor）f₊^{D→π}(0)与CKM矩阵元|V_{cd}|的乘积，以及标量流贡献（scalar current contribution）的实部与虚部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量D介子半轻衰变的分支比和衰变宽度比，检验轻子味普适性是否成立，并以更高精度确定强子跃迁形状因子f₊^{D→π}(0)与CKM矩阵元|V_{cd}|，进而检验标准模型（Standard Model）的预言。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，对D⁰→π⁻e⁺νₑ、D⁰→π⁻μ⁺ν_μ、D⁺→π⁰e⁺νₑ和D⁺→π⁰μ⁺ν_μ四个衰变道的分支比进行精确测量，并对不同四动量转移（four-momentum transfer）区间的μ子与正电子道衰变宽度比进行全面检验。通过同时拟合精确测量的部分衰变率（partial decay rates）与首次测量的前向-后向不对称性（forward-backward asymmetries），提取相关物理参数。

Result: 未发现轻子味普适性破坏的证据。以空前精度测得f₊^{D→π}(0)|V_{cd}| = 0.1425 ± 0.0005(stat.) ± 0.0003(syst.)；分别导出f₊^{D→π}(0) = 0.1425 ± 0.0005(stat.) ± 0.0003(syst.)和|V_{cd}| = 0.2262 ± 0.0008(stat.) ± 0.0005(syst.) ± 0.0018(LQCD)，精度均较以往最佳测量提高2–3倍。首次测量了c→d ℓ⁺ν_ℓ跃迁中标量流贡献的实部Re(C_S^μ) = 0.022 ± 0.023(stat.) ± 0.003(syst.)和虚部绝对值|Im(C_S^μ)| = 0.000 ± 0.038(stat.) ± 0.012(syst.)。

Conclusion: 当前数据支持轻子味普适性，未发现超出标准模型的新物理信号。所得f₊^{D→π}(0)和|V_{cd}|的测量精度显著提升，为检验标准模型和格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）计算提供了更严格的约束。首次测量的标量流贡献为进一步探索c→d跃迁中的新物理效应奠定了基础。

Abstract: The branching fractions of $D^0\to π^-e^+ν_e$, $D^0\to π^-μ^+ν_μ$, $D^+\to π^0e^+ν_e$, and $D^+\to π^0μ^+ν_μ$ are precisely measured, using 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector. The ratios of the decay widths between muon and positron channels are examined in full, across several four-momentum transfer ranges of $\ell^+ν_{\ell}$. No lepton flavor universality violation is found in the current data. From a simultaneous fit to the precisely measured partial decay rates and the first measured forward-backward asymmetries of these four decays, the product of the hadronic transition form factor, $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$, and the modulus of the $c\to d$ quark mixing element, $|V_{cd}|$, is measured with unprecedented precision to be $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)|V_{cd}|=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$. Taking the value of $|V_{cd}|$ from the standard model global fit and $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)$ derived by the lattice quantum chromodynamics calculation as input, we obtain $f^{D\toπ}_+(0)=0.1425\pm0.0005_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|V_{cd}|=0.2262\pm0.0008_{\rm stat.}\pm0.0005_{\rm syst.}\pm0.0018_{\rm LQCD.}$, respectively. The precision of each result is a factor of 2-3 better than the previous best measurements. Additionally, the real and imaginary parts of the scalar current contribution in the $c\to d \ell^+ν_{\ell}$ transition are measured for the first time to be Re $(C_S^μ)=$ $0.022 \pm 0.023_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.003_{\rm syst.}$ and $|\mathrm{Im} (C_S^μ)|=0.000 \pm 0.038_{\rm stat.}\pm 0.012_{\rm syst.}$.

</details>


### [242] [The mathematical theory of photomultiplier tube calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24250)
*L. N. Kalousis,E. A. Dris,A. Vynias*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文以教学式风格系统阐述了光电倍增管（photomultiplier tube, PMT）校准的数学理论，涵盖工作原理、电荷输出建模、增益校准方法及软电荷分量的影响，并辅以蒙特卡洛数据和实验数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为初学者和年轻研究者提供一份关于光电倍增管（PMT）校准理论的教学参考，以清晰易懂的方式解释校准过程中的数学方法和物理概念。

Method: 本文从理论角度讨论PMT的基本工作原理（光电转换、聚焦、倍增等），建立弱泊松光源下电荷输出的数学模型；介绍计算电荷放大函数 S_R(x) 的常用数值方法，并采用单光电子（SPE）响应的伽马函数模型进行示例绘制；利用玩具蒙特卡洛（toy Monte Carlo）数据检验增益校准技术的精度，并对Hamamatsu R1408型PMT的实际数据进行分析。

Result: 展示了基于伽马函数模型的单光电子（SPE）响应下的电荷放大函数 S_R(x) 及其主要特征；通过玩具蒙特卡洛数据验证了基本增益校准技术的精度；分析了Hamamatsu R1408 PMT的实验数据；揭示了软电荷分量（soft charge component）的存在会影响增益测定结果。

Conclusion: 本文可作为学生和年轻研究者学习PMT校准理论的入门参考；对于重要遗漏内容，建议读者查阅标准文献；最后对大型探测器中的原位校准（in situ calibration）给出了一般性评论。

Abstract: In this technical note we describe the main features of the mathematical theory of photomultiplier tube (PMT) calibration. Attention was paid to explain the various arguments and concepts in a simple and pedagogical manner that everybody understands. The basic operational principles of a PMT are discussed from a theoretical standpoint. The essential steps of its function (photoconversion, focusing, multiplication, etc.) are laid down together with the mathematical schemes necessary to model the charge output of a PMT when illuminated by a faint poissonian light source. In case of important omissions we direct the reader to some of the standard references. The most common numerical methods, used to calculate the charge amplification function $S_R(x)$, are also presented. As an example, we plot $S_R(x)$ utilizing a gamma function model for the single photoelectron (SPE) response and showcase its main characteristics. The basic techniques for gain calibration are also introduced, and we probed their precision using toy Monte Carlo data. Additionally, data from a Hamamatsu R1408 PMT were analyzed. Finally, we show how the presence of soft charge component can affect the results of gain determination. We conclude this report with some general comments regarding \emph{in situ} calibration of large-scale detectors. We hope that this document can serve as a reference for students and young researchers that want to learn more about the theory of PMT calibration.

</details>


### [243] [Static compilation of Julia packages for integration with existing HEP codebases: a case study with JetReconstruction.jl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24321)
*Mateusz Jakub Fila,Graeme A Stewart*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将 Julia 语言代码静态编译后集成到高能物理（HEP）现有 C++/Python 代码库中的可行性。以 JetReconstruction.jl 包为案例，比较了 PackageCompiler.jl 和 Julia v1.12 新增的静态编译功能，并评估了静态编译后代码与原生 Julia 代码及 C++ FastJet 的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理（HEP）计算社区正在考虑向 Julia 语言过渡，但这一过程将很漫长，需要实现 Julia 与现有 C++ 代码库的双向互操作性。此前已有工作将 HEP 的 C++ 包封装给 Julia 使用，但反过来——让现有 C++/Python 代码能够调用 Julia 代码——同样重要，这能显著提升 Julia 代码的实际采纳率。

Method: 本文以 JetReconstruction.jl 包（一个高性能原生 Julia 实现的序列喷注重建算法）为案例，将其进行静态编译。对比了两种 Julia 代码编译后端：已有的 PackageCompiler.jl 和 Julia v1.12 版本中新增的静态编译功能（static compilation feature）。

Result: 静态编译后的 JetReconstruction.jl 与原生 Julia 代码和 C++ FastJet 进行了性能比较。具体性能数据摘要未详述，但指出了静态编译方法正变得日益可行。

Conclusion: 借助 Julia 语言最新的静态编译能力，将 Julia 代码集成到现有 HEP 代码库中具有可行性，有望促进 Julia 在 HEP 社区中的采纳。Julia v1.12 的静态编译功能是该方法可行性的关键推动因素。

Abstract: The Julia programming language is considered a strong contender as a future language for high-energy physics (HEP) computing. However, transitioning to the Julia ecosystem will be a long process and interoperability between Julia and C++ is required. So far several successful attempts have been made to wrap HEP C++ packages for use in Julia. It is also important to explore the reverse direction, allowing Julia code to be called from existing HEP codebases, written primarily in C++ and Python, which would significantly improve potential adoption of Julia code. With recent developments in Julia enabling it to produce statically compiled code, this approach is becoming increasingly feasible, and investigating this potential for the benefit of HEP community is the focus of this work.
  This work presents a case study of statically compiling the JetReconstruction.jl package - a highly performant, native Julia implementation of sequential jet reconstruction algorithms. Two different backends for Julia code compilation are compared: the existing PackageCompiler.jl and the new static compilation feature of Julia language, which is one of the major improvements in the Julia v1.12 release. The performance of the statically compiled JetReconstruction.jl is compared with both native Julia code and C++ FastJet.

</details>


### [244] [Dark Matter Searches with Sodium Iodide Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24448)
*Maria Martinez,Reina H. Maruyama,Maria Luisa Sarsa*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了掺铊碘化钠（NaI）探测器在暗物质（dark matter）研究中的作用，重点回顾了DAMA/NaI与DAMA/LIBRA实验报告的年调制信号（annual modulation signal）及其引发的争议，并介绍了旨在验证该异常的后续NaI实验的现状与前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质身份的寻找是当前物理学最具挑战性的问题之一，而掺铊碘化钠（NaI）探测器在其中扮演了关键角色。本文旨在系统梳理以NaI探测器为核心的暗物质研究图景，特别是围绕DAMA实验尚未被其他实验复现的年调制信号（annual modulation signal）这一核心争议。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性（review）论文，通过回顾历史、描述探测器特性、分析实验结果以及总结最新进展，对NaI基暗物质探测研究进行了系统性梳理。重点讨论了DAMA/NaI与DAMA/LIBRA实验的观测结果以及后续旨在验证该异常的其他NaI实验。

Result: DAMA/NaI与DAMA/LIBRA实验报告了与暗物质相互作用（dark matter interactions）一致的年调制信号（annual modulation signal），但迄今为止其他实验均未能复现这一结果。近年来，部分针对该异常而发起的NaI基实验已取得最新成果。

Conclusion: 掺铊碘化钠（NaI）探测器在暗物质（dark matter）探测中具有重要地位，其面临的DAMA信号争议尚无定论。当前和未来的NaI基暗物质搜寻实验正持续推进，有望最终澄清这一谜团。

Abstract: The search for the identity of dark matter remains one of the most intriguing challenges in physics today, driving innovative advances in experiment and theory. Thallium-doped sodium iodide (NaI) detectors have played a pivotal role in this context. This review outlines the scientific landscape surrounding NaI detectors, emphasizing their role in the broader context of dark matter research. We begin with a brief description of characteristics that make these detectors well-suited for dark matter detection. We then provide a historical overview and explore the results from the DAMA/NaI and DAMA/LIBRA experiments, which have reported annual modulation signals consistent with dark matter interactions--a signal that to date no other experiment has replicated. We discuss recent results from NaI-based experiments launched, in part, to resolve this tantalizing anomaly. We conclude with a summary of the status and prospects for current and future NaI-based dark matter searches.

</details>
