### [245] [Evolution of the electronic and superconducting properties of Re-based quinary high-entropy alloys under chemical and physical pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23056)
*Thiagarajan Maran,Yoshiya Uwatoko,Sathea Suweatha M N,Sonika Jangid,R. P. Singh,Arumugam Sonachalam,Sathiskumar Mariappan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过对比化学压力（Re替代）和物理压力（静水压）对Re基五元高熵合金（HEAs）[Nb₀.₆₇₋ₓRex][TiZrHf]₀.₃₃（x = 0.10, 0.20, 0.56）电子性质和超导电性的影响，发现超导转变温度Tc随Re浓度增加而降低（5.4→3.9 K），且Tc的压力系数dTc/dP在bcc相（正）与hcp相（负）之间发生符号翻转，表明晶体结构是压缩下的关键调控变量；化学替代与静水压缩是互补但不等价的超导调控途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Re基五元高熵合金（high-entropy alloys, HEAs）体系中，化学压力（化学取代）与物理压力（静水压缩）对超导电性的作用是否等价尚不清楚，因此需要对二者的电子性质和超导响应进行系统比较。

Method: 选取三种Re含量（x = 0.10, 0.20, 0.56）的[Nb₀.₆₇₋ₓRex][TiZrHf]₀.₃₃合金，通过场相关输运和磁化测量表征超导参数，并在约8 GPa范围内测量其压力相关的超导转变温度Tc，结合晶体结构（bcc与hcp）差异进行对比分析。

Result: 增加Re浓度使Tc从5.4 K降至3.9 K，并产生正且成分依赖的鸡尾酒效应比（cocktail-effect ratios）。三种成分均为强第二类超导体（strongly type-II superconductors），其中x = 0.20的上临界场、Ginzburg-Landau参数最大，Maki参数接近1。Tc的压力系数dTc/dP在bcc样品中为正（~0.018和0.053 K/GPa），在hcp样品中为负（~-0.033 K/GPa），并在约8 GPa内超导和金属输运保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在单一化学关联的合金系列中，dTc/dP随bcc-hcp结构转变发生符号翻转，建立了结构关联的超导响应规律，表明晶体结构是压缩下的关键组织变量（organizing variable）。化学替代与静水压缩是互补但不等价的超导调控途径。

Abstract: We report a comparative study of chemical- and physical-pressure effects on the electronic and superconducting properties of the Re-based quinary high-entropy alloys (HEAs) [Nb0.67-xRex][TiZrHf]0.33 (x = 0.10, 0.20, and 0.56). Increasing Re concentration suppresses the superconducting transition temperature Tc from 5.4 to 3.9 K while producing positive, composition-dependent cocktail-effect ratios. Field-dependent transport and magnetization establish all three compositions as strongly type-II superconductors and identify x = 0.20 as the most distinctive composition, with the largest upper critical field and Ginzburg-Landau parameter and a Maki parameter close to unity. Most importantly, the pressure coefficient of Tc changes sign across the bcc-hcp structural change: dTc/dP is positive for the bcc x = 0.10 and 0.20 samples, with values of approximately 0.018 and 0.053 K/GPa, respectively, but negative for the hcp x = 0.56 sample, with a value of approximately -0.033 K/GPa. This systematic contrast within a single chemically related alloy series establishes a robust structure-associated superconducting response and identifies crystal structure as a key organizing variable under compression. Metallic transport and superconductivity remain robust up to approximately 8 GPa in all three compositions. These results reveal that chemical substitution and hydrostatic compression are complementary but nonequivalent routes for tuning superconductivity in Re-based HEAs.

</details>


### [246] [Bipolar Thermoelectric Superconducting Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23262)
*F. Antola,G. Marchegiani,A. Braggio,F. Giazotto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了双极性热电效应（bipolar thermoelectric effect），这是一种在互易系统（reciprocal systems）中由对称性禁止线性效应而产生的热电转换新机制，其产生的电压可在固定温度梯度下呈现两种极性，构成对传统热电效应的重要拓展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术对硬件组件及其运行的非平衡态（non-equilibrium regimes）精确建模的需求日益增长，其中热与能量流的管理成为核心挑战。热电效应（thermoelectric effects）——即热梯度直接转换为电信号——为这一控制提供了途径。该综述旨在系统介绍双极性热电效应这一新近进展。

Method: 文章首先总结双极性热电效应的基本物理原理，指出热电现象本质上是对细致平衡（detailed balance）的强破坏。随后给出获得该效应的具体物理条件，其典型范例是两超导体之间的隧道结（tunnel junction），要求超导能隙不相等且约瑟夫森耦合（Josephson coupling）被抑制。

Result: 该效应在固定温度梯度下产生的电压可呈现两种极性，这不同于传统热电效应中载流子主导决定信号符号的机制。文章讨论了该效应的实验观测现状以及相关应用提案，包括易失性存储器（volatile memories）和辐射探测（radiation detection）。

Conclusion: 文章简要展望了该领域的近期发展与未来方向，涵盖向新平台的拓展以及一种新型量子热电效应（quantum thermoelectric effect）的提议。

Abstract: Quantum technologies increasingly require accurate modeling of their hardware components and of the non-equilibrium regimes in which they operate, where managing heat and energy flow becomes a central challenge. Thermoelectric effects, the direct conversion of a thermal gradient into electrical signals, offer one such route to this control. In this review, we present an overview of the bipolar thermoelectric effect, a recent development for thermoelectric conversion in reciprocal systems, where linear effects are forbidden by symmetry. This symmetry yields a bipolar thermoelectric signal, in which the generated voltage can exhibit both polarities at a fixed temperature gradient. This represents a non-trivial novelty relative to conventional thermoelectric effects, in which carrier dominance determines the sign of the thermoelectric signal. We summarize the underlying physical principles, showing how thermoelectricity emerges as a strong violation of detailed balance. Concrete physical conditions for obtaining bipolar thermoelectricity are then outlined, of which a tunnel junction between two superconductors with unequal energy gaps and suppressed Josephson coupling is the paradigmatic example. Afterward, we discuss the experimental observation of the effect to date and related proposals for different applications, including volatile memories and radiation detection. Finally, we briefly survey recent developments and outlooks, ranging from extensions to new platforms to a proposal for a novel quantum thermoelectric effect.

</details>


### [247] [Berry curvature effects of chiral superconducting rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23764)
*Jian-Hua Zeng,Zhi Wang,Qian Niu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本论文研究了手性超导菱方石墨烯中Bogoliubov准粒子的贝里曲率效应，发现Bogoliubov费米面对反常霍尔输运产生定性调制，导致热霍尔电导偏离量子化值并显著增强自旋和轨道Nernst响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索菱方石墨烯在手性p波超导态下，Bogoliubov准粒子的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）效应，特别是正常态能带翘曲对反常输运现象的影响。

Method: 采用双带Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量描述超导准粒子，计算动量空间贝里曲率、轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment），以及反常热霍尔（anomalous thermal-Hall）和反常自旋/轨道Nernst输运（spin- and orbital-Nernst transport）。

Result: 正常态能带翘曲可导致Bogoliubov费米面（Bogoliubov Fermi surface）的出现；该费米面对反常霍尔输运产生定性调制，使热霍尔电导（thermal Hall conductivity）偏离量子化值，并强烈增强自旋和轨道Nernst响应。

Conclusion: Bogoliubov费米面是调控手性超导菱方石墨烯中反常输运行为的关键因素，其存在显著改变了热霍尔、自旋和轨道Nernst等输运性质。

Abstract: We study the Berry curvature effects of the Bogoliubov quasiparticles in chiral superconducting rhombohedral graphene. Using a two-band Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian to describe the superconducting quasiparticles, we calculate the momentum-space Berry curvature, the orbital magnetic moment, the anomalous thermal-Hall and the anomalous spin- and orbital-Nernst transport of the chiral $p$-wave superconducting states. We investigate the impact of the normal-state energy band warping in rhombohedral graphene, which can cause the Bogoliubov Fermi surface for quasiparticle excitations. We find that the Bogoliubov Fermi surface qualitatively modulates the anomalous Hall transports, inducing a deviation of the thermal Hall conductivity from the quantized value and strongly enhancing the spin- and orbital-Nernst responses.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Emergent integrable dynamics in a non-integrable Rydberg-atom chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22933)
*Gianluca Morettini,Luca Capizzi,Leonardo Mazza,Maurizio Fagotti*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文证明在相同的里德伯原子（Rydberg-atom）链中，可积（integrable）与不可积（non-integrable）动力学可以共存，具体取决于系统制备的初态。近邻近似下系统可映射为可积的弹道输运费米气体，但长程耦合会破坏可积性守恒律并产生扩散输运。研究表明，可积性破缺不仅是哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）及其耦合强度的属性，也依赖于系统的初态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为可积性破缺（integrability breaking）仅由哈密顿量及其耦合项决定，本文旨在探索初态是否也能影响可积性破缺的程度，并揭示同一哈密顿量中可积与不可积动力学共存的机制。

Method: 采用里德伯原子链模型，在近邻近似下将系统映射为可积的费米气体（Fermi gas）；同时引入长程耦合以描述更真实的动力学。通过二分协议（bipartition protocol）研究动力学行为，并随粒子密度（particle density）变化观察输运性质的转变。

Result: 发现当粒子密度变化时，系统的动力学行为出现显著定性转变，表明在同一哈密顿量下，根据局域相互作用的相关性，可以访问较弱或较强的可积性破缺动力学。长程耦合破坏了可积性相关的守恒律，使得真正的扩散输运（diffusive transport）得以出现。

Conclusion: 可积性破缺不仅是哈密顿量及其耦合强度的函数，也取决于系统制备的初态。该发现为初态调控量子多体动力学中的可积性提供了清晰的实验证据。

Abstract: We show that integrable and non-integrable dynamics can coexist in the same Rydberg-atom chain, depending on the initial state in which the system is prepared. In the setting we consider, Rydberg atoms mainly experience an effective dipolar interaction and, within the nearest-neighbor approximation, their dynamics can be mapped onto an effective integrable Fermi gas with ballistic transport. The inclusion of longer-range couplings, however, is essential for the theory to be predictive; those terms break the conservation laws associated with integrability, enabling, in particular, the emergence of genuine diffusive transport. We study the dynamics generated by a bipartition protocol and reveal a sharp qualitative change in behavior as the particle density is varied, suggesting the possibility of accessing weaker and stronger integrability breaking dynamics in the same Hamiltonian depending on the relevance of local interactions. We propose a theoretical mechanism that accounts for the differences. Our findings provide clear and concrete evidence that integrability breaking is not solely a property of the Hamiltonian and of the magnitude of the couplings that break integrability, but also of the state in which the system is prepared.

</details>


### [249] [Dynamical control of particle jets from a driven condensate in a one-dimensional lattice with double-well potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23533)
*Z. Li,L. Q. Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维双阱势阱中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）的非线性动力学，重点分析了周期性调制的原子间相互作用、阱间不对称性以及跳跃振幅调制对粒子发射行为的影响，揭示了对量子多体输运的精确操控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在周期性驱动下双阱势阱中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的非线性动力学行为，特别是粒子集体发射的激发机制，以及阱深不对称性和跳跃振幅调制对发射过程的调控作用。

Method: 基于一维晶格的双阱势阱（double-well potential）模型，对原子间相互作用进行周期性时间调制（periodically modulated in time）；同时引入阱间深度不对称性和跳跃振幅调制，系统分析不同参数下的粒子发射行为。

Result: 对称双阱情况下，在共振驱动下观察到集体粒子发射，且激发区间由驱动强度（drive strength）与跳跃振幅（hopping amplitude）的竞争关系约束。中等程度的阱间不对称性（depth asymmetry）可增强发射速率，而大的不对称性则抑制发射。跳跃振幅的不平衡会削弱粒子发射。

Conclusion: 不对称性和外部驱动在精确操控量子多体输运（quantum many-body transport）中具有关键作用，研究结果为原子电子学器件（atomtronic devices）的设计提供了理论参考。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear dynamics of a Bose-Einstein condensate trapped in a double-well potential of a one-dimensional lattice, where the interatomic interactions are periodically modulated in time. In the typical case of a symmetric double-well, we observe collective particle emission under resonant driving, where the excitation regimes are explicitly constrained by the interplay between the drive strength and the hopping amplitude. By introducing a depth asymmetry between the wells, we find that moderate bias specifically enhances the emission rate, while large asymmetry suppresses it. The particle jets can be further controlled by modulating the hopping amplitudes, where the emission is weakened for finite hopping imbalances. These results outline the roles of asymmetry and external driving in precisely manipulating quantum many-body transport, and may offer insights into the design of atomtronic devices.

</details>


### [250] [Permutationally Invariant Quantum State Tomography for Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23579)
*Shion Yamashika,Daisuke Yamamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出一种用于置换不变（permutation-invariant）且具有U(1)粒子数对称性的费米子多体态的层析成像协议，所需观测量仅随系统规模线性增长，且在当前超冷原子实验中可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析（quantum state tomography）虽然能提供量子态的完整信息，但其测量成本通常随系统规模指数增长；在多粒子量子模拟器中，局部测量和控制的有限可及性进一步加剧了这一挑战。

Method: 利用置换不变性和U(1)粒子数对称性，证明任意此类态完全由总粒子数分布和每个粒子数扇区内单个集体模的占据数决定；所需观测量仅线性依赖于系统规模。

Result: 在复杂Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型的相互作用非高斯态以及跨越Lifshitz相变（Lifshitz transition）的自由费米子链中验证了该协议的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为在实验上可行的费米子量子模拟器中对强关联巡游量子物质进行信息论表征开辟了路径。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography provides complete information about a quantum state, but its measurement cost generally grows exponentially with system size. In many-particle quantum simulators, this challenge is further compounded by the limited accessibility of local measurements and controls. Here we develop a tomography protocol for permutation-invariant fermionic many-body states with U(1) particle-number symmetry. We show that any such state is completely determined by the distribution of the total particle number and the occupation of a single collective mode within each particle-number sector, both of which are accessible in current ultracold-atom experiments. The number of required observables scales only linearly with the system size. More generally, the protocol reconstructs the permutation-symmetrized component of arbitrary U(1)-symmetric fermionic states, which can still encode nontrivial many-body and state-level structure beyond conventional few-body observables. We demonstrate this protocol in interacting non-Gaussian states of the complex Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model and in free-fermion chains across a Lifshitz transition. This framework opens a route toward information-theoretic characterization of strongly correlated itinerant quantum matter in experimentally realistic fermionic quantum simulators.

</details>


### [251] [Comment on "Observation of Kardar--Parisi--Zhang universal scaling in two dimensions"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24152)
*J. Bloch,M. Escalera,Q. Fontaine,F. Helluin,A. Minguzzi,L. Canet,S. Ravets*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文对Widmann等人发表在Science上的关于二维极化激元凝聚体（polariton condensate）显示Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（KPZ）普适标度（universal scaling）的声称提出质疑。作者重新分析了原始实验数据，发现在所研究的时空窗口内，相干性衰减（coherence decay）实际呈现高斯（Gaussian）或指数（exponential）衰减，而非拉伸指数（stretched exponential）形式。数据坍塌（data collapse）到KPZ标度函数是数据归一化（normalization）不当造成的伪迹，因此KPZ普适行为的核心主张不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Widmann等人声称在耦合谐振腔阵列中的二维极化激元凝聚体中观测到了KPZ普适标度行为，这一结论若成立将对非平衡统计场论（non-equilibrium statistical field theory）和量子流体（quantum fluids）实验产生重要影响。作者旨在检验该声称的有效性，通过重新分析原始数据来评估其分析方法的正确性。

Method: 作者重新分析了Widmann等人实验中在正方形和三角形晶格（square and triangular lattices）以及不同激发功率（excitation power）下获取的原始数据。作者在摘要所考虑的时空窗口内，测量了$|g^{(1)}(δ{\bf r}, δt)|$在$δt=0$和$|δ{\bf r}|$接近0时的衰减行为，并使用时空指数$β=0.5$和$χ=1$检验数据坍塌。

Result: 作者发现，在所有激发功率下，空间相干性（spatial coherence）呈现高斯衰减，时间相干性（temporal coherence）呈现指数衰减，而非原文声称的拉伸指数行为。使用$β=0.5$和$χ=1$时，数据可坍塌到单一曲线，但该曲线并非KPZ标度函数。原文中数据坍塌到KPZ标度函数的现象是错误数据归一化造成的伪迹。

Conclusion: Widmann等人论文的核心主张未得到支持：在所分析的时空窗口内，相干性的时空衰减并非由KPZ普适行为（KPZ universal behavior）所描述。

Abstract: In their paper published in Science 392, 221 (2026), Widmann and collaborators reported interferometry experiments to explore the emission coherence decay of a two-dimensional polariton condensate generated in an array of coupled resonators. The authors claim evidence of Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universal scaling in the measured spatio-temporal coherence decay. We argue in the following that the data were not properly analyzed. We re-analyze the experimental data acquired both with the square and the triangular lattices for various values of the excitation power. Instead of stretched exponential decays, in the space (time) windows considered in the paper we find that the measured $|g^{(1)}(δ{\bf r}, δt)|$ at $δt=0$ ($|δ{\bf r}| $ close to $0$) rather show Gaussian (exponential) decay for all excitation powers. As a result, using as temporal and spatial exponents $β=0.5$ and $χ=1$, the data for all pump powers are found to collapse onto a single curve, which is not the KPZ scaling function. In particular, we show that the data collapse onto the KPZ scaling function presented in the paper is an artifact stemming from incorrect data normalization. We thus conclude that the main claim of the paper is not justified as the spatio-temporal decays of the coherence over the space-time windows analyzed in the paper are not well described by the KPZ universal behavior.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Continuous-Time Random Walk Description of Anomalous Spin Transport in Dilute Dipolar Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22626)
*Cooper M. Selco,Christian Bengs,Ashok Ajoy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过连续时间随机游走（CTRW）模型研究天然丰度金刚石中$^{13}$C极化输运，发现无序和长程偶极耦合导致反常输运现象，表明稀释偶极自旋网络需要超越菲克扩散方程的微观网络分辨描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核自旋扩散通常被简化为单一扩散系数，但这种粗粒化描述在稀释固体中可能失效，因为位置无序和长程偶极耦合会产生宽分布的跳变速率。

Method: 发展了一种$^{13}$C极化输运的连续时间随机游走（CTRW）描述，从偶极介导的翻转-翻转（flip-flop）耦合构建速率矩阵，并采样精确的连续时间轨迹。

Result: 无序平均动力学产生反常输运：等待时间分布呈重尾（指数$α=0.64$，指数截断$t_{\rm cutoff}=19$ s）；均方位移在步数和物理时间上均呈次线性增长（指数$γ=0.56$和$δ=0.87$）；微观起源归因于几何俘获（geometric trapping）。

Conclusion: 稀释偶极自旋网络需要超越菲克（Fickian）扩散方程的微观网络分辨输运描述；CTRW框架可链接经典反应-扩散理论中的俘获研究。

Abstract: Nuclear spin diffusion is often summarized by a single diffusion coefficient, but this coarse-grained description can fail in dilute solids where positional disorder and long-range dipolar couplings generate a broad distribution of hopping rates. We develop a continuous-time random-walk (CTRW) description of $^{13}$C polarization transport in natural-abundance diamond (1.1%), constructing the rate matrix from dipolar-mediated flip-flop couplings and sampling exact continuous-time trajectories. Although site-to-site hopping is Markovian, the disorder-averaged dynamics give rise to emergent, anomalous transport. The empirical waiting-time distribution exhibits a heavy tail with exponent $α=0.64$ and exponential cutoff $t_{\rm cutoff}=19$ s; the mean jump length becomes correlated with the waiting time $τ$ for $τ\gtrsim0.1$ s; and the mean-squared displacement grows sublinearly in both step number and physical time, with exponents $γ=0.56$ and $δ=0.87$ respectively. We trace the microscopic origin of these signatures to geometric trapping: polarization can rapidly exchange within strongly coupled clusters, including dimers, while weak inter-cluster links control long-range exploration. A kinetic percolation construction links global transport to inter-cluster crossing times, and identifies a corresponding crossing time of $\sim20$ s, consistent with $t_{\rm cutoff}$. Finally, mapping paramagnetic impurities onto hard-sphere traps connects the CTRW framework to classic studies of trapping in reaction-diffusion theory and reproduces the qualitative timescale of experimentally measured relaxation, whereas a continuum diffusion equation description does not. These results show that dilute dipolar spin networks require a microscopic, network-resolved transport description beyond the Fickian diffusion equation.

</details>


### [253] [Statistical Mechanics of Thermal Diffusion in Rough Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22742)
*Soham Chattopadhyay,Blas P. Uberuaga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于质量输运的变分原理（variational principle），提出一个适用于复杂固体热扩散（thermal diffusion）的统计框架（statistical framework）。研究表明微观热力学与动力学涨落（fluctuations）主导非阿伦尼乌斯扩散（non-Arrhenius diffusion），其中动力学项可由机器学习可学习的相对扩散贡献（machine-learnable relative diffusion contributions）描述。对于固溶体（solid solutions）中的空位扩散（vacancy diffusion），当有序化效应（ordering effects）变得重要时偏离Arrhenius行为，但在更高温度下，组态熵（configurational entropy）与竞争性能量涨落有助于近似恢复Arrhenius行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散理论难以统一描述复杂固体中偏离经典阿伦尼乌斯（Arrhenius）行为的热扩散现象，缺乏一个兼具微观热力学与动力学涨落视角的统计框架。本文旨在从质量输运的变分原理出发，构建一个广泛适用的理论方法。

Method: 从质量输运的变分原理（variational principle）出发，建立一个统计框架（statistical framework），将微观热力学与动力学涨落（fluctuations）纳入扩散描述，并引入机器学习可学习的相对扩散贡献（machine-learnable relative diffusion contributions）处理动力学项。

Result: 在固溶体中的空位扩散体系中，当有序化效应开始重要时扩散偏离Arrhenius行为；而在更高温度下，组态熵与竞争性能量涨落共同促使扩散行为近似回归Arrhenius行为。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统计框架能够统一描述复杂固体中的热扩散现象，揭示了非Arrhenius扩散的微观机制，并为结合机器学习方法处理动力学贡献提供了理论路径。

Abstract: Starting from a variational principle for mass transport, we present a broadly applicable statistical framework describing thermal diffusion in complex solids. We show that microscopic thermodynamic and kinetic fluctuations govern non-Arrhenius diffusion, with kinetic terms described by machine-learnable relative diffusion contributions. For vacancy diffusion in solid solutions, deviation from Arrheniusness may occur when ordering effects start to become important but at higher temperatures, approximately Arrhenius behavior can occur, aided by configurational entropy and competing energy fluctuations.

</details>


### [254] [Seven- and eight-loop critical exponents of the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07776)
*D. Shapoval,Yu. Honchar,B. Delamotte,M. Dudka,Yu. Holovatch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过共形映射与同调变换相结合的重整化群级数重和方法，在三维伊辛普适类（three-dimensional Ising universality class）的ε展开（ε=4-d）中精确估计了临界指数η、ν和修正标度指数ω，并发现高圈阶结果与共形自举（conformal bootstrap）估计之间存在轻微但系统的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用最近计算出的七圈和八圈重整化群（renormalization group）级数，在ε展开框架下更精确地确定三维伊辛普适类的临界指数η、ν以及修正标度指数ω，并对估计值提供量化的不确定性评估。

Method: 采用共形映射（conformal mapping）与同调变换（homographic transformation）相结合的重和方法，并根据两个互补性准则对重和参数进行优化。

Result: η的误差棒（error bar）随圈阶增加而快速减小，而ν和ω的误差棒则未呈现此趋势。尽管估计值在绝对意义上准确，但其随圈阶的收敛速度较慢，导致与当前被视为基准的共形自举估计值之间存在轻微但系统的张力（tension）。

Conclusion: 讨论了上述张力可能的若干成因，以及该行为对微扰重整化群级数高阶重和（high-order resummation）的启示意义。

Abstract: We determine the critical exponents $η$, $ν$, and the correction-to-scaling exponent $ω$ of the three-dimensional Ising universality class by resumming the recently computed seven- and eight-loop renormalization-group series in the $ε=4-d$ expansion (O.~Schnetz, \textit{Phys. Rev. D} \textbf{97}, 085018 (2018); O.~Schnetz, \textit{Phys. Rev. D} \textbf{107}, 036002 (2023)). The resummation combines conformal mapping with a homographic transformation, while the resummation parameters are optimized according to two complementary criteria. This approach yields precise estimates of the critical exponents together with quantitative uncertainty estimates. We find that the error bar on $η$ decreases rapidly with increasing loop order, whereas this is the case neither for $ν$ nor for $ω$. Unexpectedly, although the estimated values are accurate in absolute terms, their slow convergence with the loop order leads to a slight but systematic tension with the conformal bootstrap estimates that are currently considered as the benchmark. We discuss several possible origins of this behavior and its implications for high-order resummations of perturbative renormalization-group series.

</details>


### [255] [Response Morphologies of a Canonical Fluctuation Diagnostic Across Ehrenfest Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22978)
*Fangfang Wang,Wei Liu,Ying Tang,Zengru Di*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过引入一种基于能量涨落不对称性的正则系综诊断量，研究了该诊断量在八态Potts模型、二维Ising模型和理想三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中的行为，发现该可观测量在不同热力学体制下展现出定性不同的响应形态，能够反映相变的本质特征，但并非直接对应Ehrenfest分类的阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微正则系综中的拐点分析（inflection-point analysis）可以从微正则熵的导数中识别MIPA型高阶相变结构，但长期以来缺乏能够直接从可测量的能量涨落中探测对应涨落级行为的正则系综（canonical ensemble）表述，而无需重建态密度（density of states）。此前的工作通过引入一种量化能量涨落不对称性的正则涨落诊断量（canonical fluctuation diagnostic）解决了这一局限，但该诊断量在不同类型相变中的具体行为尚待系统探究。

Method: 研究选取了三种分别代表Ehrenfest分类中一阶、二阶和三阶相变的模型系统：八态Potts模型（eight-state Potts model）、二维Ising模型（two-dimensional Ising model）和理想三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（ideal three-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensation）。通过分析能量涨落不对称性诊断量在这些系统相变区域附近的行为，考察其响应形态。

Result: 在八态Potts模型和二维Ising模型中，该诊断量在相变区域附近展现出稳健的配对极值结构（paired-extremum structure）；而在理想三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中，该诊断量在热力学极限下的凝聚温度处表现为不连续响应（discontinuous response）。

Conclusion: 单一的基于涨落的可观测量（fluctuation-based observable）能够在不同热力学体制下展示出定性不同的响应形态，反映出其对相变本质特征的敏感性。然而，这种响应形态并非作为Ehrenfest阶数的直接分类器（direct classifier），而是体现为对底层相变性质的感知能力。

Abstract: Microcanonical inflection-point analysis identifies MIPA-type higher-order transition structures from derivatives of the microcanonical entropy. Until recently, however, there was no canonical formulation for probing the corresponding fluctuation-level behavior directly from measurable energy fluctuations without reconstructing the density of states. Previous work addressed this limitation by introducing a canonical fluctuation diagnostic that quantifies energy-fluctuation asymmetry. Here, we investigate how this diagnostic behaves across representative first-, second-, and third-order phase transitions in the Ehrenfest classification. We consider the eight-state Potts model, the two-dimensional Ising model, and ideal three-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensation. The Potts and Ising systems exhibit a robust paired-extremum structure of the diagnostic near their respective transition regions, whereas ideal Bose--Einstein condensation exhibits a discontinuous response at the condensation temperature in the thermodynamic limit. These results show that a single fluctuation-based observable can display qualitatively distinct response morphologies across thermodynamic regimes, reflecting sensitivity to the nature of the underlying phase transition rather than serving as a direct classifier of Ehrenfest order.

</details>


### [256] [Quantum fluctuation-driven transport crossover between two liquid states in distinguishable helium-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23127)
*Mika Tanabe,Momoko Tsujimoto,Kenichi Kinugawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过路径积分质心分子动力学模拟，在服从玻尔兹曼统计的可区分氦-4中发现了由量子涨落驱动的输运交叉：低温下出现了反常的气体动力学行为（高量子色散液体HQDL），包括超扩散、超低粘度及斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系失效，揭示了核量子涨落独立诱导气体行为的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有原子交换和超流性的条件下，纯粹的核量子涨落（nuclear quantum fluctuations）是否以及如何独立地诱导出气体状的输运行为，并揭示不同液态之间的输运交叉机制。

Method: 采用路径积分质心分子动力学模拟，在0.1–3.3 K和1–60 bar范围内研究可区分氦-4的输运性质，通过速度自相关函数（velocity autocorrelation function, VAF）、普朗特数（Prandtl number）及各种输运系数分析两种液体状态。

Result: 识别出低量子色散液体（LQDL）和高量子色散液体（HQDL）两种状态；LQDL符合斯托克斯-爱因斯坦（SE）关系，而HQDL表现出超扩散、超低粘度和SE关系失效；VAF从振荡型变为单调型，定义了第二条弗伦克尔线（second Frenkel line）；在剪切粘度、运动粘度、热导率和热扩散率上观察到输运最小值。

Conclusion: 核量子涨落足以独立诱导气体状液体行为，LQDL是热输运主导的耗散流体而HQDL是动量主导的惯性流体，为可区分氦-4中无超流的输运现象提供了统一图景。

Abstract: We show the emergence of a quantum fluctuation-driven transport crossover between two liquid states in distinguishable helium-4 obeying Boltzmann statistics, in the absence of atomic exchange. Using path integral centroid molecular dynamics simulations over 0.1-3.3 K and 1-60 bar, we investigate the transport properties of two distinct liquid states: the low quantum-dispersion liquid (LQDL) and the high quantum-dispersion liquid (HQDL). While LQDL exhibits conventional liquid behavior consistent with the Stokes-Einstein (SE) relation, HQDL emerges at lower temperatures and displays anomalous gas-like transport characterized by superdiffusion and ultralow viscosity, accompanied by a breakdown of the SE relation. This counterintuitive emergence of gas-like dynamics upon cooling reflects the dominant role of nuclear quantum fluctuations, in contrast to thermal fluctuations at higher temperatures. Across the LQDL-HQDL boundary, we identify a transport crossover marked by a qualitative change in the velocity autocorrelation function (VAF), a transition in the Prandtl number, and the emergence of transport minima in shear and kinematic viscosities, thermal conductivity, and thermal diffusivity. These minima reflect a crossover from liquid-like to gas-like transport upon cooling in the low-temperature subcritical region, in addition to the universal transport minima observed in the supercritical regime. The transition from oscillatory to monotonic VAF defines a second Frenkel line, distinct from the conventional Frenkel line observed in the supercritical region. LQDL is a heat-transport-dominated dissipative fluid, whereas HQDL is a momentum-dominated inertial fluid. These results demonstrate that nuclear quantum fluctuations alone induce gas-like liquid behavior and provide a unified picture of transport phenomena in distinguishable helium-4 without superfluidity.

</details>


### [257] [Reminiscences about Hans Capel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24380)
*Constantino Tsallis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是作者为纪念 Hans Willem Capel 而撰写的个人回忆与感想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特殊场合向 Hans Willem Capel 表达敬意与怀念。

Method: 以个人回忆和主观感想的形式呈现。

Result: 文中未报告任何具体的研究结果或学术发现。

Conclusion: 本文属于纪念性随笔，未得出实质性学术结论。

Abstract: As an homage to the memory of Hans Willem Capel, I present some personal reminiscences and thoughts that come to my mind in this special occasion.

</details>


### [258] [Chiral Dynamics of an Intruder across Dilute and Hydrodynamic Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23221)
*Raphaël Maire,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并模拟了一个解析可处理模型，研究非平衡浴中任意形状入侵者的手性（chirality）输运。在稀薄与稠密两种极限下，分别通过Langevin方程和含手性扭矩密度的Stokes方程描述动力学，揭示了入侵者形状驱动棘轮效应（ratchet effects）和奇异性响应（odd response）的不同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡浴中任意形状入侵者的手性输运行为，特别是从稀薄到稠密极限下形状如何影响棘轮效应与奇异性响应，以及这些效应背后的物理机制差异。

Method: 构建了一个解析可处理的模型，入侵者的手性可源于非平衡浴、入侵者本身或二者的耦合。在稀薄极限下，从Boltzmann-Lorentz方程导出Langevin描述；在稠密极限下，利用含手性扭矩密度（chiral torque density）的Stokes方程描述入侵者动力学。

Result: 入侵者的几何形状在稀薄极限下通过Langevin方程支配棘轮效应和奇异性响应；在稠密极限下，入侵者动力学受浴的流体动力学模式（hydrodynamic modes）和边缘流（edge currents）控制，由含手性扭矩密度的Stokes方程描述。奇异性响应在稀薄与稠密两种极限下源于不同的物理机制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了形状与手性输运之间的关联，并表明奇异性响应在稀薄与稠密极限下由截然不同的机制产生。

Abstract: We introduce and simulate an analytically tractable model for an intruder of arbitrary shape in a nonequilibrium bath, with chirality originating from the bath, the intruder, or their coupling. In the dilute regime, a Langevin description derived from a Boltzmann-Lorentz equation shows how intruder geometry governs ratchet effects and odd response. In the dense regime, the dynamics of the intruder are instead governed by the hydrodynamic modes of the bath and edge currents, which are described by a Stokes equation including a chiral torque density. Our results link shape to chiral transport and show that odd response arises from distinct mechanisms in the dilute and dense limits.

</details>


### [259] [Kinetic and Hydrodynamic Theories of Chiral Intruder Dynamics in Nonequilibrium Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23223)
*Raphaël Maire,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从稀薄动力学（dilute kinetic regime）和稠密流体动力学（dense hydrodynamic regime）两个互补的极限出发，研究了浸没在非平衡浴（nonequilibrium bath）中的入侵者（intruder）的手性动力学（chiral dynamics）。研究分别推导了有效朗之万方程（Langevin equation）和论证了由扭矩密度驱动的边缘流（edge currents）机制，揭示了手性相互作用与形状手性在不同尺度下的作用差异与响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解入侵者在非平衡浴中的手性动力学机制，特别是将稀薄与稠密两种极限下的物理行为统一起来，跨越不同尺度揭示手性效应产生的根源。

Method: 在稀薄极限下，基于玻尔兹曼-洛伦兹（Boltzmann-Lorentz）描述出发，推导了有效朗之万方程，其系数由几何依赖的边界积分显式给出。在稠密极限下，依据浴流体动力学（bath hydrodynamics）和扭矩密度驱动的边缘流进行分析。

Result: 稀薄极限下，手性相互作用产生奇响应（odd response）和扭矩，而入侵者的形状手性（intruder-shape chirality）导致棘轮效应（ratchet effect）。存在类涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-dissipation-like relations），且某些对称性允许的耦合在稀薄区域消失。稠密极限下，边缘流可产生反对称阻力（antisymmetric drag）和曲率诱导的扭矩，计入惯性后呈现反对称响应（antisymmetric response）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了非平衡浴中入侵者手性动力学在稀薄与稠密两种极限下的不同机制，为跨尺度理解手性动力学提供了理论步骤。

Abstract: We study the chiral dynamics of an intruder immersed in a nonequilibrium bath in two complementary limits: the dilute kinetic regime and the dense hydrodynamic regime. In the dilute limit, starting from a Boltzmann-Lorentz description, we derive an effective Langevin equation whose coefficients are given explicitly by geometry-dependent boundary integrals. This formulation separates the effects of intruder-shape chirality from those of chiral intruder-bath interactions. We find that the chiral interactions generate an odd response and a torque, whereas the chirality of the intruder leads to a ratchet effect. We also show that fluctuation-dissipation-like relations exist and that certain symmetry-allowed couplings vanish in the dilute regime. In the dense regime, we argue that intruder dynamics are governed primarily by bath hydrodynamics and torque-density-driven edge currents not captured by the previous framework. These currents can generate both an antisymmetric drag and a curvature-induced torque, leading to an antisymmetric response when inertia is accounted for. Taken together, these results provide a step toward understanding the mechanisms governing the chiral dynamics of an intruder in a nonequilibrium bath across different scales.

</details>


### [260] [The Uhlenbeck-Ford model in two dimensions: Reference system for fluid-phase free-energy calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23824)
*Samuel Cajahuaringa,Rodolfo Paula Leite,Maurice de Koning*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了Uhlenbeck-Ford（UF）模型作为二维（2D）流体自由能计算的参考系统，精确计算了高达十阶的二维维里系数，结合分子模拟构建了状态方程和过量亥姆霍兹自由能的高精度数值表示，确定了相图和流体相的热力学稳定性范围，并通过勒纳德-琼斯（Lennard-Jones）流体的热力学积分验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维流体自由能计算建立高精度且可靠的参考系统，并明确该参考系统（UF模型）的热力学稳定区间，以避免在流体相不稳定区域误用。

Method: 精确计算二维UF模型的维里系数（virial coefficients）至十阶；结合分子模拟（molecular simulation）数据构建状态方程（equation of state）和过量亥姆霍兹自由能（excess Helmholtz free energy）的数值表示；通过确定相图（phase diagram）识别固相（solid）、六角相（hexatic）和流体相（fluid），划定流体相稳定范围。

Result: 对于缩放参数（scaling parameter）p ≲ 70，流体是唯一热力学稳定的相，与密度无关；通过二维Lennard-Jones流体的热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）计算，验证了UF模型作为参考系统的实际可行性。

Conclusion: UF模型在p ≲ 70的条件下可作为二维流体自由能计算的有效参考系统，具有明确的热力学稳定边界和实用的参考价值。

Abstract: We investigate the Uhlenbeck-Ford (UF) model as a reference system for free-energy calculations in two-dimensional (2D) fluids. The 2D virial coefficients are computed exactly up to tenth order and combined with molecular simulation data to construct highly accurate numerical representations of the equation of state and the excess Helmholtz free energy.We then determine the phase diagram of the model in order to establish the thermodynamic stability limits of the fluid phase and thereby identify the range of applicability of the UF model as a fluid reference system. In the course of this analysis, we identify the solid, hexatic, and fluid phases, and show that the fluid remains the only thermodynamically stable phase, independent of density, for scaling parameters up to $p\lesssim 70$. Finally, we demonstrate the practical applicability of the 2D UF model as a reference system through thermodynamic integration calculations of the free energy of a two-dimensional Lennard-Jones fluid.

</details>


### [261] [Thermodynamics with thermodynamic variable first-passage time. I. From stochastic trajectories to nonlinear transport equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24078)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文探索了外部非平衡热力学、非平衡统计算子方法和随机首达时间热力学的理论基础，提出了将亚稳态随机寿命引入广义分布函数、引入广义热力学势、基于热力学一致性条件封闭输运方程等方法，并推导了非马尔可夫过程输运记忆核的精确数学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在构建外部非平衡热力学（external nonequilibrium thermodynamics）、非平衡统计算子方法（nonequilibrium statistical operator method）和随机首达时间热力学（stochastic first passage time thermodynamics）之间的统一理论基础，并弥合这些方法在描述非平衡现象时的概念与方法鸿沟。

Method: 通过将亚稳态（metastable state）的随机寿命引入广义分布函数（generalized distribution function），将首达时间（first passage time）视为全权宏观坐标（fully-fledged macroscopic coordinate）；引入广义热力学势（generalized thermodynamic potential）；基于热力学一致性条件（thermodynamic consistency conditions）发展输运方程封闭程序；利用更新理论（renewal theory）推导非马尔可夫过程（non-Markovian processes）的输运记忆核（transport memory kernels）数学结构。

Result: 证明了在广义Maxwell–Cattaneo方程中，经典弛豫时间（classical relaxation time）严格被平均首达时间（mean first passage time）取代，从而赋予系统内部宏观非线性（internal macroscopic nonlinearity）；推导出了非马尔可夫过程输运记忆核的精确数学结构；并提供了所提出方法的微观论证（microscopic justification）。

Conclusion: 对各非平衡现象热力学描述方法进行了定性比较分析（qualitative comparative analysis），表明该统一框架能够更完整地描述亚稳态系统的非平衡热力学行为，并对输运过程的记忆效应提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: The theoretical foundation of combining external nonequilibrium thermodynamics, the nonequilibrium statistical operator method, and stochastic first passage time thermodynamics are explored. It is shown that including a random lifetime of a metastable state in the generalized distribution function allows the first passage time to be considered as a fully - fledged macroscopic coordinate. A microscopic justification for this approach is provided, and a generalized thermodynamic potential is introduced. A procedure for closing the transport equations based on on thermodynamic consistency conditions is developed. It is shown that in the generalized Maxwell - Catteneo equation, the classical relaxation time is strictly replaced by the mean first passage time, which imparts internal macroscopic nonlinearity to the system. Using renewal theory, the exact mathematical structure of transport memory kernels for non-Markovian processes is derived. A qualitative comparative analysis of various approaches to the thermodynamic description of nonequilibrium phenomena is presented.

</details>


### [262] [Thermodynamics with thermodynamic variable first-passage time. II. Dynamics of explosive boiling of superheated liquid, critical indices, fractional calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24094)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在随机首次通过时间热力学（stochastic first-passage time thermodynamics, TFPT）和幂律分布（Mittag-Leffler 分布与 Levy 分布）的框架下，论证了分数阶 Caputo 导数在流体动力学方程中的出现。将该框架应用于过热水液体的爆炸沸腾问题，推导出非局部熵产生的积分公式，并计算了耗散强度的临界指数，同时考察了外压引入后这些参数的定性变化。研究发现，给定时刻的耗散严格由宏观物质流 J(t) 在整个演化区间直至相爆炸时刻的积分历史决定。计算得到的临界耗散指数 z 精确描述了动力学灾变以及接近旋节线（spinodal）时 Prigogine 最小熵产生原理的违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为分数阶微积分在流体动力学（hydrodynamics）中的出现提供热力学基础，并借此解释过热水液体爆炸沸腾（explosive boiling）这一非平衡相变过程中的关键现象，特别是耗散行为的非局部特性与临界标度。

Method: 采用随机首次通过时间热力学（TFPT）和幂律分布（Mittag-Leffler 分布与 Levy 分布）作为理论框架，论证分数阶 Caputo 导数自然出现在流体动力学方程中；随后将框架应用于爆炸沸腾问题，推导非局部熵产生（nonlocal entropy production）的积分公式，并计算耗散强度（dissipation intensity）的临界指数 z。

Result: 推导出过热水液体爆炸沸腾中非局部熵产生的积分公式；计算了耗散强度的临界指数 z；发现引入外部压力会引起这些参数的定性变化。分析表明，瞬时耗散严格由宏观物质流 J(t) 的整个积分历史决定。临界耗散指数 z 精确描述了动力学灾变（kinetic catastrophe）以及 Prigogine 最小熵产生原理（Prigogine's minimum entropy production principle）在旋节线附近的违反。

Conclusion: 分数阶 Caputo 导数可以从 TFPT 和幂律分布的热力学框架中自然得出，为分数阶流体动力学提供了理论依据；爆炸沸腾过程中的非局部熵产生与历史依赖的耗散行为揭示了接近旋节线时 Prigogine 最小熵产生原理失效，且临界指数 z 可作为动力学灾变的主要特征量。

Abstract: Within the framework of stochastic first-passage time thermodynamics (TFPT) and power-law distributions (Mittag-Leffler and Levy), the appearance of fractional Caputo derivatives in hydrodynamic equations is substantiated. The developed framework is applied to the problem of explosive boiling of a superheated liquid: an integral formula for nonlocal entropy production is derived and the critical index of dissipation intensity is calculated, along with qualitative changes in these parameters upon introducing external pressure. It is found that dissipation at a given instant is strictly determined by the integral history of the macroscopic matter flow J(t) over the entire evolutionary interval up to the moment of phase explosion. The calculated critical dissipation index z strictly describes the kinetic catastrophe and the violation of Prigogine's minimum entropy production principle near spinodals.

</details>


### [263] [Dynamic hysteresis in an autocatalytic reaction network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24163)
*Sheela Yadav,Jason R. Green,Moupriya Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过周期驱动的Schlögl模型，证明自催化反应网络因内在弛豫时间与外部驱动周期之间的竞争而产生动态滞后（dynamic hysteresis）。滞后回线面积量化滞后响应幅度，并受驱动协议、内在涨落和系统尺寸调控。滞后现象在Shannon熵和总熵产生率中也有体现，连接了动态滞后与信息论和随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）的不可逆性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自催化反应网络中动态滞后的产生机制及其控制因素，探索其在化学逻辑门和化学计算中的潜在应用。

Method: 以周期驱动的Schlögl模型（periodically driven Schlögl model）作为代表性双稳态化学系统（bistable chemical system），通过分析滞后回线面积（hysteresis-loop area）研究驱动频率、涨落强度、驱动振幅和系统尺寸对滞后响应幅度的影响。

Result: 驱动频率和涨落强度的变化会导致滞后回线面积出现翻转（turnover），而驱动振幅和系统尺寸则单调地改变回线面积。迟滞浓度响应在Shannon熵和总熵产生率（total entropy production rate）中均有镜像反映。

Conclusion: 驱动协议和涨落强度是控制化学动态滞后幅度的主要因素；动态滞后可在更广泛的外部控制条件下表征反应系统响应，区别于随机共振（stochastic resonance）；该机制对构建高效化学逻辑门乃至化学计算机具有启示意义。

Abstract: Here we show that an autocatalytic reaction network can exhibit dynamic hysteresis as a result of the competition between its intrinsic relaxation time scale and that of the periodic drive. The autocatalytic reaction steps generate bistability in the concentration of the autocatalytic species, and periodic pumping of the product species provides the external drive. Hysteresis arises from the lag between the concentration response of the autocatalytic species and the external periodic drive. The resulting hysteresis-loop area quantifies the extent of the system's hysteretic response. Using the periodically driven Schlögl model as a representative bistable chemical system, we use this loop area to determine how the magnitude of the hysteretic response is controlled by the driving protocol, intrinsic fluctuations, and the size of the system. Varying the driving frequency and the strength of the fluctuations causes a turnover of the hysteresis loop area, whereas the area changes monotonically with driving amplitude and system size. These trends identify the driving protocol and fluctuation strength as primary controls on the magnitude of dynamic hysteresis. In contrast to stochastic resonance, which is typically associated with weak periodic forcing and noise-assisted amplification, dynamic hysteresis can characterize the reaction-system response over a wider range of external control conditions. We further show that the delayed concentration response is mirrored in Shannon entropy and in the total entropy production rate, connecting dynamic hysteresis to information-theoretic and stochastic-thermodynamic measures of irreversibility. Overall, we identify and interpret the role of the controlling factors in chemical dynamic hysteresis and suggest implications for efficient chemical logic gates and eventually, chemical computers.

</details>


### [264] [Non-Reciprocal yet Equilibrium Critical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24252)
*Emir Sezik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过场论重整化群（field-theoretic renormalization group）分析，研究了两个具有 U(n) 对称性、非互易耦合的 n-矢量序参量（n-vector order parameters）的动力学。在 ε=4-d 展开的最低阶，发现非互易耦合是 RG 无关的（RG-irrelevant），临界行为由带有 2n 个矢量分量的 Model A 普适类（universality class）的平衡不动点控制，表明非互易性本身可能不足以产生新的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管非互易相互作用（non-reciprocal interactions）在非平衡和生命系统中广泛存在且其现象学已被充分理解，但一个悬而未决的问题是：非互易相互作用是否能够诱导新的普适类（universality classes），以及在何种条件下能够实现。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 本文对两个非互易耦合的 n-矢量序参量（具有 U(n) 对称性）的动力学进行了场论重整化群（field-theoretic renormalization group, RG）分析，并将以往结果推广至多分量序参量的情形。分析在 ε=4-d 展开的最低阶进行。

Result: 在 ε=4-d 展开的最低阶，非互易耦合（non-reciprocal coupling）被证明是重整化群无关的（RG-irrelevant）。临界行为由 Hohenberg 和 Halperin 的 Model A 普适类（universality class）中带有 2n 个矢量分量的平衡不动点控制，尽管相变本身进入的是非平衡态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，非互易性（non-reciprocity）本身可能不足以诱导新的普适类。这为理解非互易相互作用在非平衡临界现象中的作用提供了重要的理论限制。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal interactions find broad applicability in non-equilibrium and living systems. Their canonical implementation involves asymmetric couplings between two entities, which generally induce spatio-temporal patterns and time-dependent steady states that break time-translational invariance, representing a clear deviation from equilibrium physics. Although their phenomenology is well understood, whether non-reciprocal interactions induce new universality classes, and if so, under what conditions, remains an open question. In the present work, we perform a field-theoretic renormalization group (RG) analysis of the dynamics of two non-reciprocally coupled $n$-vector order parameters possessing a $U(n)$ symmetry, generalizing previous results to order parameters with multiple components, a feature that has been shown to generate novel non-equilibrium critical behavior in certain non-reciprocal systems. To lowest order in $ε= 4-d$, we find that the non-reciprocal coupling is RG-irrelevant, and the critical behavior is governed by the equilibrium fixed point of the Model A universality class of Hohenberg and Halperin with $2n$ vector components, even though the transition is into a non-equilibrium state. Our results demonstrate that non-reciprocity alone may not be sufficient to induce novel universality classes.

</details>


### [265] [Amortized Posteriors for Estimation of Material Constitutive Parameters from Multimodal Measurements on Small Punch Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24534)
*Mohammad Ali Seyed Mahmoud,Aditya Venkatraman,Raj Mahat,Samantha Mitra,Surya R. Kalidindi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种免似然（likelihood-free）、摊销式（amortized）的贝叶斯框架，结合高斯过程（GP）替代模型与条件流匹配（CFM），直接从多模态合成数据中学习本构参数的条件后验分布，避免手工构造联合似然函数和重复MCMC采样，并通过小冲杆试验（SPT）与数字图像相关（DIC）数据的融合验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态力学测试数据的贝叶斯标定受限于构造跨维度、噪声结构和物理单位的联合似然函数（joint likelihood）的困难；后验分布常过于宽泛或强相关，导致标准马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器混合不良（poor mixing）。

Method: 采用免似然（likelihood-free）框架，利用高斯过程（Gaussian process, GP）替代模型与条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching, CFM）相结合，从合成的多模态参数-观测对数据中直接学习条件后验分布，避免手动设计似然函数和重复MCMC采样；训练完成后，对新样本采样成本极低。

Result: 在小冲杆试验（Small Punch Test, SPT）中，仅用力-位移（F-D）曲线数据所得后验分布较宽；加入数字图像相关（DIC）位移场数据后，后验收缩且向独立测量的拉伸参考值偏移，表明多模态融合提升了参数辨识能力。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个稳健的免似然框架，用于从多模态数据推断材料本构参数，并以SPT-DIC融合为例验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of material constitutive parameters from multimodal mechanical test data is often limited by the need to specify a joint likelihood across measurement modalities that differ in dimensionality, noise structure, and physical units. The resulting posteriors are often broad or strongly correlated, causing standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samplers to mix poorly. Here, we present an amortized, likelihood-free framework that combines Gaussian process (GP) surrogates with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) to learn conditional posteriors over constitutive parameters directly from synthetic multimodal parameter--observation pairs, avoiding hand-crafted likelihoods and repeated MCMC sampling. Once trained, the GP--CFM model generates posterior samples for each new specimen at negligible cost. The utility of this novel approach is demonstrated in this paper by estimating the values of Young's modulus and yield strength from the early portion of the force--displacement ($F$--$D$) curve and a Digital Image Correlation (DIC)-based displacement field measured in a Small Punch Test (SPT). It is observed that the $F$--$D$ data alone produce broad posteriors, consistent with limited parameter discrimination in the global response. Adding the DIC-measured displacement field was seen to contract the posteriors and shift them towards the independently measured tensile reference values. This work establishes a robust likelihood-free framework for the inference of material constitutive parameters from multimodal data, demonstrated through SPT--DIC integration.

</details>


### [266] [Control of morphology and topology in a lattice model of branching morphogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24619)
*Christian Hanauer,Frank Jülicher,Efe Ilker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于形态发生素控制的分支形态发生（morphogen-controlled branching morphogenesis）的晶格模型，该模型结合了非平衡物理学（non-equilibrium physics）与发育生物学（developmental biology）的概念。模型将细胞的随机占据动力学与细胞产生的信号分子（形态发生素）耦合，研究了从扩散受限聚集（diffusion-limited aggregation）极限到随机表面生长（Eden模型）极限等不同形态发生素浓度梯度下的生长模式，并通过局部算子引入拓扑控制，分析了分支模式的生长、退化和稳态动力学。拓扑保持的稳态团簇呈现回转半径（radius of gyration）随团簇大小的幂律标度（power-law scaling），在正方晶格和六角晶格中指数分别为0.68±0.01和0.67±0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解形态发生素如何调控分支形态发生这一发育生物学中的核心问题，需要一种能够将非平衡统计物理学方法与生物信号传导机制相结合的定量模型。现有模型往往无法同时捕捉随机细胞占据动力学与形态发生素浓度梯度之间的耦合，以及由此产生的多样化的生长标度行为。

Method: 采用晶格模型（lattice model），将细胞的随机占据动力学（stochastic occupation dynamics）与由细胞产生的信号分子（形态发生素）耦合。通过调节形态发生素浓度梯度，覆盖从扩散受限聚集极限到Eden模型极限的不同生长区间。引入局部算子（local operator）实现对拓扑结构（topology）的控制，从而研究分支图案的生长、退化及稳态动力学。

Result: 拓扑保持的稳态团簇（topology-preserving steady-state clusters）表现出回转半径与团簇大小之间的幂律标度关系：在正方晶格（square lattice）中指数为0.68±0.01，在六角晶格（hexagonal lattice）中指数为0.67±0.01。

Conclusion: 该模型成功将非平衡物理与发育生物学的思想融合，揭示了形态发生素调控下的分支形态发生可以呈现从扩散受限聚集到Eden模型极限的连续谱系，且拓扑控制下的稳态结构具有普适性的幂律标度行为。

Abstract: We present a lattice model for morphogen-controlled branching morphogenesis which combines ideas and concepts from non-equilibrium physics and developmental biology. In this model, the stochastic occupation dynamics of cells is coupled with signaling molecules (morphogens) produced by the cells. We investigate growth patterns governed by morphogen concentration gradients, spanning regimes ranging from the diffusion-limited aggregation limit to the stochastic surface growth (Eden model) limit. Moreover, we introduce control over topology by a local operator and study growth, degrowth, and steady-state dynamics of branched patterns. The topology-preserving steady-state clusters exhibit a power-law scaling of radius of gyration with cluster size, yielding the exponents $0.68\pm0.01$ in square lattice and $0.67\pm 0.01$ in hexagonal lattice.

</details>


### [267] [Fluctuation theorems for autonomous work in the quantum regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24690)
*Xiu-Hua Zhao,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将经典自治功（autonomous work）的涨落定理（fluctuation theorems）推广至量子领域，通过对功源和系统执行连续投影测量（projective measurements），从初始混合热态出发推导出Jarzynski型和Crooks型涨落定理；同时揭示了量子非对易性（noncommutativity）阻碍了非自治极限的恢复，并以Dicke模型为例进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中功的涨落定理已被推广至自治功情形（即功源动力学受系统反作用影响），但该推广在量子领域受不确定原理（uncertainty principle）制约，量子涨落定理目前多依赖外部预设的经典驱动方案，缺少对自治功的量子泛化。

Method: 通过对工作源和系统进行连续的投影测量，从初始混合热态出发，推导出自治包含功（autonomous inclusive work）的Jarzynski型和Crooks型涨落定理。在此基础上分别考察包含功定义和排除功（exclusive work）定义下非自治极限的恢复条件，并利用Dicke模型（单模辐射场与二能级原子系综分别充当系统和功源）进行说明。

Result: 推导的量子自治功涨落定理与经典情形形式类似，明确纳入了功源的涨落。但量子非对易性导致即使在功源很大、反作用可忽略的极限下，也无法一致地约化为非自治对应的形式；而在排除功定义下，当功源可观测算符与其裸哈密顿量（bare Hamiltonian）对易且系统反作用可忽略时，非自治极限得以恢复。

Conclusion: 本文成功将自治功涨落定理从经典拓展到量子领域，建立了自治与非自治涨落定理之间在量子情形下更复杂的映射关系，凸显了量子非对易性对涨落定理结构的根本影响。

Abstract: Fluctuation theorems for work provide universal constraints on nonequilibrium fluctuations, yet their quantum generalizations often rely on externally prescribed classical driving protocols. While for classical systems, fluctuation theorems have been extended to autonomous work, where the dynamics of the work source is subject to the backaction of the system, their generalization to the quantum regime is constrained by the uncertainty principle. Here, we extend fluctuation theorems for autonomous work from the classical regime to the quantum regime. By performing successive projective measurements over the work source and the system, we derive Jarzynski-type and Crooks-type fluctuation theorems for autonomous inclusive work from initial mixed thermal states. These relations are analogous to fluctuation theorems for autonomous work in the classical regime and explicitly incorporate the fluctuations of the work source. However, quantum noncommutativity prevents a consistent reduction to the nonautonomous counterparts, even in the limit of a large work source and correspondingly negligible backaction. By contrast, under the exclusive work definition, the nonautonomous limit is recovered when the measured observable of the work source commutes with its bare Hamiltonian and the backaction of the system on the work source is negligible. Our results are illustrated with the Dicke model, where a single-mode radiation field and an ensemble of two-level atoms act as the system of interest and the work source, respectively.

</details>

### [268] [CP2K: An electronic structure and molecular dynamics software package - Dynamics, Transport, and Spectroscopic Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22916)
*Jan Wilhelm,Anna Hehn,Hossam Elgabarty,Beliz Sertcan Gökmen,Maximilian Graml,Stepan Marek,Ritaj Tyagi,Frederick Stein,Johann V. Potoschnig,Augustin Bussy,Christian S. Ahart,Zehua Chen,Linnea Andersson,Zdenek Futera,Filip Ivanovic,Margherita Buraschi,Christoph Schran,Remi Pasquier,Leonard Prokisch,Bibek Samal,Jelena Schmitz,Shridhar Sanjay Shanbhag,Harald Forbert,Ole Schütt,Franz Pöschl,Sebastian Ehlert,Stefano Battaglia,Michele Nottoli,Benjamin Stamm,Vamsee Voora,Rustam Z. Khaliullin,Sergey K. Chulkov,Matt B. Watkins,Clotilde S. Cucinotta,Jochen Blumberger,Chao Zhang,Yang Yang,Dominik Marx,Matthias Krack,Jürg Hutter,Marcella Iannuzzi,Thomas D. Kühne*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了CP2K软件包中连接电子结构计算与动力学、输运及光谱响应的方法，重点阐述了静态响应与核运动之间的耦合机制，展示了该软件在统一量子化学与量子及统计力学方面的独特能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期代码综述的基础上，进一步阐明CP2K如何将电子结构计算转化为动力学、输运及光谱响应能力，特别强调静态响应计算与核运动之间的耦合。

Method: CP2K通过高斯-平面波（Gaussian and plane-wave）框架及其全电子推广方法实现高精度电子结构计算，并基于模块化结构耦合静态响应计算与核运动，支持从优化结构、热平均构型、时间关联函数等多种途径计算光谱。

Result: CP2K实现了多种光谱计算路径（优化结构、热平均构型、从头算或路径积分分子动力学轨迹的时间关联函数、实时电子与核动力学），以及从Kubo型线性响应到外势下开放边界方法的平衡及偏压输运模拟。

Conclusion: CP2K将量子化学与量子及统计力学统一于一个多功能、整体的模拟环境中，展现出独特的综合模拟能力。

Abstract: One of the distinguishing aspects of CP2K is its seamless integration of diverse structural and transition-state optimization techniques with advanced sampling approaches including Monte Carlo, molecular dynamics, and metadynamics, enabling the efficient exploration of complex potential- and free-energy landscapes, including rare events. These capabilities are combined with a broad hierarchy of energy and force evaluation methods, ranging from classical and machine-learned interaction potentials and mixed quantum-classical multiscale and semiempirical schemes, to highly accurate quantum-mechanical electronic-structure approaches. At the heart of the latter lies the Gaussian and plane-wave framework, along with its augmented all-electron generalization, which have been described in detail in our previous code review [T. D. Kühne et al., J. Chem. Phys. 152, 194103 (2020)]. Building on this foundation, the present work revisits the methods within CP2K that turn electronic structure into dynamics, transport, and spectroscopic response. Particular emphasis is placed on the coupling between static response calculations and nuclear motion: spectra may be evaluated at optimized structures, averaged over thermally sampled configurations, obtained from time-correlation functions along ab-initio or path integral molecular trajectories, or followed in real time together with electronic and nuclear dynamics. The same modular structure also enables equilibrium and biased transport simulations, from Kubo-type linear response to open-boundary approaches under external potentials, highlighting CP2K's unique capability to unify quantum chemistry with quantum and statistical mechanics within a versatile, holistic simulation environment.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

### [269] [Gravitational GateWave: Gravitational waves as LFO modulation amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22796)
*Carlos Darío Badilla Cerdas*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一款音频门限插件（audio gate plugin），该插件使用LIGO和Virgo探测器真实的引力波应变数据（gravitational wave strain data）作为低频振荡器（low frequency oscillator, LFO）调制源。与传统基于固定谐波含量的合成波形不同，该插件利用致密双星并合（compact binary coalescences）产生的引力波啁啾信号（chirps），将其时标缩放至LFO频率范围（0.1–10 Hz），从而产生谐波结构持续演化的调制信号，展示了天体物理信号可作为具有音乐表现力的调制源，连接声化（sonification）与创意音频处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统音频门限依赖合成波形（如正弦波、三角波、锯齿波），其固定谐波含量导致可预测的泵浦效果（pumping effects），缺乏谐波演化的丰富性。研究者希望探索真实天体物理信号（引力波啁啾）是否能为音频调制提供更具动态性和音乐表现力的替代方案。

Method: 实现了一个音频门限插件，将LIGO和Virgo探测器记录的引力波应变数据作为LFO调制源。引力波啁啾信号在亚秒时间尺度上扫过频率和振幅，经时标缩放至LFO速率（0.1–10 Hz）后，用作调制信号，产生谐波结构连续演化的效果。

Result: 该插件成功表明，天体物理信号（引力波啁啾）在时标缩放后可作为具有音乐吸引力的调制源，其谐波结构持续演化，区别于传统合成波形的固定谐波特征。

Conclusion: 该工作搭建了声化（sonification）与创意音频处理之间的桥梁，证明真实引力波数据不仅能用于科学分析，还可作为创意音频工具中富有表现力的调制来源。

Abstract: We present an audio gate plugin that uses real gravitational wave strain data from the LIGO and Virgo detectors as low frequency oscillator (LFO) modulation sources. Conventional audio gates rely on synthetic waveforms, sine, triangle, sawtooth, whose fixed harmonic content produces predictable pumping effects. By contrast, gravitational wave chirps from compact binary coalescences sweep through frequency and amplitude on subsecond timescales; when time scaled to LFO rates (0.1 to 10)Hz, these chirps yield modulation signals with continuously evolving harmonic structure. The plugin demonstrates that astrophysical signals can serve as musically compelling modulation sources, bridging sonification and creative audio processing.

</details>


### [270] [Gravitational DePhaser: A Temporal Gravitational Lensing Model for Creative Audio Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22873)
*Carlos Darío Badilla Cerdas*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文受引力透镜（gravitational lensing）启发，提出一种将音频信号视为波包在时间畸变势中传播的新模型，通过生成多条具有不同延迟、相位和增益的有效传播路径，实现可控的相长干涉（constructive interference）和相消干涉（destructive interference），以模拟经典延迟、合唱和混响效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的延迟、合唱和混响效果依赖于离散延迟或统计分布来模拟多条传播路径。本文旨在提出一种基于物理类比的新框架，以更系统的方式生成多条传播路径并控制其干涉效应。

Method: 将音频信号视为波包（wave packet），使其在时间畸变势（temporally distorted potential）中传播（而非物理时空中的传播）。通过该时间畸变势的引入，自然产生多条具有不同延迟、相位和增益的有效传播路径，从而实现对相长干涉和相消干涉的调控。

Result: 该方法能够生成多条有效的传播路径，其延迟、相位和增益均可控，并产生可控的相长干涉与相消干涉。

Conclusion: 本文提出的引力透镜启发模型为音频效果处理提供了一种新的理论框架，能够在统一机制下实现包括延迟、合唱和混响在内的经典效果，并允许对干涉效应进行精细化控制。

Abstract: Classical delay, chorus, and reverb effects model multiple pro- pagation paths using discrete delays or statistical distributions. This work proposes a model inspired by gravitational lensing, where an audio signal is treated as a wave packet traversing a temporally distorted potential rather than physical spacetime. The distortion generates multiple effective propa- gation paths with different delays, phases, and gains, producing controlled constructive and destructive interference.

</details>


### [271] [Extreme outbreaks in non-Markovian epidemics on complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24615)
*Ami Taitelbaum,Michael Assaf*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将网络上的非马尔可夫SIR传播映射为有效马尔可夫过程，推导出暴发规模分布的右尾（即极端风险），并证明该映射可通过单一边传播率（edge transmissibility）统一描述任意感染与恢复时间统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端流行病风险由暴发规模分布（outbreak-size distribution）的右尾控制，但非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）传播过程在网络上的该分布通常未知。

Method: 将非马尔可夫SIR动力学映射为有效马尔可夫（effective Markovian）描述，通过单一边传播率（edge transmissibility）纳入任意感染与恢复时间统计量；对弱异质性网络进一步约化为由键逾渗再生数（bond-percolation reproductive number）支配的均匀混合半经典理论。

Result: 对于弱异质性网络，不同等待时间分布与拓扑结构下的暴发统计数据均坍塌至同一预测曲线；对于强异质性与实证网络，对应的有效马尔可夫动力学在网络上可捕捉完整的暴发规模分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了一条从实测等待时间分布到网络层面极端暴发风险定量预测的直接路径。

Abstract: Extreme epidemic risk is controlled by the right tail of the outbreak-size distribution, but this distribution is generally unknown for non-Markovian spreading on networks. Here we determine this distribution by mapping non-Markovian SIR dynamics to an effective Markovian description. We show that arbitrary infection and recovery time statistics can be incorporated through a single edge transmissibility, yielding an effective Markovian process that reproduces the full outbreak-size statistics. For weakly heterogeneous networks, the reduction yields a universal well-mixed semiclassical theory governed by the bond-percolation reproductive number. Outbreak statistics across diverse waiting-time distributions and topologies collapse onto one predictive curve. For highly heterogeneous and empirical networks, the corresponding effective Markovian dynamics on the network captures the complete distribution. Our results provide a direct route from measured waiting-time distributions to quantitative predictions of network-level extreme-outbreak risk.

</details>
